Ārya Mañjuśrīye kumārabhūtāya namaḥ
[Abhyarcanam—Śāstraśarīram]
uttamajanā hi prāyaśo guruṁ śraddhādevatāṁ cābhyarcya karmasu pravartanta ity ayam apy aham uttamajananayaṁ anuvartī Madhyāntavibhāgasūtrabhāṣyaṁ cikīrṣur [iti jñāpanārthaṁ] tatpraṇetur vaktuś ca pūjāṁ kṛtvā tadarthavibhāgāya prayukta iti pratipādayann āha
śāstrasyāsya praṇetāram ityādi evaṁ kṛtvā ko guṇaḥ prāpyata iti guṇavadabhyo hitakāribhyaś ca pūjyānānāṁ eṣāṁ puṇyam upacīyate puṇya upacite samārambhād vighnavinayakair anupahatān alpena prayāsena samāpayantīti
atha vā praṇetṛpraṇeya[vaktṛvākyasamādāna]pravacanāt sūtrapraṇetṛvaktṛvṛttiṣu gauravotpādanārtham āha[2]
śāstrasyāsya praṇetāram iti sarvam |
tatra praṇetṛā vaktum upadiṣṭāt sūtre gauravam utpadyate |
yasmād asya kārikāśāstrasyāryaMaitreyaḥ praṇetā |
sa caikajātipratibaddhāt sarvabodhisattvābhijñādhāraṇīpratisamvitsamādhindriyakṣānticittokṣaiḥ paramampāramgataḥ sarvāsu bodhisattvabhūmiṣu niḥśeṣam api prahīṇāvaraṇaḥ |
vaktṛvisamādānadovāreṇa vṛttyām gauravam utpadyate |
vaktā punar atrācāryaAsaṅgas tasmāc chrutvācāryabhadantaVasubandhuḥ tadbhāṣyam akarot |
tāv apy uttamaprajñāvato 'bhrāntiprativedhadhāraṇopadeśasāmarthyād atra sūtrārtho 'bhrāntam upadiṣṭa iti vṛttyām gauravam utpadyate |
evam ye pudgalām pramāṇīkurvanti teṣām sūtravṛttigāuravopattiḥ |
ye ca dharmānusāriṇas teṣām sūtravṛttyoḥ śubhārthe 'vabodhaḥ |
jāte ca niścaye iyam praṇetur vaktuś ca pratijñā prabhāvanā na tu tarkāgamamātreṇa prabhāvaneti praṇetṛvaktṛviśgauravotpattiḥ |
idam idānim vaktavyam kiḍṛśam śāstrarūpam |
śāstram kim ceti nāmapadavyañjanakāyaprabhāsā vijñaptayaḥ śāstram |
atha vā lokottarajñānaprāpakaśabdaviśeṣaprabhāsā vijñaptayaḥ śāstram |
katham vijñaptayaḥ praṇīyanta ucyante vā |
praṇetṛvaktṛvijñaptiprabhavatvāt prajñaptīnām nātra doṣaḥ |
śiṣyaśīlārmako hi cilasamādhiprajñāviśeṣotpāditatvāt kāyavāṅmanasām[3]
saṃbhārānutpattikarmaṇo nivartate saṃbhārotpattikarmaṇi ca pravartate |
atha vā śāstralakṣaṇasya śāsanāc chāstram |
tac ca śāstralakṣaṇaṃ yad upadeśo bhāṣamāṇo bhyastaḥ |
savāsanākleśaprahāṇāya padyate nirantaradīrghavicudhatīvraduhkhabhūyāś ca durgater bhavāc ca saṃtrāyaḥ |
tasmāt kleśaripuśāsanāt bhavadurgatisaṃtārāc ca śāstralakṣaṇam |
etac ca dvayam api sarvasmin mahāyāne sarvasmiṃś ca tadvyākhyāne vidyate nānyatreṃ |
ata etac chāstram |
āha ca |
yac chāsti ca kleśaripūn aśeṣān saṃtrāyate durgatito bhavāc ca |
tac chāsanāt trāṇaguṇāc ca śāstram etad dvayaṃ cānyamateṣu nāsti ||
asyeti triyānadvāreṇa saptavaṣṭusaṃgrahasya kleśajñeyāvaraṇaprahīṇaprāpakasya Madhyāntavibhāgakārikāśāstrasya hṛidi sthitatvād asyeti pratyakṣopadeśaḥ |
praṇetāram iti kartāram |
yady apy ayaṃ dhātuḥ prāpaṇārthas tathāpi "pra" śabdasaṃyogād viśeṣārthako draṣṭavyaḥ |
uktaṃ hi
upasargeṇa dhātvarṭho balād anyatra nīpate |
gaṅgāsalilāmādhuryaṃ sāgareṇa yathāmbhasā ||
suvratātmajaṃ iti suṣṭhuvrataḥ savisanākleśāvaraṇāj jñey[4]
āvaraṇāc cāpratisthitaṁ nirvāṇam iti sugataḥ |
sa ca sarvavāsanāvaraṇaprahīṇaḥ sarvathā sarvadharmāvabodhasvarūpaḥ sarvavibhūtyāśrayabhūtaś cintāmaniratnavad acintyaprabhāvavigrahaḥ sarvasattvānām anābhogena sarvārtharaṇasamartho nirvikalpakajñānaviśeṣātmakaḥ sugataḥ |
tadātmatvaṁ viśuddhitathatī tajjanitatvān nirvikalpasya jñānasya tasmāt tasmin vā jātaḥ sugatātmajaḥ |
atha vā sugatātmanā jāta iti sugatātmajaḥ |
yathoktatṁ sūtrāntare jāto bhavati tathāgatavaṁśe tadātmakavastu prablābhād iti |
evaṁ sati bodhisattvasya dṛśmabhūmau pratiṣṭhitasya sarvākāraṁ jñeyaṁ vastu karatalastham īvāmalakiphalāṁ śukāvacchāditalocanaasyevābhāsam āyāti |
bhagavataḥ punar apanitalocanivaraṇasyevety ayaṁ viśeṣaḥ |
atra hi sugatātmajas tasyaiva śāstrapraṇayanasyāvabodhasaṁpat pradarśitā tābhasatkāraṇirapekṣasya śāstrapraṇetṛtvena karuṇāsaṁpat prajñāsaṁpac eeti |
vaktāram iti vyākhyānasya kartāram abhyarhyeti saṁbadhyate |
sugatātmaja ity apity apare |
sa punar āryaAsaṅgaḥ |
tasya hidaṁ śāstram abhivyaktam ākhyātaṁ cāryaMaitreyādhiṣṭhānād dharmasaṁtānena |
eti samuccaye pādapūraṇe 'dhikavacane vā |
anyān api buddhabodhisattvān arcayitvā na kevalaṁ praṇetāraṁ vaktāraṁ[5]
ceti |
kebhyo vaktāraṃ 'asmadādibhya iti vayam ādir yeṣāṃ te 'smadādayas tebhyo 'smadādibhyaḥ |
anenātmano 'mṛṣāyā upadeśo bhāṣamāṇo nirdiṣṭaḥ |
abhyarhyety abhyarcya |
abhitaḥ purataḥ sākṣād iva sthitam |
arhitvā arcayitvā kāyavāṅmanobhiḥ |
śāstrasya praṇetāraṃ
(2, b) vaktāraṃ [Tib. 20, b] cābhyarcya kiṃ kariṣyasīty āha |
yatiṣye arthavivecana iti |
yatnam ārapsye arthavivecane arthavivāraṇe pṛthagbhāvakaraṇe vā |
iyaṃ ca nimittārthā saptamī |
arthavivecananimittam ity arthaḥ |
arthaḥ punar atra śāstraśarīraṃ iti te saptārthā asmāñ śāstra ākhyāyanta iti vacanāt |
kimartham idaṃ śāstraṃ praṇītaṃ
buddhānāṃ bhagavatāṃ samyaṅnirvikalpajiñānopādānārtham |
dharmanairātmyadeśanatayā nirvikalpajiñānotpādāt tadabhyāsāc ca niḥśeṣasavāsanākleśajñeyāvaraṇaprahāṇam avāpyate |
ayaṃ punar dharmanairātmyasya virodhavādo yat sarvadharmarahitatā
dharmanairātmyam antaravyāpārapuruṣavyatiriktatā ca dharmanairātmyam ity atas tatpratisedhena tathābhūtanairātmyapratipādanārthaṃ śāstrārambhaḥ |
lakṣaṇāvaraṇādiṣv apratipannavipratipannānāṃ samyagavabodhotpādanād apratipattivipratipattyapanayārtham ity anye |
atha vā pañcākārajñeye lokadhātusattvadharmaviśeṣopāyadhātvātmake pratyekaṃ anantaprabhedatvād durvijñeyam iti bodhisattvānāṃ yaś cittasaṃkocas tadapanayanārtham āha |
lakṣaṇaṃ hy āvṛttis tattvam[6]
iti |
tatrāditaḥ śāstraśariravyavasthāpanam iti tatreti śāstrārthavivecane śāstre vā |
ādita iti prathamnata eva |
śāstraṁ vicaraṇam |
taccarariraṁ saṁkṣepaḥ piṇḍārtho vāśrayārthena vā śariram |
yathā hi bāhyādhyātmikāyatanāśrayaḥ kāyaḥ śariram ity ucyate |
evaṁ yān arthān niśritya śāstraṁ pravartate te 'rthās tasya śariram |
te ca saptārthā lakṣaṇādayaḥ |
vyavasthāpanam iti prajñaptir ucyate 'ākhyānam ity arthaḥ |
nanu śāstrāvabodhenāiva tac chariraṁ vijñāsyata iti nirarthakam ādau tadvyavasthāpanam |
na nirarthakaṁ śiṣyānugrahārthatvād gatārtho hi śiṣyaḥ sukhaṁ vistaram ucyamānaṁ pratipadyate |
dṛṣṭā bhūmiḥ nihśaṅkam aśvavāhanam iva nānyatheti |
ete saptārthā asmiñ śāstre uddiṣṭā iti śāstraśariraṁ samāptam ity arthaḥ |
eta iti ye lakṣaṇādaya uddiṣṭāḥ |
sapteti saṁkhyā paryantādhigamārtham upādānam |
arthyanta ity arthā adhigamyanta iti yāvat |
asmiñ śāstra iti Madhyāntavibhāgasaṁjñake |
uddiṣṭā ity upadiṣṭā viniścitā vā |
tathā hiti tadarthapratipādananipātaḥ |
lakṣaṇam iti lakṣyante 'neneti lakṣaṇam |
tac ca dvividhaṁ saṁkleśalakṣaṇaṁ vyavadānalakṣaṇaṁ ca |
tatra saṁkleśalakṣaṇaṁ navavidham |
abhūtaparikalpo 'sti
ity ārabhya yāvat
saptadhābhūtakalpanāt ||
iti |
śeṣeṇārdhena vyavadānalakṣaṇam ākhyātam |
yadi lakṣyante 'neneti lakṣaṇam ity evaṁ sati lakṣaṇasya saṁkleśavyavadānabhedo bhaved iti naitad e(3, a)vam |
svabhāva eva hi bhāvānāṁ[7]
lakṣaṇam tad yathā pṛthivīdhātuḥ kharalakṣaṇo na ca kharatvāt pṛthivīdhātuḥ pṛthag astīti atha vā lakṣyate tad iti lakṣaṇam
evaṃ hi saṃkleśo vyavadānaṃ ca saṃkleśavyavadānātmatvena lakṣyata iti lakṣaṇam
atha vā dvayoḥ saṃkleśavyavadānayor lakṣaṇaṃ dvividhaṃ svalakṣaṇaṃ ca sāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ ca
āvaraṇam iti idam āvṛṇoti kuśalān dharmān
vriyante ānena kuśaladharmā utpattivibandhaṃ ity āvaraṇam
tat punas tripañcāśatprakāram
tattvam iti tad evedaṃ tat
tasya bhāvas tattvam aviparyāsa ity arthaḥ tac ca daśaprakāram
vipakṣaprahāṇārthaṃ pakṣaḥ pratipakṣaḥ sa mārgaḥ
tadabhyāso bhāvanā
avasthā tasyaiva saṃtānenotpattiviśeṣaḥ
sā punar ekānnaviṃśatiprakārā gotrāvasthādikā
phalasya lābhaḥ phalaprāptiḥ
tat punaḥ pañcadaśaprakāraṃ vipākaphalaādi
yānānuttaryam iti gamyate 'nena yānam
yānaṃ ca tad ānuttaryaṃ ceti yānānuttaryam
tat punas trividhaṃ pratipattyānuttaryādi
saptamo 'rtha iti niyamārtham anukramārthaṃ cāha
etāvanta evārthā nirdiśyante nāto 'nya iti
anukramaḥ punar ayaṃ lokottarajñānānukūlārtham
tathā hi bodhisattvenādhimukticaryābhūmisthitena śīlapratiṣṭhitena prathamaṃ saṃkleśavyavadānakuśalena bhavitavyam
tataḥ kuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ yasya yad āvaraṇaṃ tasya taj jñeyam
tasyāprahāṇād vimukter asaṃbhavād avijñānāṃ ca prahātuṃ na śakyate doṣādarśanāt
tatas tadāvaraṇād yenālambane cittaṃ vimuryate tat tattvaṃ veditavyam
tatas tenālambane tad[8]
āvaraṇaṁ yena prayogeṇa kṣayo bhavati sā pratipakṣabhāvanā jñeyā |
tatas tasyāṁ pratipakṣabhāvanāyāṁ vipakṣahānyā pratipakṣavṛddhyā cāvasthā jñeyā gotrāvasthādikā |
tato lokottaradharma ābhimukhīkaraṇaṁ phalāni |
srotaāpannaphalādīni veditavyāni |
sarvaṁ apy etat sottarāc chrāvakādibhiḥ sādhāraṇaṁ bodhisattvānāṁ yathoktaṁ sūtre sa pravrajitaḥ śrāvakaśikṣācāragocarasamudācāram api śikṣate pratyekabuddhaśikṣācāragocarasamudācāram api śikṣate bodhisattvaśikṣācāragocarasamudācāram api śikṣate |
bodhisattvānāṁ tv āmuttaryaṁ asādhāraṇaṁ saptamo 'rtha āmuttaryaṁ |
anyas tv āha |
saṁkleśavyavadānalakṣaṇe kauśalopādanārtham ādau lakṣaṇam |
tatra yaḥ saṁkleśas tad āvaraṇam |
yad vyavadānaṁ tat tattvam |
āvaraṇaprahāṇaṁ ca tattvādhigamād bhavatīty ata āvaraṇatattvam |
tatprahāṇopāyapradarśanārthaṁ tataḥ saparīvārapratipakṣo mārgaḥ |
tasya mārgasyāṅmadhyānteṣu mṛdumadhyādhimātraprabhedānāṁ pradarśanārthaṁ tatrāvasthā |
avasthānurūpaṁ phalam āvahatīti tadanantaraṁ phalam |
etac ca sarvaṁ bodhisattvasya śrāvakādisādhāraṇam ity asādhāraṇamahāyānanayaprakāśanārthaṁ yānānuttaryam iti |
aparaḥ punar āhuḥ |
sadasallakṣaṇajñāpanārthaṁ prathamato lakṣaṇam ākhyātaṁ |
lakṣaṇaṁ jñātvāvaraṇaṁ prahātavyaṁ tattvaṁ ca sākṣātkatavyam iti tadanantaram āvaraṇatattvam |
tayoś ca prahāṇasākṣātkaraṇayor ayam upāya iti pratipakṣabhāvanā |
tasyāś ca tāratamyam avasthāviśeṣaḥ |
tat kṛtaṁ ca prahāṇaṁ phalam |
tadanantaraṁ yānānuttaryam iti prakāśanārtham ayam anukrama iti |[9]
anyac ca sattvasya sadasaddharmsammohasya parvadasamaropaprahinartham laksananirdesah prahınasammohasya avaranakaucalartham avaranam tenavrtam tattvam iti tattvakaucalartham tadanantaram tattvam tattvapratividdhabhavana yavaranaprahanam bhavati tattvanamtaram pratipaksabhavana
tatprabhedakaucalartham avastha avasthaya phalam prabhavitum
ity avasthanantaram phalam tatkaucalotpadanartham sarvam
etan mahayanam agamyeti yananuttaryam ante nirdisam[10]
tatra lakṣaṇam ārabhyāha
abhūtaparikalpo 'sti dvayaṃ tatra na vidyate |
śūnyatā vidyate tv atra tasyām api sa vidyate ||
iti |
tatreti lakṣaṇāvaraṇādisaptabhāvanirdiṣṭeṣu lakṣaṇam adhikṛtyoddīcyāha |
abhūtaparikalpo 'sti
ityādi |
yathoddeśas tathā nirdeśa iti kṛtvā lakṣaṇaṃ ca prāg uddiṣṭam atas tasyaiva śeṣebhyo nirdeśaḥ prāg ārabhyate |
ke cid virundhanti sarvadharmāḥ sarvathā niḥsvabhāvāḥ śaśaviṣāṇavad ity ataḥ sarvāpavādapratiṣedhārtham āha
abhūtaparikalpo 'sti
iti |
svabhāvata iti vākyaśeṣaḥ |
nanv evaṃ sūtravirodhaḥ sarvadharmāḥ śūnyā iti sūtre vacanāt |
nāsti virodhaḥ |
yasmād
dvayaṃ tatra na vidyate |
abhūtaparikalpo hi grāhyagrāhakasvabhāvarahitaḥ śūnya ucyate na tu sarvathā niḥsvabhāvaḥ |
ato na sūtravirodhaḥ |
yady evaṃ dvayaṃ śaśaviṣāṇavat sarvathā nāsti |
abhūtaparikalpaś ca paramārthataḥ svabhāvato 'sty evaṃ śūnyatā 'bhāvaprasaṅgaḥ |
naitat evaṃ yasmāc[11]
chūnyatā vidyate tv atra
iyam eva hi śūnyatā yā grāhyagrāhakarahitatā 'bhūtaparikalpa iti na śūnyatā nāstitvaṁ bhavati
yady adśayā śūnyatā sā cābhūtaparikalpe 'sti kathaṁ vayam amuktāḥ
vidyamānā ca kasmān na gṛhṇata iti saṁśayāpanayanārthaṁ
tasyām api sa vidyate
ity āha
yasmic chūnyatāyām apy abhūtaparikalpo vidyate tasmād bhavanto na muktāḥ
ata eva ca samalatvan na prasannātdhātuvad avagantum yuktam
atha vā cittacaittebhyo 'nyatra rūpādayo dravyatena santīti yeṣāṁ darśanaṁ tatpratiṣedhārtham āha
abhūtaparikalpo 'sti
dravyataḥ
nāsti rūpaṁ tadvyatiriktaṁ
dravyata iti
kiṁ kāraṇaṁ yasmād
dvayaṁ tatra na vidyate
na hy abhūtaparikalpaḥ kasya cid grāhako nāpi kena cid gṛhyate
kiṁ tarhi
grāhyagrāhakarahitaṁ bhāvamātram eva
yato vijñānād bāhye rūpādayo na gṛhyante svapnādivad vijñānaṁ rūpādyābhāsam utpadyate
na ca yady asya kāraṇaṁ tadabhāve tasyotpattir yujyate
tasmān nirālambanam eva svapnādāv iva
anyatrāpi svabījapāripākād arthābhāsaṁ vijñānam utpadyata ity avaseyam
grāhyābhāve grāhakasyābhāvād grāhye 'sati grāhakasyo 'stum na yujyate
tasmān na rūpam abhūtaparikalpāt pṛthag asti
yadi tarhi grāhyābhāvas tena viśuddhyālambanābhāvān mokṣābhāvaḥ
naitad evaṁ yasmāc[12]
śūnyatā vidyate tv atra
tu 'śabdo yasmādarthaḥ |
śūnyatā hi viśuddhyālambanā sā ca grāhyagrāhakarahitatā 'bhūtaparikalpe 'stīti na mokṣābhāvaḥ |
yady abhūtaparikalpe 'sti sā vidyamānā kimarthaṃ na gṛhyate |
abhūtaparikalpāvṛtatvān na gṛhyata ākāśanairmalyavat na tv asatvād iti pratipādayann āha
tasyām api sa vidyata iti ||
atha vā sarvāpavādapratiṣedhārtham āha
abhūtaparikalpo 'sti
iti |
na sarvam asan nāpi sadātmakaṃ vijñānapariṇāmātmanā astīti kṛtvā |
ye tu manyante yathaiva rūpādayaḥ prakhyāyante tathaivābhūtaparikalpāt svabhāvataḥ pṛthag vidyanta iti tān pratyāha abhūtasamāropapratiṣedhārthaṃ
dvayaṃ tatra na vidyate |
iti abhūtaparikalpamātraṃ vidyata ity abhiprāyaḥ |
kena cid dvayam abhāvo vandhyāputravac chedarūpo gṛhyate anyair antarvyāpārapuruṣarahitatā dharmāṇāṃ śūnyatety ucyate |
ataḥ śūnyatāpavādapratiṣedhārthaṃ bhūtanairātmya[Tib. 24, a]khyāpanārthaṃ cāha
śūnyatā vidyate tv atra
iti |
yady abhūtaparikalpe śūnyatāsty evaṃ sati sarvapraṇiṇām aprayatnena mokṣaprasaṅgaḥ |
nāsty etad yasmāt
tasyām api sa vidyate |
na hy aviśodhitāyāṃ śūnyatāyāṃ mokṣo 'sti saṃkliṣṭaś ca mahatā yatnena viśodhyata iti nāsty ayatnena mokṣaḥ |
atha vā lakṣaṇaṃ saṃkleśavyavadānābhidhānād anyaṃ nāstīty ataḥ saṃkleśavyavadānalakṣaṇaparīkṣārtham āha |[13]
abhūtapari(4, b)kalpo 'sti
iti vistaraḥ |
abhūtaparikalpasvadbhāvaḥ saṁkleśo bhrāntilakṣaṇatvāt |
katham etaj jñātavyaṁ bhrāntilakṣaṇam iti yena dvayaṁ tatra na vidyate |
svātmany avidyamānena grāhyagrāhakakāreṇa prakhyānād bhrāntiśvarūpeṇa jñāyate |
idānīṁ vyavadānaśvarūpoparīkṣārtham āha
śūnyatā vidyate tv atra
iti |
śūnyatāsvabhāvo hi vyavadānaṁ dvayābhāvasvabhāvatvāt |
atra ca śūnyatāprabhāvitatvād mārganirodhayor api grahaṇaṁ veditavyam |
saṁkleśapakṣād eva vyavadānapakṣo mārgayitavyo na punaḥ pṛthaktvam asyāstīti pradarśanārtham āha atreti |
yadi dvayaṁ nāsti kathaṁ sā vidyamānā loko bhrānta iti pṛṣṭam
ataś cāha
tasyām api sa vidyate ||
iti
grāhyagrāhakavikalpaḥ |
hastyādyākāraśūnyamāyāyām iva hastyākārādayaḥ |
abhūtam asmin dvayaṁ parikalpyate 'nena vety abhūta[Tib. 24, b]parikaḷpaḥ |
"abhūta" vacanena ca yathāyaṁ parikalpyate grāhyagrāhakatveṇa tathā nāstīti pradarśayati |
"parikalpa" vacanena tv artho yathā parikalpyate tathārtho na vidyata iti pradarśayati |
evam asya grāhyagrāhakavinimuktaṁ lakṣaṇaṁ paridīpitaṁ bhavati |
kaḥ punar asau |
atītānāgatavartamānā hetuphalabhūtās traidhātukā anādidhkālikā nirvāṇaparyavasānāḥ saṁsārānurūpāś cittavṛttā aviśeṣeṇābhūtaparikalpaḥ |[14]
vigeṣatas tu grāhyagrāhakavikalpaḥ |
tatra grāhyavikalpaḥ |
arthasatvapratibhāsaṁ vijñānam |
grāhakavikalpa ātmavijñaptipratibhāsam |
dvayaṁ grāhyaṁ grāhakaṁ ceti tatra grāhyaṁ rūpādi |
grāhakaṁ cakṣurvijñānādi |
grāhyagrāhakabhāvena rahitatā viviktatā hy abhūtaparikalpasya śūnyatā |
na tv abhūtaparikalpo 'py abhāvaḥ |
yathā śūnyā rajjuḥ sarpasyabhāvenātatsvabhāvatvāt sarvakālaṁ śūnyā na tu rajjuḥ svabhāvena |
tathehāpi |
tasyām api sa vidyata iti |
abhūtaparikalpaḥ |
'evaṁ hy āgantukāvaraṇopakliṣṭatvāt tad agrahaṇam uktam ābdhātvādivad iti |
evaṁ yasmin yan nāsti tat tena śūnyam iti samanupaśyatīti |
kiṁ kasmin nāsti |
abhūtaparikalpe dvayam |
ato 'bhūtaparikalpaṁ dvayena śūnyaṁ paśyati |
yat punar atrāvaśiṣṭaṁ tat sat |
kiṁ punar ihāvaśiṣṭam |
abhūtaparikalpaḥ śūnyatā ca |
tadubhayam
[Tib.25,a] ihāstīty anadhyāropānapavādena paśyan yathā bhūtaṁ prajānāti |
tatrābhūtaparikalpe 'dvayadarśanād anadhyāropaḥ |
abhūtaparikal̥pasya śūnyatā(5,a)yāś cāstitvadarśanād anapavādaḥ |
aviparitaṁ śūnyatālakṣaṇam udbhāvitaṁ bhavati |
yac chūnyaṁ tasya sadbhāvāt |
yena śūnyaṁ tasya tatrābhāvāt |
sarvabhāve sarvābhāve tu nāviparitaṁ śūnyatālakṣaṇam |
śūnyatāyā evābhāvaprasaṅgāt |
na śūnyasaṁjñāyām asatyāṁ śūnyatā yogyā |
dharmatāyā hi bhāvāyattatvād anityatvādivat |
dvayam astīti śūnyatābhāvaḥ |
yadi śaśaviṣāṇakalpanena dvayaṁ kathaṁ tenābhūtaparikalpasya śūnyatāsaṁbhavo 'nyena hy anyasya śūnyatā dṛṣṭā |
yathā bhikṣubhir vihāraṁ syān naitat evam |
yathā rajjur māyā vā svātmany avidyamānena sarpākāreṇa puruṣādinā prabhāsate |
tatra kasya cid grāhaṇivartamārthaṁ sarpapuruṣādinā[15]
śūnyety ucyate |
evam abhūtaparikalpo 'pi svātmany avidyamānena grāhyagrāhakākāreṇa prakhyāyamāno bālānāṃ tadgrāhābhiniveśatyājanārthaṃ dvayena śūnyam ity ucyate |
na śūnyaṃ nāpi vāśūnyam |
iti kimartham asau śloka āruḍha iti |
sarvaṃ saṃskṛtam asaṃskṛtaṃ ca vāgratakaṃyatraṃ jñāpanārtham |
evaṃ ca hy ekāntapratikṣepeṇa sarvaṃ idaṃ na śūnyaṃ nāśūnyam iti Prajñāpāramitāpāṭhābhiprāyatā niṣkṛtā bhavati |
anyathā pūrvāparaviruddham etat syāt |
kiṃ ca madhyamā pratipatprakāśanārtham |
anyathaikāntika śūnyatāiva syāt |
apavādasamāropapratisedhopasamhārārthaṃ vāsyāraṃbhaḥ |
hetupratyayapratibandhātmalābhād abhūtaparikalpaḥ saṃskṛtam |
śūnyatā punas tannirapekṣatvād asaṃskṛtam |
vidhīyate Prajñāpāramitādinmadhye paṭhyate |
sattvād ity abhūtaparikalpasya na saṃskṛtaṃ śūnyam abhūtaparikalpātmatvena |
asattvād iti dvayasya |
grāhyagrāhakātmanā śūnyam |
śūnyatāyās tu sarvam abhūtaparikalpe taddharmateti kṛtvā śūnyatāyām apy abhūtaparikalpo dharmīrūpeṇa vidyate |
evam asaṃskṛtam api dharmātārūpeṇa na śūnyam |
abhāvāsaṃjñākena dvayena svarūpaśūnyam |
sā hi madhyamā pratipad iti Ratnakūṭakmadhye 'stīti
Kāśyapāyām eko 'ntaḥ |
nāstīty ayaṃ dvitīyo 'ntaḥ |
yad etayor dvayor antayor madhyam iyam ucyate Kāśyapa madhyamā pratipad dharmāṇāṃ bhūtapratyavekṣeti [yat paṭhyate] sā ma[16]
dhyamā pratipad evam anulomakṛtā |
sarvam iti saṃskṛtam asaṃskṛtaṃ ca |
naikāntena śūnyam ity abhūtaparikalpasya tacchūnyatāyāś ca sadbhāvāt |
nāpy ekāntenāśūnyaṃ dvayasyābhāvāt |
sarvanāstitvaṃ sarvāstitvaṃ vānta eva syāt |
na madhyamā pratipat |
evam abhūtaparikalpasya sallakṣaṇam asallakṣaṇaṃ ca khyāpayitvetidaṃ sattvena lakṣyata iti sattvam eva sallakṣaṇam |
abhūtaparikalpo vidyata ity anenābhūtaparikalpasya sat pradarśayatīty arthaḥ |
evam asattvena lakṣyata iti |
asattvam evāsallakṣaṇam |
tat punar yad grāhyagrāhakabhāvenāsattvam |
yasmād abhūtaparikalpe dvayaṃ nāsti |
tasmād abhūtaparikalpo 'pi dvayātmanā nāstīty uktaṃ bhavati ||
idānīṃ svalakṣaṇam khyāpayatīti |
ko viśeṣo 'sti sallakṣaṇasvalakṣaṇayoḥ |
sallakṣaṇaṃ hi sāmānyam |
svalakṣaṇaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ |
yadi svalakṣaṇe 'nākhyāte 'tra kiṃ syāt |
carīram abhūtaparikalpasya noktaṃ syāt |
atas taccharīrapratipādanārtham ucyate
arthasattvātmavijñaptipratibhāsaṃ prajāyate |
vijñānaṃ nāsti cāsyārthas tadabhāvāt tad apy asat ||
iti |
atha vā grāhyagrāhakarahitābhūtaparikalpamatraṃety uddiṣṭam |
tasyām abhūtaparikalpamatratāyām indriyaviṣayavijñānāṃ yathā vyavasthitaṃ na jñāyata |
abhūtaparikalpapratibhāsabhedena tadvyavasthitijñāpanārtham abhūtaparikalpasya svalakṣaṇaṃ khyāpayati[17]
arthasattvātmavijñaptipratibhāsam
iti vistaraḥ
atha vā 'bhūtaparikalpo 'stīti anena tatsattvamātraṃ jñāyate na tu tatsabhāvaḥ
dvayābhāve ca yad grāhyagrāhakābhiniveśakāraṇaṃ tan na vijñāyate
dvayaṃ ca nāstīti yataḥ pratīyate tad api noktam ity atas tatpratipādanārtham āha
arthasattvātmavijñaptipratibhāsam
iti vistaraḥ
tatra vijñānasvabhāvo 'bhūtaparikalpaḥ
tac ca vijñānam atra sasaṃprayogam abhipretam
pradhānena tu vijñānaṃ gṛhītam
sa eva grāhyagrāhakābhiniveśo 'rthasattvādipratibhāsanibandhanāḥ
nāsti cāsyārthas tadabhāvāt tad apy asat
iti
dvayābhāvakāraṇam
tatrārthasattvapratibhāsam ālayavijñānaṃ sasaṃprayogam
tac ca vipākatvād avyākṛtam eva
ātmapratibhāsaṃ kliṣṭaṃ manaḥ sasaṃprayogam
tac ca nirvṛtāvyākṛtaṃ
kleśasaṃprayuktaṃ cād uktam
vijñaptipratibhāsaṃ kuśalākuśalāvyākṛtaṃ sasaṃprayogaṃ cakṣurvijñānādiṣaṭkam
tad etāny arthasattvātmavijñaptipratibhāsāny aṣṭau vijñānāni sasaṃprayogāṇi samudayasatyasaṃgṛhītāt sahakāripratyayāpekṣād ālayavijñānād yathāsaṃbhavaṃ pañcagatiṣu prabhavanti
kaś cit kuśalākuśalāvyākṛtadharmavasanāpariṇāmaviśeṣo 'sty ālayavijñānasya
yadādhipatyena parasparabhinnābhāsaṃ vijñānaṃ prajāyate
katham asaty arthādau vijñānaṃ tadābhāsam utpadyate
na hi puruṣe 'sati sthāṇaḥ puruṣābhāso bhavatīti
naiṣa doṣaḥ
arthādyabhāsaṃ hi vijñānaṃ bālā vijñānād pṛthagarthastitvam abhiniviśanti tāmirikyasya keśoṇḍukādivat
tasmāt[18]
tadabhiniveśatyājanārtham ucyate vijñānam evedam arthādyābhāsam utpadyate |
tāmirīkāṇām iva keśoṇḍukādyābhāsaṃ vināpy arthasattvādineti |
evaṃ cāṣṭavijñānavaṣṭukaḥ paratantro 'bhūta parikalpa ity uktaṃ bhavati |
rūpādihāveneti rūpaśabdagandharasasparśadharmasvarūpeṇa pratibhāsaṃ tadākāreṇotpannatvāt |
sattvapratibhāsaṃ yat pañcendriyatvena svaparasaṃtānayoḥ pratibhāsa ity adhikṛtam |
adhikaśaktisthānatvāt pañcasv indriyeṣu sattvākhyā |
tāis teṣu vā sañjata iti sattvaḥ |
tadākārotpattitām upādāya vijñānaṃ tadābhāsam iti |
ātmapratibhāsam ātmamohādisaṃprayuktatvāt kliṣṭaṃ mana iti |
kliṣṭasya manasa ātmamohena ātmadṛṣṭyā ātmatṛṣṇayā 'smimānena ca nityaṃ saṃprayuktatvāt |
teṣāñ cātmālambanatvaṃ yuktam ātmapratibhāsatvaṃ kliṣṭasya manasaḥ |
vijñaptipratibhāsaṃ ṣaḍvijñānānīti viṣayagrāhakarūpeṇa prakhyānāt tadākārotpattitām upādāya vijñaptipratibhāsam |
nāsti cāsyārtha iti catuṣṭayasyākārasyeti |
arthasattvapratibhāsasyānākāratvāt ātmavijñaptipratibhāsasya ca vitathapratibhāsatvād iti |
arthasattvapratibhāsayor grāhyarūpeṇa prakhyānād vitathapratibhāsatvāsambhavād anākāratvam evārthābhāve kāraṇam |
na tv anyayor grāhakarūpeṇa prakhyānād anākāraḥ |
vitathapratibhāsatvam evārthābhāve kāraṇam uktam |
ākāro hy ālambanasyānityādirūpeṇa grahaṇaprakāraḥ |
sa cānayor nāsti |
grāhyarūpeṇa prakhyānād ato 'nākāratvād agrāhakatvād ity arthaḥ |
ālambanasamvedanaṃ vā ākīraḥ |
tac ca tayor nāstīty upalabdhyabhāvād anākāraḥ |
yady ubhayam apy anākāraṃ [Tib.27,b] parasparabhinnañ ca svarūpam |
rūpādivac cakṣurādivac caivaṃ sati lokaśāstrapra[19]
siddhebhyo rūpādibhyaś cakṣurādibhyaś ca vijñānasya ko 'sāv ātmātiśayo yatas tān nirākṛtya tair abhinnarūpaṁ vijñānaṁ gṛhyate |
tadvyatiriktasyārthasyāsaṁbhavāt |
etad evaṁ vyavasthāpyate yasmād bhinnārthasvarūpam asann api cittasaṁtānapratiṣṭhāyamena svābijāt pratyekātmogṛhītaṁ bhinnārthādhipratibhāsaṁ vijñānaṁ prasūyate |
tathā hi pretā apaḥ pūyapurīṣamūtrādipūrṇā dhṛtadaṇḍapāṇibhir ubhayataḥ puruṣaiḥ saṁrakṣyamāṇāḥ paśyanti |
manuṣyādayaḥ punaḥ svacchaśītalodakaparipūrṇā nirvibandha ity upalabhante |
yoginaś cācubhamanasikārādyabhyastā nirantaraṁ pṛthivīṁ kaukalapūrṇāṁ paśyanti |
tathā pṛthivyādikṛtsneṣu sarvaṁ pṛthivyādibhir vyāptaṁ paśyanti |
na cārthapratibaddhātmalābhasyārtham antareṇārthasvarūpād vā bhinnākārasya vijñānasya prasutir yuktā |
tasmāt tadartham antareṇa sarvam arthasattvādinirbhāsaṁ vijñānam eva prasūyata iti niścīyate |
grāhyābhāve dvayor ātmavijñaptipratibhāsayor grāhakākāreṇa prakīryāṇād vitathapratibhāsatvam |
atha vā yathā vijñānenārthaḥ parikalpyate tathārthasyābhāvo vyāghrādiśrutyādiṣv iva vitathālambanatvaṁ vitathapratibhāsatā |
tathā hi vijñaptir api vijñaptyantaraparikalpitenātmanā śūnyeti siddhāntaḥ |
ataś cārthasattvavijñānasyevātmavijñapti[20]
arthābhāvaḥ |
arthābhāvāt tad vijñānam asat |
vijānāti vijñānam |
grāhyābhāve vijñātṛtapy ayuktam |
tasmād arthābhāyād vijñātṛtvena vijñānam asat |
na tv arthasattvātmavijñaptipratibhāsatayā |
tadasattve hi sarvathā 'bhāvaprasaṅgaḥ |
tadvyatirekena vijñānasvavrāvānirdeśanāt |
tadbhinnasvabhāve sati tat kathaṁ vijñānaṁ yujyate |
kathaṁ parasparaṁ bhinnaṁ bhavet |
evaṁ grāhyagrāhakābhāvāt tatpratibhāsavijñānasadbhāvāc ca yat pūrvaṁ pratijñātam
abhūtaparikalpo 'sti dvayaṁ tatra na vidyate |
iti tat prasiddham iti pradarśayann āha |
abhūtaparikalpatatvaṁ siddham asya bhavaty ataḥ |
abhūtaparikalptatvaṁ ca teṣāṁ caturṇāṁ vijñānānāṁ siddham |
ata ity anantaroktāt taddhetoḥ |
arthābhāvāt tad apy asad iti |
punar apy abhūtaparikalpāstitvaprasādhanārthaṁ dvayābhāvaprasādhanārthaṁ cāha |
na tathā sarvathābhāvāt |
ityādi |
atha vā |
ata iti vakṣyamāṇād dhetoḥ |
tam eva pradarśayann āha |
na tathā sarvathābhāvāt |
iti |
na tathāstitvān na ca sarvathā nāstitvāt |
tad grāhyagrāhakapratibhāsam utpadyate |
tatra catuḥprakāre 'pi |
anākāratvād vitathākāratvāc ca yathāsaṁbhavaṁ grāhakatvābhāvaḥ |
vijñaptyantaraparikalpenena cātmanā sarvasya śūnyatvād grāhyatvābhā[21]
vaḥ |
na ca sarvathābhāvo bhrāntimātrotpādād ity ātmatvābhāvo
na tu yad ākāreṇa pratibhāsaḥ sā bhrāntir ucyate |
māyāvat |
"mātra" bhavati |
bhrāntivijñānasya sadbhāvān na sarvathābhāva iti |
kimarthaṃ punas tasya bhrāntivijñānasyābhāva eva neṣyate |
grāhyagrāhakābhāvavat |
na hi kena cit tasyāstitvaṃ
kalpayituṃ śakyate |
sarvavijñānaviṣayātikrāntatvād ity abhipretam
iti cet |
yasmāt
tatkṣepān muktir iṣyate ||
ato 'syābhāva eva neṣyate |
kin tarhi |
saṃkleśavyavadānapakṣasāmarthyāt tad astitvam anumīyate |
anyatheti yadi tasya sarvathābhāva eveṣyate |
na bandho na mokṣa iti |
bhrāntimātre 'py asati
saṃkleśābhāvād bandho 'pi nāsti |
pūrvaṃbaddhād mucyata iti
kṛtvā muktir api na syāt |
atha vā kathaṃ na yathā prakhyāti tathā bhāveneṣyate |
sarvathā vibhāva iti parābhiprāyavyudāsārtham āha |
tatkṣepān muktir iṣyate ||
tasminne cāparikṣīṇe bandha ity arthaḥ |
etad uktaṃ bhavati |
anyathā hi na bandho na mokṣa iti |
saṃkleśavyavadānāpavādadoṣo
bhavati |
yathā grāhyagrāhakatvena bhrāntir udbhāsitāpi yadi
tathā syāt paramārthataḥ |
evaṃ sati nityaḥ saṃkleśaḥ syāt |
tathā ca nirvāṇābhāvaḥ |
evaṃ bhrāntimātrasyāpy abhāve saṃkleśābhāvo nityañ ca vyavadānaṃ prasajyeta |
evaṃ cobhayathāpi mokṣārthinām vyartho yatnaḥ syāt |
ato 'vaśyam abhūtaparikalpābhāvo 'bhyupagantavyo dvayābhāvaś ca ||[22]
saṃgrahalakṣaṇaṃ khyāpayati saṃgraha eva lakṣaṇam |
atha vā yena saṃgraho lakṣyate tat saṃgrahalakṣaṇam |
tac ca kimartham uktam iti |
atra hi grāhyagrāhakarahitam abhūtaparikalpamātram evāstīty uktam |
sūtrāntareṣu punas trayaḥ svabhāvāḥ paṭhyante |
ataḥ sūtrāntarair avirodhapradarśanārthaṃ teṣām iha saṃgraha ucyate |
ata eva vṛttikāra āha |
abhūtaparikalpamātram eveti vistareṇa |
kalpitaḥ paratantraś ca pariniṣpanna eva ca |
arthād abhūtakalpāc ca dvayābhāvāc ca deśitaḥ ||
iti |
grāhyaṃ grāhakaṃ ca svabhāvaśūnyatvād abhūtam apy astitvena parikalyata iti parikalpita ucyate |
sa punar dravyato'sann api vyavahārato 'stīti svabhāva ucyate |
paratantraḥ paravacaḥ |
hetupratyayapratibaddhajanmakatvāt |
āha
kalpitaḥ pratyayotthanno 'nabhilāpyaś ca sarvathā |
paratantrasvabhāvo hi śuddhalaṅkikagocaraḥ ||
yā abhūtaparikalpasya dvayarahitatā sa pariniṣpannaḥ svabhāvaḥ |
tasyāsaṃskṛtatvāt |
nirvikāratvena pariniṣpannatvād āha ca |
kalpitena svabhāvena tasya yā 'tyantaśūnyatā |
svabhāvaḥ pariniṣpanno 'vikalpajñānagocaraḥ ||
arthāta ity arthādilāpatyād uktam |
evam abhūtaparikalpād dvayābhāvāc ceti vaktavyam |
artho hi parikalpitasvabhāva ity artho 'tra rūpādayaś ca[23]
kṣurādaya ātmā vijñaptayaś ca sa ca kalpitena svabhāvenābhūtaparikalpe nāstīti asaṃ parikalpitasvabhāva ucyate |
āha ca akalpio vikalpo hi vikalpāntarakalpitaḥ |
svabhāvo nāsty asaṃ tasya vikalpāntarakalpitaḥ ||
abhūtaparikalpaḥ paratantraḥ svabhāva iti |
parair hetupratyayais tantryate janyate na tu svayaṃ bhavatīti paratantraḥ |
grāhyagrāhakābhāvaḥ pariniṣpannaḥ svabhāva iti |
nirvikārapariniṣpattyāviparītapariniṣpattyā ca pariniṣpannatvāt pariniṣpanna ucyate |
atra hy abhūtaparikalpasya dvayarahitatā grāhyagrāhakābhāva uktaḥ |
na tu dvayasyābhāvamātram |
evaṃ cābhūtaparikalpa eva hetupratyayaparatantryāt paratantraḥ |
sa eva grāhyagrāhakarūpeṇa svātmany avidyamānena prakhyānāt parikalpitaḥ |
sa eva grāhyagrāhakarahitatvāt pariniṣpannaḥ |
evam abhūtaparikalpe trayaḥ svabhāvāḥ saṃgṛhītāḥ |
evaṃ kṛtvābhūtaparikalpasya parijñeyaṃ parijñāya prahātavyaṃ parijñāya sākṣātkartavyaṃ ca vastu saṃdarśitaṃ bhavati ||
aparijñātāsallakṣaṇo hy abhūtaparikalpaḥ kleśakarmajanmasaṃkleśāya saṃpravartate |
ato 'sallakṣaṇaparijñānārthaṃ tadupāyapradarśanārthaṃ cāha |
upalabdhiṃ samāśritya
iti vistaraḥ |
abhūtaparikalpe tad upalakṣyate |
abhūtaparikalpo 'veti |[24]
grāhyagrāhakayor asattvam evāsallakṣaṇam |
tatrānupraveśas tadavabodhaḥ |
tasyopāyo yatas tadasallakṣaṇam anupraviśati |
sa punar dharmadhātoḥ sarvatrāgārthapratibodhaprayoganiśrayaviceṣaḥ kuśalo dvividhaḥ |
tadupālakṣaṇārthaṃ sa śloka uktaḥ |
vijñaptimātropalabdhiṃ āśritya arthānupalabdhir [Tib. 30, a] jāyata iti |
idam vijñaptimātratvam ālambanārtharahitaṃ svabījāparipākād rūpādyābhāsaṃ vijñānaṃ pravartate na tu rūpādiko 'rtho 'stity evaṃ grāhakopalabdhiṃ niśritya grāhyānupalabdhiṃ praviśati |
evaṃ hy anena vicārayitavyam |
utpadyamānam utpannaṃ vā vijñānaṃ viṣayam ālambeta |
tatrotpadyamānaṃ viṣayam ālambituṃ na yujyate |
utpadyamānasyāsattvāt |
notpannam api |
viṣayapratibhāsātmanotpannatvāt |
na ca viṣayapratibhāsātmanotpattiṃ muktvā vijñānasyānyā kriyā 'sti |
yāṃ kriyāṃ kurvat vijñānaṃ viṣayam ālambata ity ucyate |
yadi vālambanasya vartamāna eva vijñāne vyāpāro notpadyamāne |
evam ālambanapratyayo na vijñānotpādakaḥ |
tatparikalpо nirarthakaḥ |
vartamānālambanagrahaṇe kṣaṇabhaṅgadoṣo bhavati |
na cātmātiśayābhāve pūrvavad vijñānenālambanagrahanaṃ yujyate |
athālambanagrahanaṃkāle tasyātmātiśaya iṣyate |
evam apy ātmātiśayasyānyata eva darśanād anyad evotpannam ālambanaṃ gṛhṇātīti prasajyate |[25]
anyo manyate nirudhyamāno 'rtha evotpadyamānasya vijñānasyālambanapratyayaḥ |
sa ca svākārapratibhāsavijñānakāraṇatvena ceṣebhyaḥ pratyayebhyo viciṣyata iti |
tenāpi rūpādiparamāṇavaḥ tatsaṃūho vā 'lambanatvena parikalpyate |
ubhayathāpi cālambanābhāvaḥ |
sarvam eva hi vijñānaṃ ghaṭapaṭādipratibhāsam utpadyate na tu paramāṇupratibhāsam |
arthākāraś ca vyatirektaḥ pratibhāsānānaṃ vijñānam |
na tv ālambanāṃ vyatiriktam |
cakṣurādīnām apy ālambanatvaprasaṅga iti |
yo 'pi manyate |
samuditāḥ paramāṇava ālambanaṃ na pratyekam |
tasyāpy ayam aparihāraḥ |
samuditā api hi te pratyekam evālambanatveneṣyante |
na tatsamudāyena |
tatra paramāṇūnāṃ pratyekapratibhāsaṃ vijñānaṃ notpadyate |
tathā hi tatsamudāyapratibhāsam |
tasmān na paramāṇūnām ālambanatvāṃ saṃbhavati |
nāpi paramāṇusamūha ālambanāṃ prajñaptisataḥ kāraṇatvāsaṃbhavāt |
vijñānakāraṇaṃ hi samanantarādipratyayavad ālambanapratyayo'pisyate |
tasmān na nirudhyamāno 'py ālambanam |
atha ca nirodhatvam ālambānam |
evaṃ saty atītānāgataṃ viṣayaḥ syāt |
svapnādau tu vijñānam anālambananam iti siddham |
nānālambanāṃ svapnādivijñānāṃ nimittālambanattvād iti ke cet |
nimittaṃ punar arthapratibiṃbaṃ viprayuktasaṃskārasvabhāvam |
na hi svapnādav asaty arthe tannimittaṃ yujyate |
mukhādyabhāve tatpratibiṃbāsiddhavat |
utpadyamānaṃ utpannaṃ vā vijñānaṃ na nimittālambānam |
abhāvasya nirodhasya ca pūrvoktatvāt |
vijñānam evārthaprātibhāsatvād arthapratibimbam |
ato na viprayuktasaṃskārātmakaṃ nimittaṃ yujyate |[26]
avaśyaṃ cārthākāraṃ vijñānam abhyupagantavyam |
anākāre hi tasmin grāhyagrāhakaniyama eva na syāt |
anyas tv apratiṣṭhaṃ rūpam eva svapne yo(8, a)ginām cācubhādāv ālambanaṃ manyate |
tasyāpi varṇasaṃsthānādyātmaka[Tib. 31, a]tvād apratiṣṭhatvaṃ manomātraviṣayatvaṃ ca virudhyate |
na cāvijñaptimātrād anyad apratiṣṭhaṃ rūpaṃ śāstre varṇyate ! tasmāt kalpanāmātram eva |
aparas tv anubhūtārthaṃ svapne vijñānaviṣayam icchati |
jātyandhasvapnasya nīlādirūpāgrahaṇatvāt |
na jātyandhaḥ svapne rūpaṃ na gṛhṇāti ! avyutpannasaṃketatvāt tu nāmato na jānāty ato nānyasyācaṣṭe |
yadi cānubhūtasyaiva svapne darśanam evaṃ jātyandasyāpi rūpadarśanaṃ kiṃ neṣyate |
tenāpi pūrvajanmasu rūpam upalabdham eva |
na ca vartamānajanuṣy upalabdham eva svapne pratibhāsate |
na hy atītānāgatavartamānānubhave suptasya jāgrataś ca kaś cid viśeṣo 'sti |
tasmāt parikalpamātram evaitad anubhūtārthaviṣayaṃ svapne vijñānam iti |
atītasya cābhāvān nirviṣayaṃ vijñānam eva svapne 'rthābhāsam utpadyata iti niścayaḥ |
evaṃ vijñaptimātratvenopalaṃbhād viṣayānupalabdhir bhāṣyate |
viṣayānupalabdhim āśritya vijñaptimātrasyāpy anupalabdhir jāyata iti |
yathā na vijñānād bahiḥ parikalpitaṃ grāhyam astīti vijñaptimātratābalena manogrāhyābhāvaṃ praviśati tathā grāhyābhāvabalena vijñaptimātrasya apy abhāvaṃ pratipadyate |
na grāhyābhāve grāhakatvam yujyate |
grāhyatvāpekṣayā tadgrāhakasya vyavasthāpanāt |
evam asallakṣaṇaṃ grāhyagrāhakayoḥ parikalpitarūpayoḥ[27]
praviśati nābhūtaparikalpasyeti darśanaṁ bhavati |
kim arthaṁ punaḥ prathamata eva vijñaptimātrasya vivibhāvaṁ na vibhāvayati |
grāhyapratibaddhātvād dhi grāhakasyopalabhyārthābhāve sukhaṁ praveśaḥ syād ālambanasva bhāvavināśāt |
anyathā bhāvāpavādam eva kuryāt |
grāhyagrā hakayoḥ parasparanirapekṣatvāt |
imāṁ ca grāhyagrāhakavikal pātitāṁ jñānabhūmiṁ saṁbhāreṣu nirantaraṁ pravartamānaḥ prathamakalpasaṁkhyeyaparisāmāptau praviśati |
tathā hi vi jñaptimātraṁ idaṁ niśritya rūpādyabhāvabhāvanā lokottaramār gasya prathamasvabhāva uṣmagatākhyaḥ saparivāraḥ samādhir āmukhī bhavati |
tasyānantaraṁ mūrdhākhyaḥ |
tasyānantaram aceṣagrāhyānupalambhād grāhakānupalambhānukūlaḥ kṣāntyā khyaḥ samādhiḥ |
tasyānantaram arthānupalabdhiṁ niśritya vijñ aptimātrasyāpy anupalabdhiṁ bhāva laukikāgryasdharmākhyaḥ prajñādisahitaḥ saparivāraḥ samādhir āmukhī bhavati |
tadanan taraṁ darśanamārgaḥ |
tatraiva ca sarvatrāgadharmadhātvadhigamāt prathamāṁ bhūmiṁ praviśatīti |
ayaṁ ca tattvamānaskāro nādhimuktimānaskāro 'pramāṇādivat |
upalabdher anupalabdhisvabhāvatvam iti pradarśayann āha |
upalabdhes tataḥ siddhā nopalabdhisvabhāvatā |
iti |
atha vā yat pūrvaṁ pratijñātam abhūtaparikalpe dvayaṁ nāstīti tad anenākāreṇa pratyātmavedyatvāt siddham iti pradarśayann āha |
upalabdhes tataḥ siddhā nopalabdhisvabhāvatā |[28]
iti |
tata iti |
upalabhyārthābhāve upalabdhyabhāvāt |
upalabdhir iti bhāvakartrṭikaraṇānām anyatamāt parikalpyeta |
etat tritayaṃ apy abhāvād na yujyate
upalabdhes tataḥ siddhā nopalabdhisvabhāvatā |
ata eva vyuttikāra āha upalabhyārthābhāva upalabdhyayogād iti |
tasmāc ca samatā jñeyā nopalambhopalambhayoḥ ||
yasmād upalabdhir anupalabdhisvabhāvā tasmāt samatā |
tulyatā jñeyā |
arthānupalambhasya vijñaptimātropalambhasya cāsattvād aviśeṣataḥ |
upalabdhyanupalabdhiśabdayoḥ parasparaviruddhatvam apanayamārtham āha |
abhūtārthapratibhāsatayā tūpalabdhir ity ucyate |
na tu tayā kiṃ cid upalabhyate arthābhāvād iti |
ataḥ paramārthato 'nupalabdhisvabhāve 'pi satīti nāsti virodhaḥ |
apara āha |
yaś ca bālānām asaty arthe 'rthopalambhaḥ |
yaś cāryāṇām arthānupalambhaḥ |
tayor dvayor lakṣaṇatulyatvāt samatā jñeyā |
sarpabhrāntyanupalabdhivat |
ata eva bravīti |
abhūtārthapratibhāsatayā tūpalabdhir ity ucyate |
anupalabdhisvabhāve 'pi satīti |
sarpabhrāntyanupalabdhivyapadeśavad iti |
anyaḥ punar āha |
yaś ca bālānāṃ grāhakopalambhaḥ |
yaś cāryāṇām arthābhāvād grāhakānupalambhaḥ |
tayor dvayor upalambhānupalambhayor ubhayato 'pi samatā jñeyā grāhye 'sati grāhakābhāvāviśeṣāt |
ata evāha |
abhūtārthapratibhāsatayeti vistaraḥ |
ke cit tu manyante |
samāropāpavādapratipaḳsenāha |
tasmāc ca samatā jñeyā nopalambhopalambhayoḥ |[29]
iti |
arthābhāvād upalabdher upalabdhisvarūpatvenābhāvāt svarūpeṇa tadabhāva ucyate |
upalambha upalabdhisvabhāvo nāpanīyate nāpy anupalabdhisvabhāvaḥ prakṣipyate |
kiṃ tarhi samam etad ubhayaṃ nirvikalpatayā |
tasmān nopalambhopalambhāyoḥ samāropāpavādanirapekṣeṇa pratyātmasamatā jñeyeti |
āha ca
nāpaneyam ataḥ kiṃ cit prakṣeptavyaṃ na kiṃ cana |
draṣṭavyaṃ bhūtato bhūtaṃ bhūtadarśī vimucyate ||
yady evaṃ kathaṃ vijñaptir upalabdhir ity ucyate |
abhūtārthapratibhāsatayā loke śāstre ca tathā pratitatvād anupalabdhisvabhāve 'pi satīti ||
prabhedalakṣaṇaṃ khyāpayatīti kāmarūpārūpyadhātvātmakam abhūtaparikalpasya prabhedaḥ |
nānāprakāratā |
prabheda eva lakṣaṇam iti prabhedalakṣaṇam |
anena prabhedenābhūtaparikalpasya lakṣaṇāt |
prabhedalakṣaṇaṃ ca kimartham ākhyāpyate |
abhūtaparikalpamātre kāmarūpadhātvor ayogāt |
yo 'yaṃ dhātubhedena vipakṣapratipakṣabhedād āryapudgalādibhedaḥ sa na prāpnotīti mahāñ chīsanopaplavaḥ prasajyata ity āśaṅkāvyudāsārthaṃ prabhedalakṣaṇam ākhyātam |
atha vā yatra yo vihāravicārayoḥ sator evābhūtaparikalpo 'sti na tv anyatheti manyate |
tatas tadapanayanā(9,a)rthaṃ[30]
prabhedalakṣaṇam ucyate |
abhūtaparikalpastu cittacaittās tridhātukāḥ |
na tu yatraiva vitarkavicārav iti |
yathā rūpaṃ bhūtaṃ bhautikam ca |
evam abhūtaparikalpo 'pi cittaṃ caittaś ca na kevalaṃ cittam eva |
te ca tridhātukasvabhāvāḥ |
kāmarūpāryāvacarabhedāt tridhātukāḥ |
tatra ye tasmād abhūtaparikalpān narakādyākārā viṃśatiprakārāḥ pravartante sa kāmadhātuḥ |
ye saptadaśaprakārā brahmakāyikādyākārās te rūpadhātuḥ |
ya ākāśānantyāyatanādyākārāś catuṣprakārās ta ārūpyadhātuḥ |
anyaḥ punar āha |
aprahīṇakāmarāgā avibhūtarūpyasaṃjñāś ca kāmāvacaraḥ kāmadhātuḥ |
prahīṇakāmarāgā avibhūtarūpyasaṃjñāś ca rūpāvacaro rūpadhātuḥ |
prahīṇakāmarūparāgā vibhūtarūpyasaṃjñāś cārūpyāvacara ārūpyadhātuḥ |
kāmarāgānuśayitaḥ kāmadhātuḥ |
rūparāgānuśayita rūpadhātuḥ |
ārūpyarāgānuśayitā ārūpyadhātur ity anye |
satataviṣepāś caittasikaduhkhaprakārāśrayabhūtāḥ kāmadhātuḥ |
samāhitā vigatacaittasikaduhkhaprakārā rūpadhātuḥ |
samāhitāḥ prahīṇasukhaduhkhaprakārā ārūpyadhātur ity apare |
aprahīṇagandharasatadvijñānābhāsāḥ kāmadhātuḥ |
prahīṇagandharasatadvijñānābhāsā rūpadhātuḥ |
prahīṇapañcadvārābhāsā ārūpyadhātur ity apare |
teṣu sarveṣu vyākhyāneṣu katame ta ity avaśyaṃ pūrvavyākhyānoktā eva vaktavyā iti sākaṃkṣāṇy evaitāni vyākhyānāni |[31]
abhūtaparikalpādhikāre punar abhūtaparikalpagrahanaṃ āupalabdhyantaritatvād iti
paryāyalakṣaṇaṃ ceti cittacaittānāṃ prabhedaṃ pradarśayitvābhūtaparikalpaparyāyalakṣaṇaṃ khyāpayati kathaṃ kṛtvā
abhūtaparikalpyavastunaḥ svabhāvaviśeṣaparikalpanayā cittacaittānāṃ pravṛttatvāt
arthasvarūpaviśeṣadṛṣṭicittacaittā abhūtaparikalpaś ceti paryāyāntarbhūtāḥ
tatrārthamātradṛṣṭir vijñānam iti "mātra"śabdo viśeṣanirasanenagrīhitaviśeṣā vastusvārūpamātropalabdhir ity arthaḥ
arthaviśeṣadṛṣṭiś caitasā vedanādaya iti tena tena viśeṣarūpeṇa tatrābhipravṛtteḥ
tatrāhlādakaparitāpakatvaviceṣo yas tasya bhāvasya yat saumanasyādisthānaṃ tadgrahaṇaṃ vedanā
strīpuruṣādivyavahāralakṣaṇo yo 'rthaviśeṣas tadgrahaṇaṃ saṃjñā
evam anye 'pi yathāyogaṃ yojyāḥ
evaṃ caiṣām āśrayālambanakāladravyasamatābhiḥ saṃprayuktatvaṃ na tv ākārasamatayāpi vijñānāviśeṣaprasaṅgāt
cittaviśeṣā eva caitasā ithabhipretā iti ke cij jānanti
tad eva vijñānaṃ tādṛśasvarūpādinā vicitrapratibhāsam utpadyate mayūracandrikavat
katham ekaṃ vicitraṃ ca
naikaṃ hi lakṣaṇaṃ loke vicitram iṣyate mā bhūd ekam anekasvabhāvam iti
syād eṣa doṣo yadi dharmasvabhāvaḥ pariniṣpannaḥ syāt
bhrāntimātre tu naiṣa doṣaḥ[32]
na tathā sarvathābhāvāt
iti vacanāt
naitat evam sūtrava anaviroddhāt yā vedanā yā samjñā yac cittam yad vijñānam ca samsṛṣṭā ami dharmā na visamsṛṣṭā iti
samsargaś ca nāma satām yugapac ca bhavatiti yo hi dharmasvabhāvam apariniṣpannam icchati tasyaitat sūtram asminn arthe 'jñāpakam iti
abhūtaparikalpamātre 'nyasya cābhāve hetuphalaprakārabhedam na vijānāti tatpratipādanārtham pravṛttilakṣaṇam ca khyāpayati anena hetuphalabhāvenābhūtaparikalpa iti lakṣaṇam
pravṛttir eva lakṣaṇam pravṛttilakṣaṇam pravṛttiḥ punar dvidhā kṣaṇaparamparāpravṛttir yadādhipatyena pratyutpanna upabhogasamkleśaḥ janmāntarapravṛttiś ca yadādhipatyenāyatyām kleśakarmajamnasaṃkleśaḥ iha kṣaṇaparamparāpravṛttiḥ pravṛttilakṣaṇam uktam janmāntarapravṛttim samkleśalakṣaṇatvena khyāpayiṣyati
ekam pratyayavijñānam
iti vistaraḥ tatra ekam ity ālayavijñānam ceṣāṇām saptānām vijñānānām hetupratyayabhāvena hetur iti pratyayavijñānam
dvitīyam aupabhogikam[33]
iti vijñānam ity anuvartate |
tatphalam iti vākyaśeṣaḥ |
tat punaḥ saptavidhaṁ pravṛttivijñānam upabhogaprayojanātvād āupabhogikam |
upabhogaparicchedaprerṇāś tatra caitasāḥ ||
iti |
tatra vijñāne ye caitasās te 'pi tatphalam iti saṁbandhaḥ |
vijñānātvād ekayogakṣematvāc ca |
ālayavijñānasya hy anyavijñānapratyayatvāt pratyayavijñānam iti |
ālīyante sarve sāsravā dharmās tatra phalabhāvena
tac ca teṣu hetubhāvenety ālayaḥ |
sattvabhājanalokavijñāpanāt
tannirjñāsatvā vijñānam |
tac caikāntavipākatvād avyākṛtam |
sarveṣāṁ sāsravāṇāṁ dharmāṇāṁ bījam anubaddhyate anyeṣāṁ
ca pravṛttivijñānānāṁ hetupratyayo bhavatīti pratyayavijñānam |
tatpratyayaṁ pravṛttivijñānam āupabhogikam iti |
tasmād
ālayavijñānāt pratyetiti |
tatpratyayam utpadyata ity arthaḥ |
katham utpadyate |
pravṛttivijñānaṁ hy ālayavijñānāt pravartamānam anutpannasya tajjātīyasya pravṛttivijñānasyotpādakaṁ
bījam ālayavijñāne paripoṣayati |
tasmāt paripuṣṭābhyāt paripūrṇavṛceṣalābhāt punas tajjātīyaṁ pravṛttivijñānam utpadyata ity
evaṁ tatpratyayaṁ pravṛttivijñānaṁ bhavati |
nanv ālayavijñānam apy āupabhogikaṁ pravṛttivijñānaṁ
ca pratyayavijñānaṁ prasajyate |
aduḥkhāsukhavedanopabhogāśrayatvād ālayavijñāne ca vāsanābhāvanāt |
na pratyayabhāvavyatirekeṇānyā vāsanābhāvanāsti |
yathoktam Abhi(10, a)dharmasūtragrāthāyām[34]
sarvadharmā hi ālīnā vijñāne teṣu tat tathā |
anyonyaṁ phalabhāvena hetubhāvena sarvadā ||
iti na prasajyate |
kiṁ kāraṇam |
tadvedanāyā durupalakṣyatvāt |
na tad aupabhogīkatvena lakṣyate pravṛttivijñānavat |
prakarṣagataṁ vā jñeyaṁ sūryavat |
tathā hy upabhogativaidhavednāvṛtāvatvāṁ pravṛttivijñānasyaiva na tv ālayavijñānasyeti |
hetupratyayaś cātra vivakṣito na pratyayamātram |
tathā hi kuśalākuśaladharmādhipatyād ālayavijñānaṁ vipākaniṣyandaphalavāsanāṁ parigṛhṇāti |
avyākṛtadharmādhipatyāc ca niṣyandaphalavāsanām eveti |
ata ālayavijñānaṁ niravaśeṣāṇāṁ sāsravdharmāṇāṁ hetupratyayaḥ |
pravṛttivijñānam ālayavijñānasyādhipatipratyaya iti |
nāsti pravṛttivijñānasya pratyayatvaprasaṅgaḥ |
ahetupratyayatvāt |
upabhogo vedaneti |
triprakārāpy upabhukṣyata ity upabhogaḥ |
anubhūyata ity arthaḥ |
bhavarasasārasarūpā vedanā |
tasmād bālaḥ tatsaṁvedanārthaṁ viṣayam abhiniviśanti |
na kevalaṁ vedanārthopabddhir apy upabhoga ity anye |
tac ca na |
upabhogo vedaneti bhāṣyavirodhāt |
arthopabddhiś ca na vijñānād anyeti na tasyāś caitasatvaṁ prayujyate |
veditaparicchedaḥ saṁjñā sukhādiviśeṣodgrahaṇātmakatvāt |
upabhoge saṁjñāyāṁ ca vijñānapravartakāḥ saṁskārāḥ |
cetanāmanaskāradayaḥ |
atha vā |
upabhogo vedaneti |
yathā svam ālambanānubhavanāt |
sukhādīvedanīyakarmānubhavanāc ca |
evaṁ viṣayakarmaṇor upabhuktir vedanety upabhogaḥ |
viṣayacihnaṁ vyavahā[35]
raninittaṁ paricchinnaṁ karotīti paricchedaḥ saṁjñā |
vijñānasyāntarasminm ālambana ābhogāḥ saṁskārāḥ |
tataś cetanādyādhipatyād vijñānam ālambanāntaraṁ bhajate |
ādicabdena cchandādayo gṛhyante |
uktaṁ yato yādṛśo yadarthā ca pravṛttir iti ||
saṁkleśalakṣaṇaṁ ca khyāpayatīti kleśakarmajanmasaṁkleśā yathā pravartamānā jagataḥ parikleśāya bhavanti tat saṁkleśalakṣaṇam |
tac cāsato 'py ātmano 'bhūtaparikalpamātrakāt saṁsāraḥ prajāyata iti pradarśanārthaṁ khyāpyate |
chādanād ropaṇāc caiva nayanāt saṁparigrahāt |
iti vistaraḥ |
so 'yaṁ pravṛttipakṣam adhi dvādaśāṅgaḥ pratityasamutpādo darśitaḥ |
tatra chādanāt klicyate jagad ity antavacanaṁ sarveṇa saṁbadhyate |
kena chādanāt kathaṁ vā chādanāt klicyata iti |
ata āha |
avidyayā yathābhūtadarśanavibandhanād iti |
avidyayā hy adarśanātmakatvād bhūtadarśanaviṣaye pracchādite bhūtadarśanaṁ notpadyate |
ato bhūtadarśanotpattivibandhanād avidyā bhūtadarśanavibandhah |
bhūtadarśanaṁ punaḥ prādhānyena lokottaraprajñā |
tatpṛṣṭhodbhavāt tatpṛṣṭhalābhāt tadavagamāc ca tatprayogāt |
mayī bhūtadarśanam ity ucyate |
bhūtadarśanavibandhanād avidyā saṁskārāṇāṁ pratyaya ity evaṁ chādanād avidyayā klicyate jagad iti |
ropaṇāt klicyate jagad iti saṁbadhyate |
kena ropaṇāt kva kasya vety ata āha |
saṁskārair vijñāne karmavāsanāyā iti |[36]
tatra saṃskārā yat kāyikaṃ karma vācikaṃ mānasaṃ ca puṇyāpuṇyānījyasvarūpaṃ |
tat punarbhavaṃ abhisaṃskaroṇti saṃskāraḥ |
apraṇihitaṃ rohayatīty arthaḥ |
tad eva ca saṃskāraśabdenocyate na tu sarvam |
tac cāvidyādhipatyāt punarbhavaṃ ākṣeptuṃ samarthaṃ bhavati na sattāmātreṇeti |
avidyāpratyayāḥ saṃskārā ity ucyate |
tathā hi tāny anutpannavidyasya punarbhavaṃ saṃskurvanti |
notpannavidyasya |
na cādhipatyamātreṇāvācakyā saṃskārāṇāṃ pratyaya ucyate |
sāmānyapratyayena samutthāne 'pi |
avidyāyā hi sarvakleśasaṃprayuktatvād yathā samutthānakleśānāṃ sāmānyapratyayas tathā tatsamutthānāṃ cetanānām api |
puṇyānām api saṃskārāṇāṃ bhavabhogabhedaprārthanānvayānām |
samutthāne tatsahabhūtāvidyā sāmānyapratyayaḥ |
ānīñjyānām api tadbhūminihsaraṇadṛṣṭisamutthānānāṃ tatsahabhūtāvidyā sāmānyapratyaya iti saṃskārāṇāṃ pratyaya uktaḥ |
vijñāna ity atrālayavijñānam abhipretam |
na tu pravṛttivijñānam |
vyupara ma pravṛttivijñānasyotpattisthānanayanasamb havāt kuśalakliṣṭayoś ca samavadhānābhāvāt saṃskārais tatra karmavāsanāyāḥ pratiṣṭhāpanābhāvāt |
karmavāsanāyā iti kā punar iyaṃ karmavāsanā nāma |
anāgatasya janmano bījaṃ hetubhāvaḥ |
tad yathā vrīhiphalaṃ bhūmibhasmapurīṣādipratyayaviśeṣam apekṣya pariṇāmād aṅkurotpattihetubhāvo bījam iti |[37]
pratiṣṭhāpanād iti ropaṇaṃ eva paryāyāntareṇācaṣṭe |
pratiṣṭhāpanaṃ punar atra vijñānasaṃtāne tatprathamatas tadbījasyāpyāyanam |
na hi kasya cit sāśravasya dharmasyānāśravasyevāpūrvaṃ bījam ādhīyate |
evaṃ saṃskārair vijñāne punarbhavadbījāddhānād jagat kliśyate |
nayanāj jagat kliśyata iti kena nayanāt kva kasya veti na vijñāyata ity āha |
vijñānenotpattisthānasaṃpreṣaṇād iti |
karmaparibhāvitena vijñānena saṃtānavṛittyā cyutisthānād upapattisthāne punarbhavabījabhūtayā vāsanāyāḥ prāpaṇaṃ nayanam |
tathā ca sati na pratisaṃdhivijñāna pratyayaḥ saṃskāraḥ |
pūrvakālabhavavijñānam eva saṃskārapratyayam iti pradarśitaṃ bhavati |
na hi vinaṣṭāt kāraṇāt kāryotpattir yujyate |
ārūpyasamāpattilābhino hi yatraiva cyavante tatraivotpadyanta iti katham ārūpyeṣūpapattisthānaprāpaṇam iti tad yathāyogaṃ veditavyam |
nāmarūpavat |
parigrahāj jagat kliśyata iti kena parigrahāt kasya vety ata āha |
nāmarūpeṇātmabhāvasyeti |
te ca pratisandhim upādāya kalalārbudapeśīghanaprāṇākhyāvasthā anutpannasaḍāyatanā vijñānapratyayaṃ nāmarūpam iti |
tadbhāvitaviśeṣeṇa hy antareṣu nikāyasabhāgeṣu tad viśiṣṭaṃ sidhyati |
tasmin cotpanne manuṣyatiryagādīnikāyasabhāgabhedād ātmabhāvo bhidyata iti |
nāmarūpeṇātmabhāvaḥ parigṛhīta ity ucyate |
atha vā 'maraṇāt sarvam evātmabhāvaṃ parigṛhṇāti tat prathamataḥ sarvasya hetubhāvena vyavasthānād iti |
atha vā 'bhinnam api nāmarūpeṇātmabhāvaḥ[38]
parigṛhīta iti tat pṛthaktvena darśayati |
yathā sarvaṁ saṁskṛtaṁ pañcabhiḥ skandhaiḥ saṁgṛhītam iti |
āpnapādukānāṁ tu vijñānapratyayaṁ ṣaḍāyatanam eveti |
ato vijñānapratyayaṁ nāmarūpaṁ yathāsaṁbhavaṁ veditavyam |
pūraṇāt kliśyata iti kena pūraṇāt kasya veti |
ṣaḍāyatanena nāmarūpasaṁgṛhītaṁ śarīram |
nāmarūpāvasthāyāṁ hi śarīram aparipūrṇam ucyate |
cakṣurādyāyatanābhāvāt |
asti ca tasyām avasthāyāṁ kāyāyatanaṁ mana āyatanaṁ ca tad apy aparipūrṇam eva |
āśritāśrayaparipūryabhāvāt |
ṣaḍāyatanāvasthāyāṁ punaś cakṣurādyabhinirv̥ttita āśrayaḥ paripūrṇo bhavati |
kāyāyatanaṁ ca paripūrṇaṁ bhavati |
taccakṣurādyāśritānāṁ paripūrṇatvāt |
kathaṁ taccakṣurādyāśritam |
tatpratibaddhavṛttitvāt |
mana āyatanam api ṣaḍvijñāna[Tib. 37, b]saṁgṛhītaṁ tadānīṁ niravaśeṣāśrayaparipūritāt paripūrṇaṁ bhavati |
aṅgapratyaṅgaparipūrtāc ca ṣaḍāyatanāvasthāyām evāśrayaparipūritāt ṣaḍāyatanena kliśyate jagad ity ucyate |
trayaparicchedāj jagat kliśyata iti saṁbadhyate |
trayaṁ hi indriyaviṣayavijñānasaṁnipāte |
indriyasya sukhādivedanopattyanukulūo yas triprakāro vikāras tat pariccheda sparśaḥ |
ata eva yam indriyavikārāsādṛśyaṁ spṛśati tadākāratayeti sparśa ucyate |
atha vā ṣaḍāyatanapratyayaḥ sparśa indriyasya sukhādivedanānukulaṁ trividhaṁ vikāraṁ prasūte |
upabhogād vedanayeti |
vedanā hi tṛṣṇayopabhujyate |
āsvādyata ity arthaḥ |
puṇyādikarm[aphal]opabhogād vā vedano[39]
pabhogaḥ |
atha vopabhogo 'tra vedanāyā anubhavaḥ |
sukhādiṣu cānubhūyamāneṣu tatra sukhādiparibhogād anurāgapratiṣṭhamo hāiḥ klicyate jagat |
karṣaṇād iti karṣaṇāt kena kasya veti na vijñāyata ity ata āha |
tṛṣṇayā karmākṣiptasya punarbhavasyeti |
saṃskārair vijñāne punarbhava bījaparipoṣaṇenākṣiptasya punarbhavasyodakaka lpayā tṛṣṇayā sarvagatīṣv aviceṣeṇātmabhāvābhilāṣeti yad ārdri krītya punarbhavam utpattāv upayuṅkta utpattiṃ cāvasthāpayati tat karṣaṇam |
nibandhanāt kena nibandhanāt kva kasya veti praśnā vakāśe svayam evāha |
upādānair vijñānasyopapattyanuküleṣu kāmādiṣv iti |
abhiniveśac chandaragalakṣaṇāiś caturbhir upādānaiḥ karmākṣiptaṃ vijñānam utpattyanuküleṣu kāmadṛṣṭiśīlavratatnuvādeṣu nibandhayaty avasthāpayati ca |
vijñānaṃ hi cchandaragavacāt tatra vartate |
ābhimukhyād ity atrāpi parapraśnāvakāśe svayam evāha |
bhavena kṛtasya karmaṇaḥ punarbhavavipākadānāyābhimukhī karaṇād iti |
yathā nibhandhanaṃ vijñānasya pūrvakṛtaṃ karma paunarbhavikaṃ vāsanāvasthaṃ vipākaphalāṃ pratikṛttiḥ bhavati |
pratikṛttiṃ labdhvā bhavaḥ sadbhūta ākṣiptabhavac ca nispattaye 'bhimukhībhavati |
evaṃ bhavenaabhimukhīkaraṇāj jagat klicyate |
duḥkhitāj jātyā jarāmaraṇena ca kiṃ klicyante jagantiti |
evaṃ bhavāt punarbhavābhinirvṛttau satyām ādita eva garbhādhāne[40]
śukraśoṇite vijñānasaṁmūrcchanāt kliśyate |
tathā mātur āmāśayapakvaśayayor antarāle parivartanāt kliśyate |
tathā ca mātur
bhojanacaryayoḥ saṁkaṭalaghunoḥ aparityāgād api kliśyate |
tathā
saṁbādhanāśucinā mārgena nirgacchati kliśyate jagat |
jātaṁ
punar jarayā khālityapālityādilakṣaṇayā maraṇena ca nāmarūpabhedalakṣaṇena priyābhyāṁ yāuvanajīvitābhyāṁ bhraśyamānaṁ
kliśyate jagad iti |
gacchatīti jagat |
sabhāgāvakāśāt kṣaṇāvakāśaṁ gacchatīty
arthaḥ|
kliśyata iti jātijarāvyādhimaraṇādibhis traidhātuke kṣaṇaparamparayā javaṁjavibhāvena ca pīḍyata ity arthaḥ |
kliśyata iti na vyavadāyata ity apare |
evam ayaṁ saṁkleśalakṣaṇo dvādaśāṅgaḥ pratītyasamutpādaś "chādanād" ityādinākadaśaprakārārthena vyavadānavipakṣa utpadyate|avidyādikramaḥ
pūrvapūrvasyottarottarāvāhanāt |
katividhaḥ punar arena dvādaśāṅgena pratītyasamutpādena
samāsataḥ saṁkleśaḥ pradarśta ity ata evedam ucyate|so 'yaṁ
dvādaśāṅgaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ |
tridhā dvidhā ca saṁkleśaḥ saptadhā|
iti |
"ca"śabdaḥ samuccayaḥ parivartaś cety arthaḥ |
kleśa eva
saṁkleśa iti kleśasaṁkleśaḥ |
evaṁ karmajanmasaṁkleśāu|
kleśo
hi pravartamānaḥ svaparātmano vyābādhakatvāt saṁkleśaḥ |
yathoktaṁ sūtre 'pi rakto rāgaparita ātmavyābādhāyāpi cetayate|
paravyābādhāya 'pi cetayate|
ubhayavyābādhāyāpi cetayate|
evaṁ[41]
dveṣu mohaś cāpi veditavya iti karmajanmasaṃkleśayoś cotpādatvāt saṃkleśaḥ tathā hi kleśādhipatyena karma janmākṣipati dṛṣṭasatyasya saty api karmaṇi punarbhavākṣepābhāvāt kleśasamudācāreṇa punarbhavabījasya bhāvikaraṇāt kliṣṭacittasya ca punarbhavapratisaṃdhibandhāj janmasaṃkleśakāraṇatvam eva
tato rhatah pratisaṃdhibandhābhāvah kliṣṭacittābhāvāt karma ca śubhāśubhaṃ tadātve kāyacittayer āyāsanimittatvād āyatyāṃ ca vipākadānāt saṃkleśaḥ janmāpi sarvopadravāspadatvāt saṃkleśa iti
tredhā saṃkleśaḥ hetor dvidhābhedāt kleśasaṃkleśo 'vidyātṛṣṇopādānāni tritayam api kleśātmakatvāt karmasaṃkleśaḥ saṃskārā bhavaś ceti dvayam api karmātmakatvāt
ayan tu viśeṣaḥ svarūpāvasthaṃ karma saṃskārāḥ bījāvasthaṃ bhava iti janmasaṃkleśaḥ śeṣāṇy aṅgānīti vijñānānāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanasparśavedanājātjarāmaraṇāni eṣāṃ janmasaṃgṛhitatvāt
dvedhā saṃkleśaḥ hetuphalatābhyāṃ prabhidyate hetusaṃkleśaḥ kleśakarmasvarūpair aṅgaiḥ phalasaṃkleśaś ca śeṣair aṅgair iti tatra hetusaṃkleśatvaṃ karmakleśānāṃ vijñānādīvedanāparyantaṃ jātyātmakaṃ jarāmaraṇādīnavajānma saṃpādayitum abhipravṛttatvāt phalasaṃkleśaś ca śeṣair vijñānādibhiḥ karmakleśaphalatvatī
punaḥ sa eva pratītyasamutpādaḥ saptadhā saṃkleśa iti viparyāsākṣepopanayanaparigrahopabhogākarṣaṇodvegahetutvāt saptavidho hetuḥ tatra viparyāsahetur avidyeti[42]
avidyāgato hi tattvasammohād anityādin nityādirūpeṇa darśanād viparyāsa ucyate |
ākṣepahetuḥ saṁskārā iti vijñāne caturaṅgātma janma bījasya pratiṣṭhāpanāt |
upanayanahetur vijñānam iti |
iha cyutasya upapattivasaya upanayanāt |
parigrahahetur nāmarūpaṣaḍāyatane iti |
upānitasya nāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanābhyāṁ nikāyasabhāgaparigrahāt |
pūrvaṁ nāmarūpeṇaiva parigraha ukto jarāyujādikāṁ yonim abhipreṣya |
idānīm upapādukam adhikṛtya ṣaḍāyatanenāpi parigraha uktaḥ |
atha vā nāmarūpeṇa tatprathamata ātmabhāvamātrasya saṁgraho vivakṣitaḥ |
iha tv aparipūrṇasya paripūrṇasya ceti nāsti virodhaḥ |
upabhogahetuḥ sparśavedanā iti |
sakāraṇenānubhavena kuśalākuśalakarmavipākasyopabhogāt |
ākarṣaṇahetus tṛṣṇopādānabhavā iti sa upabhuktakarmavipāko bālas tasyāṁ tasyāṁ vedanāyāṁ tṛṣyati |
vivṛddhatṛṣṇaś ca tatsaṁyogāviyogatṛṣṇayā kāmādin upādatte |
tadupādānāc cāsya pūrvabhūtaṁ pāunarbhavikaṁ karma vāsanāvasthaṁ pariṇatiṁ yathākṣiptavipākadānāya pravṛttiṁ labdhvā bhavo bhavati |
tac ca tṛṣṇopādānabhavabhiḥ saṁskārākṣiptapunarbhavābhinirvṛttaya ākṛṣyate |
[Tib. 40, a] abhimukhīkriyata iti tṛṣṇopādānabhavā ākarṣaṇahetuḥ |
atha vā tṛṣṇāvaśena cchaṁdarāgānurūpānyatarakarmavāsanābhimukhāt tṛṣṇopādānabhavā ākarṣaṇahetuḥ |
jātijarāmaraṇāny udvegahetur ity evam atra janmākarṣaṇāt tasmiṁs tasmin sattvānikāye jātijarāmaraṇaduḥkham anyac ca śokaparidevaduḥkhadaurmanasyopāyāsaduḥkham anantaprabhedam anubhavati |
tatra tridhāsaṁkleśābhidhānena kleśakarmaṇo janmanaḥ sā[43]
dhāraṇāsādhāraṇahetutvam darśitam |
kleśo hi janmāṅkurasya sādhāraṇakāraṇam bhūmyādivat karma tv asādhāraṇakāraṇam aṅkurasyeva bījam tadbhedena janmabhedād iti |
atha vā nātra pūrvāntāparāntamadhyāntāni trīṇi kāṇḍāni kim tarhi karmakleśaphalānīti pradarśayati |
dvividhābhidhānena hetuphalamātram evedam nātrānyaḥ samklīśyate kārako vedako veti pradarśayati |
atha vātra karmakleśamātram eva hetuḥ tayor bhāvābhāvāj janmabhāvābhāvadarśanāt |
atas tatphalam eva janma |
tasmān nātra hetuḥ phalam vā sarvatra pañcaskandhikāvastheti pradarśayati |
saptadhāhetunirdeśena punar dvividhaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ pradarśitaḥ |
ākṣepalakṣaṇo 'bhinirvṛttilakṣaṇaś ceti |
tatrākṣepalakṣaṇaḥ pratītyasamutpādasya saptabhir aṅgair nirdiṣṭaḥ yanākṣipyate yathākṣipyate yac cākṣipyate tatpradarśanāt |
abhinirvṛttilakṣaṇaḥ pañcabhir aṅgaiḥ |
tasyākṣiptasya yenābhinirvṛttir yathābhinirvṛttiḥ |
yā cābhinirvṛttiḥ |
tasyām ca ya ādinava iti |
kenākṣipyate |
avidyāpratyayāḥ saṃskārāḥ |
yathā tattve mugdhvā viparītaṃ saṃjānāti kuśalākuśalānīñjyān saṃskārān abhisaṃskaroti |
katham ākṣipyate taiḥ saṃskāraiḥ |
vijñāne bījāpoṣaṇād utpattibhavyatāyām avasthāpanāt |
kim ākṣipyate paunarbhavikam nāmarūpaṃ ṣaḍāyatanaṃ sparśo vedanā ca yathāyogam |
tad evam ākṣiptaṃ kenābhinirvaryate |
yathoktakrameṇa pūrvākṣiptād ihotpannāṃ vedanāṃ pratītya yā tṛṣṇotpannā tatpratyayenopādānena |
kathaṃ tenābhinirvaryate |
tasya vijñāne vāsanāvasthasya karmaṇas tena bhavikaraṇāt |
bahuvidho hi vijñāne karmavāsanā vartate |
vicitrasaṃskārapari[44]
bhāvitatvāt upādānaviśeṣeṇa parigṛhītād yenātra punarbhavatvam vartate so 'tra bhava ucyate |
kā punaḥ sābhinirvṛttiḥ |
yā tasyākṣiptasya nāmarūpādikasyāyatyāṁ jātiḥ |
tasyām punaḥ satyāṁ ka ādinavaḥ |
jarāmaraṇaṁ priyayauvanaṁjīvitabhraṁśād iti |
atra tūpanayahetur nirartha iti cet |

ime sarve saṁkleśā abhūtaparikalpāt pravartanta iti |
cittacaittā āśrayatvāt saṁkleśasya |
uktaṁ hi tad abhūtaparikalpāś ca cittacaittās tridhātukaḥ |

asallakṣaṇaṁ dvayaṁ tatra na vidyate |
iti |
svalakṣaṇam arthasattvātmavijñaptipratibhāsaṁ prajāyate |
vijñānam |[45]
iti |
saṃgrahalakṣaṇaṃ
kalpitaḥ paratantraś ca pariniṣpanna eva ca |
iti |
asallakṣaṇānupraveśopāyalakṣaṇam
upalabdhiṃ samācṛtyānupalabdhiḥ prajāyate |
ityādi |
prabhedalakṣaṇam
abhūtaparikalpaś tu cittacaittas tridhātukaḥ |
iti |
paryāyalakṣaṇam
tatrārthadṛṣṭir vijñānaṃ tadviśeṣe tu caitasāḥ |
iti |
pravṛttilakṣaṇam
ekaṃ pratyayavijñānaṃ dvitīyam āupabhogikam |
iti |
saṃkleśalakṣaṇam
chādanād ropaṇāc caiva nayanāt saṃparigrahāt |
ityādi [Tib. 41, b] ||
[2] Śūnyatā
evam abhūtaparikalpalalakṣaṇaṃ navaparakāram uktvā yathā śūnyatā jñāyate tat khyāpayati |
ko 'trābhisaṃbandhaḥ |
dvayam anena pratijñātam abhūtaparikalpaḥ śūnyatā ca saṃkleśapūrvakaṃ ca vyavadānam |
dharmāvabodhāśrayaṃ ca dharmatāvadhāraṇam ity ato 'bhūtaparikalpaṇirdeśānantaraṃ yathā śūnyatā vijñeyā tathā nirdiśati |[46]
lakṣaṇam atha paryāya
iti vistaraḥ |
tatra lakṣaṇaṁ hi bhāvābhāvapratisedhātmatā |
sarvatra śūnyatāprabhedavyāpakatvāt |
nāmāntaraṁ paryāyaḥ |
paryāyānuguṇaṁ paryāyapravṛttinimittaṁ paryāyārthaḥ |
ākācavad abhinnalakṣaṇatvān nirvikalpatve 'pi |
āgantukopakleśasaṁyogaviyogāvasthābhedād bhedaḥ |
anyac ca pudgaladharmāsamāropabhedād bhedaḥ ṣoḍaśavidhaḥ |
sādhanāṁ śūnyatāprabhedadarśane
yuktiḥ |
kiṁ punaḥ kāraṇaṁ yad ebhiḥ prakāraiḥ śūnyatā vijñātavyā |
viśuddhyālambanatvaād viśuddhyarthibhir lakṣaṇato vijñeyā |
sūtrāntareṣu paryāyanirdeśeṣv asamohārthaṁ paryāyataḥ |
paryāyārthāvabodhārthāc chūnyatāyā viśuddhyālambanatvena niścitakvāt paryāyārthaḥ |
saṁkleśe nirākṛte sā viśuddheti tatsaṁkleśaprahāṇāya yatnotpādanārthaṁ bhedataḥ |
bheda[Tib. 42, a]sādhanāvabodhād vikārābhāve saty api bhedaṁ sukhaṁ
pratipadyata iti bhedasādhanato 'pi vijñeyeti ||
[a) Śūnyatālakṣaṇam]
kathaṁ lakṣaṇaṁ vijñeyam iti lakṣaṇaṁ hi prāgudiṣṭam
ity atas tad eva prathamataḥ pṛṣṭam
dvayābhāvo hy abhāvasya bhāvaś ca śūnyatalakṣaṇam |
iti vijñeyam |
dvayasya grāhyasya grāhakasya ca |
abhūtaparikalpe 'bhūtaparikalpena vā parikalpitātmakatvād vasturūpeṇābhāvaḥ |
tasya ca dvayābhāvasya yo bhāva etac chūnyatāyā
lakṣaṇam |
kārikānuguṇyāc cātra bhāvapratyayo luptanirdeśo[47]
draṣṭavyaḥ |
abhāvasya bhāva iti kim etat |
abhāvasyātmatvam astitvam |
anyathā dvayabhāvasyāstitvam eva syāt |
tadbhāvasya śūnyatāvidyamānatvāt |
ata evāha |
ity abhāvasvabhāvo lakṣaṇatvaṁ śūnyatāyāḥ paridīpitaṁ bhavati na bhāvarūpalakṣaṇam iti |
bhāvapratiṣedhavācakatvād abhāvaśabdasya bhāvaśabdābhāve 'py eṣo 'artho 'vagamyata iti bhāvaśabdo 'trādīkaḥ |
nādīko devyābhāvaḥ śūnyatālakṣaṇam itīyati nirdicyamāne dvayābhāvasya svātantryam evāvagamyate śaśaviṣāṇābhāvavat |
na duḥkhatādivad dharmatārūpatā |
tasmād evam ucyate dvayābhāvaḥ śūnyatā |
tasya cābhāvasyābhūtapaṛikalpe bhāvaḥ |
śūnya[Tib. 42, b]tety ucyate |
abhāvasya bhāvalakṣaṇaparigrīhītatvād dharmatārūpaṁ pradarśitaṁ |
atha vā devyā(13, b)bhāvaḥ śūnyatety abhāvaśabdasya sāmānyavācītvān na vijñāyate katamo 'trābhāvo 'bhipreta iti |
atyantābhāvapradarśanārtham ucyate |
abhūtapaṛikalpe dvayābhāvasya bhāva iti |
na hi prāgabhāvapradhvaṁsābhāvau svopādānād anyā ākhyātuṁ yujyate |
anyonyābhāvaś caikāśrayakaṁ na yujyate |
ubhayācritatvāt |
tasmād bhāvasyābhāvalakṣaṇopādānād grāhyagrāhakayor atyantābhāva eva śūnyatety etaj jñāpitaṁ bhavati |
yady abhāvātmikā śūnyatā kathaṁ paramārtha ucyate |
paramajñānaviṣayatvād anityatāvat |
na tu vastutvāt |
api cābhāvasvabhāvo nāitat |
yasmād yas tadabhāvasvabhāvaḥ sa na bhāvo nāpi svabhāvaḥ |
kathaṁ na bhāvaḥ |
yasmād dvayasyābhāvaḥ |
bhāvatve hi na[48]
dvayābhāvasyātyantābhāvaḥ syāt nābhūtaparikalphadharmatā
kathaṃ nābhāvo yasmād dvayābhāvasya bhāvaḥ
galte yaṅdagpa mayinpa kunrtogpaḥi stoṅpañid chosñid yinna
kim asau tasmād anyo vaktavya utānanya ity ato bravīti
etac chūnyatāyā lakṣaṇam iti
abhāvasya svarūpam eva
atha vā bhāva evābhāvapratiṣedhātmaka eva
tasmāt abhūtaparikalpān
na pṛthaktvāikalakṣaṇam
pṛthaktve sati dharmād anyā dharmateti na yujyate
kaḥ punar ayogaḥ
dharmād vibhinnalakṣaṇatvād dharmatā dharmāntaram eva bhavati tadanyadharmavat
na ca dharmāntaraṃ dharmāntarasya dharmatā bhavitum arhati
tatra punar dharmāntaram anveṣṭavyam ity anavasthāprasaṅgaḥ
anityatāduhkhatāvad iti
yathānityatānityebhyo nānyā duḥkhatā ca duḥkhād evaṃ śūnyatāpi na śūnyād anyeti
ekatve sati viśuddhyālambanaṃ na syāt sāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ ceti
viśudhyate 'neneti viśuddhir mārgaḥ
dharmasvalakṣaṇād ananyatvān mārgālambanaṃ na syād
dharmasvalakṣaṇavat[49]
tasmāc ca na sāmānyalakṣaṇaṁ yujyate svālakṣaṇād ananyatvāt.
tasyāpi dharmasvarūpavat parasparato bheda iti sāmānyatā hīyate.
atha vā svalakṣaṇasya tasmād ananyatvād bhāvasvarūpavad bhedābhāvaḥ tataś ca sāmānyasyāpy abhāvaḥ.
bhedāpekṣatvāt sāmānyalakṣaṇasya.
atha vā viśuddhyārtham ālambanam viśuddhyālambanam.
na bhāvasvalakṣaṇam ālambyamānaṁ viśuddhim āvahati.
sarvasattvaviśuddhiprasaṅgāt.
yady anyānanyatvenāvaktavyā kathaṁ nirgranthavādo nālambito bhavati.
yo hi bhāvasya satas tattvānyatvena vyākaroti tena nirgranthavāda ālambayate.
śūnyatā tu na bhāva iti nāsty ayaṁ doṣaḥ.
evaṁ cṣā śūnyatāṣoḍlakṣaṇā abhāvasvarūpalakṣaṇā advayalakṣaṇā ca.
tattvānyatvavinirmuktālakṣaṇā ca pariditā.
uktaṁ śūnyatālakṣaṇam.
[b) Śūnyatāparyāyaḥ]
paryāya idānīṁ ucyate.
tathatā bhūtakoṭiś cānimittaṁ paramārthatā.
dharmadhātuś ca paryāyāḥ śūnyatāyāḥ samāsataḥ.
paryāyo nāmaikasyārthasya bhinnaśabdatvaṁ pratyāyayati.
paryāyārthābhidhānaṁ hi paryāya ucyate.
kāny abhidhānāni sūtrāntareṣu śūnyataiva nirdiśyante.
etac ca paryāyapañcakaṁ yathā pradhānaṁ gāthāyām uktam evam anye 'pi paryāyā ihā[50]
nuktāḥ pravacanād upadhāryāḥ tad yathā advayatā avikalpadhātuḥ dharmatā anabhilapyatā anirodhaḥ asaṃskṛtaṃ nirvāṇādi
kathaṃ paryāyārtho jñāyata ity etad darśayati
naite śabdā gauṇāḥ kiṃ tarhy anvarthā iti ananyathāviparyāsatannirodhāryagocaraiḥ
hetutvāc cāryadharmāṇāṃ paryāyārtho yathākramam
iti ananyathārthena tathateti avikārārthenety arthaḥ tad eva pradarśanārtham āha nityaṃ tathatvād iti
nityaṃ sarvadāsaṃskṛtatvān na vikriyata ity arthaḥ aviparyāsārthena bhūtakoṭiḥ bhūtaṃ satyam aviparitam ity arthaḥ koṭiḥ paryanto yataḥ pareṇānyaj jñeyaṃ nāstity ato bhūtakoṭir bhūtaparyanta ucyate
kathaṃ tathatā jñeyāṃ ucyate jñeyāvaraṇaviśodhanajñānagocaratvāt
aviparyāsārthenety anadhyāropānapavādārthena atraiva kāraṇam āha viparyāsavastutvād iti viparyāso hi vikalpaḥ vikalpānālambanatvān na viparyāsavastu nimittanirodhād animittan iti atrānimittatvaṃ nimittanirodha ucyate
etad eva pradarśanārtham āha sarvanimittābhāvād iti sarvair eva saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtanimittaiḥ śunyatā śūnyety animittan ucyate
sarvanimittābhāvād animittaḥ animittan evānimittaḥ āryajñānagocaratvāt paramārtheti paramaṃ hi lokottarajñānaṃ tadarthaḥ paramārthaḥ
etad eva pradarśayann āha paramajñānagocaratvād iti āryadharmāhetutvād dharmadhātuḥ
dharmaśabdenātrāryadharmāḥ samyagdṛṣṭyādayaḥ samyagvimuktijñānaparyantāś ceti taddhetutvād dhātuḥ tad eva vibhāva[51]
yann āha āryadharmāṇāṁ tadālambanaprabhavatvād iti svalakṣaṇopādāya rūpadhāraṇe 'py ayaṁ dhātuśabdo vartata ity āha hetvartho hy atra dhātvarthaḥ tad yathā suvarṇadhātus tāmradhātu rāupyadhātuḥ sūtrāntareṣv anye paryāyā uktā apy anenaiva nyāyena svārthena nirdeṣṭavyāḥ
śūnyatāyā grāhyagrāhakābhāvarūpatvād bhedam asaṁbhāvayan pṛcchati atha vā paryāyārthānantaraṁ bhedo vijñeya ity uktam atas tannirdeśānantaraṁ pṛcchati kathaṁ śūnyatāyāḥ prabhedo vijñeya iti abhūtaparikalpo hi saṁkleśaḥ tasmin prakṣīṇe viśuddhir ucyate saṁkleśaviśuddhikālaś ca śūnyatāvyatirekeṇānyan nāsti yat saṁkliśyate viśudhyate vā tasmāt saṁkleśaviśuddhikālayoḥ śūnyataiva saṁkliśyate viśudhyate ceti pradarśanārtham āha
saṁkliṣṭā ca viśuddhā ca
ity asyāḥ prabhedaḥ kadā saṁkliṣṭā kadā nirmalety anavabodhāt pṛcchati kadā saṁkliśyate kadā viśudhyata iti
sā samalā nirmalā ca
iti vistaraḥ āśrayāparāvṛttiparāvṛttyapekṣayā samalā ca prahinamalā ca vyavasthāpyate yeṣām aviduṣāṁ grāhyagrāhakābhiniveśarāgādikleśamalinānaṁ cittasaṁtānānām apratipattivipratipattidoṣāc chūnyatā na prakhyāti tān prati samalā vyavasthāpyate yeṣām āryāṇāṁ tattvajñānād aviparitacetasāṁ śūnyatā nirantaram ākāśavad virajaskā prakhyāti tān prati prahinamaletyucyate evaṁ śūnyatāyā apekṣikā saṁkleśaviśuddhayoḥ draṣṭavyā[52]
yā na madīnasvarūpatvena prakṛtityā prabhāsvaratvāt |
yadi samatā syād iti vistaraḥ |
na hy avasthābhedo vikāram
antareṇa dṛṣṭaḥ |
vikāraś cotpādavināśābhyām anuşyuta iti |
ata āha |
kathaṁ vikāradharmittvād anityā na bhavanti |
na
hi saṁkliṣṭāvasthātaḥ śunyatāviśuddhāvasthāyām anyo vikāraḥ |
tattvasthititā tu svabhāvāntaram anāpadyamānā |
āgantukamalapagamāt |
yasmād
abdhātukanaḥākāśaśuddhivac chuddhir iṣyate ||
tasmād anityā na bhavanti |
yathāiva hy abdhātukanakTib.45,
bākāśānām atatsvabhāvatvān malasvabhāvasyābhāvato 'py āgantukamalavaty āgantukamalaapagame ca viśuddhāva |
svabhāvāntarapratipattim antareṇāpi |
evaṁ śunyatāpy āgantukair malaiḥ saṁkliśyate |
avikṛtasvarūpāpi tadvigamāc ca viśudhyatiti |
yo hi tam eva bhāvaṁ pūrvaṁ saṁkleśalakṣaṇaṁ paścād viśuddhisvabhāvaṁ vyavasthāpayati tasya vikāradharmatvritttir na
bhavati svabhāvavikāratvāt |
na tu yatrāpy āgantu(15, a)kaṁ
tadubhayam |
tasmān nāsau vikāradharmatāṁ spṛśatiti ||
[Şoḍaśavidhā śūnyatā]
prabhedanirdeśādhikāre sarve śūnyatāprabhedā vaktavyā ity
ata āha |
ayam aparaḥ prabheda iti şoḍaśavidhā śunyateti |
vastubhedena şoḍaśavidhā bhavati |
dvayābhāvasvarūpe tu bhedo nāsti |
sā şoḍaśavidhā śūnyatā Prajñāpāramitāyāṁ paṭhyate |
adhyātmaśūnyatā yāvad abhāvasvabhāvaśūnyateti |
saiṣā samāsato vedi[53]
tavyā
bhoktṛbhojanatatddehapratiṣṭhāvastuśūnyatā
ityevamādi sāmānyalakṣaṇaṁ śūnyatāyāḥ sarvadharmasyākvayasvarūpatvāt ato vastunānātvena tannānātvaṁ darśayati nānyathā tannānātvaṁ śakyate darśayitum iti
pūrvaṁ tāvad bhoktā vibhāvayitavyaḥ tatsnehabhiniveśatyājanārtham tatsnehabhiniveśo hi buddhatvavimokṣaprāptipratibandho bhavati tadanantaraṁ tadbhojanam
tadanantaraṁ tayor adhiṣṭhānaṁ śarīram tadanantaraṁ tadadhiṣṭhānasya śarīrasya pratiṣṭhā bhājanaloko vibhāvayitavyaḥ bhoktur upakārakatvād ātmiyasnehagrahavyāvartanārtham etac caturvidhaṁ vastu tacchūnyatā vastuśūnyatety ucyate
tatra bhoktṛśūnyatā hy ādhyātmikāyatanair ārabhdheti tāni ca cakṣurādīni yāvan manaḥparyantāni tadanyasya bhoktur abhāvāt cakṣurādīnāṁ ca viṣayopabhogapravṛttidarśanāl lokasya cakṣurādiṣv eva bhoktrabhimāna ity ataś cakṣurādyāyatanaśūnyatā bhoktṛśūnyatety ucyate
bhojanaśūnyatā bāhyair iti rūpādīni yāvad dharmaparyantāni tāni viṣayabhāvatvena bhujyanta iti bhojanam ato bāhyāyatanaśūnyatā bhojanaśūnyatety ucyate
tayor bhoktṛbhojanayoḥ śarīre parasparāvinibhāgenāvasthānāt taddehaḥ śarīram ity atas tacchūnyatādhyātmabāhyaśūnyatety ucyate[54]
pratiṣṭhāvastu bhājanalokāḥ sarvatra sattvānāṃ pratiṣṭhāvastutvaprajñānāt |
ata evāha tasya vistīrṇatvāt tacchūnyatā mahāśūnyatocyata iti |
vastuśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ abhisaṃbadhyate |
tasyaivaṃ yogino bodhisattvasya caturvidhajñeyavastuśūnyatāyāṃ savitarkeṇa yoniśomanaskāreṇa manaskrīyamāṇāyām ayam anyo nimittagrāha upatiṣṭha(15,b)te |
yenedaṃ śunyatājñānenādhyātmikabāhyāyatanādiśūnyaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tatra yo grāhyagrāhakābhiniveśo 'yam eva cātra paramārthākāro yathā [Tib. 46,b] tacchūnyatājñānena dṛṣṭam iti vikalpaḥ |
tasya dviprakārasya yogibhūmibhrāntinimittasya vikalpasya vibhāvanārthaṃ śūnyatāśūnyatā paramārthaśūnyatā ca yathākramam |
jñānākāralopāṃ kṛtvā nirdiṣṭā |
śūnyatā viṣayatvād vā tajjñānaṃ śūnyatety uktam |
tasya grāhyagrāhakabhāvena śūnyatā śūnyatāśūnyatā |
ādhyātmikāyatanādikaṃ ca tena śūnyatājñānena yathā dṛṣṭaṃ so 'tra paramārtha ity etasyākārasya śūnyatā paramārthaśūnyatā |
kiṃ kāraṇam |
paramārtho hi śūnyaḥ parikalpitena svabhāveneti |
ayam anyo 'pi nimittagrāhaḥ śūnyatābhāvanopaplavabhūtaḥ |
yadarthaṃ bodhisattvaḥ śūnyatāṃ pratipadyate tasya bhāvasvarūpatvaṃ samāropyate |
tadvibhāvanārthaṃ saṃskṛtaśūnyatādayaḥ sarvadharmacūnyatāparyantāḥ śūnyatā nirdiṣṭāḥ |
kim arthaṃ ca pratipadyate |
śubhadvayasya prāptyartham |
yāvad buddhadharmāṇāṃ viśuddhyarthaṃ śūnyatāṃ pratipadyate śūnyatāṃ prabhāvayatīty arthaḥ |
śubhadvayaṃ hi saṃskṛtam[55]
asaṃskṛtaṃ ca mārgo nirvāṇaṃ ca tayoḥ saṃbadhyate
yathākramaṃ saṃskṛtaśūnyatā 'saṃskṛtaśūnyatā ca
sadā sattvahitāya ca
iti sarvākāraṃ sarvakālaṃ ca mayā sattvahitaṃ kartavyam
iti tacchunyatā 'tyantaśūnyatā
saṃsārātyajanārthaṃ ca
iti sattvārthaṃ mayā saṃsāro na parityājyaḥ saṃsāre
hi parityakte 'tadvicā bodhisattvabodhiṃ śrāvakabodhau vyavatisthate tasya śūnyatā anavarāgraśūnyatā kimartha punas
tasya śūnyatā deśyatā ity ata āha anavarāgrasya hi saṃsārasya
śūnyatām apaśyan khinnaḥ saṃsāraṃ parityajet
akṣayāya śubhāya ca
iti mayā kuśalamūtā na kṣīyante nirupadhiśeṣe nirvāṇe 'pīti
nāvakiratīty etad evācaṣṭe notsṛjatīti yady evaṃ kathaṃ tarhi
nirupadhiśeṣo nirvāṇadhātuḥ sidhyati sāsravadharmavipākakāyābhāvāt anāsravabhāvadharmakāyasya tu buddhānāṃ bhagavatāṃ nirupadhiśeṣe nirvāṇadhātāv api na vyuparamād iti
siddhāntaḥ atas tacchunyatā anavakāraśūnyatety ucyate
gotrasya ca viśuddhyartham
tasya śūnyatā prakṛtiśūnyatā atraiva kāraṇam āha gotraṃ hi
prakṛtir iti kuta etat ata āha svābhāvikāt iti svābhāvikam[56]
anādikālikan anāgaṃtukaṃ ity arthaḥ |
yathānādisamṣāre kiṃ cic cetanaṃ kiṃ cid acetanam evam ihāpi kiṃ cit ṣaḍāyatanaṃ buddhagotraṃ kiṃ cic chrāvakādigotraṃ iti |
na cākasmikataṃ gotrasyānādiparamparāgatatvāc cetanācetanaviśeṣavad iti |
sarvasattvasya tathāgata[Tib. 47, b]gotrikatvāt atra gotram iti tathātvaṃ jñeyam ity anye |
lakṣaṇavyañjanāptaye
ity ataḥ sānuvyañjanānāṃ mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇānāṃ śunyatā lakṣaṇaśunyatety ucyate |
kiṃ ca
śuddhaye buddhadharmāṇāṃ bodhisatvaḥ prapadyate |
ante prapadyata ity abhidhānāt |
sarvatra śubhadhvayasya prāptyarthaṃ bodhisattvaḥ prapadyate |
sadā sattvahitāya ca bodhisattvaḥ prapadyata iti yojyam |
katareṣāṃ buddhadharmāṇām ity ato bravīti |
balavaiśāradyāveṇikādīnām iti |

tatradhyatmikesv ayatanesu vipakavijñanasvabhavesu balanam bhoktrisammatesu bhoktrpudgalasya kalpitalaksananam ca caksuraddinam abhavas tadabha(16, b)vasya ca sadbhavo 'dhya[58]
tmaśūnyatā |
bāhyeṣv āyataneṣu rūpādivijñaptyābhāsasvabhāveṣu bālānāṁ bhogyasammateṣu bhojanasyātmīyasya parikalpiталаkṣaṇānāṁ ca rūpādīnām abhāvas tadabhāvasya ca sadbhāvo bahirdhāśūnyatā |
taddhe śarīre bhoktr̥puḍgalasya bālajanaparikalpitānāṁ rūpādīnāṁ dehasya cābhāvas tadabhāvasya ca bhāvo 'dhyātmabahirdhāśūnyatā |
bhājanaloke sattvaloкābhāvas tatparikalpitasvarūpābhāvas tadabhāvasadbhāvaś ca mahāśūnyatā |
śūnyatājñāne paramārthākāre ca jñātur ākāragrāhitr̥puḍgalasya parikalpitalakṣaṇaśūnyatājñānasya paramārthākārasya cābhāvas tadabhāvasya ca sadbhāvo yathākramaṁ śūnyatāśūnyatā paramārthaśūnyatā ca |
yadarthaṁ bodhisattvaḥ pratipadyate teṣu saṁskr̥tādiṣu sarvabuddhadharmaparyanteṣu bodhisattvapratipattavyeṣu pudgalasya parikalpiталаkṣaṇānāṁ ca dharmāṇām abhāvas tadabhāvasya ca sadbhāvo yathākramaṁ saṁskr̥taśūnyatā yāvat sarvadharmaśūnyateti |
tathā hi nāsti saṁskr̥tasya svāmi vā prayoktā vā pudgalo nāpi saṁskr̥taṁ bālajanaparikalpiteṇātmatvena |
saṁkṣepataḥ sarvavikalpagrahapratipakṣārthaṁ sarvasūtrāntābhisaṁdhivyākaraṇārthaṁ caitā bodhisattvānāṁ śrāvakair asādhāraṇāḥ ṣoḍaśaśūnyatā nirdiṣṭāḥ |
atra ca bhagavatā śūnyatāviṣayaḥ śūnyatāsvabhāvaḥ śūnyatābhāvanāprayojanañ ca darśitam |
tatra śūnyatāviṣayo bhoktr̥ādyartho yāvad buddhadharmaparyanтаḥ |
tatpradarśanaṁ punaḥ śūnyatāyāḥ sarvadharmavyāpakatvajñāpanārtham |
śūnyatāsvabhāvo 'bhāvasvabhāvo 'bhāvabhāvasvabhāvaś ca |[59]
śūnyatāsvabhāvapradarśanaṁ punar adhyāropāpavādapratipakṣeṇa sarvaḍṛṣṭinihsaraṇātmakavajñāpanārtham śūnyatābhāvanāprayojanaṁ śubhadravyasya prāptyartham ity ārabhya buddhadharmāṇāṁ prāptaya iti paryantaṁ tatpradarśanaṁ punaḥ svaparātmano rūpadharmakāyasaṁpatprakarṣaḥ śūnyatābhāvanāyāḥ prāpya iti pradarśanārtham iti
evaṁ śūnyatāyāḥ prabhedo vijñeya iti samalāvasthāyāṁ saṁkliṣṭā nirmalāvasthāyāṁ viśuddheti
adhyātmaśūnyatādayaś ca ṣoḍaśānantaroktabhedaprakārā iti ve(17, a)ditavyāḥ
śūnyatāsādhanam
bhedoddeśānantaraṁ tatsādhanaṁ uddiṣṭam ity atas tannirdeśānantaraṁ pṛcchati kathaṁ sādhanaṁ jñeyam iti
atra kiṁ sādhyate āgantukopakleśasaṁkliṣṭatā svabhāvaviśuddhatā ca
tatra saṁkliṣṭatāsādhanam athikriyāha
yadi na syāt sa saṁkleśo muktāḥ syuḥ sarvadehinaḥ
iti vimokṣaḥ saṁkleśaprahāṇam tat saṁkleśaprahāṇaṁ ca mārgabhāvanātaḥ
tatra yadi dharmāṇāṁ śūnyatā āgantukair upakleśair anutpanne 'pi pratipakṣe 'piśabdād utpanna iva na saṁkliṣṭā bhaved evaṁ sati saṁkleśābhāvāt prayatnam antareṇa sarve sattvā muktāḥ syuḥ prayatnam antareṇeti [Tib.49,b] pratipakṣam antareṇaiva na tu pratipakṣam antareṇa prāṇināṁ asti mokṣa ity avaśyaṁ pṛthagjanavasthāyāṁ tathatāyām āgan[60]
tukair malaiḥ saṃkliṣṭatābhyupagantavyeti |
evaṃ śūnyatāyāḥ saṃkliṣṭaḥ prabhedaḥ sādhito bhavati |
idānīṃ viśuddhiprabhedaṃ sādhayann āha |
yadi na sā viśuddhiḥ syāt prayatnam aphalaṃ bhavet ||
iti |
dehināṃ iti saṃbadhyate |
athotpanne 'pi pratipakṣe 'pīśabdād anutpanna iva na viśuddhā bhaved evaṃ sati mokṣārtha ārambho niṣphalo bhavet |
pratipakṣabhāvanayāpi tanmalaviśuddhyāptāt samalasya ca vimokṣāmpapattitvāt |
na tu mokṣārtha ārambho niṣphala iṣyate |
tasmād avaśyaṃ pratipakṣābhyāsād āgantukopakleśaviśamāc chūnyatāyā viśuddhir abhyupagantavyeti |
evaṃ śūnyatāyā viśuddhiprabhedaḥ sādhito bhavati |
atra saṃkleśadharmagrahaṇāt saṃkleśo viśuddhidharmagrahaṇād viśuddhiḥ |
na tu śūnyatāyāṃ sākṣāt saṃkleśo viśuddhir veśyate |
dharmaparatantratvād dharmatāyāḥ |
ata evāha |
muktāḥ syuḥ sarvadehinaḥ |
iti |
atra hi dehina iti tadupādānam evoktam |
anyathā vā yadi śūnyatāyāḥ sākṣāt saṃkleśo viśuddhir vā bhavet tataḥ kaḥ saṃbandho dehibhir bhaved yasmāc chūnyatāviśuddheś ca dehināṃ[Tib. 50, a] viśuddhiḥ śūnyatāsaṃkleśāc ca saṃkleśa ucyate |

iti |

tac ca naravadhana mānāḥ mānādibhedena
ātmātmīyagrāhyagrāhakatvavivekena
bālajanaparikalpitadharmasvabhāvavivekena ca yat satkāyadṛṣṭidarśanaṃ tat satkāyadṛṣṭiparijnānam
tasya mānasaṃprayojanaṃ bhavaty āvaraṇam
tadutpatti ithandhanāt
kadā punaḥ katham ity ata āha
abhisamayakāle sāntareṇa vyantareṇa cāsmimānasamudācāravacena tadaprahāṇād iti
satyābhisamayakāle nirvedha bhāgīyāvasthāyāṃ
tatra śāntaraḥ pratipakṣavyavahitaḥ
vyantaraḥ pratipakṣāvyavahitaḥ
sa cādhimātre mṛdāu ca pratipakṣe veditavyaḥ
yāvat śāntareṇa vyantareṇa cāsmimānaḥ samudācarate na tāvat satkāyadṛṣṭeḥ prahāṇam asti
satkāyadṛṣṭi[72]
balenaivasmimānapravṛttitaḥ
avidyāsaṃyojanaṃ satkāyadṛṣṭivastuparijñāne āvaraṇam
iti sambadhyate
satkāyadṛṣṭer vastu pañcopādānaskandhāḥ
tadālambanotpattitaḥ
yathoktaṃ sūtre
ye ke cic chrāmaṇā brāhmaṇā vā ātmā ātmeti samanupaśyantaḥ samanupaśyanti
abhinivicamānā abhinivicante
ta imān eva pañcopādānaskandhānupādāyeti
kiṃ punas tatparijñānam
ātmātmīyatāyāḥ skandheṣu bālajanaparikalpitadharmasvabhāvaśūnyatāyāś ca yad darśanam anityato duḥkhataḥ śūnyato 'nātmataḥ samudayādito vā yad darśanaṃ tat teṣāṃ parijñānam
atraiva kāraṇam āha
tenopādānaskandhāparijñānād iti
avidyayā hi nityādiprakārāvṛteṣu skandheṣu tatparijñānābhāvād avidyā skandhaparijñānāvaraṇam
dṛṣṭisaṃyojanaṃ nirodhasatyaparijñāne āvaraṇam iti vartate
dṛṣṭisaṃyojanaṃ hi tisro dṛṣṭayaḥ satkāyāntagrāhamithyādṛṣṭayaḥ
tatra satkāyadṛṣṭiḥ pañcasūpādānaskandheṣv ātmata ātmīyata eti darśanam
antagrāhadṛṣṭir hi tasminn evātmābhikalpitabhāve cācvatadarśanam ucchedadarśanaṃ vā
yena darśanena hetuṃ vā phalaṃ vā kriyāṃ vā sad vā vastv apavadate mithyā vā parikalpayati tan mithyādṛṣṭiḥ
nirodhasatyaparijñānaṃ cāntādibhir ākāraiḥ
kathaṃ punas tat tasyāvaraṇam iti
ata evāha
satkāyāntagrāhadṛṣṭibhyāṃ taduttrāsān mithyādṛṣṭyā ca tadapavādād iti
tatra satkāyāntagrāhadṛṣṭibhyām ahaṃ tatra na bhaviṣyāmīti nirodhād uttrasati
mithyādṛṣṭyā[73]
cāpavadate nāsty eva nirodha iti |
tatra satkāyāntagrāhadṛṣṭibhyāṃ cāntataḥ praṇītato niḥsaraṇataś ca na parijānāti |
mithyādṛṣṭyā nirodho na parijñānāti |
evaṃ dṛṣṭisaṃyojanaṃ nirodhāparijñāna āvaraṇaṃ |
parāmarśasaṃyojanaṃ mārgasatyaparijñāna āvaraṇam iti |
tatra parāmarśasaṃyojanaṃ dṛṣṭiparāmarśaḥ śīlavrataparāmarśaś ca |
tatra dṛṣṭiparāmarśo dṛṣṭau dṛṣṭyāśrayeṣu ca pañcasūpādānaskandheṣu yad utkarṣādito darśanaṃ |
śīlavratapārāmarśaḥ śīlaṃ vrataṃ tadubhayaṃ tadāśrayān vā pañcopādānaskandhān chuddhito muktito nairyāṇikato vā samanupacyato yad darśanaṃ tac chīlavrataparāmarśaḥ |
mārgasya parijñānaṃ punar nairyāṇikādibhir ākāraiḥ |
kathaṃ punaḥ parāmarśasaṃyojanaṃ mārgaparijñāna āvaraṇam iti |
ata āha |
anyenākāreṇa śuddhiparāmarśanād iti |
śīlavratamātreṇa vā kāmasukhālayena vā duṣkaracaryayā vā sāṃkhyādijñānena vā śuddhiparāmarśanāt |
dṛṣṭiparāmarśanena tu yenaiva śuddhiṃ pratyeti tad evāgrataḥ parāmṛśati |
evaṃ parāmarśasaṃyojanena mārgaṃ varjaty anyena tu ākāreṇa śuddhiṃ parāmṛśatīti |
vicikitsāsaṃyojanaṃ ratnatrayaparijñāne āvaraṇam iti vartate |
katham ity ato bravīti |
tadguṇānabhiśraddhānād iti |
vicikitsāsaṃyojanaṃ satyaratneṣu vimatiḥ |
sarvaguṇadoṣasya prakṛṣṭāpanītasya paryantāśrayatvena buddharatne parijñānaṃ |
saṃsārasāgarottarakatvena sarvasahetukadurkhātyantaviyogātmakatvena tatpratipādakatvena ca dharmaratne parijñānaṃ |[75]
mumukṣūṇāṃ saṃsārāc cittam udvejayitavyam tataḥ sarvata upekṣakeṇa bhavitavyam tataḥ sarvānarthamūlatvāt satkāyadṛṣṭiḥ parijñeyā tadanantaraṃ duḥkhasamudayātmakatvena tadvastū parijñeyam tatas tannirodhō vijñeyaḥ tadanantaraṃ tābhanmārgō vijñeyaḥ tataḥ satyadarśanād ratnatraye tadguṇāvabodhād avetya prasādalābhaḥ satyadarśanabalenaiva ca lābhasatkārasaṃlekhayōr dōṣaguṇadarśībhavatīty evam āvaraṇīyadharmakramahetunā tadāvaraṇakramō 'pi veditavyaḥ
nanu sarvaṃ api sarvatra āvaraṇatvena sambhāvād vastupratiniyamenāvaraṇanirdeśō na yuktaḥ naivātra vastupratiniyamaḥ kriyate kiṃ tarhi pratyāsattitaḥ prādhānyenaiṣa nirdeśaḥ
kim etad evāvaraṇam etat tu sāmānyaṃ bodhisattvavṛttakādīnām bodhisattvānāṃ tv āvaraṇaṃ yad anyad daśaśubhādiṣu
āvaraṇaṃ ke ca śubhādaya iti na jñāyate ata āha aprayogō 'nāyatane 'yogavihitaś ca yaḥ
ityevamādy āvaraṇaṃ śubhādayaḥ punaḥ śubhaṃ bodhiḥ samādānam
ityādidaśārtham tatra śubhaṃ hi bodhihetubhūtaṃ sarvakuśa[76]
lamūlam bodhis tu tatphalam |
tatra śubhaṁ sāmānyata uddiṣṭaṁ prabhedataḥ svabhāvataś ca na vijñāyata ity atas tatpratipādanārtham ārabhyate |
samādānāṁ dhimattā 'bhrāntyānāvṛttau |
naty atrāso 'matsarītvaṁ ca |
ity etac chubham iti |
bodhir api vacanamātreṇa svabhāvato na vijñāyata ity ata etad vibhutvaṁ iti darśayati |
śubhasya bodheś ca samādānādīnām iva svātaṁtryeṇāvaraṇanirdeśāt svātaṁtryam evātra vijñāyate na tūddeśanirdeśabhāva ity eke |
utpattyāvaraṇaṁ hi śubhe vyavasthāpyate |
tac ca bodhicittādīnāṁ sāmānyaṁ tacchubbasyotpādanīyatvād dhyityādyāvaraṇaṁ tu samādānādyāvaraṇam |
samādānādiṣu vṛttyādyarthabhedāt |
bodhir apy akopyā prāptavyā cety atas tasyāḥ sthityāvaraṇaṁ prāptyāvaraṇaṁ ca vyavasthāpyate |
tasmān nāsty atra svātaṁtryaprasaṅga iti |
kramaś ca teṣāṁ saṁkṣepataḥ kuśalamūlāni niravaśeṣaṁ niṣpādya bodhiḥ prāptavyā |
vyāsataḥ punaḥ prathamata eva bodhicittam utpādayitavyam |
svaparārthasaṁpadādhārabhūtatvāt |
tataś cittotpādanānurūpā dānādiṣu pratipattir yayā bodhisattva iti labhyate |
tato 'saṁkhyeyakalpanābhyastāḥ |
puṇyajñānasaṁbhārasaṁcitād āśayaviśodhanārthaṁ pudgaladharmabhrāntipratipakṣeṇābhrāntyātmako darśanamārga utpādayitavyaḥ |
tataś cāvaraṇavigamaprabhavitoḥ bhāvanāmārgaḥ prakarṣaṁ netavyo viśuddhiviśeṣayogārthaḥ |
tataḥ śrāvakādyatikramaṇaprayogena yathopacitāni sarvāṇy etāni laukikalokottarāṇi kuśalamūlāni bodhau[77]
parinämayitavyäni |
tatac ca täm avasthäm ägato mahäbodhyäśayatväd buddhabodhisattvänäm gambhïrodäresu nirdeśesu na trasyati |
tato bahuśrutapratipattya yukto dharmam udäram nirdiśati sattvaparipacanärtham |
tatah paripakvacineyesu tadvimocanärtham vaśitvam buddhavam aväpnotti |
atra hi trimśad ävaranäny uktäny ävaranïyaśubhädidaśärthaś cety atah prcchati |

sarvesānm darśana[80]
heyānām anuśayānāṃ sarvatraga­dharma­dhātu­bodha­pratibaddhasya vākliṣṭasyājñānasyālayavijñānasanniviṣṭabījaparipośa ity anye
ābhādyabhāva ātmādiśikalpo hi viparyāsa ity apare
kleśādyāvaraṇānāṃ trayāṇām anyatama'vaśi'ṣṭatā
iti tatra kleśāvaraṇaṃ yas tivrāyatakālasamudācārikleśo rāgādikas tatpratipakṣaprayogānavakāśād abhrānter āvaraṇam
karmāvaraṇam ānantaryādikarma yad apāyanīyamanam
vipākāvaraṇam apāyādayaḥ tatrotpannasyāryadharmotpattibhāgo nāsti
teṣāṃ kleśakarmajanmanām anyatamam apy avaśiṣṭam āvaraṇaṃ na punaḥ kṛtsnam
vimuktiparipacanapārijñāyā aparipakvatā ceti vimuktyāvāhikāyāḥ prajñāyā akarmanyatā
nirvedhabhāgīyāvasthāyā 'prāptatvād iti ke cit
anye tv āhuḥ nirvikalpātra prajñā vimuktiparipacinī tatprayogikā ca dvitīya tayor aparipakvatā tattvādhigamāyogyatā tadupaniṣadbhāvāyogyatā cāparipūrṇasaṃbhāratvād yathākramam iti
kim atrāvaraṇam darśanaheyakleśasya puṣṭavāsanā kleśāvaraṇādīnām anyatama'vaśiṣṭatā prajñāparipākavibandhabhūtam ajñānaṃ saṃbhārāparipūrṇatā ca

nāheyakleśānuśayaḥ |

asaṁbhā[82]
vanā pudgale mahāyānanayābhidhāyinaḥ pudgalasya bhṛāntyāvagamo vacanaṃ ca śīlasamādhiprajñādiguṇasya vāsaṃpratyayaḥ |
anadhimuktir dharme gambhīrodāranayapratipādake buddhavacanatvenāviparītārthena cāsaṃpratyayaḥ |
yathāśabdaṃ vicāraṇā cārthe 'nutpannāniruddhādicāntādyarthaṃ yathāśabdam eva pratipādayati na cābhiprāyikārthatattvaṃ avagacchati tasmād anutpannādyartha anadhimuktiḥ |

acīrṇkāraḥ |
tenānyasantānāprāpaṇāc cirasthitikaṃ saddharmaṃ na karoti |
lābhasatkārapūjāyāṃ gauravaṃ cītrikāraḥ |
saddharmāgaurave mā 'nyo 'pi mattulyo 'dhiko vā bhaviṣyatity ācaṅkā |
tasmāl lābhādivyavadhānāṃ bhaviṣyatīti bhīto dharmaṃ na deśayati |
lābhasatkārā dhyavasāne 'py avidyamāne sattveṣv akāruṇyaṃ ceti sattveṣu karuṇā'bhāvān na dharmaṃ deśayati |
na hy akāruṇikaḥ sattvaduhkhenāpakriyate |
kim atrāvaraṇam agauravaṃ lābhasatkāraśaktir akāruṇyaṃ ceti |
vaśitvasya triṇy āvaraṇānīti vaśitvaṃ hi sarveṣāṃ cittavaśitvānāṃ prāptir buddhānāṃ vaśitvam |
tasya triṇy āvaraṇāni punar na jñāyanta ity ata āha |
yair vibhutvaṃ na labhata[83]
iti cittavacitvapratibaddhād vacitvasyā yair vacitvaṃ na labhate tāny eva vacitvasyāvaraṇānity āha vibhutvaṃ punar jñānaprahāṇaprabhāvasaṃpannatā atha vā vacitvaparyāye nirdiṣṭaṃ yair vacitvam na labhata iti buddhatvaṃ na labhata ity arthaḥ
śrutavyasanaṃ saddharmacravaneṇātyantaviyogaḥ bandhubhir atyantaviyogo bandhuvyasanam tat punaḥ śrutavyasanaṃ saddharmavyasanasaṃvartanīyakarmotpannād bhavati
