svadarśanaparāmarśasthitatvād akalyāṇamitrācritatvād vā yair avipratiśārair mahāyānasūtrāntaḥ pratikṣipto mārabhāṣito 'yam apāyahetuś ca nāyaṃ buddhabhāṣita iti anyān api tasmād vicchandayanti likhitaṃ conmṛṣanti dāhayanti plāvayantīyādiprakāraiḥ saddharmavyasanasaṃvartanīyakarmopadyate saddharmaśravaṇavidhurātmabhāvabījaparipuṣṭitaḥ asati śrutavyasane 'lpaśrutatvaṃ jñānasaṃbhārasyāparipūritaṃ avaraṇam iti prakṛtam
alpaśruto hi na śaknoti dharmān pravivekāya bahuśruto 'pi samādheś cāparikarmitatvam āvaraṇam iti saṃbandhaḥ chandādīnām anyatamābhāvāt aparipūrṇatvād bhāvanāyāṃ vāstānāṃ prahāṇasaṃskārāṇām anyatamābhāvāt samādher aparikarmītatvam
vrajopamādināṃ vā buddhasamādhīnām apariṣpannatvam aparikarmītatvam kim atrāvaraṇam dharmavyasanasaṃvartanīyakarma alpaśrutatvaṃ duṣprajñatā samādhipratyanīkaś copakleśo layauddhatyādika iti[84]
tat punar etad āvaraṇam aprayogādikam |
śubhādau vaśitvaparyantadaśavidhe vastuni |
yasmiṃ arthe daśa kāraṇāni bhavantīti |
utpattyādīni prāptiparyante yasmiṃ arthe tadāvaraṇaviparyayeṇa prayogādīni samādhiparikarmitasvāvasānāni daśa kāraṇāni bhavanti |
atra cotpattyādikāraṇatvāviśeṣād bhinnasyāpi pratyekam apy abhedanirdeśaḥ |
tadarthādhikāreṇeti |
utpattyādyarthādhipatyenety arthaḥ |
teṣāṃ hi śubhādīnām utpattyādikāraṇavibandhaṃ vartamānam utpattyādyāvaraṇam ucyate |
tad yathā |
kuḍyādikam ālokam āvṛṇvat kāryasya cakṣurvijñānasyāvaraṇam ucyate |
evaṃ kṛtvaiṣāṃ daśānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ triṃśadāvaraṇaviparyayeṇa triṃśatkāraṇaṃ nirdiṣṭam |
tatra śubhotpattikāraṇaṃ trayaṃ hi prayoga āyata(24, b)naprayogo yoniśaḥprayogaś ca |
tad yathā cakṣurādaya ity atraiva dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
cakṣurādayo vijñānasyotpattikāraṇam eva sthityādikāraṇam |
tatrāprayogādayaḥ prayogādīnāṃ sākṣād vibandhe vartamānaḥ paraṃparayā śubhotpatter api pratibandhakatvād evam utpattyāvaraṇaṃ śubhasyāvaraṇam ity āha |
tasyotpādaniyatvād iti |
śubhe sthityādyasambhavād utpattyāvaraṇam evaitad iti darśayati |
sthitikāraṇaṃ bodhau |
āśrayaparāvṛttir bodhiḥ |
āśrayo nirmalastathatā |
atra kāraṇam āha |
akopyatvād iti |
yāvad lokas tāvat avasthānād akopyatvam |
nānyathā bhavati vinivartate vā |
śrāvakādīnāṃ tu bodhiḥ svasantāna eva kṛtārthatvād vyāvartate |[85]
bodhisattvā hi sarvasattvahitasukhādhyāśayena saṃbhāreṣu pravartamānā āśrayaparāvṛttilakṣaṇā bodhir yāvat sattvās tāvad avatiṣṭhatīty ucyate na tv anutpattiniravāc chrāvakādivat.
utpattiṃ vināphāvitvāt tu sthiter utpattir apy atra bhavati.
tasmād bodhisthitikāraṇam evocyate notpattikāraṇam.
na cānyat tadvyatirekeṇa sthitikāraṇam astīty atas tad evoktam.
vipradīlhatathatā vā bodhiḥ na tathatāsvabhāvaviśuddhatvād utpattir asti.
kiṃ tarhy āgantukamalāpagamād atyantam ebhiḥ kāraṇaiḥ sthitir evocyate notpattiḥ.
sthitikāraṇāni punar bodheḥ kuśalamūlotpādo manaśikāraḥ paripūryasambhāraś ca.
atra ca śubhānutpattyādayaḥ kuśalotpattyādivibandhe vartamānā bodheḥ sthitiviyātaṃ kurvanti sthityāvaraṇam uktāḥ.
tad yathā catvāra āhārāḥ sattvānām ity atraiva nidarśanam.
na hi kavaḍakārādibhir āhārair apūrvāḥ sattvā utpadyante pūrvotpannās tu vyavasthāpyanta eva.
dhruvīkāraṇam iti dhṛtir bodhicittaṃ sarvakuśaladharmādhārabhūtatvāt.
atraiva nidarśanam yady asyādhāraṇaṃ tatra tat tasya dhṛtiḥ dhāryate 'neneti kṛtvā tad yathā bhājanalokaḥ sattvalokasyeti.
tatkāraṇaṃ gotraṃ kalyāṇamitrasampadnatā cittaparīkhedaś ca.
tatra dhruvīkāraṇa(25, a)viruddhatvād gotravaidhuryadikaṃ dhṛiter apy āvaraṇam uktam.
ata evāha dhṛtyāvaraṇaṃ samādāna āvaraṇam bodhicittasyādhārabhūta[86]
tvād iti |
abhivyaktikāraṇaṃ dhimattve bodhisattvasya prakāśaniyo |
tac ca pratipattisaṃpad ucitajanasaṃvāso rāgadracittajana saṃvāsaś ca |
atra dhimattve pratipattivaidhuryadayo 'bhivyaktyāvaraṇatvenocyante |
ata evāha abhivyaktyāvaraṇaṃ dhimattve |
tasya prakāśaniyatvād iti |
tad yathā rūpasyāvabhāsa ity atrāva nidarśanam |
avabhāso hi rūpamātrasya abhivyaktikāraṇam eva notpattyādikāraṇam |
vikārakāraṇam abhrāntau |
samāropāpavādabhrāntipari vṛttyātmakatvād abhrāntir darśanamārgaḥ |
abhrānter vikāra ucyate |
tad yathā bhasmādayaḥ kāṣṭhādīnāṃ |
tasya trīṇi kāraṇāni viparyāsa dṛṣṭhulyasyopaghaṭaḥ |
kleśādyāvaraṇānāṃ trayāṇām abhāvaḥ |
vimuktiparipaciniyāḥ prajñāyāḥ paripakva bhāvaḥ |
tad yathā agnyādayaḥ pākyādīnām iti pākyo hy odanādikas taṇḍulādivilakṣaṇas tatsaṃtānāntarbhūtaḥ |
tatkāraṇaṃ hy agnyādikam |
viśleṣakāraṇam anāvaraṇe |
darśanamārgapraheyād āvaraṇāc cheṣam āvaraṇam anena prahīyata ity anāvaraṇaṃ bhāvanāmārgaḥ |
tasya trīṇi kāraṇāni sahajadauṣṭhulyopaghaṭo viryam apramādaś ca |
teṣāṃ ca viśleṣakāraṇapratipakṣatvāt |
ata evāha viśleṣāvaraṇam anāvaraṇe |
etad api viśleṣakāraṇapratipakṣabhāvenāvāvaraṇam |
atraiva kāraṇam āha |
tasyāvaraṇavisaṃyogatvād iti |
visaṃyujyate 'neneti viśleṣaḥ |
pariṇatikāraṇaṃ pariṇāme |
tena mahābodhau kuśalamūla[87]
prayojakalakṣaṇatvāt |
tat punar bhavabhogavāimukhyaṁ līnacittatābhāvaś ca |
tataś ca pariṇatyāvaraṇaṁ pariṇatikāraṇavibandhena draṣṭavyam |
kiṁ kāraṇam |
nater mahābodhau cittapariṇatilakṣaṇatvād iti |
sampratyayakāraṇam atrāse |
he ca pudgalasaṁbhāvanā dharmādhimuktir abhi(25, b)prāyikārthādhigamaś ca |
ebhir dharmaiḥ sampratyayo bhavatīty ataś ca sampratyayakāraṇavibandhena saṁpratyayāvaraṇam atrāse |
atraiva kāraṇam āha |
asaṁpratyayena trasanād iti |
asaṁpratyayena hi dharmasya trasyati |
saṁpratyāyanakāraṇam amatsaritve |
tat punaḥ saddharmaguruvaṁ lābhasatkāra(Tib. 63, b)pūjādirapekṣatā sattveṣu ca kāruṇyam |
saṁpratyāyanakāraṇaviruddhatvāc ca saṁpratyāyanāvaraṇam amatsaritve 'vasthāpyate |
atraiva kāraṇam āha |
dharmāmatsaritvena pareṣāṁ saṁpratyāyanātvād iti |
saddharme 'gauravādito dharme mātsaryaṁ karoti |
dharmamātsarikatvāc ca pareṣāṁ dharmaṁ na deśayatīti |
ataḥ saṁpratyāyanāvaraṇam amatsaritva iti |
vaśitvasya trīṇi kāraṇāni |
dharmavyasanasaṁvartanīyakarmābhāvaḥ |
bahuśrutatvam |
samādhiparikarmitatvam |
tudviruddhatvāc chrutavyasanādikaṁ vaśitvāvaraṇaṁ vyavasthāpyate |
atraiva kāraṇam |
tasya vaśitvasya vibhutvapraptijakṣaṇatvād iti |
ke cit tu śubhādīnām evam anukrame kāraṇam āhuḥ |
kuśaladharmopastabdhasantatir bodhiprāptibhāginī |
tataḥ pra[88]
dharmataḥ śubham uktam |
tadanantaraṁ bodhir uktā |
bodhicittaṁ cāgamyaitadubhayasampādane samartho bhavatīti |
ā bodher bodhicittasya sarvakuladharmadhārabhūtatvāt |
atas tadanantaraṁ bodhicittam uktam |
utpāditabodhicittasya ṣaṭpāramitāyāṁ prayogaḥ kujanaduṣṭajanābhyāṁ ca parihāraḥ |
evam asya bodhisattvabhāvaḥ prakāśībhavati |
ataḥ samādānānantaraṁ dhīmattvam uktam |
evaṁ ca prayujyamāno viparyāsaṁ prajahāti |
aviparyastaṁ ca jñānam utpādayati |
ato dhīmattvānantaram abhyantir uktā |
sa punar darśanamārgaḥ |
tadabhyasanācavaraṇaṁ vijahātīty atas tadanantaram āvaraṇaparihāṇam anāvaraṇam uktam |
nirāvaraṇasya bhavabhogaśaktyabhāvād buddhātva eva mānasaṁ pariṇāmayati nānyatheti |
atas tadanantaraṁ natir uktā |
evaṁbhūtasya ca sugambhīreṣv api buddhadharmeṣu prāmodyotpattiḥ |
atrāsa ity atas tadanantaram atrāsa uktaḥ |
evaṁ dharmapratyakṣeṇa yena yenopāyena sattvavinayaṁ paśyati taṁ tam evopāyaṁ bhajata iti tasya kuto mātsaryaṁ bhaved ity atrāsānantaram amātsaryam uktam |
evaṁ prahīṇāvaraṇaḥ paripūrṇaśukladharmaś ca bodhisattvaś citte vaśitvaṁ pravartata ity atas tadanantaraṁ vaśitvam uktam |
eṣo 'nukrama iti |[89]
asmin paricchede āvaraṇaprasaṅga ity ataḥ śubhādīnām utpattikāraṇāny āvaraṇavat sūtritāni |
prasaṅgāgatānām antaraślokena saṃgrahaḥ kriyate |
kāraṇaṃ daśadhotpattau sthitau dhṛtyāṃ prakāśane |
vikāraviśleṣaṇatau śraddhāgamakalaptyādiṣu ||
eṣa dvitīyo 'ntaraśloka udāharaṇasaṃgrahaṇārthaṃ cakṣurbhojanādyapavahnyādis tadudāhṛtiḥ |
dātraśilpajñatā dhūmahetumārgādayo matā ||
iti ||
[4) Bodhipakṣapāramitābhūmiṣv āvaraṇam]
[a) Bodhipakṣeṣv āvaraṇam]
pakṣapāramitābhūmiṣv anyad āvaraṇaṃ punaḥ |
pūrvaṃ hy aviśeṣeṇa śubhaṃ nirdiṣṭaṃ tadāvaraṇaṃ ca |
idānīṃ tu pakṣaś cetyādibhedena sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇabhedena ca śubhaṃ nirdeśayati |
vastv akauśalakausidya |
ityādibhedena ca pakṣādiśubhānām āvaraṇam |
tatra sādhā[90]
raṇaṃ bodhipakṣāḥ śrāvakabodhisattvayoraviśeṣeṇa tatrāvikārāt|
asādhāraṇaṃ tu pāramitā bhūmayaś ca bodhisattvānāṃ karaṇam bodhisattvānām eva tadādhipatyāt|
pūrvaṃ hi śubhādhetvāvaraṇadvāreṇa phalāvaraṇam uktam|
iha tu sākṣāt phalāvaraṇam eveti pūrvasmād anyad ucyate|
atha vā'prayogādibhyo vastvakauśalādikam anyad eveti tad anyad ucyate|
atha vā śubhaṃ bodhiś cety atra bodhisattvasyaivopāyasahitayoś caryāphalayoraprayogādikam āvaraṇam uktam|
iha tu śubhasyaiva sādhāraṇasyāsādhāraṇasya ca bodhipakṣādivibhāgena vastvakauśalādikam aprayogādikād anyad āvaraṇaṃ veditavyam|
tatra śrāvakādibhedena bodhitrayam|
tadanurūpeṇa smṛtyupasthānādayo mārgaparyantā bodhipakṣā ity ucyate|
daśa pāramitā daśa ca bhūmayo yathāsūtram|
smṛtyupasthāneṣu vastvakauśalam āvaraṇam iti tāni punaḥ kāyavedanācittadharmasmṛtyupasthānāni catvāri|
teṣāṃ punar vastu kāyavedanācittadharmāḥ|
tadviṣayatvāt|
tatra śrayābhiniveśavastu kāyaḥ|
ātmaparibhogābhinive(26, b)śavastu vedanā|
ātmābhiniveśavastu cittam|
ātmasaṃkleśavyavadānābhiniveśavastu dharma iti|
kāyādiṣu yaḥ svasāmānyalakṣaṇānavabodhaḥ sa tatrākauśalam|
tatra svalakṣaṇam anekācucidrāvyeṇa saṃgṛhītātmatvaṃ kāyasya|
sukhādisadbhāvo vedanāyāḥ|
viṣayaprativijñaptir vijñānasya|
dharmāṇāṃ yathāsaṃbhavam|
sāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ punar anityāduḥkhaśūnyānātmādyākārāḥ|
atha vā kāyādiṣv aśuciduḥkhānityānātmākāreṇāpratipattiḥ śucisukhanityātmākāreṇa vā vikalpo vastvakauśalam|
yasmād ajñānaṃ darśanaṃ vā smṛtyupasthāneṣv āvaraṇam|
prajñāsmṛtisvabhāvye[91]
sati smṛtyupasthānānāṃ kasmād vastvakāuśalam evāvaraṇam ucyate na tu vastvasmaraṇam apiti prajñādhīnapravṛttitivāt |
tathā hi darśanātmakatvāt prajñāyāḥ prajñāpṛṣṭhalabdhā ālambane smṛtyādayaḥ pravartante na tu svātantryabhāvaḥ prajñāvat |
tasmān nāsmaraṇam atrāvaraṇaṃ uktam iti |
samyakprahāṇeṣu kāusidyam kim āvaraṇam iti prakṛtam |
viryaṃ hy atrotpannānutpannavīpakṣaprahāṇānutpattaye utpannānutpannapratipakṣabhāvyobhāvotpādāya ca pravartamānaṃ samyakprahāṇākhyāṃ pratilabhate |
tat punar bhedābhāve 'pi phalabhedāc caturdhā vyavasthāpyata iti smṛtiḥ |
ataḥ samyakprahāṇānāṃ viryātmakatvād ālasyam āvaraṇam uktam |
ṛiddhipādeṣu samādher dvayahīnatā āvaraṇam ity anenardhyata ity ṛiddhiḥ |
sā ca chandasviryacittamīmāṃsāsamādhiḥ |
evarddhipādaḥ |
ākāśagamananirmāṇādikāyā ṛiddheḥ pratiṣṭhābhūtatvāt |
gamanaṃ vā ṛiddhiḥ |
ākāśagamanādikā samṛiddhir [Tib. 65, b] ity arthaḥ |
tasyāḥ pāda ṛiddhipādāḥ |
tadādhāratvāt |
śaiva chandasamādhyādicaturviddhā samādhiḥ |
tatra chandasamādhāu chandaprādhānyam |
taddvāreṇa samādhilābhāt |
na tu viryādayas tatra na santi |
chandasahāyatvād apradhānās tu te tatreti na te prakācitāḥ |
evam viryacittamīmāṃsāsamādhiṣu vācyam |
samādher dvayahīnatā āvaraṇam uktam |
na taj jñāyate |
ata āha |
aparipūrṇena chandasviryacittamīmāṃsānām anyatama(27,a)vaikalyād[vikala]bhāvanayā ca prahāṇasaṃskāravaikalyād[92]
iti |
tatra paripūrihinatā tāsāṃ chandaviryacittamimāṃsānām anyatamavaikalyāt tadādhipatyasya samādhер abhāvāt |
bhāvanā hīyata ity aṣṭaprahāṇasaṃskārāṇām anyatamavaikalyāt |
te śraddhācchandaviryapraśrabdhisṃṛtisaṃprajanyacetanopekṣātmakāḥ |
eṣāṃ hi prahāṇasaṃskārāṇāṃ bhāvanayā samādhāu vaśitvam avāpyate nānyathety ata eṣāṃ vaikalyād bhāvanāhinateṃ na samādhāu vaśitvam avāpyate |
kim atrāvaraṇam |
chandādiprakṣāṇāṃ prahāṇasaṃskārāṇāṃ ca vipakṣapakṣyaḥ |
yad uta |
kāusidyam ālambanasaṃpramoṣaḥ |
layauddhatye 'saṃskāraḥ saṃskāraś ceti |
indriyeṣu mokṣabhāgīyānām aropaṇam iti |
saṃsārād uttrasto mokṣam adhikṛtya kuśalamūlaṃ yo 'bhisaṃskaroti tena mokṣalabdhau niścitaṃ mokṣabhāgīyam ucyate |
[Tib. 66, a] tasmiṃś cāropite śraddhādaya indriyākhyāṃ pratilabhante nānyathety ato mokṣabhāgīyānām aropaṇam indriyeṣv āvaraṇam uktam |
kim atrāvaraṇam |
yenopakleśena mokṣabhāgyaṃ na ropyate |
sā punarbhavasaktir nirvāṇabhītiś ca |
bahuṣu teṣām indriyāṇāṃ daurbalyam |
āvaraṇam iti prakṛtam |
kathaṃ punar indriyāṇāṃ daurbalyam ity ata āha |
vipakṣavyavakiraṇād iti |
āśraddhyakāusidyamuṣitasmṛtivikṣepaduṣprajñātmakena vipakṣeṇābhibhūtād ity arthaḥ |
indriyāṇi hy uṣṇagalamdrdhanābhyāṃ prabhāvitāni |
etac ca dvividham api nirvethabhāgīyaṃ durbalam āśraddhyādivipakṣābhibhavāt |
ata eva tasmāt parihāṇisambhavaḥ |
nirjitavipakṣatvāt tu śraddhādīni kṣānti laukikā gradharmāvasthāyāṃ balākhyāṃ pratilabhante |[93]
tasmād yat teṣām eva śraddhādīnām uṣmagatamūrdhānavasthāyāṃ vipakṣābhibhavād daurbalyaṃ tad balānām āvaraṇam |
tasmin sati balāvasthāsambhavād iti |
kim atrāvaraṇam |
sa evāśradddhyādiko vipakṣaḥ |
bodhyaṅgeṣu dṛṣṭidoṣaḥ |
āvaraṇam iti prakṛtam |
bodhīty atra darśanamārgo 'bhipretaḥ |
tasyās tāni smṛtidharmapravicayavīryapṛti(27,b)praśrabdhisamādhyupekṣātmakāni saptabodhyaṅgāni darśanaheya kleśaprahāṇakāla utpadyanta ity aṅgānity ucyante |
yadi tu [Tib. 66, b] bodher anukūlatvād aṅgāni syur evaṃ smṛtyupasthānādīnām api bodhyaṅgatvaṃ prasajyate |
katham idaṃ bodhyaṅgeṣu dṛṣṭidoṣa āvaraṇam ity ata āha |
teṣāṃ darśanamārgaprabhāvitatvād iti |
darśanamārgavyavasthāp.itatvād ity arthaḥ |
kaḥ punar ayaṃ dṛṣṭidoṣaḥ |
parikalpit āḥ pañcadṛṣṭayo vicikitsā avidyā tadālambanāś ca rāgādayaḥ sasaṃprayogāḥ kleśopakleśāḥ |
nanu vicikitsādayo 'py atrāvaraṇam |
āvaraṇaṃ bhavatu |
iha tu pradhānavivakṣayā dṛṣṭidoṣopadeśa iti na teṣām anupadeśo virudhyate |
teṣāṃ ca dṛṣṭyādīnām ālayavijñānasanniviṣṭaṃ bījam eva darśanamārgapraheyatvāt tadāvaraṇam ucyate |
mārgāṅgeṣu samyagdṛṣṭisaṃkalpaväkkarman tājīvavyāyāmasmṛtisamādhyākhyeṣu dauṣṭhulyādoṣa āvaraṇam iti prastutam |
atraiva kāraṇam āha |
teṣāṃ mārgāṅgānāṃ bhāvanāmārgaprabhāvitatvād iti |
bhāvanāmārgavyavasthāpitatvāt |
kiṃ sa dauṣṭhulyadoṣaḥ |
vyāsataḥ satkāyāntagrāhadṛṣṭi sahaje sasaṃprayoge tadālambanā viṣayālambanāś ca rāgādayaḥ kleśopakleśāḥ sasaṃprayogāḥ |
teṣām ālayavijñānasanniv.ṣṭaṃ yad bījaṃ tad dau[94]
sthūlyaṃ |
saṃkṣepatas tu darśanaprahātavyād anyā sāsravā tacchaḥkir ālayavijñānasaṃnviṣṭā dauṣṭhulyam |
tac ca bhāvanāmārgapraheyatvān mārgāṅgeṣv āvaraṇatvena vyavasthāpyate |
uktaṃ bodhipakṣeṣv āvaraṇam ||
tadanantaraṃ pāramitāsu vaktavyam ity ata āha |
pāramitāsv āvaraṇam |
aiśvaryasya sugatyāś ca sattvātyāgasya cāvṛtiḥ |
iti vistaraḥ |
atra daśānāṃ pāramitānāṃ yasyāḥ pāramitāyā yat phalaṃ tadāvaraṇena tasyā āvaraṇam udbhāvitam |
kim atra kāraṇaṃ yan mātsaryādayo vastuto dānādivibandhe vartamānās tatphalāvaraṇatvenocyante na punaḥ pāramitāvaraṇatveneti |
tatphālārthena dānādibhyaḥ sarvaṃ pravartate |
tasmān mātsaryādi(28, a)prahāṇa ādarotpādanārthaṃ phalāvaraṇam uktam |
na pāramitāvaraṇam iti |
athā vā mātsaryādikam āvaraṇam aiśvaryādivipakṣabhūtaṃ dāridryādiphalaṃ janayad aiśvaryādiphalapratibandhat tadāvaraṇam ucyate |
kiṃ tāsāṃ phalam |
tatra dānapāramitāyāḥ phalaṃ prādhānyenaiśvaryādhipatyam |
śīlapāramitāyāḥ sugatiḥ |
kṣāntipāramitāyāḥ sattvāparityāgaḥ |
vīryapāramitāyā doṣaguṇahānivṛddhi |
dhyānapāramitāyāḥ śāsane sattvāvatāraṇam |
prajñāpāramitāyās tāṃ guruṃ vimocayati |
upāyakauśalyapāramitā bodhipariṇāmanayā kuśalākṣayatvam |
praṇidhānapāramitāyāḥ śubhanairantaryam |
balapāramitāyāḥ kuśaladharmaniryātikaraṇam |
jñānapāramitāyā dharmopa[95]
bhogaḥ sattvapariṇācanāṁ ca |
āiśvaryādhipatya āvaraṇaṁ iti tatrāiśvaryaṁ dhanopakaraṇabahutā |
adhipatyaṁ tadupabhogavaśitvaṁ |
asyobhayasyāvaraṇaṁ āvaraṇaṁ dānapāramitāyās tatphalatvāt |
kiṁ punas tan mātsaryam |
sugatyāvaraṇam iti śobhanagatīnāṁ sugatiḥ |
manuṣyo devāś ca |
tasyāvaraṇam āvaraṇaṁ śīlapāramitāyās tatphalatvāt |
tat punar dauḥśīlyaṁ kutsitaṁ kāyavākkarma |
sattvāparityāga ity upakārivad apakārisattvopādānam |
etac ca kṣānti pāramitā phalaṁ |
tadāvaraṇaṁ krodhaḥ |
doṣaguṇahānivṛddhiḥ svapārāpakāraprayuktānām utpannānāṁ prahāṇam anutpannānāṁ cānutpādo doṣāṇāṁ hāniḥ |
svapara anugrahaprāvṛttānām utpannānāṁ bhūyobhāvo 'nutpannānāṁ cotpādo guṇānāṁ vṛddhiḥ |
kiṁ punar atrāvaraṇam kausidyam |
vineyāvatāraṇāvaraṇam iti |
vineyānāṁ śāsane 'vatāraṇaṁ svargāpavargalābhakāraṇatvenābhyupagamanāt |
upacāntya gaurāva utpadyamāne sattvāḥ saṁjālaparddhy aprayatnena śāsanaṁ pratipadyante |
atra ca vikṣepa āvaraṇam |
vimocanāvaraṇam iti vimocanaṁ kleśaprahāṇam |
tac cānuśāsanyā bhavati |
anuśāsanī ca prajñayā nānyathety ataḥ prajñāpāramitāphalaṁ vimocanam |
tadāvaraṇaṁ kliṣṭaprajñā moho duṣprajñā vā |
dānādyakṣayāvaraṇam iti dānaśīladīnām akṣayatve yad āvaraṇaṁ tad upāyapāramitāyā āvaraṇam |
tat punar dānādyakṣayatvaṁ kathaṁ bhavatīty ata āha |
bodhipariṇāmanayā[96]
tadakṣayatvād iti |
mahābodhipariṇataṃ dānādikuśalamūlaṃ na kṣīyate buddhatvam iva |
tadāvaraṇaṃ mahābodhipariṇāmanā lakṣaṇānupāyajñatety eke |
anupāyakauśalyaṃ trimaṇḍalagrāhas tadāvaraṇam ity anye |
sarvajānmasu kuśalanairantaryapravṛttyāvaraṇam iti kuśalanairantaryaṃ pratidinaṃ pratikṣaṇaṃ vā |
tac ca praṇidhānapāramitāyā phalam |
katham ity ata āha |
praṇidhānavaśena tadanukulopapattiparigraḥād iti |
praṇidhānavaśena hi dānādīnāṃ nairantaryapravṛttyānuguṇām upapattiṃ parigṛhṇāti |
tasyāvaraṇaṃ dānādyanukulopapattāv apraṇidhānam |
tasyaiva kuśalasya niścayāvaraṇam iti |
pratidinaṃ pratikṣaṇaṃ vaikikaraṇaṃ niścayaḥ |
atraiva kāraṇam āha |
pratisaṃkhyānabhāvanābalibhyāṃ vipakṣānabhibhavād iti |
atra pratisaṃkhyānabalaṃ dhairyasahitā prajñā |
dhairyaṃ punaḥ prajñaptidharmaḥ |
sahaje vīryasamādhiprajñātraye [Tib. 68, b] pratisaṃkhyānatvāt |
bhāvanābalam abhyāsasahitenāprayatnena dānādiṣu pravartanāt tatpratipakṣeṇa mītsaryādīnā anabhibhavāt |
kim atrāvaraṇam |
apratisaṃkhyānabhāvanā daurbalyam |
ātmaparayor dharmasaṃbhogaparipācanāvaraṇam iti saṃbhogikaparṣanmaṇḍale vyavasthāno gambhīrodāramahāyānadharmasy saṃbhogaś ca vicitrākārair nirmāṇakarmabhir dharamadeśanayā sattvaparipākaś ca phalam |
tadāvaraṇaṃ punar yathābhūtaśrutārthavicāraṇā mandaprajñatā cāvaraṇam iti |
atra ca dravyataḥ ṣaṭpāramitā |
nāmato daśa |
prajñāpāra[97]
mitāprabhāvitaśca catasṛṇāṃ pāramitānāṃ |
tatra prajñāpāramitā lokottaranirvikalpaṃjñānaṃ |
tena jñānena krameṇa sarvāvaraṇaprahāṇam |
upāyapraṇidhānabalajñānapāramitāḥ punar lokottaramārgapṛṣṭhalabdhajñānasaṃgṛhītāḥ |
jñānapāramitā prakṛṣṭā sati kathaṃ na nirvikalpa iti |
jñānapāramitā hi nirvikalpa jñānapṛṣṭhalabdhajñānam |
tajjñānāvabuddhakvaṃ paricchedya svayaṃ ca dharmasaṃbhogaṃ pratyanubhavati |
parāṃś ca paripācayati |
tac caitan nirvikalpe dvayam api nāstity ato nāvikalpā jñānapāramiteti |
uktaṃ pāramitānām āvaraṇam ||
tadanantaraṃ bhūmiṣu vaktavyam ity ata āha |
bhūmiṣv api yathākramam |
sarvatraga[Tib. 69, a]rtho 'gryārthaś ca
iti vistaraḥ |
tatra bhūmayo daśa |
pramuditā vimalā prabhākarī arciṣmatī sudurjayā |
abhimukhī dūraṅgamā |
acalā sādhumatī dharmameghā ca |
tatra dharmadhātoḥ sarvatragādyarthe 'dhigamād avakāśaviśeṣā uttarottarapratipatti sthānarūpeṇa tadutpannaguṇavasturūpeṇa ca bhūmaya ucyante |
dharmadhātoḥ sarvatragādyarthe yena yena vihāreṇa bodhisattvo bahulaṃ viharati tasmiṃs tasmin vihāre 'vasthānārthena bhūmyākhyety apare |
vyavasthānaṃ punar bhūmibhiḥ pramuditāyāṃ darśanamārgotpattivād bodhisattvo dharmam abhisameti |
abhisamitadharmo vimalāyāṃ svarasavāhinam adhiśīlaṃ śikṣāviśeṣaṃ niṣpādayati |[88]
sarvasūkṣmāpattidaucilyavigamāt prabhākaryāṃ janmāntare 'py acyavanadharmakarṇādhicittaṃ śikṣāviśeṣaṃ niṣpādyati |
arciṣmatīsudurjayābhimukhīṣu bodhipakṣasatyapratītyasamutpādālambanaṃ adhiprajñāṃ śikṣāviśeṣaṃ niṣpādayati |
tāsām eva śikṣāṇāṃ śeṣāsu bhūmiṣu caturvidhaṃ phalaṃ veditavyam |
tatra dūraṅgamāyāṃ prayatnasahitam 'animittatajñāne 'vasthānaṃ niṣpādayati |
acalāyām anāhośenānimittatajñāne 'vasthānaṃ sārdhaṃ kṣetraviśuddhyā niṣpādayati |
sādhumatyāṃ pratisaṃvidguṇaviśeṣaṃ niṣpādayati |
yena dhārmakathito bhavati |
[Tib. 69, b] dharmameghāyāṃ samādhidhāraṇīguṇaviśeṣaṃ niṣpādayati |
sarvatrāgārthādiṣu daśavidhādharmādhātuṣu iti dharmādhātau daśabhir bodhisattvabhūmibhiḥ sarvatrāgādyartho 'dhigamyate |
tatra yad akliṣṭam ajñānaṃ tad daśasu bodhisattvabhūmiṣu daśavidham āvaraṇaṃ yathākramam |
tadvipakṣatvād iti |
bhūmivipakṣatvāt |
bhūmayo hi lokottarasamādhiprajñādyavakāśaviśeṣātmatā |
tadutpattivibandhād a(29, b)kliṣṭam ajñānaṃ vipakṣaḥ |
na cātrākliṣṭam evājñānam āvaraṇaṃ kiṃ tarhy anye 'pi kleśopakleśāḥ |
tathā hi |
pramuditāyāḥ sarva eva darśanamārgapraheyā āvaraṇam |
yasmāt sarvatrāgārthāvabodhena darśanamārgeṇa satyeṣu sarve vipratipattyanucayāḥ prahīyante |
bhāvanāpraheyāḥ punaḥ śeṣābhir bhūmibhis |
te tv aprahīṇā api prahīṇavat |
bodhisattvānāṃ na saṃkleśakarā bhavantīti |
imam eva cārtham adhikṛtyoktā gāthā
kleśo bodhyaṅga āpanno mahopāyaprayogīnaḥ |
saṃsāro 'py upaśāntyātmā tato 'cintyas tathāgataḥ ||[99]
daśasu ca bhūmiṣu daśa pāramitā yathākramam atiriktatarā bhavantīti vacanāt tadvipakṣasyāpi mātsaryādikasya yathākramaṁ prahāṇam iti gamyate |
yady anyo 'pi kleśa āvaraṇaṁ syāt kasmād uktaṁ bhūmiṣu akliṣṭam evājñānam āvaraṇam iti |
asādhāraṇatvāt |
etad dhi bodhisattvānām eva nirvāṇādhigamavibandhaír vartate na śrāvakādīnām |
tathā hi |
śrāvakāḥ pratyekabuddhāś ca tasmin saty eva mucyante na tu bodhisattvā ity ato 'sādhāraṇam |
etad bhūmiṣu āvaraṇam uktaṁ nāpi tad eva bhūmiṣu āvaraṇam iti kṛtvā kleśas tu śrāvakādibhir bodhisattvānāṁ samāna iti tasyāvaraṇatvena bodhisattvabhūmiṣu na vidhir na pratiṣedha iti tasyāvacane 'py āvaraṇatvaṁ na nivartate |
anya āha |
akliṣṭam ajñānaṁ śrāvakādibhir bodhisattvānāṁ samānam iti bhūmīnāṁ viśeṣeṇāvaraṇam ucyate |
kleśas tv ānuṣaṅgika iti noktam |
kleśo 'pi śrāvakādibhir bodhisattvānāṁ samāna evety asāv ānuṣaṅgiko na syād iti |
anye tv āhuḥ |
yad bhūmīnām ajñānam āvaraṇaṁ tac chrāvakāṇām akliṣṭam ajñānaṁ san nāpi vimuktivibandhanam |
yasmāc chrāvakā bhūmisv ajñāne saty eva vimucyante |
bodhisattvānān tu kliṣṭaṁ nirvāṇādhigamane vibandhahetubhūtād iti |
naítad evam ity anye akliṣṭā daśadhāvṛttir iti |[100]
prathamayā hi bhūmyā dharmadhātoḥ sarvatragatvam pratividhyati tatra prathamā bhūmiḥ prathamā lokottara prajñā saparivārā darśanamārgasaṃgṛhītā dharmadhātuḥ śūnyatā sāmānyalakṣaṇenaikatra yathā sarvatrāpi
tatheti sarvatragatvāt sarvatragaḥ
dharmadhātur vinirmukto yasmād dharmo na vidyate
iti vacanaṃ dharmadhātoḥ sarvatragajñāpakam pratividhyatiḥ sākṣātkarotīty arthaḥ tena prativednena svaparasamatiṃ pratilabhate yo 'haṃ sa paro yo vā paraḥ so 'ham iti paratratmani ca śunyatāyā abhedadarśanāt svaparātmanor abhedaṃ manyate ata evāsyāṃ bhūmau dānapāramitādhriktatvā bhavati
tato 'tra bodhisattvo yathā svārthe parārthe pravartate etad dhi prativedhāḥ pratipakṣaḥ pratipakṣaphalaṃ codbhāvitam etac ca tritayam itarāsv api bhūmiṣu vijñeyam
dvitīyayā'grārthaṃ dharmadhātoḥ pratividhyatīti saṃbadhyate prakṛtiprabhāsvaradarśanāt tenāsyaitad bhavati tasmāt tarhy asmābhiḥ samābhinirhāre sarvākārapariśodhanābhinirhāra eva yogaḥ karaṇīya ity āgamaṃ darśayati sūtre hy
uktam yasmād iti daśakuśalāḥ karmapathāḥ prajñākāreṇa paribhāvyamānā yāvac chrāvakayānaṃ saṃvartayanti tata uttarataraṃ pariśodhitā yāvat pratyekabuddhayānaṃ saṃvartayanti
tata uttarataraṃ pariśodhitā yāvad bodhisattvabhūmipariśuddhyai[101]
pāramitāpariśuddhyai caryāvipulatvāya saṁvartante |
tata uttarataraṁ pariśodhitāḥ sarvākārapariśodhitatvād yāvad daśabalabalāya saṁvartante tasmāt tarhy asmābhiḥ samābhinirḥāra iti vistaraḥ |
yasmād etad asyaivaṁ bhavati dvitīyāyāṁ bhūmau tasmāt tarhityādi |
tasmād dvitīyayā'grārthaṁ pratividhyatīti vijñāyate |
abhi[Tib. 71, a]nirḥāraḥ prayatnaḥ kṛityaṁ vā |
sarvākārapariśodhanaṁ dharmadhātau kliṣṭākliṣṭamohāvaraṇam |
tasmāt tasyāṁ bhūmau śilapāramitāviśiṣṭatarā bhavati |
viśiṣṭārthapārthānayā sutarāṁ ātmamātrikāraṇāt |
tritīyayā tannisyandāgrārtham |
pratividhyatīti prakṛitam |
dharmadhātuprativedhaprabḥāvitatvād buddhavacanasya |
yasmāt sarvākārapariśuddhadharmaḳāyasaṁjñakena dharmadhātuvaśena prabhāvito dharma(30,b)dhātunisyandaḥ sūtrādiko deśanādharmaḥ|dharmadhātvagrātayā tannisyandadeśanādharmāgratāyā dharmadhātupariśuddhinimittatvāc ca |
tena dharmadhātunisyandasya śrutasyāgrataṁ viditveti vistaraḥ |
teneti pratiśiddhadharmadḥātunā |
śrutasyeti śrutasūtrādidharmasya |
tasyāgratāṁ viditveti nirvikalpena jñānena tathā pratyātmaṁ jñātvety arthaḥ |
tadartham iti buddhavacanaśravaṇārtham |
trisāhasramahāsāhasrapramiṇāyām apy agnikhadāyām ātmānaṁ prakṣiped ity uktaṁ hi sūtre |
sa ced idaṁ kaścid evaṁ brūyāt |
evam ahaṁ tūbhyam idaṁ dharmapadaṁ samyaksaṁbuddho[Tib. 71, b]panitaṁ bodhi[102]
sattvacariyāpariśodhanaiṁ saṁcrāvayeyaṁ sa cet tvaṁ mahatyām agnikhadāyāṁ samprajvalitāyām ekajvālībhūtāyām ātmānaṁ prapātayeḥ |
mahāntaṁ ca duḥkhāvedanopakramaṁ svaśarīreṇopādadyā iti |
tasyaivaṁ bhavati |
utsahe 'ham ekasyāpi dharmapadasya samyaksambuddhopanitasya bodhisattvacariyāpariśodhanasyārthāya trisāhasramahāsāhasralokadhātav agniparipūrṇe brahmalokād ātmānam utsrasṭum |
kiṁ punaḥ prākṛtāyām agnikhadāyām |
api tu khalu punaḥ sarvair nirayāpāyaduḥkhasaṁvāsair apy asmābhir buddhadharmāḥ paryeṣitavyāḥ kiṁ punar manuṣyaduḥkhasaṁvāsair iti |
ata evāsyāṁ bhūmau kṣāntipāramitātirikatatarā bhavati |
śrutārthaṁ sarvaklecasahanādibhir apy abhedyāt |
caturthyā'parigrahatvārtham |
dharmadhātoḥ pratividhyatiti saṁbadhyate |
caturthyāṁ bhūmau samatvād anātmīyatvaṁ dharmadhātoḥ pratividhyati |
yasmān na dharmadhātoḥ kaścid ātmā nāstīti |
atha vā sarvaviparyāsāviṣayatvān na kasya cit parigrahaḥ |
svayam aviparyāsatvād atra kaścit parigraho nāsti |
yasmād dharmatṛṣṇāpi vyāvartata ity āgamavacanam |
evam arcismatyāṁ niṣprapañcena jñānena dharmadhātvadhigamād bodhipakṣadharmāprāptiḥ |
bodhisattvasya yāpi sūtrādidharmatṛṣṇā[103]
pūrvam āśit sāpi vyāvartate dhyānasamādhisamāpatti dharmatrṣṇety anye |
tasmāt tasyāṃ bhūmau viryapāramitātiriktarā bhavati |
abhikṣṇaṃ vāñmanojalpasamatikrānte bodhipakṣair viharaṇāt |
pañcamyā santānābhinnārtham dharmadhātoḥ pratividhyatity adhikṛtam |
atītānāgatavartamānānāṃ sarvabuddhānāṃ bhagavatāṃ paraṃparato bodhisattvānāṃ ca saṃtānair ātmano 'bhinnasamatāṃ paśyati |
daśabhir viśuddhacittāśayasamatābhir ity āgamaṃ darśayati |
atītabuddhadharmaviśuddhyāśayasamatayā ca |
anāgatabuddhadharmaviśuddhyāśayasamatayā ca |
pratyutpannabuddhadharmaviśuddhyāśayasamatayā ca |
cittaviśuddhyāśayasamatayā ca |
cittaviśuddhyāśayasamatayā ca |
dṛṣṭikāṅkṣāvimatvilekkhāpanayanviśuddhyāśayasamatayā ca |
mārgāmārgajñānaviśuddhyāśayasamatayā ca |
pratipatprahāṇajñānaviśuddhyāśayasamatayā ca |
sarvabodhipakṣyadharmottarottarāvibhāvanaviśuddhyāśayasamatayā ca |
sarvasattvaparipācanaviśuddhyāśayasamatayā ca |
ābhiḥ samatābhiḥ sarvabuddhānāṃ saṃtānābhedaṃ pratividhyati |
ālayavijñānaparāvṛttilakṣaṇadharmakāyasyābhinnatvāt |
tasmāt tasyāṃ bhūmau dhyānapāra[104]
mūḍhviktatarā bhavati paramārthatah satyākārabhāvanābahulatvāt
ṣaṣṭhyā niḥsaṁkleśaviśuddhyarthaṁ dharmadhātoḥ pratividhyatīti saṁbadhyate
pratītyasamutpādalakṣaṇaḥ saṁkleśas tasmiṁ āgantujāt prakṛtyā na saṁkliṣṭaḥ
prākṛtikavicuddher na vicudhyati
evaṁ svasaṁvedyākāreṇa [Tib. 72, b] nirvikalpena jñānena dharmadhātuṁ pratividhyatīty atraivāgamaṁ darśayati
pratītyasamutpāde nāsti sa kaś cid dharmo yaḥ saṁkliśyate vā viśudhyate veti prativedhanāt
ṣaṣṭhyāṁ bhūmau bodhisattvaḥ pratītyasamutpādaṁ viviḍhyati
tatra ca na kasya cid dharmasya saṁkleśaṁ vyavadānam vā pratividhyati
dharmadhātoḥ prakṛtiprabhaāsvaratvāt
pratītyasamutpādāṅgānāṁ ca saṁkleśamātratvāt
na ca tadvyatiriktó 'nyā ātmāsy ātmiyo vā yasmin saṁkleśaḥ saṁkalpyate viśuddhir vā
tasmād e(31,b)vāsyāṁ bhūmau prajñāpāramitā'tiriktatarā bhavati
niḥsaṁkleśaviśuddhyarthena pratītyasamutpādagāmbhīryāvabodhād iti
saptamyā'nānātvārthaṁ dharmadhātoḥ pratividhyatīty adhikṛtam
tasyāṁ bhūmau bodhisattvo 'bhūbhavati sarvanimitlasamudācārābhāvāt
nimittasamudācāradvāratać ca nānātvasamudācāra iti
ato nimittasamudācārābhāvāt saptamyā'nānātvārthaṁ dharmadhātoḥ pratividhyatīty ucyate
imam evārthaṁ pradarśayann āha
nimittābhāvena sūtrādidharmalakṣaṇasya nānātvāsamudācārād iti
yaiḥ sūtrādidharmanimitlair dharmadhātuṁ praviśati tāni balān nānātvena tatpraveśatatpṛṣṭhalabdhayor jñānayor abhimukhībhavanti
yāvat ṣaṣṭhī bhūmiḥ
saptamyāṁ tu bhūmāu sarvanimitlaikarasa̅kāreṇa jñānena dharmadhātor nimittābhā[105]
vatvam prativiḍhyati tāni nimittāni na samudācaranti |
tasmāt
tasyām bhūmāv upāyapāramitā'tiriktaratā bhavati |
yataḥ sarvanimittāny animittatvena pratividhyati nimittakṛitaṁ
ca vyavahāran na virodhayati |
tatprativedhaniśc ca tasyām eva
vatvam pratilabhate |
aṣṭamyā 'napakarsānukarṣārthām |
dharmadhātoḥ pratividhyatīti sambhadhyate |
dharmadhātoḥ prakṛitiparicuddhatvāt
samalāmalāvasthayoḥ viśeṣābhāvāt |
ata evāsu nityam tathaiveti
kṛitvā tathatety ucyate |
nimittavigate kva cid apy apakarṣaś ca
nāsti |
nimittagate kva cid apy utkarṣaś ca nāsti |
tathā hi nimittam
evānimittam iti kim cit |
anutpattikadharmakṣāntilābhād ity
atraiva kāraṇam |
aṣṭamyām hi bhūmau bodhisattvo 'nutpattikadharmakṣāntipratilambhād dharmadhātor abhyuccayābhāvatvam
apacayābhāvatvam caivam pratividhyati |
kaś cid apacayadharmo nāsti samkleśe |
abhyuccayo vā nāsti vyavadāna ity apūrvadharmānutpādāt |
caturdhaāvaśīta nirvikalpaviśīta kṣetrapariśuddhivaśitājñānavaśitā karmavaśitā ca |
tatra nirvikalpajñānavaśitāśrayatvam dharmadhātor veditavyam |
anābhogena sarvaṇimittasamudācārābhāvāt |
saptamyām api nimittasamudācārābhāva sambhavaty abhisamskāreṇeha tv anabhisaṁskāreṇeti viśeṣaḥ |
kṣetraparicuddhivaśitāśrayatvaṁ cāṣṭamyā pratividhyatīti prakṛitam |
kṣetraparicuddhivaśitāśrayatvaṁ dharma[106]
dhātor yatheṣṭaṃ buddhakṣetra parịśodhana parṣanmaṇḍalasaṃdarśanaśaktipratilambbhāt |
tasmāt tasyāṃ bhūmau praṇidhānapāramitā'tiriktatarā bhavati |
nirvikalpakaṣetravacitālābhena kucalanirantara prayogāt |
jñānavacitā"crayatvaṃ navamyā dharmadhātoḥ pratividhyatīti prakṛtam |
katham etad vijñāyate navamyā jñānavacitā"crayatvaṃ pratividhyatīti |
ata āha |
pratisaṃvitprāpteḥ |
asyāṃ hi bhūmau dharmārthaniruktipratibbānapratisaṃvido 'nyair aparyāptāḥ pratilabhate |
ato 'tra jñānavacitā"crayatvaṃ dharmadhātoḥ pratividham iti vijñāyate |
tasmāt tasyāṃ bhūmau balapāramitā 'tiriktatarā bhavati |
prajñābalaviśeṣayogāt |
karmavacitā"crayatvaṃ daśamyā pratividhyatīti saṃbadhyate |
katham etad gamyate |
ata āha |
yatheccchaṃ nirmāṇaiḥ sattvārthakaraṇād iti |
niścayenānena daśamyāṃ bhūmau dharmadhātoḥ karmavacitā"crayatvaṃ pratividhyati |
tena tathāgatakarmasv ādhipatyaṃ pratilabhate |
tathā hi sugatavad daśadiganantalokadhātusthitānāṃ sattvānāṃ nirmāṇakāyair yatheṣṭaṃ yasya yathā yasmin kāle yat kartavyaṃ tasya tathā tasmin kāle tat karoti |
tasmād asyāṃ bhūmau jñānapāramitātiriktatarā bhavati |
dharmaparibhogasattvaparipākaviśeṣayogād iti |
evam āsāṃ yathoktānāṃ bodhisattvabhūmīnān daśavidhadharmadḥātupratiyedhasarvākārapariśodhanavibandhaḥūtaṃ yad akliṣṭam ajñānam ekaikasyām api bhūmyām āvaraṇaṃ tatpratipakṣā bodhisattvadaśabhūmir vyavasthitā |
navamyā daśamyāś ca bhūmeḥ phalena viśeṣaḥ paridīpito na tv ālambanabhedaprativedhaviceṣeṇa |
tadvihārasya kām apy anirūpyāvasthāṃ gatatvāt |
sarvatragādyarthapratiyedaprayojanaṃ daśabhū[107]
miṣu daśānāṃ pāramitānāṃ viśeṣagater yathākramam|
tasmād etad e(32, b)voktaṃ bhūmyāvaraṇaṃ pāramitāviśeṣagatyāvaraṇam iti|
atha vā sūtroktam eva samādhisamāpattyādikam uttarottarabhūmiviśiṣṭaṃ sarvākāraṃ nānāvasānāṃ phalaṃ sarvatragadyarthaprativedhaprayojanam iti ||
[5] Āvaraṇasamāsaḥ]
prāguktāni bahuviddhāni sarvāvaraṇāni dvayāvaraṇāntargatānīti pradarśanārtham āha |
samāsena punaḥ
kleśāvaraṇam ākhyātaṃ jñeyāvaraṇam eva ca |
katham etad gamyate |
samāsato dvayam eva na punas tṛtīyam apy astīti |
ata āha |
sarvāṇy āvaraṇāniha |
iti |
atrāntargatānīti vākyaśeṣaḥ |
sarvāvaraṇāni dvayāvaraṇāntargatāni nānyat tṛtīyam astīti gamyate |
yataś ca sarvāvaraṇāny atraivāntargatāny atas tasyaiva sarvāvaraṇasaṃgrāhakasya kleśajñeyāvaraṇadvayasya kṣayāt sarvāvaraṇebhyo muktir iṣyate |
sarvāvaraṇānāṃ prahāṇam ity arthaḥ |
buddhabhumyāvaraṇe 'nākhyāte katham atrāivāntarbhāvaḥ |
na hy abhidhānād antarbhāvaḥ |
kiṃ tarhi svabhāvāt |
api ca vyāpyāvaraṇavacanāt tad apy uktam eva |
anyathā ca pradeśāvaraṇam eva bhavati |
atha vā [Tib. 74, b] buddhabhumyāvaraṇasaṃgrahārtham āha |[108]
sarvāṇy āvaraṇānīha tatksayād muktir iṣyate
buddhāvaraṇākṣaye na muktir astīti |
ataḥ ceṣāvaraṇavat tad apy āvaraṇam iti pratipāditaṁ bhavati |
saukṣmyaprakarṣāvasthaṁ tv akliṣṭam ajñānaṁ kleśavāsanāś ca buddhabhuṃer āvaraṇam ucyate |
tasmāt saukṣmyaprakarṣād anyathā prekṣituṁ na śakyata ity evam ākhyātaṁ bhavati ||
[Āvaraṇapiṇḍārthaḥ]
mahad āvaraṇaṁ yad vyāpi kleśajñeyāvaraṇaṁ bodhisattvagotṝāṇām |
pratanv āvaraṇaṁ yat prādeśikaṁ kleśāvaraṇam eva śrāvakādigotrāṇām |
prayogāvaraṇaṁ yad udriktam iti teṣām eva rāgādicaritānām tena prayogatvam aprāptam |
prāptyāvaraṇaṁ yat samānam iti samabhāgacaritānāṁ tad dhi prāptivibandhe vartate |
prāptiviśeṣāvaraṇaṁ yad ādānaprahāṇam iti bodhisattvagotṝāṇām adhigamaviśeṣāvaraṇatvāt |
apratiṣṭhitaṁ nirvāṇam adhigamaviśeṣaḥ |
eṣa caiva phalaviśeṣo veditavyaḥ |
saṁyakprayogāvaraṇaṁ (33.a)yan navadhākleśalakṣaṇam iti navasamyojanāny āvaraṇam iti vacanāt|
tāni samyakprayogasyāvaraṇaṁ bhavanti yathā tathoktam |
hetvāvaraṇaṁ yac chubhādau daśavidhahetvādhikārād iti tad dhi śubhādihetvibandhatvāreṇāvaraṇaṁ bhavatīti hetvāvaraṇam ucyate |
tattvapaveśāvaraṇaṁ yad bodhipakṣeṣv (Tib. 75.a) iti tair hi tattvaṁ praviśati |
śubhānuttaryāvaraṇaṁ yat pāramitāsu tata uttariśubhābhāvāt |[109]
tadviceṣagatyāvaraṇaṁ yad bhūmiṣv iti pāramitā eva hi lokottararuceṣācaryabhūtā bhūmaya ucyante |
saṁgrahitāvaraṇaṁ yat
samāsena dvividham iti kleśāvaraṇaṁ jñeyāvaraṇaṁ ca etena sarvāvaraṇasaṁgrahāt |
piṇḍārthavacanaṁ sukhodgrahaṇadhāraṇārthaṁ |
piṇḍīkṛtaṁ hi sukham udgṛhyate dhāryate ceti ||
āgamānusāriṇyāṁ Madhyāntavibhāgaṭīkāyām ācāryabhadanta Sthiramatīyākhyāṭīyām Āvaraṇaparicchedo dvitīyaḥ samāptaḥ ||[110]
āvaraṇanirdeśānantaraṃ tattvanirdeśāvasaraḥ |
tadanantaraṃ uddiṣṭatvād ity atas tattvam adhikṛtyāhety āha |
tattvasya ca bahuvidhaṃ vidyate |
atas tadbhedānāvabodhārthaṃ tattvasvarūpaṃ duravagamaṃ ity atas tattvaprabhedam evādau pratipādayann āha |
mūlalakṣaṇatattvam
iti vistareṇa daśavidhaṃ tattvam |
tatra mūlatattve 'nyatattvāntarbhāvasaṃdarśanārthaṃ mūlatattvavacanam |
mūlatattve parijñānaprahāṇasākṣātkaraṇasaṃprayuktasya doṣaprahāṇārthaṃ lakṣaṇatattvavacanam|
saṃsārād udvegopāyapradarśanārtham aviparyāsatattvavacanam |
udvignasya śrāvakayānaniryāṇasaṃdarśanārthaṃ phalahetutattvavacanam |
śrāvako hi caturārya[Tib. 75, b]satyaprativedhabhāvanābhyāṃ niryāti |
sarvākārajñānapratipattupāyasaṃdarśanārtham āudārikatattvavacanam |
sarvāvaraṇaprahāṇopāyasaṃdarśanārthaṃ sūkṣmatattvavacanam |
suvyavasthitadharmanirdeśopāyasaṃdarśanārthaṃ sarvaparapravādinigraḥopāyasaṃdarśanārthaṃ ca prasiddhatattvavacanam |
same 'pi dharmadhātau gotrādiniyaśayabhedena viśuddhībhedasaṃdarśanārthaṃ viśuddhigocaratattvavacanam |
sarvākārasaṃkleśavyavadānapraveśopāyasaṃdarśanārthaṃ saṃgrahatattvacanam |
tathātām ārabhya[111]
sarvasaṃdehanivaṛtanasāmarthyasaṃdarśanārthaṃ prabhedatattvavacanam |
sarvaprakārātmagrāhaṃ nivartya nairātmyavyavasthānasāmarthyasaṃdarśanārthaṃ kauśalyatattvavacanam iti |
anye punar āhuḥ mūlatattvaṃ bodhisattvasyāsādhāraṇam |
tatraiva mithyāprayogapratipakṣatattvaṃ dvitīyam |
samyagyogānukūlatattvaṃ tṛtīyam |
śrāvakapratyekabuddhasādhāraṇatattvaṃ caturtham |
pratipattiyupāyatattvaṃ pañcamam |
lokasādhāraṇatattvaṃ ṣaṣṭham |
tadasādhāraṇatattvaṃ saptamam |
jñeyasamāsatattvaṃ aṣṭamaṃ |
prakṛtitattvaṃ navamaṃ |
saṃkleśamūlanirghātanatattvaṃ daśamam iti |
etāni samāsato daśatattvāni tathāgatānāṃ sarvābhisandhivacanāśrayabhūtāni |
vistareṇa tattvam aprameyam |
atra tattvānukūlāt saṃvṛttir api tattvam ity uktam ity eke |
sarvāṇy evaitāni yathoktaiḥ prakārair avisaṃvādat tattvānity anye |
yad yena prakāreṇa na visaṃvadati tat tathā tattvam iti kṛttvā ||
[1] Mūlatattvam]
tatra mūlatattvaṃ trividhaḥ svabhāva ity etad aprasiddhaṃ ity ata āha |
parikalpitaḥ paratantraḥ pariniṣpannaś ceti |
yena kāraṇena svabhāvas trividho mūlatattvam ity ucyate tat pradarśayann āha |
tatrānyatattvavyavasthāpanād iti |
tatrānyasya lakṣaṇāditattvasyāntargatattvam ity arthaḥ |[112]
kimarthaṃ svabhāvas trividha upadiśyaḥ |
vyavahāraparamārthatadāśrayapradarśanārtham ity eke |
viparyāsatannimittatatpratipakṣālambanapradarśanārtham ity anye |
bodhisattvānām āvaraṇaviśaṃyogārthaṃ prahāṇaparijñāsākṣātkrīyāvastupradarśanārtham ity anye |
prakṛtiśāmbhīryasya prajñāpāramitārūpasya trisvabhāvadvāreṇābhrāntipratipattyartham ity anye |
evaṃ hy uktam Abhidharmasūtragathadhyaye
māyādideśanā bhūte kalpitān nāstideśanā |
caturvidhaviśuddhes tu pariniṣpannadeśanā ||
śuddhiḥ prakṛtivaimalyam ālambanaṃ ca mārgaḥ |
viśuddhānāṃ hi dharmāṇāṃ caturvidhagṛhītatvam ||
iti |
laukika(34, a)lokottaratatpṛṣṭhalabdhajñānaviṣayatvapradarśanārthaṃ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ trayopādināṃ svabhāvatvam ity anye |
kim atra svabhāvatraye tattvam iṣyata iti |
aviparītārtho hi tattvārthaḥ kim atra svabhāvatraye 'viparītatvam iti |
yasmāt[113]
svabhāvatraye tattvam ityata iti pricchābhiprāyaḥ |
trisvabhāvo hy asan nityam |
ityādivistāraḥ |
parikalpiatalakṣaṇaṁ nityam asad iti |
yatra hi bālānāṁ grāhyagrāhakavācyavācakābhiniveṣaḥ svapna iva pravartate sa svarūpābhāvāt kalpitasvabhāvaḥ |
tasmād asadātmakatvād ābhrāntivāc ca parikalpitalakṣaṇam asattvam |
ataḥ parikalpitasvabhāve 'sattvam eva tattvam ucyate |
aviparitatvād iti |
aviparitārtho 'tra tattvārtha iti darṣayati |
paratantralakṣaṇaṁ sat |
na ca tattvata iti |
grāhyagrāhakādikalpitavyavahārāśrayatvāt sattvam |
grāhyagrāhakādyātmakatvenaśatvān na tathā tattvata iti |
etad eva pratipādayann āha |
bhrāntimātratvād iti |
etad uktaṁ bhavati |
yat khyāti māyopādānavat tad asti |
yathā khyāti grāhyagrāhakākāreṇa nāitad evam |
māyāpuruṣādivat |
ato bhrāntir iti |
tad d'u paratantrasvabhāve tattvam |
anyathā vidyamānasya svātmany avidyamānenākāreṇa prakhyānam |
atrāpy aviparitatvād iti prakṛtatvāt saṁbadhyate |
pariniṣpannalakṣaṇaṁ sadasattattvam iti |
sad asac ca tattvaṁ pariniṣpannalakṣaṇam |
dvayābhāvabhāvātmakatvāt sattvam |
dvayābhāvātmakatvād asattvaṁ ca |
viṣuddhyālambanatvāt tattvam |
etat pariniṣpanne svabhāve tattvam |
aviparitatvād iti saṁbadhyate ||[114]
svabhāvatrayasya tattvam uktaṃ na tu lakṣaṇam ity ataḥ pṛcchati atha vā mūlatattvānantaraṃ lakṣaṇatattvam uktam tac cāprasiddham iti pṛcchati lakṣaṇatattvam katamad iti |
ata āha |
samāropāpavādasya dharmapudgalayor iha |
iti vistaraḥ |
pudgaladharmayoḥ samāropāpavādadarśanaṃ yajjñānān na pravartate tat parikalpitasvabhāve tattvalakṣaṇam iti |
ātmā rū(34, b)pādayaś ca dharmās tatpratibhāsa(Tib. 77, b)vijñānavyatirikatasya tadālambanabhūtāḥ paramārthataḥ santīty abhiniveśaḥ samāropadarśanam |
vyavahārato 'pi na santīty apavādadarśanam |
anya āhuḥ |
pudgaladharmapratibhāsavijñānaparihāro 'pavādadarśanam iti |
grāhāpakṣepa iti cintyam etat |
pudgaladharmayoḥ samāropāpavādadarśanaṃ yasya lakṣaṇasya paricchedān na pravartate tat parikalpitasvabhāve tattvalakṣaṇam |
samāropāpavādavarjitaṃ veditavyam |
tac ca vyavahāreṇa tattvaṃ na tu svabhāvena |
yasmāt parikalpitāvabodhād dharmapudgalayoḥ samāropāpavādadarśanaṃ na pravartate |
na jāyata ity arthaḥ |
grāhyagrāhakayoḥ samāropāpavādadarśanam iti vistaraḥ |
grāhyagrāhakapratibhāsabhrāntir yā dharmeṇa pudgalena ca gṛhyate sā yadi tattvenāstīty abhinivisate |
evaṃ grāhyagrāhakasamāropaḥ |
atha grāhyasyeva grāhakasyāpy abhāvam abhinivi[115]
śate evam grāhyagrāhakāpavādaḥ |
yasya lakṣaṇasya jñānād grāhyagrāhakayoḥ samāropāpavādadarśanam na pravartate tat paratantrasvabhāve tattvalalakṣaṇam samāropāpavādavarjitam jñātavyam |
tat punaḥ kalpitayā grāhyagrāhakātmatayāsattvam tadvyavahārāśrayatvena ca sattvam |
evam hi parijñānāt paratantre samāropāpavādadarśanam na pravartate |
bhāvābhāvayoḥ samāropāpavāda(Tib. 78, a) darśanam iti vistaraḥ |
ke cid āhuḥ |
yadi śūnyatām bhāva eveti samanupacyati |
evam bhāvasamāropadarśanam bhavati |
athābhāva evety evam abhāvadarśanam |
etad ubhayam api bhāvābhāvadarśanam |
yasya lakṣaṇasya jñānān na te bhāvābhāvadarśane pravartetḥ tat pariniṣpannasvabhāve tattvalalakṣaṇam iti |
tac ca bhāvābhāvavimuktasvabhāvam |
tajjñānād dhi pariniṣpanne svabhāve bhāvābhāvadarśanam na pravartata ity eke |
anye punar āhuḥ |
dvayābhāvaḥ svarūpeṇa śūnyateti bhāvasamāropāntaḥ |
bhāvābhāvavimuktatmaketve bhāvasvarūpasamāropayāt |
bhāvābhāvavimuktena dvayābhāvasvarūpe(35, a)ṇa nirvikalpajñānagocarenāpi nāstity abhāvāpavādāntaḥ |
bhāvābhāvayoḥ pratyekam samāropāpavādadarśanasyābhisambandāc cintyam etad vyākhyānadvayam ity aparaḥ |
bhāvo 'tra dvayābhāvasya bhāvaḥ |
tam yadi bhāvam eva grihṇāty evam bhāvasamāropito bhavati |
yady abhāvam eva grihṇāty evam[116]
apodito bhavati |
abhāvo 'pi dvayābhāvas taṁ yady ucchedato gṛhṇāty evam abhāvo 'tyantābhāvatvena samāropito bhavati |
atha dvayata eva gṛhṇāty evam apodito bhavati |
yasmād etad dvayābhāvena prabhāvitam |
atha vā [Tib. 78,b] taṁ bhāvasvarūpeṇa gṛhṇāty evaṁ samāropito bhavati |
athābhāvam eva gṛhṇāty evaṁ saṁkleśo vyavadānaṁ cā'poditaṁ bhavati•ti |
etad antadvayavighātanaṁ pariniṣpannatve tattvālakṣaṇam |
nanu pūrvam api trisvabhāvālakṣaṇam uktam
arthād abhūtakalpāc ca dvayābhāvāc ca deśitaḥ ||
iti |
tatra svabhāvo lakṣaṇam iha tu cihnam iti nāsti punaruktātā |
yajjñānān na pravartete tat tattvālakṣaṇam iti |
pudgalādisamāropāpavādadarśanaṁ yajjñānān na pravartate tan mūlatatvasya lakṣaṇam ||
[3) Aviparyāsatattvam]
lakṣaṇatattvānantaram aviparyāsatatvaṁ vaktavyam |
tadanantaram uddiṣṭatvād ity ata āha |
aviparyāsatattvam iti vistaraḥ |
anityaḥduḥkhacūnyānātmano hi nityasukhaśuddhyātmaviparyāsapratipakṣatvād aviparyāsatattvam ity ucyate |
mūlatatve tadanyatattvāntarbhāvaḥ pūrvaṁ pratijñāta ity ataḥ pṛcchati |
mūlatatve katham iti vistaraḥ |
anityāditeti |
anityādayo ya anityaḥduḥkhacūnyānātmaprakārās te 'nityādayaḥ |
taddhāvo 'nityāditā |
mūlatatve 'viparyāsatattvasyāntarbhāvapradarśanārtham āha |
asadarśo hy anityārtha[117]
iti vistaraḥ |
trividho hy anityārthaḥ asadarthaḥ utpādavyayārthaḥ samalāmalārthaś ca |
yaḥ sarvadā tathāsti sa nityaḥ |
tad evāparāto 'nityaḥ |
teṣu yathākramam iti tatra
sarvadā'sadarthānityārthena parikalpitasvabhāvo 'nityaḥ |
asannityam anityam iti niruktinyāyena veditavyam ity anye |
pratikṣaṇam utpādavyayalakṣaṇenānityārthenānityaḥ paratantrasyabhāvaḥ |
tasya pratyayotpādatvād vināśe cāhetukatvāt |
samalāmalārthalakṣaṇenāgantukenānityārthenānityo 'vikāradharmo 'pi pariniṣpannasvabhāva iti |
duḥkham ādānalakṣmākhyam
iti vistaraḥ |
duḥkham api trividham |
upādānaduḥkhaṃ lakṣaṇaduḥkhaṃ sambandhaduḥkhaṃ ca |
tatra kalpitasvabhāva upādānato duḥkham |
aparijñātenehāmutra vā duḥkham upādīyata iti duḥkham |
yathā ca tenāparijñātena duḥkham upādīyate tat pradarśayann āha |
pudgaladharmābhiniveśopādānād iti |
tadbhāvagrāhadvāreṇa pudgaladharmaṃ abhiniveśata iti pudgaladharmābhiniveśaḥ |
sa ca dvayapatitayvāt parikalpitaḥ svabhāvaḥ |
parikalpitasvabhāvābhiniveśād duḥkhaprabandha ity evam uktam |
na punaḥ parikalpita eva duḥkham |
tadabhāvāt |
atha vā parikalpitasvabhāvaḥ pudgaladharmābhiniveśena vasturūpeṇopādiyata ity evam upādānataḥ kalpitaḥ svabhāvo duḥkham |
paratantraḥ svabhāvo lakṣaṇato duḥkham iti
sambadhyate |
tisro hi duḥkhatā |
duḥkhaduḥkhatā vipariṇāmaduḥkhatā saṃskāraduḥkhatā ca |
tāś ca tisro 'pi duḥkhatāḥ para[118]
tantraduḥkhatā iti lakṣaṇato duḥkham
pariniṣpannaḥ svabhāvaḥ saṃbandhato duḥkham caśabdaḥ samuccayārthaḥ
kathaṃ saṃbandhata ity ata āha duḥkhasambandkād iti
paratantrasvabhāvo duḥkham taddharmasya pariniṣpannasyāpi duḥkhasambandād duḥkham ucyate
atraiva mūlatatve yathākramam iti tatropādānato duḥkhaṃ parikalptiaḥ
lakṣaṇataḥ paratantraḥ
saṃbandhataḥ pariniṣpannaḥ
abhāvaś cāpy atadbhāvaḥ prakṛtiḥ śūnyatā matā
iti śūnyatā trividhā
abhāvaśūnya(36, a)tā atadbhāvaśūnyatā prakṛtiśūnyatā ca
tatrābhāvaśūnyatā parikalpitasya svabhāvasya
etat pratipādayann āha parikalpitalakṣaṇaṃ śaśaviṣāṇadisvarūpavat pratyakṣānanāakāreṇa yenāpi sattvaṃ grāhyaṃ nāsti abhāvatāt tacchunyat:ti
atadbhāvaśūnyatā paratantrasya
etad eva pratipādayann āha paratantralakṣaṇaṃ tathā nāsti yathā bālair dvayātmanā parikalpyate na tu sarvathā nāsti yathā cāsti tathā śuddhalaukikajñānenādkigamyate tasmād atadbhāvāt tacchunyateti
prakṛtiśūnyatā [Tib. 80, a] pariniṣpannasya ata evāha pariniṣpannalakṣaṇaṃ dvayābhāvasvabhāvatvāc chūnyatāsvabhāvam eveti
tasmāt prakṛtir eva svabhāva evāsya śūnyatā
alakṣaṇād hi nairātmyam
iti vistaraḥ nairātmyaṃ ca trividham alakṣaṇanairātmyaṃ vilakṣaṇanairātmyaṃ svalakṣaṇanairātmyaṃ ca
tatra parikalpitasya svabhāvasya lakṣaṇābhāvād alakṣaṇam eva nairātmyam[119]
ata evāha parikalpitasya svabhāvasya lakṣaṇāsttvād alakṣaṇam eva tannairātmyaṁ nānyena prakāreṇeti darśayati |
paratantrasya vilakṣaṇanairātmyam yasmāt paratanttrasyāsti lakṣaṇaṁ vasturūpam na tu tad yathā bālaiḥ parikalpyate dvayatvam |
tadviruddhālakṣaṇam iti parikalpitalakṣaṇenāsādṛśyam paratantralakṣaṇaiḥ tannairātmyam |
evaṁ parikalpitātmatvena nairātmyam asya darśitam bhavati |
svalakṣaṇaṁ nairātmyaṁ pariniṣpannasya yataḥ pāriniṣpannaḥ svabhāvo grāhyagrāhakābhāvarūpatvān nairātmyam atas svalakṣaṇam asya nairātmyam iti pariniṣpannaṁ dharmaṁ nairātmyasvabhāvatāṁ darśayati |
kaḥ punar anayoḥ śūnyatā'nātmatayor viśeṣaḥ |
yad yatra nāsti tat tena śūnyam yat tu yasyātmā [Tib. 80, b] svabhāvo na bhavati tat tenānātmety ayam anayor viśeṣa ity eke |
arthataḥ ko 'pi viśeṣo nāsti vyavasthānabhedena tu bhedaḥ yasmād (36, b) yad yatra nāsti tena tasya niḥsvabhāvatām upādāya nairātmyam itarasya tu tatrāniṣpannasvabhāvatām upādāya śūnyatvam iti [kiṁ cit] |
trividhe mūlatatve samāropāpavādāntayoḥ pratipakṣeṇāgantukamalapradarśanena ca yathākramaṁ trividhānityatā saṁdarśitā |
sa'sādardhānityatā utpādabhaṅgānityatā samalanirmalānityatā ca |
evaṁ yāvat trividhaṁ nairātmyam ity uktam aviparyāsattvam ||
[4] Phalahetutattvam
tadanantaraṁ phalahetutatvaṁ vaktavyam ity ata āha |[120]
mūlatatve phalahetutatvam iti vistaraḥ tatra saṃkṣepapakṣam
adhikṛtya duḥkhasatyaṃ phalatattvaṃ |
samudayasatyaṃ hetutattvaṃ |
vyavadānapakṣe 'pi nirodhasatyaṃ phalatattvaṃ mārgasatyaṃ hetutattvaṃ |
evaṃ duḥkhādīni catvāri satyāni phalahetutattvaṃ |
mūlatatve duḥkhasamudayanirodhamārgasatyānīty uktam |
tac ca na vijñāyate kathaṃ mūlatattvaṃ duḥkhādisatyasvabhāvam ity ataḥ pṛcchati |
kathaṃ trividhaṃ mūlatattvaṃ duḥkhādisatyam iti |
ata āha |
yatas tadanityādilakṣaṇena
duḥkhasatyaṃ tato matam |
iti |
anityaḥ duḥkhacūnyānātmatayā duḥkhasatyalakṣaṇatvāt tac ca samastaṃ pratyekaṃ kalpitādiṣv astīti vistarenoktam |
ataḥ kalpitādayaḥ pratyekaṃ duḥkhasatyatvenābhipretā iti |
trividhena samudayārthena mūlatattvaṃ samudayasatyaṃ yathākramaṃ |
sa eva samudayo na jñāyata ity ata āha |
trividhaḥ samudayārthaḥ |
vāsanātha samutthānam avisaṃyoga eva ca ||
iti |
trividhaḥ samudayo vāsanāsamudayaḥ samutthānasamudayaḥ |
avisaṃyogasamudayaś ca |
tatra vāsanāsamudayaḥ parikalpitasvabhāvo veditavyaḥ |
ata āha |
parikalpitasvabhāvābhiniveśavāsaneti |
asati hi parikalpite svabhāve 'stīty abhiniveśam upādāya laukikā dharmā vikalpakleśavāsanāṃ vyavasthāpayanti |
na tu vigatābhiniveśā lokottarā |
tataḥ parikalpitasvabhāvo vāsanāsamudaya ucyate |
samutthānasamu(37, a)dayaḥ parataṃtraḥ[121]
svabhāvaḥ |
samutthāna ātmalābhaḥ |
sa yato bhavati sa samutthānasamudayaḥ samudety asmād iti kṛtvā |
sa punaḥ kiṁsvarūpa ity ata āha |
karmakleśā iti |
te ca vasturūpatvāt paratantrasvabhāvaḥ |
avisaṁyogasamudayas tathateti |
katham ity ata āha |
āvaraṇāvisaṁyoga iti |
etad uktaṁ bhavati |
yāvad āvaraṇair na visaṁyuktā tathatā tāvat samudeti duḥkham iti |
āvaraṇair avisaṁyuktā vā tathatā samudayadharmateti samudayanāmalābha iti |
trividhena nirodhena mūlatattvaṁ nirodhasatyam |
tac ca na vijñāyata ity ata āha |
trividho nirodhaḥ
svabhāvadvayanotpattir malacāntidvayam matam iti |
tatra svabhāvānutpattiḥ parikalpitasya |
tasya hi niḥsvabhāvatvād ātyantikānutpatir vandhyāputravat |
grāhyagrāhakayor anutpattiḥ paratantrasya |
tasyāpi grāhyagrāhakasvabhāvābhyām atyantarahitatvād dvayabhrāntyātmanā'nutpattiḥ |
malacāntidvayaṁ pariniṣpannaḥ |
caṣabdaḥ samuccayārthaḥ |
tat punar na vijñāyata ity ata āha |
pratisaṁkhyānirodhas tathatā cocyata iti |
malaṁ dvividham |
rāgādyāvaraṇaṁ parikalpiṭaś ca |
tatrānāsraveṇa jñānenāśrayād bīja uddhṛte rāgādimalānām atyantam anutpādātmikā cāntiḥ pratisaṁkhyānirodhaḥ |
tathatāgocareṇadvayajñānena parikalpitamalasyānutpādātmikā cāntiḥ tathatā |
etad dhi dvayaṁ pariniṣpannaḥ svabhāvaḥ |
eṣa trividho nirodhaḥ kalpitādau mūlatrattve yathāsaṁkhyaṁ vijñeyaḥ |
anye tu tathatāprativedḥena kleśaparikalpitamaladvayacāntis tathatety ucyate |
dvayanirodhaḥ prakṛtinirodhaś ceti |[122]
vacanāt |
prakṛtir iti tathatā |
prakṛtiprabhāsvaratvāc cittasyeti |
atas tathatālambanād yo nirodhaḥ sa prakṛtinirodha ity ucyate |
niruddhamātraṃ pratisaṃkhyānirodhaḥ |
nirodhyate 'syām iti nirodhas tathetyapare |
nirodhasya punar anutpādātmikatvāt sarvaṃ svabhāvānutpattyādikaṃ saṃkṣipya nirodhasatyam ity ucyate |
ukto nirodhasatyasya mūlatatve 'ntarbhāvaḥ |
tadanantaraṃ tu mārgasatyam antarbhāvayitavyam ity ataḥ pṛcchati |
mārgasatyaṃ trividhamūlatatve kathaṃ pratyavasthāpyata ity ataḥ
parijñānaṃ prahāṇaṃ ca |
ityādivacanaṃ |
parikalpitasya parijñāna iti |
parikalpito 'tyantam asann eveti tasya parijñānam eva na prahāṇam |
na hy asataḥ prahāṇaṃ yujyate |
paratantrasya parijñāne prahāṇe ca paratantro hi yathā khyāti tathasattvaṃ vijñeyaṃ na tu sarvātmatvenaasattvaṃ kalpitavat |
karmakleśayor bhāvātmatvāt prahātavyaś ca |
pariniṣpannasya parijñāne prāptisākṣātkaraṇe 'ceti pariniṣpanno hi bhāvābhāvavinirmuktālakṣaṇataḥ parijñeya āśrayaparāvṛttitāyāś ca |
āśrayaparāvṛttitā vinirmuktadharmatāyatvetena sākṣātkartavyeti cintayā parijñeyā |
nirodhaḥ sākṣātkartavyaḥ prāptisākṣātkriyayā |
ubhayaṃ cobhayathety anye |
dvividhā hi parijñā jñānaparijñā prahāṇaparijñā ca |
tatra jñānaparijñāparijñeyaṃ prathamaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca |
dvitīyaṃ tv ubhayaparijñeyam |
sākṣātkriyāpi dvidhā jñānasākṣātkriyā prāptisākṣātkriyā ca |
tatra jñānasākṣātkriyayā[123]
trayam api sākṣātkartavyam |
prāptisākṣātkriyayā tu tritīyam eva |
evam atra mūlatatve parijñānaprahāṇasākṣātkaraṇair mārgasatyaṃ vyavasthāpitaṃ veditavyam |
na tu tatrāntarbhāvāt |
atra ca satyacatuṣṭayena yaḥ saṃkleśo yataś ca tayoś ca viśuddhir yā yataś ca tat paridīpitam |
anukramas tv eṣāṃ yathābhisamayam iti ||
[5] Audārikasūkṣmatattvam]
audārikaṃ tattvaṃ sūkṣmaṃ ceti |
audārikatattvaṃ saṃvṛttisatyaṃ sūkṣmatattvaṃ tu paramārthasatyam |
yata ādāv audārikeṇa sattvān paripācayati paripakvaṃs tu sūkṣmeṇa vimocayati |
ataḥ pūrvam audārikaṃ paścāt sūkṣmaṃ |
kārikānuguṇyād viparyayoktiḥ |
tatrāsamāhitajñānaviṣayatvāt saṃvṛttisatyam audārikatattvam |
samāhitajñānaviṣayatvāt para(38, a)mārthasatyaṃ sūkṣmatattvam |
atha vā vijñānaviṣayatvād audārikam |
jñānaviṣayāt sūkṣmam |
sarvatattvānāṃ mūlatatve 'ntarbhāvaḥ pratijñāta ity ataḥ pṛcchati |
tan mūlatatve kathaṃ veditavyam ity ata āha |
prajñaptih pratipattitā |
uttaram udbhāvanācaivam |
iti|
prajñaptiḥ pratipattir udbhāvanā caudārikatattvam |
mūlatatve veditavyam |
etad eva pratipādayann āha |
trividhā hi saṃvṛttir[124]
iti vistaraḥ |
asato 'rthasya rūpaṁ ghataḥ paṭaś ceti vyavasthānaṁ prajñaptisaṁvṛttiḥ |
rūpavedanāsaṁjñādisvabhāvaviśeṣeṇa nāmābhilāpaḥ prajñaptisaṁvṛttir ity anye |
abhilāpāpekṣayā yad vyavahriyate rūpaṁ vedaneti vā tat prajñaptisaṁvṛttir iti ke cit |
cittacaittavadaśattvāt parikalpataḥ svabhāvaḥ |
pratipattisamvṛttir vikalpena yena rūpādayo ghaṭādayaś ca vijñānanirbhāsād abahirbhūtān api bahirbhūtān iva vasturūpeṇa yathā vyavasthānam abhinivicạte sa vikalpaḥ pratipattisaṁvṛttiḥ |
udbhāvanāsaṁvṛttiś ceti caśabdaḥ samuccayārthaḥ |
vikalpabhūtāpatikrāntasyāpi pariniṣpannasya śūnyatātathatāsamalanirmalādibhiḥ paryāyaiḥ saṁdarśanam udbhāvanāsaṁvṛttiḥ |
tayā trividhayā saṁvṛttyā saṁvṛttisatyatvaṁ [Tib. 83, b]
mūlatattve yathākramaṁ veditavyam |
saṁvṛtter vyavahāraḥ |
prajñaptir ity abhidhānam |
arthābhāve 'bhidhānamātreṇa vyavahāraḥ prajñaptisaṁvṛttiḥ |
ata evārthābhāvāt prajñaptisaṁvṛttyā parikalpitaḥ saṁvṛttisatyam |
avidyamāne 'rthe 'rthābhiniveśaḥ pratipattis |
tayā vyavahāraḥ pratipattisaṁvṛttiḥ |
sā punar abhiniśecavikalpaḥ |
tasmāt pratipattisaṁvṛttyā saṁvṛttisatyaṁ parataṁtraḥ |
tathatādiqabdair nirabhilāpyasya dharmadhātor yā saṁsūcanā sodbhāvanā |
tayā dharmadhātor vyavahāra udbhāvanāsaṁvṛttiḥ |
ataś codbhāvanāsaṁvṛttyā pariniṣpannaḥ saṁvṛttisatyam |[125]
tatra prajñaptyudbhāvanāsaṃvṛtitvam avasara(38, b)saṃgrahavyavasthānaṃ veditavyam na tu svabhāvata iti uktaṃ saṃvṛttisatyam
idānīṃ paramārthasatyaṃ vaktavyam ity ata āha
paramārthan tu ekataḥ
iti kalpitaparatantrayoḥ paramārthato 'saṃbhāvyam satyaṃ punar ekataḥ pariniṣpannasvabhāvo veditavyam sa punaḥ pariniṣpannaḥ kena kāraṇena paramārtha ucyata ity ata āha
arthapräptiprayatyā hi paramārthas tridhā mataḥ
iti trividho hi paramārthaḥ arthaparamārthaḥ prāptiparamārthaḥ prayatparamārthaś ca tadvārthaparamārthas tathatā
paramo hi lokottaraṃ jñānam tasyārtho viṣaya iti kṛtvā tathatā paramārtha ity ucyate indriyārthavat tad eva pradarśayann āha paramasya jñānasyārtha [Tib. 84, a] iti kṛtveti ālambanabhāvena vyavasthānād ity arthaḥ jñānāpṛthaktvāt tasmin kāla ālambanam na vartate vijñaptimātrapratibhāsavat
tad yathā yeṣāṃ vijñaptimātraṃ kṣamate na teṣāṃ rūpādivijñaptimātraṃ muktvā rūpādipratibhāsā vidyante
prāptiparamārtho nirvāṇam ekāntanirmalatathataprāpyaparāvṛttilakṣaṇam kathaṃ paramārtha ity ata āha arthasya parama iti kṛtvā paramaś cāsāv arthaś ceti kṛtvā paramārthaḥ
tatra saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtadharmāgratvāt paramo mārgaprayojanatva
tvād arthaḥ tathā hi mārgo nirvāṇaprāptiprayojanaḥ nirvāṇaṃ tv arthaḥ sarvāpakāradoṣarahitatvāt yasmād doṣasahite 'nartha ucyate[126]
pratipattiparamārtho mārgaḥ kiṃ kāraṇam paramo
'syārtha iti kṛtvā viṣayaḥ prayojanaṃ vārthaḥ viṣayas
tathatā prayojanaṃ nirvāṇam viṣayadvāreṇa parama ucyamānaḥ katham anyonyasamarthanaṃ na syāt anyonyasamarthanaṃ
sat ko doṣa iti niścayābhāvaḥ naivaṃ bhaviṣyaty anyonyasamarthanāt siṃhavanavat
katham asaṃskṛtaṃ nirvāṇākhyaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ ca mārgākhyaṃ pariniṣpannasvabhāva ucyate paramārthasatyaṃ hi pariniṣpannasvabhāvo nirdiṣṭam mārgaś tu pariniṣpanno 'yogyaḥ
na pūrvāparabhāgābhāva iti kṛtvā ta(39, a)smān na mārgaḥ
[Tib. 84, b] paramārthasatyaṃ na cāpariniṣpanne paramārthasatyasaṃgraha iti ata āha
ananyathāviparyāsapariniṣpattito dvayam
iti atha vā katham asaṃskṛtaṃ nirvāṇākhyaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ ca
mārgākhyaṃ pariniṣpannasvabhāva ucyate pariniṣpanno hi
tathatā eṣā ca dvayalakṣaṇarahitatvaṃ paratantrasyeti na nirvāṇamārgayoḥ pariniṣpannatā yujyate tad idam anupālambhayogyaṃ yatas tatra dvayābhāvaḥ pariniṣpanna eva na tu dvayābhāva eva pariniṣpanna iti tadanyapratiṣeddhaḥ atas tadanyac
ca ananyathāviparyāsapariniṣpannadvayam api pariniṣpanna iti
saṃbadhyate asaṃskṛtaṃ nirvāṇaṃ tathatā ca asaṃskṛtam[127]
anananyathābhāvād avikārapariniṣpattyā pariniṣpannaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ mārgasaṃgṛhītam nānyatra aviparyāsapariniṣpattyā pariniṣpannam iti saṃbadhyate katham ity ato 'bravīt punar jñeyavastuni aviparyāsād iti yasmān na punar jñeye viparyāsam āpadyate tasmān mārgo 'pi pariniṣpanna ity ataś ca dvayoraṃ api saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtayoḥ pariniṣpannaḥ pṛthagabhiprāyād ity avirodhaḥ
[6] Prasiddhatattvam
tatra
lokaprasiddham ekasyād
iti yathābhidhānam asambhavāt parikalpitād eva tan nānyasmāt [Tib. 85, a] tasya tu svarūpan na jñāyata ity ata āha
yasmin vastunīti vistaraḥ saṃketaḥ saṃjñāsaṃjñāsaṃbandhajñānam saṃstavas tadvāraṃvāraṃprayogadarśanam tatrānupraveśayā buddhyeti kaḥ punar buddheḥ saṃketasaṃstavānupraveśaḥ tat smaraṇam etad uktam bhavati saṃketasaṃstavānusmaraṇānugatayā buddhyā sarveṣāṃ laukikānāṃ śāstravidāṃ tadavidāṃ ca vyavahriyatabhāve kva cid iyaṃ bhūmir itydādidarśanaṃ samānam iti etat lokaprasiddhaṃ tattvam lokapra[128]
siddhyā tat tathaiveti kṛtvā
trayād yuktiprasiddhakam
iti trividham api hi svabhāvam adhikṛtya yuktiḥ kriyata iti
ataḥ svabhāvatrayeṇa yuktiprasiddhatatvam vyavasthāpyate
tac ca na vijñāyata ity ataḥ pradarśayann āha
yat satāṃ yuktārthapanditānām ityādi
tatra santo ye yuktārthapanditāḥ
tārkikā ye mīmāṃsakāḥ
pūrvasya vyākhyānāśrayasya paścād vacanaṃ uktam
atha vā santo 'nveṣakāḥ kiṃ kuśalam iti
yuktārthapanditā apekṣākāryakāraṇopapattidharmātāyukticatuṣṭayāviruddhārthakṛtakauśalāḥ
tārkikās tūhāpohasamarthās tathāgatapravacanasamāśrayeṇāviparitaśāstrapraṇetāraḥ
mīmāṃsakāḥ svaparaśāstrapanditāḥ śreyo'doṣaparīkṣakāḥ
ke cit tu mīmāṃsakā iti na pathanti
ta evaṃ āhuḥ
satām ity ukte ke punaḥ santa ity ata āha
yuktārthapanditā iti
te ca tarkabhūmau bhāvanābhūmau ca vyavasthitatvād dvividhā
tasmāt tārkikā ity ucyanta iti
teṣāṃ ca kaś cid lokaprasiddhānusaraṇa iti
ata āha
pramāṇatrayaṃ niśrityeti pramāṇatrayāvirodhene[ty arthaḥ]
pramāṇatrayaṃ punaḥ pratyakṣam anumānam āgamaś ca
tatra pratyakṣaṃ pāñcendriyajaṃ mānasaṃ ca sukhaduḥkhādisaṃvedanam
trirūpaliṅgenānumeyārthajñānam anumānam
āptavāg āgamaḥ
āptāḥ punar anṛtahetuvimuktāḥ
atha vā pratyakṣānumānāgamaprasiddhena hetunā dṛṣṭāntena ca tatprasiddhasyaiva sādhyasya parāṃpratisādhyatvāt pramāṇatrayaṃ niśrityeti
upapattisādhanayuktyā prasiddhabhāva iti
upapattis trirūpaliṅgaṃ sādhyapratipādanopāyatvāt
upapatteḥ sādhanam upapattisādhanam
trirūpaliṅgākhyānaṃ tena tatpratipādanāt[129]
yuktir upāyo yena prayoganīyamena liṅgasya trairūpyam ākhyāyate |
atha vopapattir ananyathāsaṃbhavaḥ |
sādhanayuktir avayavasaṃbandhaḥ |
upapattisādhanayuktir iti vigrahaḥ |
atha vopapattir eva sādhanayuktir iti vigrahaḥ |
[Tib. 86, a] anyasādhanayuktibhyo vyavacchedārtham upapattigrahaṇam |
evaṃ pramāṇatrayaṃ niśrityopapattisādhanayuktyā yaḥ prasiddhabhāvas trayāṇāṃ svabhāvānām anyatamo yuktiprasiddha ucyate |
uktaṃ prasiddha(40, a)tattvam ||
[7) Viśuddhiviṣayatattvam]
tadanantaraṃ tu viśuddhiviṣayaṃ vaktavyam ity ata āha |
viśuddhigocaratatvaṃ dvividham iti |
tat tu yathā dvividhaṃ tat pradarśayann āha |
kleśāvaraṇaviśuddhijñānagocaraṃ jñeyāvaraṇaviśuddhijñānagocaraṃ ceti |
kleśāvaraṇaviśuddhiḥ kleśāvaraṇaprahāṇam |
kleśāvaraṇaviśuddhaye kleśāvaraṇaviśuddhau vā jñānaṃ kleśāvaraṇaviśuddhijñānam |
kleśāvaraṇaviśuddhiprayojanaṃ tannimittaṃr vety arthaḥ |
tasya kleśāvaraṇaviśuddhijñānasya gocaram iti viṣaya ity arthaḥ |
evaṃ jñeyāvaraṇaviśuddhijñānasya gocaram api vaktavyam |
tatra kleśāvaraṇaṃ sarve darśanabhāvanāmārgaprahātavyāḥ kleśopakleśā ity ato darśanabhāvanātadupacārajñānaviṣayaṃ śrāvakapratyekabuddhānāṃ yat tattvaṃ duḥkhasamudayanirodhāmārgāṇāṃ satyam ucyate |
tat kleśāvaraṇaviśuddhi[Tib. 86, b][130]
jñānasya gocaram |
jñātvyaṁ jñeyam tac ca sarvātmanā pañcavidyāsthānāni tāni punar adhyātmavyākaraṇahetucikitsāsarvacilpakarmasthānākhyāni |
tatra jñānavibandabhūtam akliṣṭam ajñānaṁ jñeyāvaraṇam |
tadvigodhanāṁ lokottarajñānānulomādhinuktivaryābhūmisaṁgṛhītaṁ pramuditādyekādaśabhūmisaṁgṛhītam |
tatpṛṣṭhalabdhaṁ ca jñānam |
tat punar bodhisattvatathāgatasaṁtānavartī tasya ca tathatā sarvatragādyarthamukhenālambanam ity ato jñeyāvaraṇaviśuddhijñānagocasya tattvaṁ tathatā |
phalahetutattvoktyā sūkṣmatattvoktyā ca viśuddhigocaraṁ dvividham api nirdiṣṭam eveti punar asya vidhānaṁ vyartham |
na vyartham |
yatas tat phalahetutatvaṁ sūkṣmaṁ ca vastuvyavasthānata uktam |
atra tv āvaraṇaṁ yathā viśudhyate |
tata etan nirdiṣṭam |
idaṁ dvividhaṁ viśuddhigocaram ekasmāt pariniṣpannād eva svabhāvāt kīrtitam |
atraiva kāraṇam āha |
na hy anyaḥ svabhāvo viśuddhijñānagocaro bhavatīti |
kiṁ kāraṇam abhūtaparikalpasya dauṣṭhulyamaladvaena samalabhūtatvāt parikalpitasya cāsattvāt |
dvividhajñānaviṣayasya tu dharmatayā viśu(40; b)ddhiviṣayatattvaṁ pariniṣpannād eva [Tib. 87, a] vyavasthāpyata iti |
uktaṁ viśuddhigocaratattvam ||[131]
tadanantaraṃ tu saṃgrahatattvaṃ vaktavyam ity ataḥ pṛcchati kathaṃ trividhe mūlatatve saṃgrahatattvaṃ veditavyam iti saṃgrahatattvaṃ trividhe mūlatatve saṃgṛhītaṃ pradarśayann āha
nimittasya vikalpasya nāmnaś ca dvayasaṃgrahaḥ
iti vistaraḥ dvaye paratantre parikalpite ca saṃgrahaḥ tatra saṃgrahatattvaṃ pañcavastunīty ata āha yathāyogaṃ pañcavastūny ārabhyeti yathāyogaṃ mūlatatve pañcavastūni saṃgṛhītāni veditavyāni na tu yathākramam pañca punar vastūni
nimittaṃ nāma vikalpaḥ tathatā samyagjñānaṃ ceti sarvasya jñeyasyāsmin pañcavidhe vastuni saṃgrahād etat pañcavidhaṃ vastu saṃgrahatattvaṃ ity ucyate
tatra nimittam ālayavijñānaṃ kliṣṭamanaḥ pravṛttivijñānāni ca anyonyanimittabhāvād nimittam nāma yat tasyaiva nimittasyānabhilāpyasyāpi sato 'kṣinikocavat sūcakam abhidhānam
yathoktanimittasvabhāvaviśeṣavikalpakās traidhātukasaṃyuktāś cittacaittā vikalpāḥ śūnyatā tathatā samyagjñānaṃ lokottaraṃ tathatālambanaṃ kramaḥ punar eṣāṃ tatra saṃkleśaprajñaptivastutvaṃ nimittam ādau saṃkleśaprajñaptivāt tadanantaraṃ nāma yathā nāma tathā saṃkleśa[Tib. 87, b]prajñaptivastuvikalpakatvāt tadanantaraṃ vikalpaḥ sa ca saṃkleśas tathatāsaṃyagjñānena nirudhyata iti tadanantaraṃ tathatā[132]
samyagjñānaṃ ceti ke cit |
bhrāntinimittaṃ bhrāntir
dauṣṭhulyaṃ tannimittam iti |
tasmād bhāntis tasya nimittaṃ
prakṛtiśamas tatra ca jñānam ||
ity anayā gāthayā'sya pañcavidhasya vastuno 'rtha ity anye |
tatrādhyātmikacakṣurādivijñānasaṃgṛhītāyā bhrānter nimittaṃ
bāhyādhyātmikā rūpādicakṣurādisaṃgṛhītā bhrāntiḥ |
tasyā
bāhyādhyātmikāyā bhrānter nimittaṃ dauṣṭhulyam |
tat punar ālayavijñānavāsanā |
tasyā vāsanāyā nimittaṃ saiva bāhyādhyātmikabhrāntiḥ |
tasmāt tatrābhilāpapra(41, a)vṛttiḥ |
abhilāpavāśāc
ca vāsanāyā avasthānam ālayavijñāne |
prakṛtiśama iti tathatā |
prakṛtyākliṣṭatvād dharmadhātoḥ |
tatra ca jñānam iti tathatālambanaṃ mārgasaṃgṛhītaṃ yaj jñānam |
tatra nimittavikalpadvayasya paratantrenaḥsaṃgṛhītaṃ hetupratyayotpāditatvāt |
nāmnaḥ parikalpiteneti |
yathā nāmārthābhāvān nāmnaḥ parikalpitena saṃgrahaḥ |
evam adhikārasaṃgrahaḥ parikalpitena nāmno na svabhāvasaṃgraha iti |
yad uktaṃ
niḥsvabhāvasiddhatvāt parikalpaḥ svabhāvo nimittādipañcavastubhir na saṃgṛhīta iti tan na virudhyate |
tatra svabhāvasaṃgrahasya vivakṣitatvāt |[133]
saṁyagjñānasatatvasya
iti sadā śobhanaṁ vā tattvaṁ satatvaṁ tathatā
śūnyatālambanādvayajñānaṁ tatpṛṣṭhalabdhacuddhatāṁkikaṁ ca saṁyagjñānam
tathatāsamyagjñānayor avikārāviparyāsapariṇiṣpattyā yathākramaṁ pariṇiṣpannatvād ekenāiva pariṇiṣpannena svabhāvena saṁgraha iti
tad uktaṁ saṁgrahatattvam
[9] Prabhedatattvam
idānīṁ prabhedatattve katividhaṁ kathaṁ ca tad mūlatattve saṁgṛhītaṁ na vijñāyata ity ataḥ pṛcchati prabhedatatvaṁ mūlatattve kathaṁ veditavyam iti
ata āha saptavidhaṁ prabhedatattvam
katham ity ata āha pravṛttitattvaṁ lakṣaṇatattvam iti vistaraḥ
yā Saṁdhinirmocanā uktā saptavidhā tathatā pravṛttitatthatā yāvat saṁyakpratipattitatheti sāivātra saptavidhaṁ prabhedatattvam ity uktā pravṛttitattvaṁ yāvat saṁyakpratipattitattvaṁ iti
tatra pravṛttitattvaṁ yā saṁsārānavarāgratā saiva ca pravṛttitatthatā
yasmān na jātu tasyāgratāyogo bhavati
lakṣaṇatattvaṁ pudgaladharmanairātmyam ananyathātvāc caturvidhalakṣaṇatathatā
vijñaptitattvaṁ vijñaptimātratā dharmāṇām
nirvikalpaṁ jñānaṁ vijñaptyāśrayabhāvād vijñaptitattvaṁ ity apare
ananyathābhāvatvād vi[134]
jñaptitattvasya vijñaptitattvatocyate |
saṃniveśatattvaṃ duḥkhasatyaṃ |
saṃskārāṇāṃ duḥkhasa(41, b)nniviṣṭatvāt |
saiva ca tathatā nityaṃ tathaiveti kṛtvā |
mithyāpratipattitattvaṃ samudayasatyaṃ |
sā punar yā teṣu saṃskāreṣu guṇadarśanena pravṛttiḥ |
viśuddhitattvaṃ kleśāvaraṇaviśuddhiḥ |
sā punas tathatā nirodhasatyaṃ ca |
āśrayaparāvṛttyātmatā nirodhātmatā nirodhasatyam evety apare |
samyakpratipattitattvaṃ mārgasatyam |
vijñaptitattvaṃ yoniśaḥ prayogaḥ |
samyakpratipattitattvaṃ vijñaptitattvaṃ ca mārgasatyam evety anye |
etāny api trīṇi tattvāni nityaṃ tathaiveti tathatāvacanaṃ bhavati |
tatra
pravṛttitattvaṃ dvividham |
mūlatatve veditavyaṃ parikalpitaparatantralakṣaṇaṃ tatra pravṛttir janminām anādimān prabandhaḥ |
sa cāvidyamānatvāt parikalpit aḥ svabhāvaḥ |
yeṣāṃ tu sa prabandhas te hetuprabhāvitatvāt paratantraḥ svabhāvaḥ |
evaṃ yeṣāṃ yā ca pravṛttis tat pravṛttitattvaṃ iti nirdiṣṭaṃ bhavati |
atha vā kleśakarmajanmanām anādimān utpādaḥ saṃsārapravṛttir ity ucyate |
sā ca parikalpitasvabhāvābhiniveśaprabhāvitety ataḥ
pravṛttitattvaṃ dvividham |
mūlatattvam ity uktam |
evaṃ
saṃniveśaḥ kupannatā |
iti |
mithyā(Tib. 89. a)pratipattīḥ kupannatā |
tathaiva dvividhaṃ[135]
mūlatattvam iti duḥkhasamudayayor hetupratyayaprabhāvitatvāt parikalpitaviṣayatvāc ca sanniveśamithyāpratipattitattvaṃ dvividhe saṃgṛhītam
ekaṃ lakṣaṇavijñaptau śuddhiḥ saṃpratipannatā
lakṣaṇatattvam vijñaptitattvaṃ viśuddhitattvaṃ samyakpratipattitattvaṃ etāni catvāri tattvāny ekam eva mūlatattvaṃ yad uta pariniṣpannaḥ svabhāvaḥ
tatra lakṣaṇatattvaviśuddhitattvayorananyathāpariniṣpannatvāt pariniṣpannaḥ
vijñaptitattvaṃ samyakpratipattitattvaṃ cāviparyāsapariniṣpattyā pariniṣpannaḥ
atra pravṛttisanniveśamithyāpratipattittatvais tribhiḥ sattvasāmyaṃ darśitam
lakṣaṇavijñaptittattvābhyāṃ dharmasāmyam
viśuddhitattvena bodhisāmyam
samyakpratipattittvena prajñāsāmyaṃ pradarśitam iti
āha ca
sattvasāmyaṃ tribhiḥ proktaṃ dharmasāmyam dvayena tu
bodhiprajñāsāmyam evam ekaikaśaḥ pradarśitam
iti
[10] Kauśalyatattvam
prabhedatattvānantaraṃ kauśalyatattvam uddiṣtam
atas tat pradarśayann āha
daśadhā kauśalyatattvam ātmadṛṣṭipratiṣedhatā[135]
tat punaḥ skandhādiṣu kathaṃ daśavidham ātmadarśanam iti na jñāyata ity ataḥ pṛcchati katham etad daśavidham ātmadarśanam ity ata āha
ekahetutvabhoktṛtve kartṛtvavaśavartane
adhipatyarthanīyatve kleśaviśuddhiniśraye
yogyamuktavimuktave eteṣu hy ātmadarśanam
iti skandhādiṣv iti saṃbadhyate eṣa tu daśavidhātmāsaṃgrāha ity ātmano 'sattvād asaṃ grāha ity āha yady ātmā nāsti kathaṃ grāhyaparatantraḥ san nirviṣayo grāhaḥ pravartata
iti na tv eṣa nirviṣayaḥ skandhādiṣu pravartate tasmāt teṣāṃ pratipakṣatvena daśavidhaṃ skandhādikaucalyam uktam
kathaṃ punaḥ skandhādiṣv evāyam ātmagrāho na tv ātmā san niścīyate yataḥ skandhādivyatirikta ātmā yuktyā parīkṣyamāṇo nopalabhyate yad ātmavādinām ātmakriyāsvarūpaṃ tat sarvam api pravartate skandhādiṣv evety ataḥ skandhādaya evātmagrāhanibandhanam iti pratīyate
ebhir vidhair daśabhir ātmābhiniveśānām ātmagrāhaḥ pravartate nānyair ity ataḥ skandhādikaucalyaṃ sarvātmagrāhasya pratipakṣatvena veditavyam
tathā hi ekatvagrāha iti pañcopādānaskaṃdheṣu piṇḍātmagrāhataḥ tatpratipakṣeṇa skandhakaucalyam hetutvāgrāha iti cakṣurādi[137]
nām prayojakatvād ātmani hetutvagṛāhaḥ |
[Tib. 90, a] tatpratipakṣeṇa dhātukāuśalyam |
evam anyeṣv api vipakṣapratipakṣaṁ vaktavyam |
atha vātmatvāt sarvaṁ pravartata ity ayaṁ hetutvagṛāhaḥ |
yasyeṣṭam aniṣṭaṁ ceha janmani phalaṁ tasya śubhāśubhasya karmaṇa ātmā hetur iti cetanā hetutvagṛāhaḥ |
bhoktṛtvagṛāha iti |
ātmā bhoktā cetanatvāt |
vyaktāvyaktaṁ tu bhojanam acetanatvāt |
atha vā yaḥ śubhāśubhaiḥ karmabhir ut
padyate sa eva tatpha(42, b)lānāṁ bhokteti bhoktṛtvagṛāhaḥ |
kartṛtvagṛāha iti |
ātmaiva sukṛtaduṣkṛtānāṁ karmaṇāṁ kartreti yo gṛāhaḥ |
yo vā karaṇādyupetas tās tāḥ kriyāḥ karoti sa ātmeti kartṛtvagṛāhaḥ |
svatantratvagṛāha iti yat svairam vacitvam sa ātmā |
yo 'dhikāraḥ svātmeti yo gṛāhaḥ |
hetvanuṛūpasyaiva vā phalasyotpattau vacitvam asyeti svatantratvagṛāhaḥ |
adhipatitvagṛāha iti yo 'dhipatiḥ prabhur īśvaraḥ sa ātmeti |
phalānuṛūpasyaiva vā taccaraṇasyādhipatitvam ātmety adhipatitvagṛāhaḥ |
śāśvatatvagṛāha iti hetvādevashnā paṁcavidhenāviśuddho 'py ātmā nitya eva |
anyathā tv akṛtam abhyāgamaḥ |[138]
kṛtasya nāśaś ca tathā smṛiteḥ pratyabhijñānasya cābhāvaḥ prasajyate |
saṁkliṣṭavyavadānatvagṛaha iti saṁkleśavyavadānāśrayatvāt |
yogikagṛāha iti |
ātmani manaso dhāraṇaṁ yogaḥ prāṇamanasaor vā nigrahaḥ |
ātmano manasā saṁyogaviśeṣo yogaḥ |
so 'syāstīti yogī |
amuktamuktatvagṛāhaś ceti |
ātmanaḥ pūrvabandhhād vimukta iti yo vikalpaḥ |
etad daśaviddhaṁ kauśalyaṁ kathaṁ mūlatatve 'ntarbhavati |
kauśalyaṁ hi skandhādiṣu vaicakṣaṇyam |
tat kathaṁ triṣu svabhāveṣv antarbhavati |
asaṁbhāvayataḥ praśnaḥ |
yatas triṣu svabhāveṣu te skandhādayo 'ntarbhūtā iti kauśalyaviṣaya etatkauśalyam iti śabdenodbāvitam |
na tu tasminn eva kauśalya iti |
ataḥ kauśalyatattvam apy adhikāratas tatrāntarbhāvo na tu svarūpata iti veditavyam |
tad yathā phalahetutattvamārgasatyaṁ triṣv antarbhūtam uktam iti |
nany evam api naiva vijñāyante yathā'ntarbhūtā iti pṛṣṭam katham antarbhavantīti |
ataś cāha |
vikalpakalpitārthena dharmatārthena tatra te |
iti |
teṣu triṣu svabhāveṣu te skandhādayo 'ntarbhūtā ity adhikṛtam |
tatra dhātvādibhyaḥ pūrvaṁ skandhānāṁ prathamāntarbhūtatvaṁ prakāśayitavyaṁ nirdiṣṭatvāt |
tatrāpi rūpasyety ata āha |
trividhaṁ rūpam |
katham |
parikalpitarūpaṁ vikalpitarūpaṁ dharmatārūpaṁ ca |
tatra kiṁ parikalpitarūpam |
yo[139]
rūpasya parikalpitaḥ svabhāva iti |
sa ca yo 'nyatra nānopalabdhes tatra viṣayasya bhāvaḥ parikalpyate |
so 'tyantam asattvāt parikalpita ity ucyate |
vikalpitaṁ rūpaṁ yo rūpasya paratantraḥ svabhāva iti hetupratyayapratibaddha ity arthaḥ |
kim arthaṁ punar ayaṁ vikalpitaṁ rūpam ucyata ity ata āha |
tatra rūpavikalpaḥ kriyata iti |
yasmād rūpakhyātavijñāne 'dṛṣṭatattvair aparijñāya rūpasaṁjñatvād rūpābhiniveśaḥ kriyate |
yathā skandheṣv ātmagrāhaḥ |
tasmāt paratantre vikalpitaṁ rūpam ity ucyate |
dharmatārūpaṁ yo rūpasya pariniṣpannaḥ svabhāva iti tadvikalpitarūpaparikalpitarūpavigatātmakā śūnyatā |
yathā rūpam iti yathā rūpaṁ tridhā bhittvā triṣu svabhāveṣv antarbhāvitam evaṁ vedanādayaḥ skaṁdhadhātvādayaś ca yojyā iti vedanāsaṁjñāsaṁskāravijñānaskandhā dhātvāyatanāni pratīyasamutpādāṅgāny āyatanānāyatanāny indriyāṇi kālatrayaṁ satyacatuṣṭayaṁ yānatrayaṁ saṁskṛtāsaṁskṛtaṁ cety etad api vedanādikaṁ dhātvādikaṁ ca pratyekaṁ parikalpita
vikalpitadharmatāsvabhāvabhedatas tridhā bhittvā triṣu svabhāveṣv antarbhāvitavyam |
tatra pratibhāseṣu vedanādiṣu yo 'tyantam asadbhūto grāhyagrāhakabhāvo rūpapratibhāseṣu yathā grāhyabhāvaḥ parikalpyate sā parikalpitā vedanā yāvat parikalpitaṁ vijñānam |
yasmin grāhyagrāhakena vikalpaḥ kriyate sā vikalpitā[140]
vedanā yāvad vikalpitaṃ vijñānam |
dharmatāvedanā yo vedanāyāḥ pariniṣpannasvabhāvaḥ |
evaṃ yāvad dharmatāvijñānaṃ veditavyam |
evaṃ dhātvāyatan diśv api yathāsaṃbhavaṃ vistareṇa vaktavyam |
rūpavedane yāvat saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtam iti yo nāmābhidheyo bhāvaḥ so 'pi parikalpitaparatantra pariniṣpannasvabhāvo veditavyaḥ |
katham |
tatra rūpādisvabhāvaprajñaptatvāt parikalpitaḥ |
prajñaptyadhiṣṭhānabhāvāt paratantraḥ pratyayaparatantratvāt tasyaiva |
rūpādibhāvātyantavigatatvam pariniṣpannaḥ |
nanu paratantra eva rūpādibhāvena nānnocyate na parikalpita ity anarthaka eva parikalpitakalpaḥ |
nānarthakaḥ |
tatra rūpādisvabhāvasyādhyāropitatvāt |
yathā hi nāmārthe pravartate tathā tathaiva nāsti bālās tu tatsvabhāvatvam abhiniveśanti |
tac ca mithyādhyavasitam na kv api bhūtārtheneti kuta etat |
nāmārthayoḥ sārūpye vairūpye ca vācyavācakabhāvavyāvṛttitaḥ |
yadi vṛkṣādiśabdānāṃ tadarthānāṃ cābhinnarūpatā |
evaṃ nāmnas tadartharūpāvyatirekād vācakatvaṃ vyāvartate 'rthavat |
arthasyāpi nāmasvarūpāvyatirekād vācyatvaṃ vihīyate niyamahetvabhāvāt |
nāmno 'pi vānyad vācakam arthasya ca vācyaṃ prasajyata ity anavasthātvāt |
ekasya cārthasya nāmabahutve nāmnaś cārthabahutve 'nekarūpatā prasajyate |
ekasya tu bahusvarūpatvam ayogyam |[141]
abhāvavācakānāṃ cāsattvaṃ arthavat tadarthasyābhāvarūpatvāt |
tadarthasyāpi vā sattvaṃ nāmno 'stitvād iti |
vairūpye
'pi śabdānurūpyeṇārthapratyayābhāvaḥ |
śabda eva hi tatra
vṛikṣo nārtho 'rthas tu na vṛikṣo nāpy avṛikṣaḥ |
tasmād
vṛikṣaśabdārthasya na vṛikṣāvṛikṣasādhāraṇatvam tadarthasyāvṛikṣātmakatvāt |
tadvīdhaṅ ca śabdārthayor vācyavācakabhāvavyāvṛittiḥ |
vailakṣaṇye ca vācyavācakaniyamo na syāt |
niyamakāraṇābhāvāt |
nāma cārtham utpādayed abhivyañjayed vā |
tatra notpādiyaḥ utpanne 'rthe nāmakaraṇāt |
utpanno 'pi punaḥ punar utpādiyitavya iti prasaṅgaḥ |
punaḥ punar nāmakaraṇāt |
nāpy abhivyañjayati [Tib. 92, b] |
yasmād arthaṃ gṛihitvā nāma kriyate
na ca gṛihitasyābhivyañjakaiṃ yuktam |
tenaiva nimna tam
evārtham anyo na gṛihnāti yas tan na jānāti |
tad eva tasyaivārthasya bhivyañjakaiṃ cānabhivyañjakaiṃ ceti na yuktam |
nābhivyañjakapradīpādayo vyaṅgam arthaṃ vyutpattyapekṣayā
vyañjayanti |
na ca vyaṅgeṣu ghaṭapaṭādiṣu vyañjakaniyamo
dṛiṣṭa iti sarvo 'rthaḥ sarveṇa nāmnā'bhivyajyeta |
na ca gandhādito niyamābhāvaḥ |
yasmāt kuthārajāṭādito gandhādyutpādayitṛitvam iṣṭāḥ |
na tu vyañjakatvam |
tasmād vācyavāca[44,a]kābhiniveśo grāhyagrāhakābhiniveśavad asadartha eveti pratīyate |
evaṃ triṣu svabhāveṣu skandhādīnām antarbhāvād daśavidhaṃ kāñcalyatattvaṃ mūlatatva eva draṣṭavyam |
skandhādikāñcalyam idam ātmadarśanapratipakṣatvena uktam |
ya[142]
smims tv arthe skandhādikāucalyaṃ vipakṣātmadrṣṭipratipakṣaḥ skandhādyartho nokta ity ataḥ sa idānīm ucyate |
anekatvābhisaṃkṣepaparicchiedārtha āditaḥ ||
ādita ity ādāv ucyata iti saṃbadhyate |
āditas tāvat skandhā ucyante |
te ca trividhenārthena veditavyāḥ |
anekārtho yat kiṃ ca rūpam |
atītānāgatapratyutpannam ādhyātmikaṃ vā bāhyaṃ vā |
audārikaṃ vā sūkṣmaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā praṇītaṃ vā yad vā dūre yad vā'ntika ity evam anekārthena bahūnām atītānāgatādīnāṃ dravyāṇāṃ skandhaśabdoktatvāt |
abhisaṃkṣipyetvacanād abhisaṃkṣepārthena skandha iti |
abhisaṃkṣipyety ekīkṛtyam |
paricchiedārthena ca skandha iti saṃbadhyate |
caśabdaḥ samuccayārthaḥ |
anekārthābhisaṃkṣepārthasdvayasyāpi |
atra kāraṇam āha |
skandhānāṃ rūpalakṣaṇena vedanādilakṣaṇebhyaḥ pṛthagvyavasthāpanād iti |
pṛthakparicchinna iti paryāyaḥ |
kim atra kāraṇam ebhis tribhir aṅgaiḥ skandhārtho vyavasthāpyate |
rāśyartho hi skandhārtha ity ata evaṃ vyavasthāpanam |
loke 'pi dṛṣṭam |
evaṃ loke 'pi dṛṣṭaṃ yad anekābhisaṃkṣepaparicchiedaṃ tad rāśir ity ucyate |
yathā rūpaskaṃdha evaṃ vedanādayo 'pi skandhā veditavyāḥ |
tatra yo 'yam ekakātmagrāho yathā puruṣa evedaṃ sarvaṃ yad bhūtaṃ[143]
yac ca bhāvyam ityādi tasya pratipakṣeṇa yathā skandhārthe kāṃcatyakṛtyam uktam |
katham iti |
ātmādibhinnarūpādiṣv akadhyam abhisaṃkṣipteṣu rūpādiskandhaprajñāpanāt |
etac ca trividhasaṃmohapratipakṣeṇa trividhaskandhavy avasthāpanam |
trividhaḥ saṃmoha ekatvasaṃmohaḥ saṃvṛttisaṃmoho lakṣaṇasaṃkīrṇasaṃmohaś ca |
(b) Dhātvarthaḥ
skandhānantaraṃ dhātava uddiṣṭāḥ |
tasmāt skandhārthānan taram dhātvartham āha |
grāhaka grāhyatadgrāhabījārthaś cāparo mataḥ |
iti |
aparaśabdo 'tra dhātāv eva saṃpadhyate skandhānantaram uddiṣṭatvāt |
ata eva bhāṣyakāro 'py āha katamo 'paraḥ |
dhātava iti |
bījārtho dhātvarthaḥ |
tadyathā svarṇe sati svarṇadhātur ucyate |
svarṇabījam iti gamyate |
sa ca phalabhedāt tridhā bhidyate |
grāhakadhātuḥ |
grāhyadhātuḥ |
tadgrāhadhātuś ca |
tatra grāhakabījārthaś cakṣurdhātvādayaḥ |
cakṣurdhātur ādir yeṣāṃ manaḥparyantās te cakṣurdhātvādayaḥ |
teṣāṃ ca rūpādiviṣayagrāhāśrayabhūtatvād grāhakāḥ |
sajātīyānāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ hetutvād dhātava ucyante |
grāhyabījārtho rūpadhātvādayo dharmadhātuparyantāḥ |
cakṣurādivijñānaviṣayatvād grāhyāḥ |
te 'py evaṃ sabhāgānāṃ rūpādīnāṃ hetubhāvād dhātava[144]
ucyante |
tadgrāhaṇīyārthaś cakṣurvijñānadhātvādayaḥ |
rūpādipratipattisvabhāvatvād rūpādigrāhāś cakṣurvijñānādayo manovijñānaparyantāḥ |
te 'pi sabhāgānāṃ eva cakṣurādivijñānānāṃ hetubhūtatvād dhātava ucyante cakṣurvijñānadhātvādayo manovijñānadhātuparyantāḥ |
anye tv āhuḥ cakṣurādy abhiniveśakarmavasitam ālayavijñānaṃ yathākramaṁ cakṣurādīnāṁ bījam |
tathā rūpādyabhiniveśakarmavasitam ālayavijñānaṁ yathākramaṃ rūpādīnāṃ bījam |
tathā cakṣurādivijñānābhiniveśakarmavasitam ālayavijñānam eva cakṣurādivijñānānāṁ yathākramaṁ bījam iti |
evaṁ tv ālayavijñānam eva dhātuśabdavācyaṁ na cakṣurādaya iti cintyam etat |
kartṛkriyākarmasaṁmohatyāvartanārthaṁ trividhe bījārthe dhātur vyavasthāpitaḥ |
atra pūrvacakṣurādaya uttaracakṣurādīnāṃ eva hetavo bhavanti na tatrānyaḥ kaś cid ātmānyo vā hetur vidyata ity evaṁ dhātukauśalyād ātmani hetugrāhavyāvṛttiḥ |
dhātvarthānantaram āyatanānāṃ artho vaktavyaḥ ata āha vedamārthaparicchede bhogāyadvāratāparam ||
iti dhātvaparasya hy āya(45, a)tanapratiṇyasamutpādādīnāṁ bahutvād aparaśabdasya ca sāmānyatvāt praśnaḥ kim aparam iti dhātubhyo 'nantaram uddiṣṭatvād arthata āyataneṣv evāyam aparaśabdo 'vatiṣṭhata iti jñāpanārtham āha āyatanam iti |
tatra ṣaḍ ādhyātmikāny āyatanāni vedan opabhogāya[145]
dvārārthena arthaparicchedo pabhogāyadvārārthena ṣaḍ bāhyānīti āyadvārārtha āyatanārthaḥ |
āya āgamaḥ |
taṁ veditārtha paricchedo pabhogayos tanvantity āyatanāni |
veditasaṁjñopa bhogāyadvārabhede nādhyātmikabāhyaprabhedaḥ |
veditaṁ hi su khaduḥkhāduḥkhāsukham |
tad evopabhujyata ity upabhogaḥ |
anubhūyata ity arthaḥ |
tasya vedanopabhogasyāyad dvāram ādhyātmikāny evāyatanāny uktāni |
saty api viṣayāṇāṁ tadāya dvāre cakṣurādīnām āgrayāṇām anugrahopaghātatvena tatpravi ṇ[āpravi]ṇatvena cāsādhāraṇatvenāgrayatvāt sati cakṣurādau tadadvāreṇa vedanopabhogān nāsatīti |
arthapariccheda saṁ jñā |
arthanimittodgrahaṇātmakatvāt |
sāivārthapratipattiyātmaka tvād viṣayam upabhuṅkte |
tadāyadvāra bhāvena bāhyāny āyata nāny uktāni |
na tv ādhyātmikāni |
saty api tadāyadvārabhā ve yasmād viṣayābhāve 'rthaparicchedo pabhogābhāvaḥ saty apy ādhyātmike cakṣurādyāyatane |
veditārthapariccheda yoḥ sasaṁprayogayor āyadvārabhāvatvād etāny āyatanāni vedita vyāni |
prādhānyaṁ hi dvayagrahaṇam |
grahaṇaṁ prādhānyaṁ punaḥ karmaphalo pabhogād arthaparicche dātmakatvāc ca [Tib. 95; a] |
veditārthaparicchedo pabhogamukhena bhoktṛvikalpaḥ pravartata ity ato dvividhenārthe nāyatane vyavasthānaṁ kṛtam |
evam āyatanakāṇḍalyaṁ bhoktṛtvāmikāny āyatanāni bhojanam āyatanāni bāhyāni nānyo bhokteṣv ātmani bhoktṛtvagraho vyāvartate |
yad uktaṁ cakṣurādyāyatanānām anugrahopaghātāt tad adhiṣṭhāna eva bhoktṛtvavyavahāra iti tad ayuktam |
rūpapra[146]
api janyajanakabhāve tadbhāvābhāvayor vijñānabhāvābhāvavyabhicārāt pratyayapratyayitvaṃ vyavasthāpyate |
rūpanirbhāsavijñānayor abhinnakād dṛṣṭāntena mukhyato 'sādṛśyam iti |
na bhedopacārāt |
tadyathā grāhyagrāhakavikalpādhiṣṭhānatvāt svapnamāyāgandharvanagarādivijñānaṃ paramārthato 'bhinnam api [Tib. 96, a] grāhyagrāhakākāreṇa [vyavahāra]bhinnam iva vyavasthāpyata ālambyālambakabhāvena tathānyatrāpi nāsti viro(46, a)dhaḥ |
api cālambyālambakabhāvaḥ sarvatropacaryata eva na mukhyato 'sti |
na hi vijñānam utpannam anutpannaṃ vā viṣayam ālambate |
utpattyanantaravinācitvād [ālamba]kriyākāle 'navasthānād asattvāc ca |
viṣayaś cotpanno 'nutpanno vobhayato 'py ālambanatvenāyogyaḥ |
utpannasyānavasthānād anutpannasya cāsattvāt |
na hi nirādhārā ālambanakrīyā yujyate |
yasyāpi sattāmātreṇa kāryakāraṇabhāva evālambyālambakabhāvo [vyavasthāpyate] tasyāpi vartamānasya vijñānasyālambanataṃ nirarthakaṃ bhavati |
tadātmatvena siddhaḥ |
utpadyamānasyāpi nirarthakam asattvāt |
notpadyamānasya nirarthakam |
yasmād viṣayasadbhāve vijñānaṃ tannirbhāsam ātmānaṃ pratilabhate |
yadi viṣayanirbhāso vijñānasya bāhyārthanimitaḥ |
evam atītenāgatasvapnādiviṣayasya nirākāratvaṃ vartate |
tadarthābhāvād vāsattvam |
na vikalaiḥ pratyayaiḥ kāryam utpadyate |
atiprasaṅgāt |
na cāsa[148]
taḥ pratyayatvaṁ yujyate |
na ca bāhyārthā ālaṁbanaprayayo vijñānasya |
tena vināpi bhāvāt |
tasmāt sarvam eva vijñānaṁ tāimirikādīnāṁ bāhyārthālambananirapekṣakām evābhyupagantavyam ||
āyatanārthānantaraṁ pratītyasamutpādārtho vaktavya ity ata āha |
pratītyasamutpādārthaḥ |
punar hetuphalāyāsānāropānapavādataḥ |
nirihānityasamarthaprayayotpannārthaḥ |
yathoktaṁ sūtre 'hy etad asmin satīdaṁ bhavati |
asyotpādād idam utpadyate yad utāvidyāpratyayāḥ saṁskārā iti vistaraḥ |
ayaṁ ca pratītyasamutpādo hetuphalakṛiyāṇāṁ samāropāpavādapratiṣedheneṣyata iti pratipādayann āha |
hetuphalakṛiyāṇām anāropānapavādārthaḥ pratīyasamutpādārtha iti |
āyāsaśabdena tu kriyoktā |
tatra hetusamāropaḥ |
avidyādin hitvā puruṣeśvarānupradhānādināṁ hetutvakalpanāt |
etad eva pratipādayann āha |
saṁskārādīnāṁ jarāmaraṇaparyantānāṁ viṣamahetukalpanād iti |
saṁskārādīnāṁ hy anityānāṁ nityaḥ puruṣādi ko hetur viṣamo visadṛśa ity arthaḥ |
atha vā hetuḥ svarūpavikṛiyā'pekṣo bījādiko 'ṅkurādināṁ dṛṣṭaḥ |
na ca nityasya svarūpavikṛiyāsti |
viṣamahetau hetutvakalpanād hetusamāropaḥ |[149]
hetvapavādo 'hetukakalpaṇād iti saṁskārādīnāṁ ahetūkotpādābhyupagamād avidyādīnāṁ saṁskārādihetutvam apodhaṁ bhavati |
phalasamāropaḥ sātmakānāṁ saṁskārādīnām avidyādipratyayapravṛttikalpaṇād iti |
saty ātmani saṁskārādīnām avidyādipratyayapravṛttitvam na tv asatiḥ saṁskārādiphaleṣv ātmasamāropa ity eke |
ātmadravyasamavāyitvena tadanugrahopaghātakartṛtvena vā sātmakānāṁ saṁskārādīnām avidyādipratyayapravṛttikalpaṇāt phalasamāropa ity apare |
phalāpavādaḥ saṁskārādīnām avidyādipratyayāpravṛttikalpaṇād iti tadyathā |
sucaritaduścaritaparalokāpavādināṁ nāsti sucaritaṁ nāsti duścaritaṁ nāsti paraloka iti |
kriyāsamāropo 'vidyādīnāṁ saṁskārādyutpattau vyāpārakalpaṇād iti |
yadi vasturūpam arthāntarabhūtaṁ saṁskārādyutpattāv avidyādayaḥ kurvantīti kalpyeta |
evaṁ kriyāsamāropaḥ |
kriyāpavādo niḥsāmarthyakalpaṇād iti |
avidyādīnāṁ sattāmātreṇa saṁskārāṇāṁ hy utpādanasamarthānām api niḥsāmarthyakalpaṇāt kriyāpavādaḥ |
tadabhāvād asamāropāṇapavādo veditavya iti |
tasya hetuphalakriyāṇāṁ samāropāpavādasyābhāvād asamāropāṇapavādo veditavyaḥ |
evaṁ idaṁ pratityasamutpādakauśalyam avidyādivyatirekeṇātmany anyasmin vā yaḥ kartṛtvagṛhas[150]
tasya pratipakṣeṇa veditavyam |
na hy atrātmā'nyo vā'vidyāvyatirekeṇa śubhāśubhānāṃ karmaṇāṃ kartā |
anyaḥ saṃskāratyatirekeṇa vijñānasya kartā nāsti|
sarvatrāpi yathāyogaṃ vaktavyam |
nirhebhyo 'nityebhyaḥ samarthabhyaś cāvidyādibhyaḥ
saṃskārādyutpattiḥ ||
pratītyasamutpādārthānantaraṃ sthānāsthānārtho vaktavya
ity ata āha |
aniṣṭeṣṭaviśuddhinām |
iti vistaraḥ |

asthānam ahetuḥ |
