punar asaṃbhāvaḥ |

parā[Tib. 98, a]yattatety
arthaḥ |
tac ca saptavidham api triṣv antarbhavati karmakleśajanmapāratantryeṣu |
tatrāniṣṭe pāratantryam iti |
aniṣṭā durgatayaḥ |
tatra gamana[m]duścaritāyattatvaṃ pāratantryaṃ ceti pradarśayann āha |
duścaritena prāṇātipātādinā kāyavāṅmanaḥkarmasaṃgṛhītena[151]
saprayogapṛṣṭhenānicchatoʼpi durgatigamād iti durgatayo narakāḥ pretās tiryañcaś ca teṣām ekāntena duḥkhatvān na kasya cit tatra gamanecchā bhavati karmaṇāṁ balavattvād durgatihetutvāc ca duścaritasyānicchatoʼpi tatra karmavaśād eva gamanam bhavati |
iṣṭe pāratantryaṁ sucaritena sugatigamanād iti iṣṭaṁ sugatigamanam |
sugatir devā manuṣyāś ca |
sucaritaṁ saprayogapṛṣṭhebhyaḥ prāṇātipātādibhyo viratiḥ dānamānasatkāradikriyā śāstrīgurugurṇānvitādiṣu maitrī karuṇādayaś ca guṇaviśeṣāḥ |
pāratantryaṁ sucaritam antareṇānyathā prārthayatām api sugatigamanābhāvāt |
sucaritapratibaddhataivasugatyutpatteḥ |
viśuddhāu pāratantryam iti vistaraḥ viśuddhiḥ kleśaprahāṇaṁ pañca nivaraṇāni kāmacchando vyāpādaḥ styānamiddham auddhatya[Tib. 98, b]kaukṛtyaṁ vicikitsā ca |
styānamiddham ekīkṛtya auddhatyakaukṛtyañ ca pañca bhavati |
abhedena kuśalapakṣaṁ nivṛiṇvantīti nivaraṇāni |
bhedena punar yathākramaṁ pravrajyābhiratiṁ tadanurūpe kāyavākkarmaṇi sabrahmacāribhiḥ samyakcoditasya samyakpratipattim |
śamathakāle 'linataṁ pragrahakāle 'vikṣepam śamathavipaśyānākāle svarasavāhitāṁ chādayantīti nivaraṇāni |
tāny aprahāyātyaktvā yāvat saptabodhyaṅgāny abhāvayitveti (18, b)yāvad iti paryantopadecāt smṛtyupasthānasamyakprahā[152]
narddhipādendriyabalānām api grahaṇam |
duḥkhasyāntākaraṇād iti duḥkhasyānto yatra duḥkhaṁ nāsti tac ca nirvāṇaṁ tasyākaraṇam aprāptir nirvāṇaṁ na prāpnotity arthaḥ |
atha vā duḥkhasyānto yasmād duḥkhād anyaduḥkhaṁ na sandhīyate tat tādṛśyaṁ duḥkhaṁ na karotīti pratisandhikaṁ duḥkhaṁ na karotīty arthaḥ |
pañcanīvaraṇaprahāṇapratibaddhatā viśuddhau pāratantryam |
samotpattau pāratantryaṁ tathāgatacakravartinor dvayor apūrvāparayor iti sahitayor ity arthaḥ |
tathāgatayoś cakravartino caikasmim̐l lokadhātāv anutpādād iti |
tathāgatayor trisāhasramahāsāhasro lokadhātur abhipretaḥ |
cakravartinos tu cāturdvīpaka iti ke cit |
tathāgatayor api cāturdvīpaka evety apare |
buddhā hi bhagavanto dharmakāyādhipatyāt trisāhasramahāsāhasre lokadhātau pratyekaṁ cāturdvīpakeṣu nirmāṇakāyenābhisaṁbodhiṁ parinirvāṇaṁ ca darśayanti |
tathā hi yad ekasmiñ cāturmahādvīpake tuṣiteṣu manuṣyeṣu ca cyutijanmaparigṛaho bodhisattvānāṁ prayojanam |
tad itareṣv api cāturdvīpakeṣu samānam |
tasmād akanīṣṭhabhavana evābhisaṁbuddho bhagavān nirmāṇakāyena trisāhasramahāsāhasre lokadhātāv antargateṣu sarvacāturdvīpakeṣu tuṣiteṣu upapattiṁ pracyutiṁ manuṣyeṣu ca janmaparigrahādi[153]
kaṁ sarvaṁ darśayatīti manyante |
tatra ca samasaṁvartanīya•karmāyattātā buddhacakravartyutpatteḥ sahabhāve pāratantryam |
ādhipatye pāratantryam striyā cakravartittvādyakaraṇād iti cakravartisamvartanīyasya karmaṇaḥ purṇātmabhāva eva vipākadānasamarthyāt |
na hi strīḥ stryādiratnopaghogasam• arthā |
ādicabdena cakratvādir gṛhyate |
saṁprāptau pāratantryaṁ stryā pratyekānuttarabodhyaṇa• bhisaṁbodhāt [Tib. 99, b] |
prāptir buddhapratyekabuddhatvā• dhigamaḥ |
tatra pāratantryaṁ puruṣasya prāptir na tu striyaḥ |
tasyā hi saṁsargābhāvāt khadgavat tribhūṁicāsarī cāsaṁbhavād yathākramaṁ |
sā punaḥ (47, a) sarvadhūrtajanagamyā |
alpapra• jñatvād anācāryakābhisaṁbodhāyogyā ceti |
samudācāre pāratantryaṁ dṛṣṭisampannasya bādhādya• samudācārāt |
pṛthagjanasya ca samudācārād iti dṛṣṭisaṁpanno dṛṣṭasatyaḥ |
bādhaḥ prāṇātipātaḥ |
ādiśabdenādattādānādayaḥ saṁgṛhyante |
te bādhādyaḥ kāyavākkriyāsvabhāvāḥ |
dṛṣṭi• saṁpannasya na samudācaranti |
darśanamārgeṇa bādhādiduc• caritasamutthāpakaklecaprahāṇāt |
pṛthagjanasya samudācar• anti |
tadaprahāṇāt |
tadaprahāṇaṁ punas tatpratipakṣeṇa mār• gānutpanntvāt |
evam aniṣṭādipāratantryānugataṁ āsthānakauśalyaṁ puru• ṣecvarādau svātantryagrāhapratipakṣaḥ |
yadi hi svatantraḥ kaś cit syān na duścaritena durgatiṁ gacchet |
evam anyatrāpi[154]
vācyam ||
sthānāsthānārthanirdeśānantaram indriyārtho vaktavya ity
ata āha |
indriyaṃ dvividham iti |
cakṣurindriyaṃ yāvad
ajñātāvindriyāvasānam |
atra punar ādhipatyārtha
indriyārthaḥ |
kasya punaḥ kvādhipatyam ity ata āha |
grahaṇasthānasaṃdhānabhoge śuddhidvayārthatāḥ ||
iti |
atra ca dvayādhipatyārthatā iti yuktāu kārikānukulārtham
ādhipatyaśabdo lupto draṣṭavyaḥ |
tasmād bhāṣyakāra āha |
teṣu tadādhipatyād iti |
grahaṇārtheneti |
arthaśabdo 'tra prayojanavācī |
grahaṇaprayojanaṃ nimittaṃ tatrendriyam iti
darśayati |
evam anyatrāpi yathāyogaṃ vaktavyam |
teṣv ity
arthagrahaṇādiṣu |
ādhipatyam ity adhikaprabhutvam ity ādhipatyaṃ hetutvam ity arthaḥ |
rūpādayo dharmaparyantā rūpādiviṣayāḥ |
teṣāṃ grahaṇaṃ cakṣurādivijñānam |
tatra cakṣurādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇām ādhipatyam |
cakṣuṣo niravaśeṣarūpaprabhedavijñānaśrayatvād rūpādīnām adhikaprabhutvam itīndriyam ucyate |
na tu nilarūpam pitarūpādivijñānasyālambanam bhavati |
evaṃ śrotrādiṣv api yathāsambhavaṃ vistareṇa vācyam |
sthāne jīvitendriyasyeti |
jīvitendriyaṃ punaḥ [pūrva]karmavāsanāviddhaṃ vipākasaṃtāne 'sati vicchittipratyaye pūrvapūrvakṣaṇasarūpeṣu uttarottarakṣaṇeṣu [Tib. 100, b] pratisaṃdhisāmarthyam |
sthā(47, b)ne jīvitendriyasyādhipatyam ity atraiva[155]
kāraṇam āha tadādhipatyenāmaraṇād iti amaraṇaṁ pūrvakarmākṣiptanikāyasabhāgānuvṛttiḥ saty api sattvasthitikāraṇatve nāhārādīnām indriyatvam tatsadbhāve 'py āyuṣaḥ kṣaye 'vasthānāsadbhāvāt rūpārūpyadhātāv asaty api kavaḍiṅkāhāre jīvitendriyād evāvasthānasadbhāvāt na ca tatra sparśādīnām avasthāna ādhipatyaṁ yujyate nirodhāsaṁjñīsamāpattyavasthāyām āsaṁjñike ca sparśādyabhāve 'pi sattvānāṁ sthitisaṁbhāvād iti
kulasaṁdhāne strīpuruṣendriyayor ādhipatyam iti putrotpattau kulāvyuparatau vartata iti putrotpattiḥ kulasaṁdhānam etad eva darśayann āha prasavādhipatyād iti kāyendriyapradeśa eva kaś cid ādhipatyaviśeṣāt strīpuruṣendriyākhyāṁ pratilabhate yatra strindriyaṁ puruṣendriyaṁ ca stas tatra kulavyavasthānam asti tadvaśena kulo na vyuparamate kāmāvacaresv api ca deveṣu strindriye puruṣendriye ca sati kulavyavasthānaṁ nāśatīti na hi gandharvādīnāṁ tatrādhipatyam saty api gandharvādau paṇḍakādīṣu strīpuṣendriyāsatsu putraprasavasaṁarthābhāvāt āhārādīnāṁ punaḥ sattve 'saṁkhyātatvāt saty apy ādhipatye nendriyatvam
upabhoge vedanendriyāṇām ādhipatyam ity adhikṛtam katham ity ata āha kuśalākuśalakarmaphalopa bhogād iti vedanendriyaṁ pañca sukhaṁ duḥkhaṁ saumanasyaṁ daurmanasyam upekṣā ca teṣāṁ hi kuśalākuśalakarmaphalam upabhujyate anubhavātmakatvāt na nāmarūpasya tadādhipatyaṁ vedanāsambhārabhāvāt tatsambhārabhāvaś ca vedanā pradhānatvād vipākasyeti[156]
laukikaviśuddhiḥ prahāṇaṁ laukikaklecānām |
tatra śraddhādīnām iti |
śraddhāvīryasmṛtisanādhiprajñāpañcendriyāṇām |
ādhipatyam iti vartate |
teṣām aśraddhādipratipakṣatvena laukikamārgaprabhāvitatvāt |
mokṣabhāgīyaprabhāvitatvād ity apare |
lokottaraviśuddhau anājñātamājñāsyāmīndriyādīnām ity ādhipatyam iti vartate |
lokottaramārgeṇa kleśānāṁ (48, a) prahāṇaṁ lokottaraviśuddhiḥ |
tatrānājñātamājñāsyāmīndriyājñendriyājñātāvindriyāṇāṁ trayāṇām ādhipatyam |
teṣāṁ darśanabhāvanā'śaikṣamārgeṣu yathākramaṁ saṁgṛhītatvāt |
tatra śraddhādīni pañca manaḥ sukhaśaumanasyopekṣāṇi cānāgamyādyāśritaviśeṣeṇa saṁbhavam adhikṛtyaitāni navendriyāṇi [Tib. 101, b]
darśanamārge 'nājñātamājñāsyāmīndriyam |
bhāvanāmārga ājñendriyam |
aśaikṣamārga ājñābhāvindriyam ity ucyaṁte |
yogācārāṇāṁ tv anyathā vyavasthānam |
anājñātamājñāsyāmīndriyājñendriye dvaye daśendriyāṇi |
pūrvoktesṣu navasu dāurmanasyam adhikaṁ prakṣipya daśa bhavanti |
ājñātāvindriyan tu navāiva |
tatra prayogamārge nirvedhabhāgīyātmake paṁcadaśasu ca darśanamārgakṣaṇeṣu manaindriyaṁ pañca śraddhādīny anāgamyādyāśritaviśeṣeṇa yathāsaṁbhavaṁ sukhaśaumanasyopekṣendriyāṇāṁ cānyatamad dāurmanasyendriyaṁ punaḥ prayogakāle nirvedhabhāgīyāpṛṣṭhenottāravimokṣaspṛhāsaṁgṛhitaṁ veditavyam |
tad etat saṁbhavato daśavidham indriyam |
anājñātaṁ svājñātuṁ pravṛttād anājñātamājñāsyāmīndriyam ucyate |
tad eva daśavidham indriyaṁ[157]
ṣoḍaśadarśanamārgākṣaṇād yāvad vajropamaḥ samādhir ity etasmiñ cāikṣamārga ājñendriyam ity ucyate apūrvājñātājñeyabhāvād
acāikṣamārge daurmānasyendriyavyatiriktāni riṣṭāny ājñātāvindriyākhyāni pratilabhante
ājñābhāvindriyam iti ājñātāvindriyam ucyate
evam indriyakuśalasya yaḥ puruṣanārāyaṇeśvarādiṣv adhipati[Tib. 102, a]grahaḥ sa vyāvartate
[g] Adhvārthaḥ
indriyārthānantaram adhvatrayārtho vaktavya ity ata āha
phalahetūpayogatvād anubhogāt tathāparam
iti tatra janmakāraṇaṁ hetuḥ phalaṁ janmasaṁgrihitam
phaladānaṁ hetor upayogaḥ phalasyopayogo 'nubhūtasya parikṣayaḥ
evaṁprakāraś cātito hetus tatphalaṁ cety ataḥ phalasya taddhetos copayogārthenātīto 'dhvā
tenāiva prakāreṇa phalasya hetoś cānupayogārthenānāgato 'dhvā vartamānaḥ (48, b)
punar adhvā vartamānaṁ janma hetor dattaphalatvād hetūpayogārthena tatphalaṁ ca vartamānaṁ janmānuvartata eveti phalānupayogārthena ca veditavyaḥ
bhāvānāṁ ca pratikṣaṇaṁ hetuphalaṇbhāvatvena kṣaṇatvam adhikṛtyādhvavyavasthānaṁ[158]
veditavyam |
tatra hetūpayogaḥ svaphalasyotpādanāt |
phalopayogaḥ phalasya janmānantaravināśāt |
evaṁ phalahetūpayogārthenatito 'dhvā phalahetvor anupayogārthenānāgato 'dhveti prakṛtam |
kāryānutpādād anutpannasya ca vināśābhāvāt phalahetvor anupayogo veditavyaḥ |
hetūpayogaphalaṁnupayogārtheneti kāryotpādanād dhetūpayogaḥ |
kāryāvināśāt phalānupayogaḥ |
evam adhvakuśalasya nityātmeti grāho vyāvartate |
adhvavyatiriktasyānyasyānupalabdhatvāt ||
Catuḥsatyārthaḥ
adhvatrayārthānantaraṁ catuḥsatyārtho vaktavya ity ata āha |
vedanāsanimittārthatanmitta pratipattitaḥ ||
iti vitaraḥ |
vedanā duḥkhārtha iti loke rūḍham |
śāstre ca yat kiṁ cid veditam idam atra duḥkhasyeti vacanāt |
satyavyavasthāyāṁ sarvā sāsravā sanimittā sukhā duḥkhāsukhāduḥkhā ca vedanā duḥkhasatyārtha iti pradarśayan bhāṣyakāra āha |
tatra duḥkhasatyaṁ vedanāsanimittārtheneti |
sarvā vedanā duḥkham iti kuta etat |
ata āha |
yat kiṁ cid veditam idam atra duḥkhasyeti vacanād iti |
sā ca vedanā duḥkhaduḥkhatāvipariṇāmaduḥkhatāsaṁskāraduḥkhatābhir yathāsambhavaṁ duḥkhā |
saṁskāraduḥkhatayaiva vā duḥkhā |
vedanānimittaṁ punar vedanāsthānīyā dharmā veditavyā iti |
sukhaduḥkhāduḥkhāsukhavedanīyā dharmā vedanāsthānāni |
evaṁ pañca skandhā duḥkhasatyaṁ nirdiṣṭāḥ |
tanmimittaṁ pratipattitaḥ samudayasatyam iti |
tacchabdena duḥkhasatyam abhisaṁbadhyate |
duḥkhaṁ[159]
yayā pratipattyā janyate sā pratipattiḥ samudayasatyam |
sā punar vedanāyāṃ vedanāsthānīyeṣu ca dharmeṣu tṛṣṇāhetupūrvakā kāyavāṅmanaḥpracalanasvārūpā |
saṃkṣepataś ca bhavasamud bhavikam karma samudayasatyam ity uktaṃ bhavati |
tayoḥ caṃārthena nirodhasatyam iti tayor iti [Tib. 103, a]
duḥkhasamudayasatyayoḥ |
caṃārthenety anutpādārthena |
evaṃ ca sahetukaduhkhānutpādalakṣaṇatvān nirodho dvividhenoktaḥ |
samudayacaṃārthena sopadhiśeṣaḥ |
duḥkhopacaṃārthena nirupadhiśeṣa iti |
tayor evādhikṛtayor duḥkhasamudayayoḥ pratipakṣārthena duḥkhasamudayanirodhaḥ gāminī pratipan mārgasatyam |
tad evaṃ satyakuśalasya saṃkleśavyavadānamātrāvagatatvāt kadaryaḥ saṃkleśavyavadānagrāho vyāvartate |
tasyābhāvād iti ||
[1] Yānatrayārthaḥ
satyārthānantaraṃ yānatrayārthaṃ pratipādyata ity ata āha |
guṇadoṣavikalpena jñānena |
iti vistaraḥ |
jñānaśabda ubhayatra saṃbadhyate |
guṇadoṣajñānenavikalpajñānena ceti |
atha vāntare vikalpacabdo lupto draṣṭavyaḥ |
tatra guṇadoṣajñānena parataḥ svayaṃ ceti vibhajya vaktavyam |
avikalpena svayam evety ata evāha vṛttau yathāyogam iti |
tatra nirvāṇasaṃsārayor guṇadoṣajñāneneti |
nirvāṇaśabdenātra mārganiroddhau saṃgṛhītau |
yathā praśāntaśabdaḥ |
tatra nirodhe cāntādyākārair [Tib. 103, b] mārge nairyāṇikādy[160]
ākārair jñānaṃ nirvāṇe guṇajñānam |
saṃsāro 'pi duḥkhasam­udayātmakaḥ |
tatrāpy anityadu­ḥkhādyākāraiḥ samudayapratya­yādyākāraiś ca jñānaṃ saṃsāre doṣajñānam |
parataḥ śrutveti buddhabodhisattvādibhyo nirvāṇasaṃsārayor guṇān yathoktān doṣāṃś ca śrutveti |
niryāṇārtheneti saṃsāranirga­mārthena |
śrāvakayānam iti paraghoṣasaṃniśrayeṇa niryāṇāt |
śrāvaka­yānam iti pratyekabuddhayānād viśeṣaṇaṃ darśayati |
tenaiveti nirvāṇasaṃsārayor guṇadoṣajñānena svayaṃ parato buddhabo­dhisatvabhyo 'śrutvā niryāṇārthena pratyekabuddhayānam |
sva­yaṃ [parato] 'śrutveti śrāvakayānād viśeṣaṇam |
avikalpena jñāneneti saṃsāranirvāṇayor anabhilāpyadharmată­prativedhād avikalpaṃ jñānam |
svayam eva parato 'śrutvā niryāṇārthena mahāyānam |
atra hi jñānena pratyekabuddhayānād vi(49, b)śeṣaḥ |
anye tv āhuḥ |
pudgalanairātmyaviṣayeṇa guṇadoṣavikalpe­na jñānena parataḥ śrutvā niryāṇaṃ śrāvakayānam |
śrava­ṇanirapekṣayā svayaṃ niryāṇaṃ pratyekabuddhayānam |
dharmanairātmyaviṣayeṇāpratiṣṭhitanirvāṇaprāpakeṇ [Tib. 104, a]­avikalpena jñānena svayaṃ niryāṇaṃ mahāyānam ucyate |
etad ākhyānaṃ bhāṣyeṇa virudhyate |
evaṃ yānatrayakuśalasya yogamātradarśanād ātmā yogiti grāho vyāvartate ||
[j) Saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtārthaḥ ]
idānīṃ saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtārthaṃ pratipādayann āha |
saprajñaptisahetukam |[161]
nimittaṃ praśanaḥ sārthaḥ paścād eva prakāśitam
iti ko 'yaṃ paścād uddeśa ity ata āha saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtam
iti sarvapaścād uddiṣṭatvāt tatra saṃskṛtaṃ saprajñaptikāt
sahetukāc ca nimittād asaṃskṛtaṃ punaḥ sārthāt praśanād
vyavasthāpitam prajñaptihetunimittaṃ punar ajñānāt tat pradarśayann āha tatra prajñaptir nāmakāyādaya iti prajñapyete
svabhāvaviśeṣāu bhāvānāṃ nāmapadakāyābhyām iti nāmapadakāyau prajñaptiḥ vyañjanakāyas tu tadubbhayābhivyañjakatvāt prajñaptiḥ hetuḥ punar bijasaṃgṛhītam ālayavijñānam iti
tac ca sarvasāsravadharmavasanāṃviśan na dattaphalaṃ samudayasatyasaṃgṛhītam ata eva dattaphalād vyavacchedārthaṃ
bijasaṃgṛhītam ity āha nimittaṃ pratiṣṭhādehabhogasaṃgṛhitam ity ālayavijñānam iti prakṛtam pratyuttikvijñāna
saṃgṛhītaṃ manarudgrahavikalpaṃ iti tatra pratiṣṭhā bhājanadokaḥ tadābhāsam ālayavijñānam pratiṣṭhātmanā saṃgṛhītam
iti pratiṣṭhāsaṃgṛhītam dehaḥ sendriyaṃ śarīraṃ tadābhāsam
ālayavijñānam dehasaṃgṛhītaṃ dehātmanopagatam ity
arthaḥ bhogasaṃgṛhītam etad evottaram uktam taddvayaṃ
śeṣāṇi vijñānāny adhipativastutvena bhuñjata iti bhogaḥ atha
vā annapānādiko 'rtho bhujyata iti bhogaḥ tannīrbhāsaṃ
vijñānaṃ bhogasaṃgṛhītam etat saprajñaptisahetukaṃ nimittaṃ sasaṃprayogaṃ paratantrasvabhāvasaṃgṛhītatvāt saṃskṛtaṃ veditavyam saṃprayogaś caitaḥ tat saṃskṛitaṃ na keva[162]
laṃ tad eveti darśayati |
kasya punas tan nimittam |
nāmakāyādīnāṃ teṣāṃ tatsvabhāvaviśeṣasaṃsūcakatvāt tadanusāreṇa ca svabhāvaviśeṣābhiniveśināṃ cittacaittānāṃ manaudgrahavikalpākhyānāṃ nimittam |
manaudgrahavikaḷpaṃ cālayavijñānasya nimittam |
tasmin vāsanāpoṣakatvād ity eke |
anye tu manyante |
svabhāvaviśeṣaprajñāpanāt prajñapter nimittaṃ saṃjñā nimittodgrahaṇātmiketi vacanāt |
pratiṣṭhādekabhoga saṃgṛhitam ālayavijñānaṃ nidānanimittaṃ vāsanāpoṣakatvāt |
ālambananimittaṃ ca manaudgrahavikalpā ālambananimittīkaraṇārthena |
ālambanālambakanimittatṃ cobhayathā nimittīkaraṇād iti |
tatra mano yan nityaṃ mananākāraṃ iti |
ahaṃ mama veti yan nityaṃ manyate tan mana ucyate |
tac ca saṃprayuktam ātmanoḷātmadrṣṭyātmasneḷātmamānaiḥ pañcabhiś ca sarvatragair vedanāsaṃjñācetanāsparśamanaskārair iti |
udgrahaḥ pañcavijñānakāyā iti |
viṣayasvarūpagrahaṇalakṣaṇatvāt |
svasaṃvedyā ity ākhyātum aśakyatvād udgraha ucyate |
vikalpo manovijñānaṃ |
tasya dharmasvasāmānyalakṣaṇavikalpatvād iti |
etat sarvaṃ saprajñaptisahetukaṃ nimittaṃ karmakleśābhyām abhisaṃskṛtatvāt saṃskṛtam |
asaṃskṛtaṃ punaḥ praśamaś ca nirodhaḥ praśamārthaś ca tathateti |
yuktaṃ nirodhasya praśamasvarūpatvaṃ sanimittaduḥkhapraśamalakṣaṇāt |
kathaṃ tathatā praśamārtha ucyata iti |
praśamasyārtha iti kṛtvā |
nanu ca nirodhena tathatayā na[163]
kaś cit sambandho 'sti kathaṁ praśamārthatvam tathātāyā mārgālaṁbanatvāt mārgasya punaḥ kathaṁ praśamatvam tena praśamakaraṇāt nirvāṇaṁ praśamaḥ praśamāt anena ca praśamakaraṇāt praśamo mārgaḥ evaṁ vigrahabhedād ekābhidhānena nirvāṇamārgayor grahaṇaṁ praśama iti mārgasatyaṁ punaḥ kiṁ saṁskṛtam asaṁskṛtam saṁskṛtam utpādyatvāt yadi karmakleśābhyām anabhisaṁskṛtād asaṁskṛtena ca prabhāvitād saṁskṛtam iti brūyān na doṣaḥ syād iti
anenārthena skandhādiṣu jñānaṁ skandhādikauśalyaṁ veditavyam iti
anekatvābhisaṁkṣepaparicchēdārtha āditaḥ
iti vistareṇa sarvatra vācyam
[Tattvasamāsaḥ]
tattvapindārthaḥ samāsataḥ tattvaṁ dvividham vistareṇa daśavidham uktam ataḥ samāsato dvividham ity āha
ādarśatattvaṁ dṛśyatattvaṁ ca tatra mūlatattvaṁ ādarśatattvam
tat punas trayaḥ svabhāvāḥ kim arthaṁ punas tad ādarśatattvam ity ucyate śeṣāṇāṁ lakṣaṇatattvādināṁ taddarśanāt
dṛśyatattvaṁ yad lakṣaṇatattvādikaṁ pūrvanirdiṣṭaṁ navavidham kim arthaṁ tad dṛśyatattvaṁ ity ucyate tasya mūla[164]
tattve darśanāt nirabhimānadṛiśyatattvam lakṣaṇatattvam
tena hi nirabhimāno bhavati pudgaladharmayoḥ grāhyagrāhakayoḥ bhāvābhāvayoḥ cāsamāropānapavādatvāt
viparyāyapratipakṣadṛiśyatattvam aviparyāsalakṣaṇam tad dhi nityādiviparyāyapratipakṣabhūtam
śrāvakayānanīryāṇadṛiśyatattvam phalabhetumayam tattvam caturāryasatyaprativedhabhāvanayā śrāvakanīryāṇam
mahāyānanīryāṇadṛiśyatattvam sūkṣmāndārikatattavam āudārikena samvṛttisatyena sattvaparipācanāt sūkṣmeṇa paramārthasatyena vimocanād mahāyānena niryāti
paravādinigrahadṛiśyatattvam prasiddhatattvam katham ity ata āha dṛiṣṭāntam niśritya yuktyā nigrahād iti nigrahe lokaprasiddho dṛiṣṭāntaḥ yuktiprasiddhaśabdāśrayād yuktiḥ
mahāyānābhidyotanadṛiśyatattvam viśuddhijñānagocaratattvam
tad dhi kleśajñeyāvaraṇaviśuddhijñānagocarasya anyāgamābhidyotanam tat punar mahāyānam
sarvajñeyapraveśadṛiśyatattvam samgrahatattvam tac ca pāmca vastūni tatra sarvajñeyānupraveśāt
avitathathathatā abhidyotanadṛiśyatattvam prabhedatattvam tena hi bhāvānām avitathatathatānyatathatāyor abhidyotanāt
ātmagrāhavastusarvābhiprāyapraveśadṛiśyatattvam ceti kauśalyatattvam tena hy ātmagrāhavastusarvābhiprāyamukhena praviśatiti yathā pūrvam uktam daśabhir abhiprāyāiḥ skandhādiṣv ātmagrāhapravṛttikam
tattvapinḍārthavacanam sukhadhāraṇārtham arthād grantham ākarṣatiti asammohāparikhedārthañ ca paridṛiṣṭādhikārādiparyantasya hi na parikheda upajñāyate nāpi sammoho[165]
yathoddeśanirdeśādliṅgamād utpadyate |
uktaṃ tattvam ||
āgamānusāriṇyāṃ Madhyāntavibhāgaṭīkāyām ācāryabhadantaSthiramatyuparacitāyāṃ Tattvaparicchedas tṛtīyaḥ samāptaḥ ||[166]
tattvapratipādanānantaraṃ pratipakṣabhāvanā vaktavyā |
tatrāvasthā phalaptrāptiś ca |
atas tatpratipādanārthaṃ caturthaparicchedārambhaḥ |
tattvānantaram uddiṣṭatvāt pratipakṣabhāvanāpratibandhhatvāc ca tadavasthāphalapraptyyor uddeśaḥ |
sūtre pratipakṣatvena bodhipakṣo 'numata iti pradarśanārtham āha |
pratipakṣabhāvanā bodhipakṣabhāvaneti |
te hi yathoktāvaraṇavipakṣaprahāṇāya bhāvyamānāḥ prabhavantīti pratipakṣā ucyante |
te punaḥ saptatriṃśat |
catvāri smṛtyupasthānāni yāvad āryāṣṭāṅgamārgāḥ |
tadbhāvanā bṛṃhaṇāropaṇā abhyāso bahulā kriyeti paryāyāḥ |
sedānīṃ vaktavyā |
tattvānantaram uddiṣṭatvāt |
kiṃ kāraṇaṃ smṛtyupasthānabhāvanāiva pūrvam ucyate nānyā bodhipakṣabhāvanā |
āha |
tatra tāvad ādau dūṣṭhulyatṛṣṇāhetukād[167]
iti vistaraḥ |
tatreti bodhipakṣabhāvanāyāṃ |
tāvacchabdaḥ kramārthaḥ |
ādau tāvat smṛtyupasthānabhāvanocyate |
paścād anyeṣām apīti |
nirvāṇasaṃsārayor guṇadoṣānavodhāt saṃskāreṣu sukhacuciniryātmābhiniveśena bālānāṃ bhavābhoge 'bhīmandanaṃ nirvāṇārthe trāsaḥ |
sa ca guṇadoṣānabodhaḥ sukhādyabhiniveśaś ca satyadarśanād vinivartata ity ataḥ saṃsārād udvejanārthaṃ nirvāṇe cābhimukhīkaraṇārthaṃ catuḥsatyāvatārāya smṛtyupasthānabhāvanādivādāv uktā |
kāyena dāuṣṭhulyaṃ prabhāvyata iti dāuṣṭhulyaṃ duḥsthitatā |
tathā hi pratidinam abhisaṃskriyamāṇo vikriyata eva kāya iti kāyena dāuṣṭhulyaṃ prabhāvyate |
tataś ca kāyaparikṣayā duḥkhasatyam avatarati |
kim artham etad ity āha |
tallakṣaṇasya hi sadāuṣṭhulyasaṃskāratvād iti |
yasmād duḥkhasatyasya sadāuṣṭhulyāḥ saṃskārā lakṣaṇaṃ svabhāvaḥ kāyena ca dāuṣṭhulyaṃ prabhāvyate |
tasmāt kāyaparikṣayā duḥkhasatyam avatarati |
dāuṣṭhulyaṃ na vijñāyata ity ata āha |
dāuṣṭhulyaṃ hi [Tib. 107, b] saṃskāraduḥkhateti |
saṃskāro bhāvānām utpattiḥ |
sa ca sarvaviplavavastu iti dāuṣṭhulyam āryāṇāṃ pratikulatvāc ca duḥkhatā |
yathoktam anartho janmedaṃ sati hi janane santi ripavo jarāmṛtyuvyādhivyasanavadhabandhaprabhṛtayaḥ |
anarthās te na syur yady avinaśo 'nutpādaḥ syāt|
vṛkṣe 'nutpanne 'gnivāyuvanāgnibhraṃśo na syād iti |
tayeti saṃskāraduḥkhatayā |
na vipariṇāmaduḥkhatāduḥkhaduḥkhatābhyām |
tayor avyāpitvāt saṃskāraduḥkha[168]
tānimittatvāc ca |
sarvaṃ sāsravaṃ vastv āryair duḥkhatvena lokitaṃ nānāsravam |
mārgasatyasya janmotpattipratipakṣatvāt |
na tu pṛthagjanair viparyāsavihīnacayatvāt |
sūtre 'py uktam |
samāsataḥ pañcopādānaskandhā duḥkham iti |
saṃskāraduḥkhatā'karmanyatety anye |
na dauṣṭhulyam akarmanyatety apare |
kiṃ kāraṇam |
akarmanyatā sāsravatvād avacyaṃ sāsravatā bījakāraṇotpannābhyupagantavyā |
sā tv abījāpy arhatsaṃtāne kadā cid āhāradyapacārād bhavati |
na cāsau dauṣṭhulyaṃ bhavitum arhaty anāsravatvād iti |
ataś ca sāsravadharmabījam ālayavijñānasanniviṣṭaṃ dauṣṭhulyenābhyucyate |
ayaṃ sukhaduḥkhāsukhāduḥkhasahitaḥ kāyas tatphalam |
tasmāt sukhādyavakāśasa hitena kāyena pratikṣaṇabhāvinā tad dauṣṭhulyaṃ prabhāvyate |
pratīyata ity arthaḥ |
tadyathā khalavilāntargataṃ bījam aṅkureti |
tasmāt kāyaparikṣayā duḥkhasatyam avatarati |
atra kāraṇam āha |
tallakṣaṇasya hi sadauṣṭhulyasaṃskāratvād iti |
sabījasaṃskārātmakatvād ity arthaḥ |
saṃskāraduḥkhatā hi vipariṇāmaduḥkhatāduḥkhaduḥkhatādauṣṭhulyābhyām anugatatvāt |
kāraṇatvāc ca dauṣṭhulyam ity uktā |
ata eva saṃskāraduḥkhatayā sarvasāsravaṃ vastu āryair duḥkhatvenālokitam |
evaṃ kāyasurūpyupasthānena duḥkhasatyam avataratīti |
tṛṣṇāhetur vedanā |
avidyāsaṃsparśajety abhiprāyāt |
anāsravāyā ataddhetutvāt |
tatra sukhā vedanā tatsaṃyogāviyoga[169]
tṛṣṇāyā hetuḥ |
duḥkhā tadasaṃyogāviyogatṛṣṇāyā hetuḥ |
aduḥkhasukhatā tadavasthābhiratitṛṣṇāyās tatsaṃyogāviyogatṛṣṇāyāś ca kasyāṃ cit samāpattau hetur iti |
sa duḥkhavedanayā spṛṣṭaḥ kāmasukham abhinandatīti vacanāt |
duḥkhāpi vedanā sukhāya tṛṣṇāyā hetur bhavati |
tatparikṣayā samudayasatyam avatarati |
sa hi vedanāṃ svarūpataḥ kāraṇataḥ kāryato 'nityato duḥkhataś copaparikṣamāṇaḥ samudayasatyam avatarati |
tṛṣṇāṃ tatkāryatvenāvagacchatīty arthaḥ |
tṛṣṇā hi sūtre samudayasatyam ity uktā |
ko duḥkhasamudayaḥ |
paṭhyate tṛṣṇā punararbhavikānandā rāgasahagateti vistaraḥ |
evaṃ vedanāsmṛtyupasthānena samudayasatyam avatarati |
ātmābhiniveśavastu cittam it |
anyasya nityādiguṇaviśiṣṭasyātmano 'bhāvād ātmābhiniveśālambanatvaṃ cittam eva |
tasmāt tatparikṣayā nirodhasatyam avatarati śāntatvena |
atraiva kāraṇam āha |
ātmocchedabhayāpagamād it |
citte hy anityatvādiparikṣayā'tmābhiniveśo vyāvartate duḥkhasaṃjñā pravartate |
ataś cātmocchedabhayāpagamād duḥkhamātrastyocched dānnnirodhasatyaṃ śāntatvenāvataratīti |
evaṃ cittasmṛtyupasthānena nirodhasatyam avataratīti |
dharmaparikṣayā sāṃkleśikavyāvadānikadharmāsaṃmohān mārgasatyam avataratīti |
tatra sāṃkleśikā dharmāḥ kleśopakleśāḥ |
vyāvadānikās tu kleśopakleśapratipakṣa[170]
bhūtāḥ pramāṇātryādayaḥ |
teṣāṃ sāṃkleśikānāṃ vyāvadānikānāṃ ca vipakṣapratipakṣabhāvaṃ buddhvā sarvopadravātyantaprahāṇopāye mārgasatye 'bhimukhībhavati |
evaṃ dharmasmṛtyupasthānena mārgasatyam avataratīti |
avatāro 'tra pratyekaṃ satye satyalakṣaṇāvabodhaḥ |
tatra kāyasmṛtyupasthānabhāvanā anityato duḥkhataḥ śūnyato 'nātmato 'pi |
anekāśucidravyasvabhāvato 'pi |
aśucibījato 'pi |
aśuciniṣyandato 'pi |
aśucisthitihetuto 'pi |
aśucivikārato 'pi phenabhaṅga svāsārato 'pi galito 'pi yonito 'pi |
vedanācittadharmasmṛtyupasthānabhāvanā punar anityaḍuḥkhaśūnyānātmato 'pi svalakṣaṇato 'pi hetuto 'pi phalato 'pi dhātubhūmigatiito 'pi kuśalākuśalāvyākṛtato 'pi vipakṣapratipakṣato 'pi |
bodhisattvānāṃ punaḥ smṛtyupasthānabhāvanā evaṃ cānupalambhākāreṇa ca vikalpaprahāṇārtham |
smṛtyupasthānānāṃ tu prajñāsvābhāvye 'pi smṛtyupasthānavacanāṃ smṛtibalenopaṣṭhānād etāsāṃ prajñānāṃ smṛtyupasthānam ucyate |
[b) Catvāri samyakprahāṇāni]
smṛtyupasthānabhāvanānantaraṃ samyak[Tib. 109, b]prahāṇabhāvanoktā |
kiṃ kāraṇam ata āha |
yasmāt parijñāte vipakṣe ca
iti vistaraḥ |
smṛtyupasthānabhāvanayeti |
aviśeṣābhidhāne 'pi dharmasmṛtyupasthānabhāvanayātra |
iti veditavyam |
dhātubhū[171]
nyavakāśaiḥ sāmānyalakṣaṇādyākārais tathā sāsravānāsravadhātubhūmiśaikṣāśaikṣādyākārais tathā sāsravānāsravadhātubhūmiśaikṣāśaikṣādibhedena ca vipakṣe pratipakṣe ca sarvaprakāraparijñāte vipakṣāpagamāyeti vipakṣāpagamo 'trotpannānāṃ prabandhacchedo 'nutpannānāṃ cotpādaḥ |
pratipakṣāgamāya ceti pratipakṣāgama utpannānāṃ prabandhānuvṛttir anutpannānāṃ cotpādaḥ |
viryacaturdhā pravartata iti vipakṣapratipakṣayor utpannānutpannabhedena caturdhā viryasya pravṛttiḥ |
utpannānāṃ pāpakānām akuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ prahāṇāyeti vistara itidam āgamena viryasyaiva caturdhā pravṛttiṃ darśayati |
vistaravacanena śeṣagranthābhisambandhaḥ |
tatrāyaṃ śeṣo granthaḥ |
prahāṇāya cchandaṃ janayati vyāyacchate viryam ārabhate cittaṃ pragṛhṇāti samyakpradadhāti |
evam anutpannānāṃ pāpakānām akuśalānāṃ dharmāṇām anutpādāya cchandaṃ janayatīti vistaraḥ |
evam anutpannānāṃ kuśalānāṃ dharmāṇām utpādāya cchandaṃ janayatīti vistaraḥ |
utpannānāṃ kuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ sthitaye bhūyobhāvatāyai asaṃpramoṣāya paripūraṇāya cchandaṃ janayati vyāyacchate viryam ārabhate |
cittaṃ pragṛhṇāti |
samyakpradadhāti |
utpannānāṃ sthitir aparihāriṇiḥ |
bhūyobhāvaḥ prabandhenotpādaḥ |
paripūriḥ prakarṣāgamanaṃ chandaṃ janayatīti sagauravodyuktavīryasamṛddhiṃ darśayati |
vyāyacchata iti kausīdyaprahāṇāya kāyacitte 'bhimukhīkarotīty arthaḥ |
viryam ārabhata iti layauddhatyāpakarṣaṇārtham |[172]
katham ārabhate līnaṃ cittaṃ pragṛhṇāti saṃvedanīyapramodanīyadharmamanaskāraiḥ uddhataṃ cittaṃ tasminn evālambane samyakpradadhāti saṃvedanīyapramodanīyadharmamanaskārair dhārayatīty arthaḥ evaṃ ca samyakprahāṇāni vīryātmakānīti jñāpitaṃ bhavati samyag ebhiḥ kāyavāṅmanāṃsi dhārayantīti samyakpradhānāni
Catvāra ṛddhipādāḥ
sthitikarmaṇyatā tatra
iti vistaraḥ tatreti tasyāṃ vipakṣāpagamavīryabhāvanāyāṃ
karmaṇyatā cittasthiteḥ sarvārthasaṃṛddhiyogyatā sā ca karmaṇyatā cchandavīryacittamīmāṃsādhipatyaprabhavetya ata āha karmaṇyatā catvāra ṛddhipādā iti (54, b) sarvasyābhijñādikasyārthasya samṛddhir ṛddhiḥ [Tib. 110, b] tasyāḥ pratiṣṭhārthena ṛddhipādā ṛddhibhetava ity arthaḥ ata evāha sarvārthasaṃṛddhihetutvād iti te punaś chandasamādhiḥ prahāṇasaṃskārasamanvāgata ṛddhipādaḥ vīryasamādhiḥ prahāṇasaṃskārasamanvāgata ṛddhipādaḥ cittasamādhiḥ prahāṇasaṃskārasamanvāgata ṛddhipādaḥ mīmāṃsāsamādhiḥ prahāṇasaṃskārasamanvāgata ṛddhipāda iti tatra cchandasamādhir yadā cchandādhipatyā vīryabhāvanāyāṃ sagauravaṃ pravartamānaḥ spṛśati cittasyaikāgratāṃ vīryasamādhir yadā vīryam ārabhamāṇaḥ sātatyaprayogāt spṛśati cittasyaikāgratāṃ citta[173]
samādhir yadā pūrvasamādhibījam āgamya citta eva cittaṃ dhārayan spṛiśati cittasyaikāgratām |
mīmāṃsāsamādhir yadātam tanapravicito 'nuspṛśati cittasyaikāgratām iti |
sthitir atra cittasthitiḥ samādhir veditavya iti samādhigrahaṇaṃ saṃskṛta lakṣaṇād vyavacchedārtham |
yata ārabdhavīryasya cittakar maṇyatā catvāra ṛiddhipādā bhavanti tata samyakprahāṇānanta ram ṛiddhipādā nirdiṣṭāḥ |
sā punaḥ karmaṇyatā kiṃprayoja nam kiṃhetukā cety ata āha pañcadoṣaprahāṇāyāśaprahā ṇasaṃskārabhāvanānvayā veditavyeti |
anvayo hetuḥ |
te ca pañcadoṣā na vijñāyanta ity ataḥ pṛṣṭaṃ ka ete pañca doṣā iti tat pradarśayann āha
kausīdyam avayādasya
saṃmoṣo laya udbhavaḥ |
iti vistaraḥ |
ete hi ṣaḍ doṣā ity ata āha tatra layauddhatyam eko doṣaḥ kriyata iti pañca bhavanti |
tatra kausīdyaṃ prayoga kāle doṣas tenāprayogāt |
udyuktasyāvavādasaṃmoṣo doṣas tena cittasamādhānābhāvāt |
samāhitasya layauddhatyaṃ doṣas tena cittakarmaṇyatā 'bhāvāt |
layauddhatya praśāntyā yasthā yām anabhisaṃskāro doṣaḥ anabhisaṃskāra upekṣā tayā taddeśapraśamanāt |
abhisaṃskāraḥ praśāntau doṣa iti[174]
vartate |
abhisaṃskāraś cetanā layāuddhatyavigamād dhī samatāprāptatvāt praśāntam ity ucyate |
[ Aṣṭa prahāṇasaṃskārāḥ ]
prahāṇasaṃskārāḥ prahāṇāya saṃskārāḥ |
kasya prahāṇāya |
anantaroktānāṃ pañcānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ prahāṇāya |
yady evaṃ tena vaktavyaṃ teṣāṃ prahāṇāya katham aṣṭaprahāṇasaṃskārā vyavasthāpyanta iti |
tatra kausidyaprahāṇāya catvāraś chandavyāyāmaśraddhāprasrabdhayaḥ |
tatra cchando 'bhilāṣaḥ |
vyāyāmo viryam |
śraddhā'bhisaṃpratyayaḥ |
prasrabdhiḥ karmaṇyatā |
kathaṃ punar ete kausidyaprahāṇāya vyavasthāpyanta ity ata āha |
te ca veditavyā yathākramam
āśrayo 'thāśritas tasya nimittaṃ phalam eva ca |
iti |
ato 'nyonyaprabhāvitatvād ete kausidyaprahāṇāya saṃvartaṃta iti darśayati |
āśrayaś chando vyāyāmasya[Tib. III, b]•
śrito vyāyāmaḥ |
abhilāṣasya vyāyāmapūrvakatvāt |
tasyāśrayacchandasya nimittaṃ śraddhā |
katham ity ato bravīti saṃpratyaye saty abhilāṣād iti |
śraddadhānasya hi hetuphale kriyābhilāṣo bhavati |
tasyāśritasya vyāyāmasya phalaṃ prasrabdhiḥ |
katham ity ata āha |
ārabdhaviryasya samādhiviśeṣagamanād iti |
samādhiviśeṣagamanaṃ hi prasrabdhinimittam |
ata ārabdhaviryasya samādhiviśeṣagamanāt prasrabdhir vyāyāmaphalam
ity uktam |
tathā hy ārabdhaviryasya dauṣṭhulyavikalpadoṣavigamāt pramoda utpadyate |
manaḥpramodāt kāyacittayoḥ prasrabdhiḥ karmaṇyatālakṣaṇatā |
tataḥ samādhiviśeṣagamanam
iti |
atra ca viryaṃ sahetukaṃ saphalaṃ ca kausidyaprati[175]
pakṣeṇoktam |
caturṇāṃ doṣāṇāṃ yathā saṃkhyaṃ pratipakṣa iti tatra smṛtir avavādasaṃmośasya pratipakṣaḥ |
saṃprajanyaṃ layāuddhatyasya |
cetanābhisaṃskārasya abhisaṃskārasyopekṣeti saṃskāropekṣātra veditavyā |
smṛtyādīnāṃ punar lakṣaṇavivakṣayedam āha te punaḥ smṛtyādayo veditavyā yathākramam
iti |
smṛtir ālambane 'saṃmoṣa iti vistarikṛta ālambanam iti cittasthāpanīyam (55, b) avavādavasty abhilapanam ity arthaḥ |
saṃprajanyaṃ smṛtyasaṃpramoṣe sati layāuddhatyāvabodha iti upasthitasmṛtir hi saṃprajanyavān bhavaty
evety ata āha smṛtyasaṃpramoṣe satīti anubuddhya layāuddhatyaṃ ṣamadhavipacyane yuganaddhād aprayatnena pravarteta
ity arthaḥ |
upekṣeti viṣayāntarānabhisaṃskāra ity arthaḥ |
etad uktaṃ bhavati sūpasthitasmṛtir avavāde līnaṃ cittam uddhataṃ vā yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti layāuddhatyavigamāya ca
tad abhisaṃskaroti layāuddhatyavigamāc copekṣāṃ karotīti |
evaṃ tasya cittapraśathatā karmanyatā |
atra ca karmanyatāsthitir
iti svārthe bhāvapratyayaḥ svapeṭatvādivat ||[176]
ṛiddhipādānantaraṁ paṁcendriyāṇi śraddhādīny uktānīti
saṁbadhyate |
teṣāṁ kathaṁ vyavasthānam iti kenārthenety
abhiprāyaḥ |
ata āha
ropite mokṣabhāgīye chandayogādhipatyataḥ
iti vistaraḥ |
adhipatyata iti vartata ity anena cchaṁdayogādhipatyata ity atrādhipatyaśabdaḥ prayukta uttaratrāpy anuvartata
iti darśayati |
ṛiddhipādaiḥ karmanyacittasya mokṣabhāgīyakuśalamūlaropaṇasyeti karmanyacittasya vimokṣabhāgīyaṁ kuśalamūlaṁ cittasaṁdhāne pratiṣṭhāṁ pratilabhate |
chaṁdādhipatyato
yāvat pravicayādhipatyataḥ |
na tv akarmanyacittasya |
sā ca
cittakarmanyatā ṛiddhipādair bhavati |
tasmād ṛiddhipādānantaraṁ vimokṣabhāgīyakuśalamūlaropaṇādhipatyataḥ śraddhādīni paṁcendriyāṇīti vyavasthāpyante |
chandādhipatyata
iti kāraṇe kāryopacārāc chraddhaivātra cchandaśabdenoktā |
tadyathā dadhitrapuṣaṁ sadyojvara iti |
atha vā'stitvaguṇaśaktiṣu yathākramam abhisaṁpratyayaprasādābhilāṣāḥ śraddhāyā
lakṣaṇam |
ata cchandagrahaṇenātrābhilāṣalakṣaṇā śraddhaiva
gṛihyate na tu cchanda iti |
prayogādhipatyata iti prayujyate
'neneti prayogaḥ |
prayogaśabdena vīryam abhipretam |
ālambanāsammośādhipatyata iti smṛtīndriyādhipatyataḥ |
ālabanābhilapaṇalakṣaṇaṁ smṛtīndriyam |
avisārādhipatyata iti
samādhindriyādhipatyataḥ |
avisāro hi samādhindriyaṁ cittaikāgratalakṣaṇatvāt |
pravicayādhipatyataś ceti prajñendriyādhi[177]
tyataḥ prajñendriyasya dharmapravicayātmakatvāt cakṣabdo vimokṣabhāgīyakuśalamūlaropanādhipatyād yathākramaṃ pañca śraddhādīnindriyāṇi veditavyānīti darśayati |
śraddadhāno hi saṃsāracārakān niḥsaraṇārthaṃ viryam ārabhate ārabbhaviryasya triguṇānuṣpattih smṛtisamādhiprajñendriyāṇi yathākramam |
evaṃ darśanamārgaprayogabhūmāv indriyāṇāṃ prādhānyam iṣyate |
anye tv āhuḥ na mokṣabhāgīyakuśalamūlaropanādhipatyataḥ śraddhādīnām indriyatvam |
kiṃ tarhi ropite mokṣabhāgīyakuśalamūle chandādīnām ādhipatyata etāny indriyāṇy ucyante |
etad eva pradarśayann āha chandādhipatyataḥ śraddhāyā indriyatvam iti saṃbadhyate |
prayogo vyāpāraḥ |
asaṃmoṣa alaṃbanāvipraṇāśaḥ |
avisaraṇam avisāraḥ |
pravicayanaṃ pravicayaḥ |
teṣāṃ prayogāsammoṣāvisārapravicayānām ādhipatyād viryasmṛtisamādhiprajñākhyāś caitā indriyākhyā bhavantīti |
atra tv etad vaktavyam ropite mokṣabhāgīye kuśalamūle ropitāvasthātaś chaṃdādyādhipatyasya śraddhādīnāṃ ca kaś cid viśiṣṭasvabhāvo nāsti |
yasmāt tadavasthāyāṃ tadādhipatyāt teṣām indriyatvam avasthāpyata iti ||
tāny eva śraddhādīni balavanti balāny ucyante indriyāntaram uktānīti |
teṣāṃ punar balavattvaṃ kuta ity ata āha
vipakṣasya [hi] saṃlekhād[178]
iti vipakṣasya saṃlīkḷitatvāt etad eva pradarśayann āha yadā
tānīti śraddhādīndriyāṇi āśraddhyādibhir vipakṣair iti āśraddhyakauṣīdyavisṃṛtivikṣepāsaṃprajanyaiḥ śraddhādivipakṣair
na vyavakiryante atyarthaṃ tanutvaād antarāntarāsamudācārābhāvān na vyāmiśryanta ity arthaḥ tadā balāni
ucyante tānīndriyāṇi vipakṣair vyavakiryante tadā tadvipakṣasyānirlikḷitatvāt
yady anirjitavipakṣāṇīndriyāṇi tat kathaṃ kauśalyatattve
laukikavicuddhyādhipatyena śraddhādīnāṃ indriyatvam uktam
tatra hy abhedenendriyanāṃ abhipretaṃ atra tu nirvedhabhāgīyasya kuśalamūlasya bhedapradarśanārtham tasmān nāsti
virodhaḥ ata evātrāha etāny evendriyāṇi balāny ucyanta
iti
śraddadhāno hi hetuphale vīryam ārabhate ayaṃ hetur
asyaiva phalasya idaṃ ca phalam asyaiva hetor ity evaṃ
śraddadhānaḥ phalaśraddhāyās taddheto vīryam ārabhate ārabdhavīryasya tatparatvāt smṛtir upatiṣṭhate upasthitasmṛter
vikṣepābhāvāc cittaṃ samādhīyate ekāgraṃ bhavati samāhitacitto yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti samādhyanugrihītaprajñā sphuṭatvaṃ bhavati
ropitvāmokṣabhāgīyasyendriyāṇy uktāni mokṣabhāgīyānantaraṃ nirvedhabhāgīyānīti indriyānantaraṃ ca balāny ato vaktavyaṃ nirvedhabhāgīyāni kim indriyāvasthāyāṃ veditavyāny
āhosvid balāvasthāyām tathā kiṃ nirvedhabhāgīyānīndriyabala[179]
svabhāvāny āhosvit tadavyatiriktasvabhāvānīty ata āha |
dvāu dvāu nirvedhabhāgīyāv [Tib. 114, a] indriyāṇi balāni ca |
iti |
atha vā yathā mokṣabhāgīyāni pratipakṣabhāvanāprabhāvitāni śraddhādiprabhāvitāni vā bhavanti tathā nirvedhabhāgīyāny api |
ata āha |
ropitamokṣabhāgīyasyendriyāṇy uktānīti vistaraḥ |
ata āha
dvāu dvāu nirvedhabhāgīyāv
iti sarvam |
uṣṇagatāni mūrdhānaś cendriyāṇīti mṛdutvād ayuktatvād eṣāṁ kuśalānām eṣu balānām indriyāṇy eva |
kṣāntayo laukikāś cāgradharmā balānīti |
adhimātratvād eṣām indriyāṇām asambhavāt kṣāntayo 'gradharmāś ca balāny eva |
mṛdumadhyādhimātrabhedena hi trividhāni śraddhādīni |
tatra mṛdūni madhyāni cendriyāṇi |
adhimātrāṇi balāni |
yāni mṛdūni tāny uṣṇagatāni |
madhyāni mūrdhānaḥ |
(56, a) adhimātrāṇi punaś caturdhā kriyante |
tatra mṛdumadhyādhimātrāṇi yathākramaṁ mṛdumadhyādhimātrāḥ kṣāntayaḥ |
adhimātrādhimātrāṇi laukikā agradharmā iti |
etāni ca nirvedhabhāgīyāni sarvāṇi samādhiprajñāsvabhāvāni sasaṁprayogāṇi |
tatroṣṇaprakāra uṣṇagatam |
yathā'raṇinirmathanāt taddahanasamarthasyāgner [Tib. 114, b] utpatticihnaṁ pūrvam uṣmotpadyate |
evaṁ sarvaklecendhanadahanasamarthasyāryamārgāgneḥ pūrvarūpatvāt tad uṣṇagatam[180]
ity ucyate |
calakuśalamūlebhyo mūrdhopagamanataścād mūrdhā |
kṣaṇiḥ satyārthe kṣaṇiāśrattvāt |
tadanyaśāsravadharmaśreṣṭatvāc ca laukikā agradharmā ity ucyante |
teṣām tv agratā tadanantaraṁ darśanamārgotpatteḥ |
te ca kṣaṇikāḥ śeṣāṇi nirvedhabhāgīyāni saṁtānavanti |
tatra mokṣabhāgīyaṁ kuśalamūlaṁ saṁsāraprabhāvanaśaktighātayogena veditavyam |
nirvedhabhāgīyam anāsravadharmotpattiśaktyādhānayogeneti ||
Sapta bodhyaṅgāni
agradharmānantaraṁ darśanamārgotpattes tasya [ca] bodhyaṅgaprabhāvitatvād āha |
tadānantaraṁ bodhyaṅgānīti |
katham etāni vyavasthāpyanta iti kim arthaṁ vyavasthānam ity abhiprāyaḥ |
ata āha |
āśrayāṅgaṁ svabhāvāṅgam
iti vistaraḥ |
darśanamārge bodhāv aṅgāni bodhyaṅgānīti |
adṛṣṭapūrvasya tattvasya tatprathamata ālocanād darśanam |
āryabhūmiprapakatvān mārga ity ato darśanamārga ity ucyate |
nirvedhabhāgīyāvasthāyāṁ hi pelavavastrantaritānivōpalakṣyante satyāni |
darśanamārgāvasthāyāṁ tadapanīta iveti |
tatra tatvābhisaṁbodho bodhinirvikalpaṁ jñānaṁ [Tib. 115, a] tatra
ṣaṭ smṛtiyādīni bodhyanukulāt tadaṅgāni |
dharmapravicayas tv avabodhatmakatvād bodhiḥ |
itareṣāṁ ca sahāyabhāvād aṅgatvam |
anyaḥ punar āha |
ānantaryamārggṛhīto dharmapravicayo bodhyaṅgam |
vimuktimārgasaṅgṛhītas tu bodhir iti |[181]
tatra bodher āśrayāṅgaṁ smṛtiḥ smṛtibālena tasmin ālambane vikṣepaṇāt
svabhāvāṅgaṁ dharmapravicayo jñānasvabhāvatvād bodheḥ
niryāṇāṅgaṁ viryam viryeṇa pṛthagjanahhūmisamatikramāt
anuśaṁsāṅgaṁ prītiḥ tayā kāyacittānugrahāt
asaṁkleśāṅgaṁ tridhā prasrabdhisamādhyupekṣāḥ tāsāṁ saṁkleśapratipakṣabhūtatvāt
atha vāsaṁkleśanidānāśrayasvabhāvabhedāt
kim arthaṁ punar asaṁkleśāṅgaṁ tridhā deśitam iti kenārthena tridhā deśitam ity abhiprāyaḥ
ata āha nidānenāśrayeṇeha svabhāvena ca deśitam
iti prasrabdhir asaṁkleśanidānam asaṁkleśo viśuddhaḥ
nidānaṁ kāraṇam kathaṁ nidānam ity ato braviti dauṣṭhulyahetukatvāt saṁkleśasya tasyāś ca tatpratipakṣatvād iti
etad uktam bhavati saṁkleśa[Tib. 115, b]nidānapratipakṣabhūtatvād asaṁkleśanidānam iti kāyacittakarmanyatā dauṣṭhulyam tasmin sati cittaikāgratā'sambhavāc cittavikṣepaḥ
vikṣiptacittasya ca rāgādisamkleśaḥ pravartate
evaṁ dauṣṭhulyaṁ saṁkleśasya hetuḥ tasya dauṣṭhulyasya pratipakṣaḥ prasrabdhiḥ
katham iti prasrabdhyā kāyacittayoḥ karmanyatāpādanāt
evaṁ prasrabdhir asaṁkleśanidānaṁ vyavasthāpyate
āśrayaḥ samādhiḥ prasrabdhyanugrihitakāyacittasya cittaṁ samādhīyate
samāhite citte yathābhūtaṁ prajānāti yathābhūtadarśanāt kleśāḥ prahīyante
evam asaṁkleśasya samādhir āśrayaḥ
svabhāva upekṣeti saṁkliṣṭavihārānukulāyāś cittāsamatāyāś cittapraśāthatāyāś cittābhogāvasthitatāyāś ca pratipakṣatvāt
evam upekṣā[182]
asaṃkleśasvabhāvaḥ |
anye tv āhuḥ |
prahāṇair mārgair darśanaheyesaṃkleśe prahīṇe tatsaṃkleśānubandhavimuktā svabhāvaviśuddhā vimuktimārgasaṃgṛhītā jñeye suniścitā yā prajñā prajāyate sā'tra prajñā upekṣety ucyate |
kṛtakṛtyatvāt kṣaye kṣaya ity upekṣākaraṇād iti |
(58, a)
bodhyaṅgānantaraṃ mārgāṅgāni |
teṣāṃ kathaṃ vyavasthānam iti darśanamārgānantaraṃ bhāvanāmārgas tatra ca mārgāṅgānity ato bodhyaṅgānantaraṃ mārgāṅgānity ucyaṃte |
teṣāṃ kenārthena vyavasthānam ato bravīti |
paricchedaḥ prāpaṇaṃ ca |
iti vistaraḥ |
mithyādṛṣṭivipakṣeṇa yāvan mithyāsamādhivipakṣeṇa samyagdṛṣṭyādīnām aṣṭānāṃ mārgāṅgānāṃ yathākramaṃ vyavasthānam |
kramaḥ punar eṣāṃ kārikānukrameṇa bhāvanāmārga iti vistaraḥ |
niṣevaṇabhāvanayā bhāvyata iti bhāvanāmārgaḥ |
tasmin bhāvanāmārge darśanamārgasya prāptibhāvanābhavitasya paricchedāṅgaṃ samyagdṛṣṭiḥ |
laukikīti savikalpat vā laukikī |
tadutpattisāmarthyalābhān mārgasamāpannasyāpi sā vyavasthāpyate |
lokottarāt tu nirvikalpāt |
nirvikalpasya sato 'pi samyagdṛṣṭyāśrayabhūtatvāt samyagdṛṣṭir ucyate |
ata evāha |
lokottarapṛṣṭhalabdheti |
lokottarasya darśanamārgasya pṛṣṭhena balena labdhotpannety arthaḥ |
sā punaḥ kiṃviṣayety[183]
ata āha yayā svādhigamaṃ iti |
darśanamārgādhigamaṃ paricchinatti evaṃ mayādhigataṃ iti darśanamārgasya viṣayatvāt samyagdṛṣṭir ity ucyate |
parasaṃprāpaṇāṅgam iti vistaras tayā samyagdṛṣṭyā paricchinnasya tattvasya samyagvācā samyaksaṃkalpena ca paratra prāpaṇāt samyaksaṅkalpaḥ samyagvāk ca parasaṃprāpaṇāṅgam |
ata evāha sasamutthānayā vācā tatprāpaṇād iti samyaksaṃkalpo hi vācaḥ samutthānam nāvitarkya nāvicārya vācaṃ bhāṣata iti sūtre vacanāt |
samāhito 'pi tābhyāṃ samanvāgato vedhatyastatutpattirlābhāt na tu samāhitaḥ saṃkalpayati bhāṣate vā |
parasambhāvanāṅgaṃ tridhā samyagvākkarmāntājīvāḥ katham ity ata āha tair hi samyagvākkarmāntājīvair yathākramam
dṛṣṭau śīle 'tha saṃlekhe paravijñaptikā matā iti |
samyagvāceti vistaraḥ katheti dharmadeśanā |
saṃkathyaviniścayaḥ paraiḥ sahābhisamayam ārabhya codyaparihārakriyā anena kathāsaṃkathyaviniścayena prajñāyāṃ saṃbhāvanā bhavati pareṣāṃ niyogād anena tattvam adhigataṃ tathā hy upaśamasaṃsaktaṃ suviniścitāviruddhārthaṃ sphuṭārthaṃ vacanam idam iti |
samyakkarmāntena śīla saṃbhāvanā bhavatīti vartate |
katham ity ata āha akṛtyākaraṇād iti |
samyakkarmānto hi duścaritavirodhity atas tena saṃbandhāgato jīvitahetor apy akṛtyaṃ na karotity ata āryakāntaśīlavān ayam iti saṃbhāvanā bhavati |
samyagā[184]
jīvena saṃlekha [iti] sambhāvanā bhavatīti saṃbadhyate |
katham ity ata āha |
dharmeṇa mātrayā ca civarādyeṣaṇād iti |
kuhanādidoṣarahito jīvitopakararaṇārthaḥ kāyavāksamācāraḥ samyagājīva ḥ |
tena saṃlekhe sambhāvanā bhavati niścayena•
syopakaraṇatṛṣṇā prahīṇā tathā hy asau kevalam āśrayasthi•
tyartham eva nyāyena mātrayā ca civarādin paryeṣate |
tatra samyagdṛṣṭyā ābhimāniko na bhavati |
yathābhūtaṃ svādūguṇaparijñānāt |
samyaksaṅkalpena samutthāpitavācā siṃ•
hanādaṃ nadati |
samyagvākarmāntājīvaiḥ svāṃ pratijñāṃ samudbhāvayati |
tatra vipakṣapratipakṣāṅgaṃ trividhā |
tat tu na jñāyata ity āha |
samyagvyāyāmasmṛtisamādhaya iti |
te ca kasya vipakṣasya pratipakṣa ity ata āha |
teṣāṃ punar yathākramaṃ kleśopakleśavāibhutvavipakṣapratipakṣatā ||
iti tatra prathamasya samyagvyāyāma [Tib. 117, b] iti |
bhāva•
nāheyo hi kleśaḥ prathamo vipakṣaḥ |
tasya samyagvyāyāmaḥ pratipakṣaḥ |
katham ity ata āha |
teneti |
samyagvyāyāmena mārgabhāvanāt |
samyagdṛṣṭiparigṛhītaṃ layauddhatyavigataṃ samādhyavasthānaṃ vīryaṃ samyagvyāyāmaḥ |
tena bhāvanā•
prahātavyānāṃ kleśānāṃ prahāṇāya tatpratipakṣam mārgaṃ bhāvayatīti samyaksamādhipratipādanāt samyagvyāyāmaḥ pra•
thama(53, a)sya pratipakṣa ucyate |
tasyām eva mārgabhāva•[185]
nāyaṃ layāuddhatyalakṣaṇasya dvitīyasyopakleśasya samyaksmṛtir iti pratipakṣa iti prakṛtam |
katham ity ato bravīti |
śamathādinimitteṣu sūpasthitasmṛter layāuddhatyavigamād iti
ādiśabdaḥ pragrahaṇimittam upekṣānimittaṃ ca veditavyam |
tatra vikṣepanimittopacamanāc chamathaḥ |
śamathādinimittaṃ
śamathādikaraṇam |
tatroddhate citte āuddhatyābhicaṃkini vā
saṃvejaniyavastumanaḥkāraḥ śamathanimitam |
tine citte layābhicaṅkini vādhinandaniyavastumanaḥkāraḥ pragrahaṇimittam |
śamathaikāntike vipacyānaikāntike vā mārge yuganaddhavāhini vā tadubhayopakleśānupakliṣṭe 'nābhogamanaḥkāra
upekṣānimittam |
abhijñādiguṇasabhāvasya vibhutvasya yad
vipakṣaḥ [Tib. 118, a] samāpattyāvaraṇātmakam akliṣṭam ajñānam |
tasya tṛtīyasya samyaksamādhiḥ pratipakṣa iti prakṛtam |
katham ity ata āha |
dhyānasannicrayeṇābhijñādiguṇābhinirhāraṇād iti |
dhyānaṃ hi sarvaviciṣṭaguṇābhinirhārāśraya
ity abhijñādiguṇābhinirhāravipakṣabhūtasya samādhyāvaraṇasya
pratipakṣaḥ |
tad evaṃ samyagvyāyāmena kleśāvaraṇavisaṃyogaṃ khyāpayati |
samyaksmṛtyā samādhipariśuddhim |
samyaksamādhyā samāpattyāvaraṇena viṣamam iti |
[h) Pratipakṣabhāvanāsamāsaḥ]
saiṣā pratipakṣabhāvanā samāsena trividhā veditavyā |
ārambhapratilambhaniṣpattibhedena |
vyāsatas tv anekavidhā |
katham[186]
anukūlā viparyastā
iti vistaraḥ |
tatrāviparyāsānukūlā viparyastā |
aviparyāso darśanamārgaḥ |
tattvavișayatvāt |
tadanukūlatvād aviparyāsānukūlā |
iyaṃ ca pṛthagjanabhāvanety atattvaviṣayatvād viparyastā |
pṛthagjana sya niravaśeṣaprahīṇakleśatvāt |
kathaṃ viparyastā
satī |
aviparyāsānukūlā bhavati |
suviśuddhadharmadhatunișyandāyāḥ śrutacintanāyā utpannāt |
sā ca smṛtyupasthāna
bhāvanām ārabhya yāvad balabhāvaneti |
aviparyastā
viparyāsānubandhetī |
iyaṃ hi caikṣāṇāṃ bodhyaṅgabhāvanā
mārgabhāvanā ca |
tattvaviṣayatvād aviparyastā |
bhāvanāprahātavyakleśasadbhāvāc ca viparyāsānubandhā |
aviparyastā
viparyāsāniranubandheti |
iyam acaikṣamārgabhāvanā |
tattvaviṣayatvād aviparyastā |
niravaśeṣamalaprahāṇād arhatāṃ viparyāsaniranubaindhā ||
idānīṃ śrāvakādibhyo bodhisattvānāṃ pratipakṣabhāvanāyā
viśeṣapradarśanārtham āha |
bodhisattvānāṃ tu
ālambanād viśiṣṭā sā manaskārād ca prāptitaḥ |
iti |
tatrālambanato viśiṣṭatā śrāvakapratyekabuddhānāṃ hi
svāsantānikaḥ kāyādayaḥ |
kāyavedanācittadharmā ālambanam |
bodhisattvānāṃ punaḥ svaparasāntānikaḥ kāyavedanācittadharmā ālambanam |
kim artham ālambanam bhinnam |
yasmāc
chrāvakādīnāṃ svārtham evādhikṛtya pratipakṣabhāvanāpravṛttiḥ |
bodhisattvānāṃ punaḥ svaparārtham adhikṛtyeti |
anityādibhir ākārair iti |
anityaduḥkhaśunyānātmākāraiḥ |
kasmāc chrāvakapratyekabuddhānām anityādibhir evaṃ manaskāraḥ |
tebhya udvegararthaṃ |
na hy anudvignas tair visaṃyujya[187]
te bodhisattvā anupalambhayogeneti kalpitasvabhāvānupalambhaprakāreṇety arthaḥ kim artham evaṁ manasi kurvanti sarvavikalpaprahāṇārtham eṣa hi teṣu viśeṣa ucyate na kevalam anupalambhayogenaiva manasi kurvanti
kiṁ tarhy anityādbhir apy ākāraiḥ evaṁ manasikāraviśeṣād viśiṣṭatā
yāvad eva kāyādināṁ visaṁyogāyeti kāyādināṁ visaṁyogo nirupadhiśeṣanirvāṇaṁ tadālambanakleśaprahāṇaṁ ca
bodhisattvā na visaṁyogāya nāvisaṁyogāya kiṁ kāraṇam
ekāntena hi kāyādibhir visaṁyuktāś ca avisaṁyuktāś ca sattvārthakaraṇasamarthā na syuḥ kṣīṇakleśetarasattvavat yadi na visaṁyogāya nāvisaṁyogāya kimarthaṁ tarhi pratipakṣaṁ bhāvayatīty ata āha yāvad evāpratiṣṭhitanirvāṇārthaṁ bhāvayatīti
kiṁ tad apratiṣṭhitaṁ nirvāṇam bodhisattvāvasthāyāṁ tāvat karmopapattivāśitāsanniśrayeṇa kāruṇikatvāt saṁsāropapattiḥ prajñābalena tatrāsaṁkleśaḥ tad idaṁ bodhisattvānām apratiṣṭhitaṁ nirvāṇaṁ vidhīyate
saṁsārāvasthito 'pi pṛthagjanavat saṁkliṣṭo na vartate na śrāvakādivan nirupādāno nirvāṇa iti tathāgatāvasthāyām api kleśajñeyāvaraṇaprahāṇān na saṁsāre pratiṣṭhitaḥ nirupadhiśeṣe nirvāṇe naiva pratiṣṭhito dharmakāyasyānucchedāt sāmbhogikanairmaṇikakāyayor yāvat lokas tāvat parārthakāraṇāt
evam eṣā pratipakṣabhāvanā bodhisattvānāṁ śrāvakādibhya ālambanamanaḥkāraphalviśeṣair viśiṣṭeti uktā pratipakṣabhāvanā[188]
pratipakṣabhāvanānantaraṃ tatrāvasthoktety ataḥ pṛcchati
tatrāvasthā kā iti |
tatreti tasyāṃ pratipakṣabhāvanāyām ata āha
hetvavasthavatārākhyā
iti vistaraḥ |
tatra hetvavastā gotrasthasya pudgalasyeti gotraṃ hetur bijam ity arthaḥ |
tatra yo gotra eva vartate na cittotpādādiṣu sa gotrastha ihābhipretaḥ |
sa punar uttarāvasthāsv anuvartate na nivartate |
ke cit tac ca gotraṃ kuśalamūlasvabhāvam iti varṇayanti |
kuśalamūlāny eva hi kāni cic chrāvakagotraṃ ity ucyante |
evaṃ yāvat kāni cid bodhisattvagotraṃ iti |
pṛthagjanavasthām ārabhyendriyabhedo gotram ucyate |
cittabījacaktir gotram ity etad apy eka āhuḥ |
tasmin sati pṛthagjanāvasthāyāṃ śaikṣāvasthāyāṃ ca śrāvakagotra ity ucyate yāvad bodhisattvagotra iti |
aśaikṣāvasthāyāṃ tu śrāvaka ity ucyate pratyekabuddha iti vā |
na tad gotraṃ tasya [Tib. 120, a] prakarṣāvasthāyāḥ |
tac ca śrāvakādibhedena trividhā bhidyate |
niyatāniyataprabhede na ṣaḍvidhaṃ bhavati |
avatārāvasthī utpāditabodhicittasyeti sa ca śrāvakapratyekabuddhatathāgatagotrāṇāṃ yathākramaṃ śrāvakapratyekabuddhasaṃyaksaṃbuddhabodhi ṣu cittotpādāt trividhī bhavati |[189]
tasya prayoganulabhutatvat tasminn upanne prayogavatara iti tadu tpadah prayogavataravastvety ucyate |
prayogavastha
cittotpadad urdhvam aprapte phala iti |
utpaditabodhicittasya danacīladiko balabhavanāparyavasanah kucala mula samarambhah prathamaphalaprāpaka upāya iti sa prayoga ucyate |
katham hetvavataravastha bodhipaksabhavanavasthaity ucyate |

sthābhiṣekādyavasthāvasānāḥ punar uktāḥ |
buddhānāṃ tu kāyatrayaprabhedapraderśanārthaṃ prāptyavasthādikāḥ punas tisro nirdiṣṭāḥ |
tatra hetvavasthā bodhisattvaśyānyena sādhāraṇatvāṃ veditavyā |
tatrādhimuktyavasthā bodhisattvānāṃ sarvasyām adhimukticaryābhūmāv iti cittotpādād ūrdhvaṃ yāvan na bhūmipraveśo 'trāntare 'dṛṣṭatatvo 'pi dṛṣṭatattva iva buddhabodhisattveṣv adhimuktyā svabhāvatrayamukhena dharmān pratipadyate |
tadanyasattvānāṃ duṣkaradānacitādiṣu buddhitvanispādaśambhāreṣu pravartata ity ata iyam avasthādhimuktipra bhāvitatvād abhimukticaryety ucyate |
sā ca kalpāsaṃkhyeyam adhikaṃ vā |
praveśāvasthā prathamāyāṃ bhūmāv iti svabhā vatrayasyāvikalpena jñānena tatprathemata eva tattvāsvasaṃ vedanāpratibaddha ity praveśāvasthā ucyate |
eṣaiva bodhisattvasya darśanamārgaḥ |
niryāṇāvasthā taduttarāsu ṣaṭsu bhūmiṣv iti bhāvanāprahātavyasya nimittānātmābhiniveśasya prahāṇaniṣ ṭhāgamanāt sābhogaprayoganiṣṭhāgamanāc caita bhūmayo nir yāṇāvasthety ucyate |
vyākaraṇāvasthā 'ṣṭamyāṃ bhūmāv iti sarvathā sarvanimittābhogasaṃjñāvigamād aṣṭamyāṃ bhūmau niyatipatitoṃ bhavati buddhātva iti buddhair bhagavadbhiḥ sam mukhavyākaraṇena vyākriyate |
kathikāvasthā navamyāṃ iti navamyāṃ bhūmau śrāvakapratyekabuddhebhyaḥ prativiśiṣṭāś catasraḥ pratisaṃvidaḥ pratilabhata ity anuttaro mahādharma kathiko bhavati |
abhiṣekāvasthā daśamyāṃ bhūmāv iti da śamyāṃ hi bhūmau bodhisattvo mārganīṣṭhāgato daśadigloka[191]
dhātuṣvavasthitānāṁ buddhabhagatāṁ raśmibhyo 'bhiṣekeṇābhiṣicyate |
prāptyavasthā buddhānāṁ dharmakāya iti sarvāvaraṇaprahāṇāt tatpratipakṣānāsravadharmabijaprācayāc cāśrayaparāvṛttyātmakaḥ sarvadharmavaśavarti anālaya iti
buddhānāṁ dharmakāyaḥ |
tena dharmapratīvedhaniṣṭhādhigamanāt prāptyavasthety ucyate |
anye tu niḥśeṣāgantukamālāpagamāt suviśuddho dharmadhātur eva dharmatākāyo dharmakāya iti varṇayanti |
sarvasminn api jñeye 'saktāpratihatapravṛttisvātantryajñānāṁ dharmakāya ity apare |
anucaṁsāvasthā sāṁbhogikaḥ
kāya iti svābhāviko yasmin kāye vyavasthite 'bhisaṁbudhyate yena kāyena niṣṭhāgatair bodhisattvaiḥ saha parṣanmaṇḍalo dharmasaṁgītim anubhavati sa sāṁbhogikaḥ kāyaḥ |
kṛtyānuṣṭhānāvasthā nairmāṇikaḥ kāya iti svābhāvikakāyacitaḥ sattvāśayālocano vineyārthaṁ prakṛtyāpravṛttāv anantaprabhedo nairmāṇikaḥ kāyaḥ |
sarvāpy eṣā bahuvidha'vasthā'bhisamasya veditavyā dharmadhātau tridhā punaḥ ||
iti |

[3] Phalaprāptiḥ
avasthāyāḥ phalapūrvakatvād avasthānantaraṁ phalabhāṣa ṇārthaṁ pṛcchati phalaprāptiḥ katamety ata āha
bhājanatvam vipākākhyam
iti vistaraḥ |
tad idaṁ samāsena paṁcavidhaṁ phalam |
bhā janatvam balaṁ rucir vṛddhiś cetiḥ |
tatra bhājanatvam[193]
yaḥ kuśalānukūlo vipāka iti sa punar aṣṭākṣaṇaprahāṇaḥ sāsravāniṣṭaṣaḍāyatano bodhipakṣadharmavipakaḥ sarvakuśaladharmoṭpattyāśrayatvād bhājanam ucyate |
balaṃ vā bhājanatvadhipatyād iti |
āśrayakleśopapattimandatām upādāya kuśalamūlasyādhimātratā puṣṭatety arthaḥ |
idam ucyate balam |
rucir yā pūrvābhyāsena kuśalarucir ity atīteṣu janmasu kuśaladharmābhyasād vartamāne janmani kuśaladharmābhiprāyo nadīsrotonyāyena sā ruciḥ |
vṛiddhir yā pratyutpannakuśalamūladharmābhyāsād apūrvaviśiṣṭakuśaladharmotpattiḥ |
kuśalamūlapuṣṭir ity abhiprāyaḥ |
ayaṃ balavṛiddhiviśeṣaḥ |
balaṃ hi mandakuśalavipākāpekṣyaṃ dāṛḍhyam |
vṛiddhiḥ punaḥ kuśalasyottarottaraviśeṣaḥ |
anye punaḥ kuśalamūlapuṣṭir iti viśiṣṭakuśaladharmavāsanāpuṣṭir ihābhipreteti manyante |
viśuddhiḥ yad āvaraṇaprahāṇam iti |
tatra śrāvakapratyekabuddhāgotrāṇāṃ viśuddhiḥ kleśāvaraṇaprahāṇaṃ viśuddhiḥ |
bodhisattvāgotrāṇāṃ kleśajñeyāvaraṇaprahāṇaṃ viśuddhiḥ |
etad yathākramaṃ pañcavidhaṃ phalam iti vipākaphalaṃ yaḥ kuśaladharmānukūlo vipākaḥ |
adhipatiphalaṃ yā bhājanatvadhipatyat kuśalasyādhimātratā |
niṣyandaphalaṃ yā pūrvābhyāsāt kuśale ruciḥ |
puruṣakāraphalaṃ yā pratyutpannakuśaladharmābhyāsāt kuśalamūlapuṣṭiḥ |
visaṃyogaphalaṃ yad āvaraṇaprahāṇam |
asyaiva pañcavidhasya phalasya samāsoktasya prabhedaḥ[194]
samāsena nirdiṣṭa
uttarottaram ādiś ca
iti vistaraḥ |

uttaraphalaṃ bodhisattvabhūmayaḥ |

atha vā pratipakṣabhāvanāvasthāphalapraptyayaḥ śrāvakādīnāṁ bodhisattvaiḥ sādhāraṇaṁ nirdiṣṭāḥ asādhāraṇaprasaṅgena yānānuttaryan tu bodhisattvānāṁ śrāvakādibhir asādhāraṇam iha ca prādhānyenocyata iti pradarśayann āha yānānuttaryam idanīṁ vaktavyam tad ucyata iti nismād uttaram asty ānuttaryaśabdo viśiṣṭavyaparyāyaḥ kiṁ punar etat mahāyānam tathā hi śrāvakapratyekabuddhayānābhyāṁ mahāyānaṁ viśiṣṭaṁ naiva mahāyānād anyad viśiṣṭataram asti tatra hetuphalayos taratamabhedāt anuttarasya bhāva ānuttaryaṁ kiṁ punas tad ity ata āha
pratipatter ānuttaryam
iti vistaraḥ mahāyānasya hi trividham ānuttaryam iti kiṁ punas tan mahāyānam etad eva tritayaṁ pratipattir ālambanaṁ samudāgamaś ca tatra pratipatter ānuttaryaṁ daśapāramitāniṣpādakatvam pāramitāniṣpādakatve bodhisattvapratipattir ānuttaryam ucyate ālambanasya punaḥ pāra[200]
mitadayo dharma dharmadhatus ca anena hy alambanena bodhisattvanam asesapararthakaranasamartha bhavantity atah pararthakaranasamarthyahetutvam alambanānuttaryam
kim karanam tadvattad tad alambanam bodhisattvānam anuttaryam ucyate
samudagamo gotradhimukticittotpadadinam phalam
samudagacchanti prapnuvanti gotradibhyas tad iti samudagamah
sa ca tato 'nyavicistasanudagamabhavad anuttara ity ucyate
tato 'tra tasmat samudagamad anyavicistahhavavastutvam anuttaryam

[2] Pratipattyānuttaryam
ālambanapūrvikā pratipattir ity ālambanapūrvanirdeśayuktau pratipattih pūrvam uktā bodhisattvānāṃ pratipattisāratāsaṃdarśanārtham tatra kathaṃ pratipattyānuttaryam ity ata āha pratipattyānuttaryan daśapāramitāpratipattito veditavyam iti pāramitāpratipattih pāramitānuṣṭhānam sā ca pāramitāpratipattir mahāyāna eva nirdiṣṭā nānyatra yāna iti mahāyānam evānuttaram iti darśayati sā ca pratpattir yaiḥ prakāraiḥ pāramitāsu prayujyamānasyānuttarā bhavati tān prakārān darśayann āha

[a] Paramāpratipattih
sā ca pāramitāpratipattir na vijñāyaḥ kathaṃ paramā yāvat kathaṃ aviśiṣṭety ata āha tatra[202]
paramā dvādaśātmikā
iti |

iti vistarah |

sampattir abhyudayah |
sa ca dvidhākārah prāptaś cāprāptaś ca |
tatra prāpte na sajjate |
aprāptaṁ na prārthayati svasukhanirapeṣaparaduḥkhāpanayaprayuktatvāt |

akṣayaparamatā |
akṣayatvena paramatā |[203]
akṣayatvaṃ punaḥ kuta ity ata āha mahābodhipariṇāmanayātyantam aparyādānād iti mahābodhipariṇāmitā hi dānādayaḥ sarvopapattiyātaneṣu vipākaphalāṃ datvāpi nātyantaṃ samucchidyante āśrayaparāvṛttilakṣaṇasya dharmakāyasya niṣpādanāt tasya cākṣayatvān nirupadhiśeṣe 'pi nirvāṇe na paryādīyante śrāvakādibodhipariṇāmanayā tu nirupadhiśeṣe nirvāṇadhātau sarvathā''śrayasya samucchedāt samucchidyante
mahābodhipariṇāmanayā vā dānādīny akṣayāṇi bhavanti prabhūtavipākatvāt prabhūtam apy akṣayam ucyate vipākadānenātyantam aparyādānāt na tv evaṃ śrāvakādibodhipariṇāmanayām akṣayam
atha vā sattvārthāṃ mahābodhipariṇāmitāni kuśalamūlāni bodhisattvānāṃ sattvadhātuvad akṣayāṇi bhavantīty ataś ca sattvadhātuvan nātyantaṃ paryādīyanta iti
nairantaryaparamatā ātmaparasamatādhimokṣāt sarvasattveṣu dānādipāramitāparipūraṇād iti pramudītāyāṃ hi bhūmau dharmadhātoḥ sarvatragardhāvabodhād bodhisattva ātmaparasamatām adhimucyate sa svaparasamatādhimokṣāt prahīṇasvaparavikalpaḥ svadānādibhir iva paradānādibhir api sumudītacittaṃ labhate tataḥ pratikṣaṇaṃ pratimuhūrtaṃ pratidivasaṃ ca nirantaraṃ dānādipāramitāḥ paripūryanta iti
akṛcchratvaparamatā anumodanāmātreṇa paradānādīnāṃ pāramitāparipūraṇād iti tathā bodhisattvāḥ sattvānāṃ dānādikuśalamūlāni samuditena manasā'numodayanti yathaiṣām anumodanāmātreṇa dānādipāramitāḥ paripūryanta iti
vittavaparamatā gagaṇagañjasamādhyādibhir dānādipari[204]
pūraṇād iti bodhisattvā dharmadhātuprativedhabhāvanayā gaganaganñjāsaṃjñakaṃ samādhiṃ pratilabhante
tasya pratilambhāt sattvānāṃ sarvābhiprāyaparipūraṇād dānapāramitāṃ paripūrayanti
tadādhipatyena hi gaganam eva ganñjo bhavatīti samādhir gaganaganñja ity abhidhīyate
yasmāt tatsamādhiśaktyā sarvopakaraṇāni gaganāj jāyante
ādiśabdena prajñāpāramitādisutrapaṭhitānāṃ samādhinaṃ grahaṇam

evaṃ yāvaj jñātṛjñeyajñānatrayānupalambhān nirvikalpaṃ jñānam

pāramitāḥ pratilabhante |
pūrvaṃ tu prathamabhūmiprayogasaṃgṛhītā tānkikā evety ataḥ prathamāyāṃ bhūmau pratilambhaparamatety ucyate |
niṣyandaparamatā tadanyāsv aṣṭāsv iti prathamabhūmipratilabdhānāṃ pāramitānāṃ tadanyāsv aṣṭāsu bhūmiṣu uttarottaravṛṣṭapratilambho niṣyanda iti niṣyandaparamatā |
atha vā'dhimuktiTib. 130, acaryābhūmau pāramitābhāvanābalena prathamabhūmipratilambhaḥ pratilambhaparamatā |

jātau tathāgatasyotpannaḥ |

atha vā prathamabhūmipratilabdhānāṃ pāramitānām aṣṭau bhūmayo niṣyandabhūtatvān niṣyandaparamatā |
niṣpattiparamatā 65, b daśamyāṃ bhūmau tāthāgatyāṃ ca bodhisattvaniṣpattyā buddhaniṣpattyā ca daśamyāṃ hi bhūmau bodhisattvaḥ sarvabodhisattvacaryānirjāta iti tatra tathāgatair yauvararājye 'bhiṣicyate |
acintyapuṇyajñānasaṃbhāraparipūrṇatvāt sarvadharmavikalpavigatāt sarvathā svapararīthasaṃpādakatvāc ca bodhisattvānāṃ niṣpattiparamatā |
tāthāgatyāṃ ca bhūmau prahāṇajñānaprabhāvātiśayalābhād bhagavān sarvasya jagataḥ śāstā bhavatīty ato buddhapariniṣpattyā niṣpattiḥ |
kā punar iyaṃ niṣpattiḥ |
bodhisattvadharmāṇāṃ buddhadharmāṇāṃ ca sarvaprakārābhinirhāraḥ |[206]
atha vā pāramitānāṃ caikṣabhūmisaṃgṛhītānāṃ prakarṣo daśamī bhūmiḥ atas tasyāṃ bodhisattvapariniṣpattiḥ acaikṣabhūmisaṃgṛhītānāṃ kāṣṭhā buddhabhūmiḥ
tasyāṃ bhūmau pāramitānāṃ caikṣabhāvād buddhapariniṣpattyā pariniṣpattir ity evaṃ bodhisattvapariniṣpattyā daśamyāṃ bhūmau tathāgatyāṃ ca buddhapariniṣpattyā pariniṣpattiparamatā veditavyā
tataś ca paramārthena daśapāramitā matā
iti yata iyaṃ dvādaśavidhā paramatā bodhisattvānāṃ dānādiṣu saṃvidyate na śrāvakādīnāṃ dānādiṣu tataḥ paramā ity anenarthena bodhisattvānām eva dānādayo jñānaparyantā daśapāramitā matā iti niruktinyāyeneti daśapāramitānāṃ yā pratipattih sā punaḥ paramety ucyate
ekeṣāṃ iti yeṣāṃ pāramitānāṃ nāmamātram api na vyutpannaṃ teṣāṃ tan nāma vyutpādanārtham ucyate
dānaṃ śīlaṃ kṣamā vīryaṃ dhyānaṃ prajñā upāyatā
praṇidhānaṃ balaṃ jñānam etāḥ pāramitā daśa
iti adhikāraparamatā sarvasattvārthakrīyoddeśād ity uktam ato 'yaṃ praśnaḥ kim āsāṃ pratyekaṃ karmeti ata āha
anugraho 'vighātaś ca karma tasya ca marṣaṇam
iti vistaraḥ dānena hi bodhisattvaḥ sattvān anugṛhṇātīti tatra pūjānugrahakāṅkṣayā bāhyādhyātmikavastu niravaśyāt pratyu pakāravipākādinirapeḳṣo yayā cetanayā parityajati kāyavākkarmaṇā ca pratipādayatidaṃ bodhisattvasyāmiṣadā[207]
nam anena kṣutpipāsādiduhkhāpanayanāt sattvān anugṛhṇāti
sattvānupaghātakānām ājīvaśāstrakalāpuṇānāṃ karuṇāpūrvakānām upadeśaḥ sugatiṃokṣamārgopadeśaś ca dharmadānam
anenāpy ajīvikādurgatisaṃsāraduhkhāpanayanāt sattvān anugṛhṇāti
rājacauradāyadavyālādibhir āghṛātānāṃ tebhyo vimokṣaṇam abhayadānam
anenāpi jīvabhogaputradārādivipabhayāpanayanāt sattvān anugṛhṇātīti
śīlenopaghatam eṣān na karoti sarvakāyavāgduścaritaviratir lokānuvṛttyā ca pratiṣiddhād vihitasya ca kāyavākkarmaṇo viratir anuṣṭhānaṃ ca śīlam
asmin śīle vyavasthānāt sattveṣu apakāraduṣṭopasaṃhāraṃ jīvitahetor api svayaṃ na karoti nāpi kārayatīti
kṣāntyā paraiḥ kṛtam upaghatarh marṣayatiti karuṇāpurahsareṇa cetasā sarvākāraduhkhamarṣaṇādhivāsanātmikā kṣāntiḥ
tenānyair hiṃsabandhanatāḍanādikṛtam upaghatarh marṣayati pratyapakārasamīhanāt
vīryeṇa guṇān vardhayati dānādin adhikṛtyotsāho vīryam
tena dānādiguṇābhyāsād viśiṣṭaguṇaniṣpattir vṛiddhiḥ
dhyānena ṛiddhyādibhir āvarjyāvatārayatīti ādiśabdena cittaparyāyajñānaṃ parigṛhyate
dhyānaṃ samādhiś cittasyaikāgratā
ṛiddhir ākāśagamanādikā
avatāraṇaṃ śāsanadharmatāpratipādanam
dhyānaphalābhyāṃ hi ṛiddhyādeśanāprātihāryābhyām āvarjitāḥ śakyante śāsana avatartum
prajñayā samyagavavādadānād vimocanād vimocayati[208]
sarvākāradharmapravicayalakṣaṇā prajñā tasyāś ca karma samyagavavādadānaṃ kleśaprahāṇāṃ civiparitānuśāsanapratibaddham ataḥ prajñayā samyagavavādadānād vimocayati
upāyakauśalyapāramitayā mahābodhipariṇāmanād dānādin akṣayāṃ karoti yasya dharmasya niṣpattaye vivṛddhaye prāptaye prahāṇāya vā yā yuktiḥ sa tatropāyas tatra kauśalyaṃ vācakṣaṇyam upāyakauśalyam
tasya kṛtopacitānāṃ dānādīnāṃ mahābodhipariṇāmanayākṣaya karaṇaṃ karma
mahābodhipariṇāmanāni kuśalamūlāni mahābodhivad akṣayāṇi bhavantīti
praṇidhānapāramitayā'nukülopapattiparigraḥāt sarvajānmasu buddhotpādarāgaṇato dānādiṣu sadā pravartate
praṇidhānam anekavaidhaiṃ prārthanātmakatvāt kuśalānukülopapattipraṃrthanam atra praṇidhānam abhipretaṃ tasya janmani janmani buddhotpādarāgaṇato dānādiṣu sadā pravartanaṃ karma
balapāramitayā pratisamkhyānābhāvanābalābhyāṃ niyataṃ dānādiṣu pravartate
vipakṣānabhibhavād iti pratisamkhyānaṃ prajñā bhāvanā tu kuśaladharmābhyāsaḥ tadubhayaṃ vipakṣeṇānabhibhūtaṃ balapāramitety ucyate
tasyāḥ kṣaṇanāiyamyena dānādiṣu pravṛttiḥ karma niyataśabdasya satataparyāyatvāt
na hi pratisamkhyānabhāvanābalād dānādikuśalaṃ vipakṣeṇabhibhūtam
jñānapāramitayā yathārutadharmasammoḥāpagamād dānādyādhipatyeyadharmasambhogaṃ ca pratyanubhavati sattvāṃś ca paripācayatīti sarvasmin mahāyānadeśanādharme yathāruta[209]
sammohavigatajñānāṃ jñānapāramitā ayaṃ punar yathārutasammohaḥ kaḥ yenābhiprāyikam arthaṃ tyaktvā yathārutam eva kalpayaty ayaṃ yathārutasammohaḥ
dānam adhikṛtya dānam ālambya yaḥ sūtrādiko dharmo deśitaḥ sa dānādhipateyaḥ
evaṃ śīlakṣāntiviryādyādhipateyo draṣṭavyaḥ
mahāyānam ārabhya buddhabodhisattvenāviparitasaṃkathyaviniścayakaraṇāt sattvānāṃ cāviparitayā dharmadeśanayā paripācanād dharmasaṃbhogānubhavanaṃ sattvaparipācanaṃ ca jñānapāramitāyāḥ karma
sūtrādidharmopadeśasya jñānapāramitāprabhāvitatvāt
[b) Manaskārapratipattiḥ]
paramāpratipattinirdeśānantaraṃ manasikārapratipatir vaktavyā
tataḥ pṛcchati manasikārapratipattiḥ katamety ata āha
yathā prajñaptito dharmo mahāyānamanaskṛyā
iti vistaraḥ
dānādīny adhikṛtyeti dānaśīlakṣāntiviryādīny uddīśya
sūtrādīnāṃ yathāprajñaptadharmāṇām iti atra sūtrageyavyākaraṇādidharma yena prakāreṇa dānādīny adhikṛtya bhagavatā deśitās teṣāṃ tathaivārthato vyañjanataś cādhyāropāpavādarahitānāṃ manasīkaraṇaṃ manasikārapratipattiḥ
nānyatheti mahāyānam iti na śrāvakayānaṃ
tatra hi svārthā eva dānādayo deśitā na svaparārthā yathā mahāyāne
tasmān mahāyāna eva dānādīny adhikṛtya yathāprajñaptānāṃ[210]
sūtrādidharmāṇāṃ bodhisattvair manasikaraṇaṃ manasikārapratipattih śrāvakayāne dānādīny adhikṛtya bhāṣitānāṃ sūtrādīnāṃ manasikaraṇaṃ mithyāmanasikārapratipattiḥ svārthamātraśrayatvād iti jñāpitam bhavati |
te ca prāyeṇa cittasamatāṃ asaktim anunnatim anupalambhaṃ cādhikṛtya prajñaptā iti |
tatreyaṃ cittasamatā yā guṇyaguṇināṃ samam upakāraprārthanā |
tatreyam asaktir yo bhavabhogaprārthanāvigatasya parārtham eva dānādiṣu prayogaḥ |
tatreyam anunnatir yad dānādībhir nātmānaṃ manyate na parān paribhavati |
tatrāyam anupalambho yā trimaṇḍalaparicuddhir dānadāyakapratigrahakānām anupalambhanyāyeneti |
abhīkṣṇam iti punaḥ punaḥ |
śrutacintābhāvanāmayyā prajñayeti tatrāptavacanapramāṇyajo niścayaḥ śrutamayi |
yuktinidhyānajac cintāmayi |
[Tib. I 33, b] bhāvanājo bhāvanāmayīti |
sarvā hi pravṛttiḥ phalārtham ataḥ pṛcchati tayā triprakārayā prajñayā manasikṛtyā kaṃ guṇam āvahatīty ata āha |
dhātupuṣṭyāi praveśāya cārthasiddhyai bhavaty asau ||
dhātur gotraṃ bījam |
tasya puṣṭir vṛddhiḥ |
dhātupuṣṭyā cāyatyāṃ viśadamahāprajño bhavati |
tasya śrutasyārthaṃ bhāvena praviśatīti tasya sūtrādikasya śrutadharmasyārthe yuktibavotpādikāe cintā(67, b)mayyā prajñaya'rthasadbhāvam ācayataḥ[211]
praviśatīti bhāvanāmayyārthasiddhiṁ prāpnoti arthasiddhis tattvādhigamaḥ katham ity ata āha bhūmipraveśapariśodhanād iti smṛtyupasthānādiparicuddhyā hi bhūmiṁ praviśatīty ataḥ smṛtyupasthānādibhir eva bhūmiṁ praviśatīti nirdiṣṭam
tataḥ smṛtyupasthānādīnāṁ bhūmipraveśabhūtānāṁ bhāvanāmayyā prajñayā pariśodhanād arthasiddhiṁ prāpnotīti bhūmiṁ praviśatīty arthaḥ
sā punar iyaṁ manasikārapratipattīḥ saparivārā vijñeyā na kevalā kaḥ tatparivāraḥ āha
sā dharmacaritair jñeyā saṁyuktā daśabhiḥ punaḥ
iti parigṛhītā veditavyeti parivāritety arthaḥ dharmātmakāni dharmādhikārikāṇi vā caritāni dharmācaritāni
tāni punar na jñāyanta ity ataḥ pṛcchati kāni daśavidhāni dharmacaritānīty ata āha
lekhanaṁ pūjanaṁ dānam
iti vistaraḥ tatra mahāyānasya svayaṁ pareṇa vā lekhanaṁ cirasthityarthaṁ śravaṇavācanārthaṁ vā pūjanaṁ puṣpadhūpagandhadībhiḥ mahāyānasyeti prakṛtam mahāyānapratiṣṭasya likhitapustakādīnāṁ pareṣu satkṛtyadānam pareṇa vācyamānasya satkṛtyaśravaṇam mahatā gauraveṇa svayaṁ vācanam udgrahaṇaṁ mahatā prasādenātmasātkaraṇam
mahatā yatnena yogyebhyo granthasyārthasya vā āviṣkaraṇaṁ prakāśanā svādhyāyanam uddhṛtasya punaḥ punar abhyasanam cintanaṁ yuktidvāreṇa tadarthaṁ nirūpaṇam bhavanam[212]
abhyasanaṁ samāhitayā prajñayā |
caśabdaḥ samuccayārthaḥ |
tatra lekhanā pūjanā dānaṁ śravaṇam ity ebhiś caturbhiḥ padaiḥ kāyakarma pradarśitam |
vācanam udgrahaṇaṁ prakāśanā svādhyāyanam ity ebhiś caturbhiḥ padair vākkarma pradarśitam |
cintanā bhāvanā cety ābhyām api manaḥkarma pradarśitam |
ameyapuṇyaskandhaṁ hi caritaṁ tad daśātmakam ||
yad etad daśaprakāraṁ dharmacaritam uktaṁ tata ekākasmād dharmacaritād yaḥ puṇyaskandhodbbhavo na sa punaḥ svayaṁ pareṇa vā pramātuṁ śakyate |
atibahutvād iti |
kasmāt punar dānaśīladhyānādidharmānirdeśe tulye mahāyāna eva dharmacaritam atyarthaṁ mahāphalaṁ deśyate sūtreṣu na punaḥ śrāvakayāne sūtreṣu dharmacaritaṁ mahāphalaṁ deśyata iti saṁbadhyate |
dvābhyāṁ kāraṇābhyām iti mahāyāne dharmacaritam atyarthaṁ mahāphalaṁ diśyata iti saṁbadhyate |
katamābhyām ity ata āha |
viśeṣād akṣayatvāc ca |
iti |
etad api na jñāyata ity ataḥ pṛcchati kathaṁ viśeṣāt katham akṣayatvād ity ata āha |[213]
parānugrahato 'śamāt |
iti |
mahāyāne hi bodhisattvānāṁ dānādipratipattitaḥ sarvasattvārthakaraṇaikāṁ deśyate |
śrāvakayāne tv ātmārthapradhānety
ataḥ śrāvakayānān mahāyānaṁ parānugrahapravṛttatvād viśiṣyate |
evaṁ tāvad dhetusamākhyānataḥ śrāvakayānān mahāyānasya viśiṣṭatvam |
acintyapuṇyajñānasambhāraphalasya trikāyasaṁgṛhitasya yāval lokas tāvat sattvahitakriyāpravṛttasya
nirupadhiśeṣe 'pi nirvāṇadhātāv anuparatasyāśrayaparāvṛttilakṣaṇasya dyotanād akṣayatvam |
na tv evaṁ śrāvakayāne |
śrāvakasambhāraphalaṁ hi kṣayatvam ākhyātaṁ nirupadhiśeṣanirvṛtānāṁ sarvātmanā samucchedāt |
evaṁ nirvāṇe 'py anuparataṁ phalaṁ dyotanāc chrāvakayānān mahāyānaṁ viśiṣyate |
atha vā mahāyānasyārtho dānādikaḥ sarvasattvānugrahapravṛttaḥ |
na tv evaṁ śrāvakayānārthaḥ |
śrāvakāṇāṁ svārthaparatvāt |
dānādayaś ca bodhisattvānāṁ dharmā anāśravakāyatvena pariṇṛttāḥ parinirvāṇe 'pi na vicchidyante |
śrāvakāṇāṁ
tu mārgasya kāulopamatvāt parinirvāṇe samucchidyanta iti na
teṣām akṣayatvam |
tasmād yuktaṁ mahāyānasyaiva dharmacaritaṁ mahāphalaṁ na śrāvakayāna iti |
manasikārapratipattyanantaram anudharmapratipattir vaktavyā |
ity ataḥ pṛcchati |
anudharmapratipattih katamety ata
āha[214]
avikṣiptāviparyāsapraṇatāv ānudhārmikī ||
iti |
dhyātavyajñeyadharmānugatā anupraviṣṭaśamatha(68b)vipaśyanābhāvanātmikā pratipattiḥ anudharmapratipattiḥ |
tatra
śamathabhāvanātmikā'vikṣiptā |
vipaśyanābhāvanātmikā'viparyāsapariṇatā |
iyaṁ dvividhā'nudharmapratipattiḥ |
ayamākāro bahuṣv artheṣu vartata iti kathaṁ avikṣipteti
na vijñāyata ity ata āha |
tatra ṣaḍvidhavikṣepābhāvād avikṣipteti |
vikṣepo 'pi na jñāyate kathaṁ ṣaḍvidha
ity ata āha |
ṣaḍvidho vikṣepaḥ prakṛtivikṣepaḥ |
bahirdhāvikṣepa iti vistaraḥ |
evam api prabhedamātraṁ darśitaṁ na
vikṣepasvarūpam iti pṛcchati |
etatsaḍvidhalakṣaṇo vikṣepaḥ
katham veditavya ity ata āha |
vyutthānaṁ viṣaye sāraḥ |
iti vistaraḥ |
dhimatā |
iti dhimān bodhisattvaḥ |
tatra vyutthānaṁ samādhitaḥ paṁcabhir vijñānakāyair iti yadā hi samāhitasya punaḥ paṁcānāṁ
cakṣurādivijñānānāṁ anyatamaḍ ekam utpadyate tadā samādhito
vyutthito bhavati |
ayaṁ ca prakṛtivikṣepaḥ |
bahirmukhapravṛttatvāt |
paṁcānāṁ vijñānakāyānāṁ vikṣepātmakatvāt paṁcabhir vijñānakāyaiḥ samādhito vyutthānaṁ prakṛtivikṣepa
ucyate |
viṣaye sāro bahirdhāvikṣepa iti yadā samāhitasya samādhy[215]
ālambanād akāmakasyālambanāntare manovijñānaṃ pravartate
tadālambanāntarasañcārād bahirdhāvikṣepa ity eke |
samāhitasya samādhyālambanam hitvā manovijñānasya viṣayāntarasañcāro bahirdhāvikṣepa ity apare |
samādher āsvādanā layauddhatyaṃ cādhyātmavikṣepa iti
yadi samāhitas tam eva samādhiṃ tṛṣṇādinā kleśenāsvādayati tataḥ śuddhakāt samādher vikṣepo bhavati kliṣṭasamādhipratyupasthānāt |
layauddhatyaṃ ca śamathavīpaśyanāsamavāhinaḥ samādher vikṣepo bhavati |
asamāhitatvāc cāsvādanālayauddhatyānām āsvādanādayo 'dhyātmavikṣepa ity ucyante |
sambhāvanābhisandhir nimittavikṣepaḥ dhyāyy ayaṃ mahātmety evaṃ sambhāvanābhimatty anena nimittena samādhau prayuktasyāśayavilayatvāl labdhād alabdhād vā kuśalap akṣād vikṣepo nimittavikṣepa ity ucyate atraiva kāraṇam āha tan nimittaṃ kṛtvā prayogād iti |
jñātukāmatayā taṃ sambhāvanābhisandhiṃ nimittaṃ kṛtveti sambhāvanātmanīti samādhiprayuktatvād iti |
sāhaṃkāramanaskāratā dauṣṭhulyavikṣepa iti āśayena samādhiguṇārthino 'pi sāhaṃkāramanaskāro 'smimānasamprayukto 'ham asmi dhyāyīti tataś cātmotkarsam anyatrāvajñāṃ karoti |
kasmād eṣa dauṣṭhulyamanaskāraḥ ity ata āha dauṣṭhulyavaśenāsmimānasamudācārād iti etad uktaṃ bhavati
dauṣṭhulyaprabhāvito vikṣepo dauṣṭhulyavikṣepa iti |
kiṃ punar dauṣṭhulyam asmimānavāsanāparipuṣṭis tayā hy asmimānaḥ samudācaratīti tathāśenāsmimānasamudācārāt |[216]
satkāyadṛṣṭir dauṣṭhulyam ity anye
hīnacittatvaṁ manasikāravikṣepa iti aniyataśrāvakādigotrasya mahāyānamanasikāraprayuktasya saṁsāraduḥkhabhirutayā sattvavipratipannatvenākalyānamitraṇiṣevanena ca tac chrāvakapratyekabuddhacittam utpadyate
tac cittaṁ hīnaṁ svārthamātraparāyaṇatvāt
tac chrāvakapratyekabuddhacittaṁ mahāyānamanasikāravikṣepakatvān manasikāravikṣepa kathyate
atrāiva kāraṇam āha hīnayānena tanmanasikārasamudācārād iti
hīnayānaṁ śrāvakapratyekabuddhayānam ātmaparinirvāṇādhikārapravṛttatvāt tanmanasikārasamudācāro mahāyānamanasikārebhyo vikṣipatīti hīnaṁ cittaṁ manasikāravikṣepa ity ucyate
evam iyaṁ bodhisattvasya ṣaḍvidhavikṣepadoṣarahitā camathabhāvanā veditavyā
[β) Aviparyāsapariṇatā]
evam avikṣiptena yathā'viparyāsapariṇatā'nudharmapratipattir bhāvayitavyā tathocyate aviparyāsātmanā pravṛttā'viparyāsapariṇatā
tatra yad yathā vyavasthitaṁ tad vastu tathā jñānam aviparyāsaḥ tadvastu punar na jñāyate katividhe vastunīty ata āha tatrāviparyāso daśavidhe vastuni veditavya iti
aviparyāsavastupradarśanārthaṁ bravīti yad uta
vyañjanārthamanaskāraviśāre lakṣaṇadvaye
iti vistaraḥ
idānīṁ teṣu vastuṣu yathāviparyāso bhavati tatpradarśanārtham āha tatra
saṁyogāt sambhavāc caiva viyogād apy asambhavāt[217]
arthasattvam asattvam ca vyañjane so 'viparyayah |
iti |
samyoge sati vyañjanānām iti samyogo hi parasparasamśleṣah |
na cāsau vyañjanānām vidyate yaugapadyābhāvāt |
vyañjanānām yugapaduccāraṇam ayogyam |
pūrvavikalpānām
uttaravyañjanoccāraṇakāle 'navasthānam uccāraṇānantaram eva
vinaṣṭatvād ity āśaṅkāvyudaśārtham āha |
avicchinnocārānatayeti |
etad uktam bhavati |
aviparyastāvilambitoccāraṇam atra
vyañjanānām samyoga iṣyate na tu vyañjanānām parasparasamśleṣayam iti |
asya cedam nāmeti sambhavād iti yadi cāsya
vastuna idam nāmeti samketo 'bhyasto bhavati |

jñānam |
so 'viparyāso vyañjane veditavya [Tib. 137, b] iti
samvṛttyā 'na tu paramārthataḥ |
paramārthenāviparyāsasya
nirvikalpakatvād ity anenāviparyāsena bodhisattvah śabdārtharahitān sarvadharmān pratividhyati |

tathā cāvidyamānatā |

arthasya sattvena varjita ity atraiva kāraṇam āha |
grāhyagrāhakābhāvād iti |

asattvena varjitaḥ |
kiṃ kāraṇam |
tatpratibhāsabhrāntisadbhāvād iti grāhyagrāhakapratibhāsabhrāntisadbhāvād ity arthaḥ |
ayam api saṃvṛttyā aviparyāsa ucyate na paramārthataḥ |
vikalpatvāt |
anenāviparyāsajñānena bodhisattvo grāhyagrāhakasvabhāvarahitān sarvadharmān pravidhyatīti |
idānīṃ grāhyagrāhakaprakhyānanimitte ālayavijñāne 'viparyāsapradarśanārtham āha |
tajjalpabhāvito jalpamānaskāras tadāśrayaḥ ||
iti vistaraḥ |
grāhyagrāhakajalpena paribhāvito jalpamānaskāra iti tasmin grāhyagrāhakaprakhyānalakṣaṇe 'rthe 'yaṃ grāhyo 'yaṃ grāhaka iti vāṅmanobhyāṃ yad abhilapanaṃ sa jalpa ity ucyate |
anena jalpena paribhāvito 'nāgatatajjātīyajalpotpādāya bījaparipoṣaṇam iti bhāvita ity ucyate |
so 'yaṃ jalpaparibhāvitatvāj jalpamānaskāra ity ākhyāyate kārye kāraṇopacārāt |
jalpavāsanā tv atra jalpaḥ |
