tasya grāhyagrāhakavikalpasya jalpākhyasya bījādhāra[219]
tvāt |
punaś ca jalpasya tatsanniśrayeṇotpatter āśrayo bhavati|
ty ayaṁ manaskāre 'viparyāsa ity aviparitaṁ jñānam ity arthaḥ |
sa tu manaskāro na vijñāyate yasminn aviparyāsa ity ataḥ pṛcchati kasmin manaskāre 'viparyāsa iti |
ato manaskāraviśeṣaṇārtham āha |
grāhya grāhakasamprakhyānakāraṇe |
ālayavijñānaviśeṣātmaka ity arthaḥ |
yathāsau grāhyagrāhakasamprakhyānakāraṇaṁ bhavati tatpradarśanārtham āha |
sa hy asau jalpamanaskāro 'bhilāpasaṁjñāparibhāvitatvād iti |
grāhyagrāhakaprakhydnakāraṇaṁ bhavatiti saṁbadhyate |
atha vā jalpamanaskāra ity ato 'bhidhānapravṛttinim.ttam iti pradarśanārtham āha |
abhilāpasaṁjñāparibhāvitatvāj jalpamanaskāra ucyata iti |
abhilāpasamutthā abhilāpasamutthāpikā vā saṁjñā abhilāpasaṁjñā |
tayā sasaṁprayogayā jalpamanaskāre svabījaparipoṣaṇāj jalpamanasikāraḥ paribhāvita ucyate |
ayam api saṁvṛttyaivāviparyāso na paramārthataḥ |
anena cāviparyāsena bodhisattvo viparyāsanidānaṁ grāhyagrāhakābhiniveśalakṣaṇaṁ parivarjayati |
evaṁ tad dauṣṭhulyaṁ sukhena varjitaṁ bhavatīti |
idānīm avśāre 'viparyāsapradarśanārtham āha |
māyādivad asattvaṁ ca sattvaṁ cārthasya tan matam ||
iti vistaraḥ |
yat tad arthasyāsattvaṁ sattvaṁ cānantaram uktam iti katham anantaram uktam iti |
dvayena pratibhāsatvaṁ tathā cāvidhyamānatā ||
ayam arthe 'viparyāsaḥ sadasattvena varjitaḥ |
iti |
tan māyādivan matam |
māyādīnām iva mā[220]
yādīvat |
etad eva pradarśayann āha yathā māyā svātmany avidyamāne na hastyādyātmanā prakhyānād hastyādibhāvena nāstīti hastyādibhāvena nāstīti sarvathaiva nāsti kiṃ tarhi nāpi nāsti tasya bhrāntimātrāstitvād iti tasya hastyādyākāreṇa prakhyāyamānasya bhrāntimātrasya astitvān na sarvathā nāsti |
tadartho 'pi yathā grāhyagrāhakatvena prabhāsate tathā naivāsti |
tasya traidhātukacittacittasvarūpasya tathāprakhyāyamānasya bhrāntimātrāstitvān nāpi nāsti |
ādiśabdena maricisvapnodakacamdrādayo dṛṣṭāntā yathāyogaṃ veditavyā iti na hi maricisvapnodakacamdrādayo yathā strīpumātmanā prakhyāyante tathā santi na ca na santy eva bhrāntimātrāstitvāt |
evam artho 'pīti vistareṇa pratyekaṃ yojayitavyam |
atra cādiśabdena gandharvanagarapratiśrutkādayo veditavyāḥ |
evaṃ yan māyādyupamārthadarśanāt sattvāsattvapakṣayoś cittasyāvisāratvenāvikṣepatvena darśanaṃ so 'visāra (71, a)viparyāsaḥ |
kaḥ punar avisāraḥ madhyamā pratipatsamādhir ity anye |
yady evaṃ katham avisāre 'viparyāsa ity ata āha tena bhāvābhāvayoś cittasyāvisāraṇād iti |
anenāviparyāsena [Tib. 139, b] bodhisattvo bhāvābhāvavikalpārahitac chūnyatālambane 'vikṣepaḥ |
tasmād avisāre viparyāsa ucyate |
ayam api saṃvṛttyaivāviparyāso na paramārthataḥ tasya nirvikalpakatvāt |
idānīṃ svalakṣaṇe 'viparyāsapradarśanārtham āha sarvasya nāmamātratvam sarvakalpāpravartanāt ||[221]
iti vistaraḥ |
sarvaṃ idaṃ nāmamātraṃ yad etac cakṣurūpaṃ yāvan manodharma iti |
ato dvādaśāyatanātmakaṃ jñeyaṃ sarvaṃ nāmamātram |
tatra nāmasvabhāvaviśeṣaprajñaptiḥ |
na ca prajñāpyasyārthasya prajñapter vā svabhāvo 'sti |
tenātmanā parikalpitaṃ |
parikalpitasya cāsattvāt |
anyathā nāmanirapekṣayā cakṣurāditvena grahaṇaṃ syāt |
na cāsty avyutpannasaṃketasya grahaṇam |
tasmāc cakṣurūpam ityādy asyārthasya'tatsvabhāvatvān nāmamātram eva cakṣurūpaṃ yāvan manodharma ity evaṃ yaj jñānaṃ sarvavikalpasya pratipakṣa ity ayaṃ svalakṣaṇe 'viparyāsa iti parikalpitadharmamukhena sarvadharmesṣu sarvavikalpapravṛttiḥ |
ato nāmamātrapratividhajñānaṃ sarvavikalpapratipakṣaḥ svalakṣaṇe 'viparyāsa ity uktam |
tatra na jñāyate kiṃ paramārthe svalakṣaṇe 'hosvit saṃvṛttāv ity ata pṛcchati |
katamasmin svalakṣaṇa iti |
[Tib. 140, a] ata āha |
paramārthe svalakṣaṇe |
ayam aviparyāsaḥ |
paramārthasya svalakṣaṇasyānabhilāpyatvān na saṃvṛtteḥ |
kiṃ kāraṇam |
saṃvṛttyā yato nedaṃ nāmamātram iti gṛhṇāti |
abhilāpadvarena dharmasvarūpe saṃkalpapravṛtteḥ |
ayam api saṃvṛttyaiva na paramārthato nāmamātram iti vikalpanāt |
anena cāviparyāsena bodhisattvaḥ saṃvṛttiparamārthakuśalo bhavati |
idānīṃ sāmānyalakṣaṇe 'viparyāsapradarśanāyāha |
dharmadhātur vinirmukto yasmād dharmo na vidyate ||
iti vistaraḥ |
na (71, b) dharmanairātmyena vinā kaś cid rūpy[222]
arūpī vā dharmo vidyate sarvadharmāṇām anabhilāpyādvayaprakṛtitvād grāhyagrāhakabhāvena rahitatvāt |
tasmād dharmadhātuḥ sarvadharmāṇāṁ sāmānyaṁ lakṣaṇam iti sarvadharmesv aprithaktvena tatsat sāmānyalakṣaṇam ucyate |
yad evaṁ jñānam ayaṁ sāmānyalakṣaṇatve 'viparyāsaḥ saṁvṛittyā na tu paramārthataḥ |
tasya nirvikalpatvāt |
tad anena cāviparyāsena bodhisattvo viśuddhyālambanakaśalo bhavati nirvikalpajñānaviṣayatvāt |
viśeṣaḥ sāmānyalakṣaṇasvalakṣaṇayoḥ ko vidyate abhilāpasvabhāvasamāropitatvasya pratipakṣeṇānabhilāpyasvabhāvatā paramārthalakṣaṇam |
parikalpitasvabhāva nairātmyaṁ punaḥ sarvadharmāṇāṁ prakṛtir iti sāmānyalakṣaṇam |
evaṁ darśanabhedavacād viśeṣo bhavati na tv arthād iti |
idānīṁ dharmadhātvaviśuddhiviśuddhāv aviparyāsapradarśanārtham āha |
viparyastumanaḥkārāvihānipariṇāntaḥ ||
iti vistaraḥ |
viparyastumanaḥkārāprahāṇaṁ tasya dharmadhātor aviśuddhiḥ |
tatprahāṇaṁ viśuddhir iti tatra viparyastumanaḥkāra ālayavijñānahetur viparyayavikalpaḥ |
tasyāprahāṇaṁ dharmadhātor aviśuddhiḥ prahāṇaṁ viśuddhiḥ yad evaṁ jñānam ayam aviśuddhau viśuddhau cāviparyāso yathākramam iti viparyastumanaḥkārāprahāṇaṁ tasya dharmadhātor aviśuddhir iti dharmadhātor aviśuddhāv aviparyāsaḥ |
tasya prahāṇaṁ[223]
viśuddhir iti viśuddhāv aviparyāsaḥ |
kiṁ kāraṇam |
dharmadhātor āgantukenopakleśenopakliṣṭatvāt |
ayam api saṁvṛttyaivāviparyāso na paramārthataḥ |
anena cāviparyāsena bodhisattvo 'prahīṇāvaraṇaṁ dharmadhātoḥ prahīṇaṁ ca prajānāti |
idānīṁ dharmadhātor aviśuddher viśuddheś cāgantukatve 'viparyāsapradarśanārtham āha |
dharmadhātor viśuddhatvāt prakṛtyā vyomavat punaḥ ||
iti vistaraḥ |
dharmadhātoḥ punar ākāśavat prakṛtiviśuddhatvād dvayam apy etad āgaṁtukam aviśuddhir viśuddhiś ca paścād iti |
tatra dharmadhātor abhūtavikalpopaklisṭatvād aśuddhir uktā |
paścāc ca pratipakṣodayena tatprahāṇād viśuddhir etad dvayam api dharmadhātāv āgantukam |
kiṁ kāraṇam ākāśavad dharmadhātoḥ prakṛtiviśuddhatvāt |
prakṛtiviśuddhasyāviśuddhir āgantukī bhaved viśuddhes tu nityaviśuddhitvāt katham āgantukībhava iti |
yā malavigamātmatayā viśuddhiḥ sātra vivakṣiteti nāyaṁ doṣaḥ |
ata evoktatrā viśuddhiś ca paścād iti |
yad evaṁ dharmadhātor jñānam etad dharmadhātor aviśuddhiviśuddhyor āgantukatve 'viparyāsaḥ |
ayam api saṁvṛttyā na tu paramārthataḥ |
anena cāviparyāsena bodhisattvo dharmadhātoḥ saṁkleśavyavadānāvasthayoḥ kṛtrimatvaṁ prajānātīti |
idānīm atrāse 'nunnatau cāviparyāsapradarśanārtham āha |
saṁkleśaś ca viśuddhiś ca dharmapudgalayor na hi ||
iti vistaraḥ |
na pudgalasya saṁkleśo 'sti na viśuddhiḥ |
nāpi dharmasya |
saṁkleśo viśuddhiś ceti vartate |
atraiva kāraṇam[224]
āha yasmān na pudgalo 'sti na dharmaḥ asity anuvartate
yaḥ saṃkleśavyavadānavyatirekeṇa vidyate yasya saṃkleśo
vyavadānaṃ vā parikalpayi tuṃ yujyate yatpṛthaktvena ca hānivṛddhi syātām sa tādṛśaḥ pudgalo nāsti pratyakṣānumānāviṣayatvāt
dharmo 'pi parikalpitātmako naivāsti
ekasmin vastuny anekaparikalpa pravṛtteḥ na caikasyānekātmatā yujyate
ato 'sattvāt pudgaladharmayoḥ dvayoḥ bandhyāputrādivan na saṃkleśo na vyavadānam
parataṃtrasyāpi śuddhalaukikajñānagocarasya saṃkleśaviśuddhi naiveṣyete
kiṃ kāraṇam tasya nirabhilāpyādvayaprakṛtitvāt
yadi parataṃtrasyāpi neṣyete katamasya te syātām tasmin asati saṃkleśa parihāravyavadānaprāptiyatnavāiyarthyaṃ
dharmadhātoḥ saṃkleśaviśuddhi bhavata ity ayam adoṣaḥ
dharmadhātor apy atanmayasyaiva te vyavasthāpyete dharmadhātau tayor āgantukatvād dharmadhātoś cākāśavad avikārāt
tataḥ saṃkleśapakṣe kā cid dhānir nāsti vyavadānapakṣe vā kaś cid viśeṣo yatas trāso vā syān māno vety ayam atrāse 'nunnatau cāviparyāsa iti
anye tv āhuḥ na saṃkleśapakṣe kasya cid dhānir na vyavadānapakṣe viśeṣo 'sti tadudhayavataḥ kasya cid abhāvāt
yataḥ punas ta evaṃ bhavatas tatra kim iti[225]
trāso bhavaty ayaṃ me nāstīti kiṃ iti māno bhavaty ayaṃ me viśeṣa iti |
apare tu manyante |
pudgalas tāvan naivāsti |
dharmo 'pi naivāsti |
avasthādvayaṃ yo 'nubhavati yasyaitad dvayaṃ kalpyeta |
kiṃ tarhi pratītyasamutpādamātram etat saṃkleṣavyavadānam iti |
ayam api saṃvṛttyā |
na tu paramārthataḥ |
tasya nirvikalpakatvāt |
anena cāviparyāsena kṣayavṛiddhyaśadbhāvād avikāritvaṃ dharmadhātoḥ pratividhyatīti |
anena cāviparyāsena sarvam āvaraṇaṃ prahāya nirāvaraṇaṃ niryātīty anye |
atha vānena saṃvṛttyātmakena daśāviparyāsena paramārthāviparyāso 'tra sucitaḥ |
tasyanyathā nirdeśānupapatteh |
anyaḥ punar āha |
ya ete vyañjane 'rthe manaskāre 'visāre svalaḳṣaṇe sāmānyalaḳṣaṇe 'śuddhāv śuddhāv āgantuke 'trāse 'unnatāv cāviparyāsakalpita āśraye śāste svabhāvatrayam adhikrityeti |
tatra vyañjanārthāviparyāsāv atra parikalpitaṃ svabhāvam adhikṛtya |
saṃyogino dvayapatitasya cārthasya kalpitatvāt |
atattvaviṣayatve 'pi saty evaṃ tadkāryam aviparyāsa eva |
aviparyāsas tathātveti |
manaskāre 'viparyāsaḥ
[Tib. 142, b] paratantrasvabhāvam adhikṛtya |
jalpamanaskāra[226]
sya prakṛtyā ālayavijñānātmakatvāt avisāre 'viparyāsaḥ pariṇiṣpannasvabhāvam adhikṛtya kuta etannirviḳalpasya jñānasya bhāvābhāvayor viśeṣavigatatvād aviparyāsapariṇiṣpattyā ca pariṇiṣpannasvabhāvasaṃgṛhītatvāt svasāmānyalakṣaṇayor aviparyāso dharmadhātāv eva tasya vikalpavīṣayātikrāntasvarūpatvāt aśuddhau śuddhau cāviparyāso dharmadhātor avasthāviśeṣe āgantuke viparyāso dharmadhātāv eva tasya hy ākāśavat prakṛtiviśuddhasya cittasaṃkleśāc cittaviśuddhyā cāgantukam etad ubhayaṃ vyavasthāpyate atrāsānunnabhāvāviparyāso dharmadhātāv eva tasyākāśavad akṛtrimatvād hānivṛddhyabhāvāt evam ete daśāviparyāsāḥ svabhāvatraye veditavyāḥ
[Daśa vajrapadāni]
ete cānantaroktā daśāviparyāsāḥ sūtrokteṣu daśasu vajrapadeṣu yathākramaṃ yojayitavyāḥ daśa vajrapadāni katamā[227]
ni sadasattāviparyāsaḥ āśrayaḥ māyopamato nirukalpatvam prakṛtiprabhasvararatvam samkleśavyavadānam ākāśopamatā ahināviciṣṭatā ceti |
tatra vajram iva vajram sarvaviparyāsabheditvaṃ taiś cābhidyatvāt anantaroktam aviparyāsajñānam |
tac ca vajram etaiḥ pratiyate avagamyata iti vajrapadāni vajravyākhyānam ity arthaḥ |
atha punar vyañjanārthamanaskārādaya eva padaśabdenocyante tato vajrasya padāny ālambanānīty arthaḥ |
atha vā durbhedatayā vajravad etāny eva padāni vajrapadānīty ucyante |
atrāviparyāsānām sutrayojayitavyārtham vajrapadopanyāsaḥ |
vajrapadānām vā daśavidhāviparyāsamukhenedam vyākhyānam iti pradarśanārtham |
tatra vyañjanānām arthasattvam asattvam ca sadasattety ato vyañjaneṣv aviparyāso vajrapadeṣu sadasattyā veditavyaḥ |
dvayaprabhāsaḥ parikalpaḥ sa cātrārthaḥ |
sa ca tathā na vidyata ity ayam atrāviparyāsa ity arthāviparyāso vajrapadeṣv aviparyāse yojayitavyaḥ |
jalpamanaskāra evātrāśraya ity ato manaskāre viparyāso vajrapadeṣv āśraye yojayitavyaḥ |
māyādyupamatayā yaś cetaso visāre viparyāsaḥ sa vajrapadeṣu māyopamatāyām yojayitavyaḥ |
dharmadhātusvalakṣaṇam eva kalpanārahitatvād avikalpanatā tena svalakṣaṇāviparyāso nirukalpatvena draṣṭavyaḥ |
dharmādhātusāmānyam prakṛtiprabhasvararatvam tena sāmānya[228]
lakṣaṇāviparyāsaḥ prakṛtiprabhāsvaratayā saṃgṛhītaḥ |
dharmadhātor aviśuddhiviśeṣo viśuddhiviśeṣaś ca saṃkleśo vyavadānaṃ cety ato 'śuddhau viśuddhau cāviparyāsaḥ saṃkleśavyavadānāntare saṃgṛhyate |
dharmmadhātoḥ saṃkleśavyavadānayor āgraṃtakatve 'viparyāso 'sāv apy ākāśopamatāyāṃ yojayitavyaḥ |
saṃkleśavyavadānāvasthayoḥ dharmmadhātoḥ kṣayavṛddhyabhāvād atrāse 'nunnatau cāviparyāso vajrapadeṣv ahināviśiṣṭatāyāṃ yojayitavyaḥ |
evam ete dṛśāviparyāsā daśasu vajrapadeṣu yathākramaṃ yojayitavyāḥ |
vajrapadānāṃ śarīravyavasthānam iti yān arthān adhikṛtya vajrapadānāṃ pravṛttis te 'rthās teṣāṃ śarīram |
teṣāṃ tu caturdhāvyavasthānaṃ svabhāvataḥ |
ālambanataḥ |
avikalpanatah |
codyaparihārataś ca |
tatra trayaḥ svabhāvāḥ pariniṣpannaparikalpitaparatantrakṛhyāḥ |
ādyais tribhiḥ padair yathākramam iti tatrādau bhavānyād yāni tāni punaḥ sadasattvāviparyāsa āśrayaś ceti |
tatra sadasattyā pariniṣpanna uktaḥ |
aviparyāsena parikalpita uktaḥ |
āśrayatvena paratantra uktaḥ |
katham iti |
pariniṣpannasya sadasattāyāḥ sadasattayā nirdeśa iti ke cit |
kathaṃ[229]
sadasallakṣaṇatvāt pariṇiṣpannasya sadasattayā 'bhidhānaṃ ity eke
saṃyogāt saṃstavāc cākṣarāṇāṃ kalpitenārthena sārthakatvaṃ paśyan viparyayāc ca nirarthakatvam pariṇiṣpannaḥ svabhāvo 'nabhilāpya ity avagamyate
atas tatsadasattā pariṇiṣpannaḥ svabhāva ity uktam ity anye
mahāyānadeśanādharmaḥ suviśuddhadharmadhātunisyandatvād viparyāsapratipakṣālambanatvāc ca pariṇiṣpannaḥ svabhāva ity ucyate
so 'rthaḥ kalpitasvabhāvasya bhavati na tu parikalpitaḥ ataḥ sadasattā pariṇiṣpanna ity uktam ity apare
anye tv āhuḥ sadasattā 'viparyastatvāt pariṇiṣpannaḥ svabhāva ukta iti
aviparyāsena parikalpitas tasyāvidyamānena dvayarūpeṇa prakhyāti(75.a)lakṣaṇāt
āśrayeṇa paratantras tasyābhidhānam hetutvād āśraya ity ucyate phalatvāt paratantra iti
etad eva svabhāvatrayam ālambanato jñeyaṃ
avikalpanata ity atra vigrahabhedad āha yena ca na vikalpayati nirvikalpena jñānena yac ca na vikalpayati prakṛtiprabhāsvaratāṃ tatrāyaṃ vighrahabhedo na [Tib. 144, b] vikalpayaty anenety avikalpanaṃ na vikalpyate tad ity avikalpanam
tadbhāvo 'vikalpanatā evam avikalpanatā dvābhyāṃ vajrapadābhyāṃ paridīpiteti darśayati
tad anena jñeyajñānavyavasthānaṃ yathākramaṃ vijñeyaṃ tad yathā tribhiḥ svabhāvair avikalpanayayā ceti
tatra tribhiḥ svabhāvair jñeyavyavasthānaṃ grāhyatvāt avikalpanayayā[230]
jñānavyavasthānaṁ grāhakatvāt |
codyaparihārata upayuktebhyaḥ ciṣṭāni vajrapadāni |
atra kiṁ cid vajrapadaṁ codyasya parihāra eva |
tad yathā māyopa mata |
ākāśopamatā |
ahināviciṣṭatā ceti |
kiṁ cit punaś codyam eva |
tad yathā saṁkleśo vyavadānam iti |
tasmāc codyapari hāraś ca codyasya parihāraś ca codyaparihārau |
asarūpaika śeṣaṁ kṛtvā |
tatra codyaṁ yady ete parikalpipaparatantralakṣa ṇā dharmā na saṁvidyante grāhyagrāhakatvenet vākyaśeṣaḥ |
katham upalabhyante grāhyagrāhakatvenetyabhiprāyaḥ |
yadi saṁvidyante dharmāṇāṁ prakṛtiprabhāsvaratā na yujyate |
grā hyagrāhakatvātmanā vidyamānatvāt |
etac codyam |
tan mā yopamatayā pariharati |
yathā māyākṛtaṁ na saṁvidyata upa labhyate ceti |
na hi māyopalabhyatayā saṁvidyate tato 'py evaṁ saṁvidyata iti jñātuṁ śakyate |
yadi prakṛtipra bhāsvaratā dharmāṇāṁ tat kathaṁ pūrvasaṁkleśaḥ paścād vyavadānam iti codyam |
na hi prakṛtiviśuddhe kadā cit saṁkliṣṭatopapadyate |
asya parihāraḥ saṁkleśavyavadānam ākāśopamatayā veditavyam |
tad yathā prakṛtiviśuddha ākāśa āgantuke(75, b)nopakleśena saṁkliśyate |
tadapagamād vyava dīyate ceti |
evaṁ dharmadhātoḥ prakṛtiviśuddhatve 'py āgan tukair upakleśaiḥ saṁkleśaḥ |
tadapagamāc ca vyavadānam iti |
yady aprameyabuddhotpāde saty aprameyāṇāṁ sattvānāṁ saṁkleśo nirākṛiyate katham anādisaṁsāre pratyekasyāpi buddhot pādasyāprameyāṇāṁ sattvānāṁ saṁsārāpagamān nirvāṇaprave śāc ca na saṁsārasamucchedo na nirvāṇavṛddhir veti codyam |[231]
asyāhīnāviśiṣṭatayā parihāraḥ aprameyatvāt sattvadhātor vya•vadānapakṣasya ceti parimāṇavato hi vastuno hāniḥ samucchedo vā prajñāyate aprameyaś ca sattvadātuḥ tasya hānir api na prajñāyate kuta eva samucchedaḥ prajñāsyate ākāśavat
vyavadānasyāpy aprameyatvād viśeṣa eva nāsti tasya kathaṁ vṛddhir bhaviṣyati ākāśat yasyāvaraṇāpaga•mād āvaraṇod bhāvāc ceti
dvitīyaṁ śarīravyavasthānam iti vajrapadānām ity adhikṛ•tam
yatra yā ca yato bhrāntir abhrāntir vā ca yatra ca
bhrāntyabhrāntiphale caiva paryantaś ca tayor iti
vajrapadair bhrāntiviṣayo bhrāntisvarūpatā bhrāntihetur bhrānti•phalaṁ bhrāntiparyantaś ca nirdiṣṭaḥ tathā'bhrāntisvarūpam abhrāntiviṣayo 'bhrāntiphalaṁ tatparyantaś ceti tatra yatra bhrāntir iti sadasattāyāṁ vyañjanānāṁ sārthakatvānirarthaka•tvalakṣaṇāyām abhidhānābhidheyayos tatsaṁbandhasya ca bhāvasvarūpeṇa parikalpitaṭvāt yā ca bhrāntir iti viparyāso bhrāntiḥ svātmany avidyamānena dvayākāreṇa prakhyānāt
yato bhrāntir ity āśrayād bhrāntir āśrayaḥ punar jalpapari•bhāvito manaskāra ālayavijñānātmakaḥ abhrāntir yā ceti mā•yādivad arthataḥ sadasattāvisāro 'bhrāntiḥ bhāvābhāvābhinive•śabhrāntipratipakṣaś cābhrāntiḥ sā yathākramaṁ māyopama•tayā 'vikalpanatā ca nirdiṣṭā yatra ceti yatra ca sā'bhrāntiḥ
sā punaḥ prakṛtiprabhāsvaratāyāṁ pariniṣpannasvabhāvalakṣa•[232]
nyāyaṃ |
bhrāntyabhrāntiphale cāiveti tatra bhrāntiphalaṃ hi |
saṃkleśaḥ |
bhrāntiniṣyanda iti vākyaśeṣaḥ |
abhrāntiniṣyandāyā abhrānter abhrāntiphalatvāt |
abhrāntiphalaṃ vyavadānaṃ sasaṃprayogam iti vākyaśeṣaḥ |
evakārakaraṇaṃ tatphale saṃkleśo vyavadānam ity anenaivoktte |
ākāśopama tayā tu paśyate dharmadhātor yasya bhrāntyabhrāntiphalaṃ tat saṃkleśavyavadānātmakaṃ |
nedaṃ tatprakṛtitvam iti pradarśanārtham |
paryantaś ca tayor iti saṃkleśavyavadānayoḥ paryanto nirvāṇam |
tac cāhinānadhikatvena paryantam uktam ity eke |
anye tv āhuḥ |
saṃsārasyāprameyatayā 'pacayābhāvād ahinatāyuktaḥ saṃkleśaparyantaḥ |
nirvāṇasyāprameyatayopacayābhāvād avṛddhiṣṭatāyukto vyavadānaparyanta iti |
etāni daśa vajrapadāny aviparyāsaprasaṅgadvāreṇoktāni |
na tu sūtritānity atas tāny antaraślokābhyāṃ saṃgṛhṇāti |
sadasattāviparyāso māyopamatvaṃ āśrayaḥ |
nirvikalpaḥ prakṛtyā ca prabhāsvarā sadaiva hi ||
saṃkleśo vyavadānaṃ ca ākāśopamatā tathā |
ahīnatvādhikatvaṃ ca daśavajrapadāni hi ||
atra ca pariniṣpannaśabdārthasaṃbandhhadarśanaṃ viparyāsaḥ |
yathā pratyavabhāsate tathā'rthānāṃ svabhāvasattādarśanaṃ viparyāsaḥ |
tadarthapravṛttikāraṇadarśanaṃ viparyāsaḥ |
arthasamāropāpavādadarśanaṃ viparyāsaḥ |
yathānāmārthasvabhāvadarśanaṃ viparyāsaḥ |
bhāvānāṃ svasāmānyalakṣaṇa[233]
yor vastutvena darśanaṁ viparyāsāḥ svastyasvastisamcārad kacmect viśuddhir aviśuddhiś ceti darśanaṁ viparyāsāḥ ekasyaivāvuddhiviśuddhidarśanaṁ viparyāsāḥ saṁkleśavyavadānaprahāṇapratilambhayor hāniviśeṣadarśanāt trāsamānapravṛttir viparyāsaḥ ity ete daśa viparyāsāḥ bodhisattvasya tadadbhāvād aviparyāsāvasthitasya yathoktesv arthesv apariṇatā vidarśanā bhāvanā veditavyā uktānudharmapratipattiḥ
anudharmapratipattyānantaram antadvayavarjane pratipattir vaktavyeti ataḥ pṛcchati antadvayavarjane pratipattiḥ katamety ata āha yā Ratnakūṭanāmadharmaparyāye(76,b)na samyakpratipatteḥ svabhāvapratipadanārthāṁ madhyamā pratipad upadiṣṭā tad yathā Kāśyapa iha mahāratnakūṭe dharmaparyāye cikṣitukāmena bodhisattvena yoniśodharmaprayuktena bhavitavyam katamaś ca yoniśodharmaprayogaḥ yad uta sarvadharmāṇāṁ bhūtapratyavekṣā yā nāmapratyavekṣā nasattvanajīvanapoṣaṇapudgalanamanujanamānavapratyavekṣā iyam ucyate Kāśyapa madhyamā pratipad iti vistareṇa yāvad dvitīyapradīpoddharaṇaparyantaḥ paṭhyate katamasyantsya varjanāt sā veditavyā katamasyantsya varjanān madhyamā pratipattir jñātavyety arthaḥ ata āha pṛthaktvaikatvam antaś ca tirthyaśrāvakayor api
iti vistaraḥ ayaṁ paṅcadaśavidho 'ntadvayabhedaḥ asya[234]
varjinīḥ sā veditavyā |
atra pṛthagādidarśanam antaḥ |
ekaikapakṣābhiniviṣṭatvāt kārye kāraṇopacāraṃ kṛtvā |
tatra rūpādibhyaḥ pṛthaktvam ātmana ity antaḥ |
ekatvam ity antaḥ |
tatra ye rūpādin skandhān āyatanāni dhātuṃś ca nityātmana upabhogavastutvena kalpayanti te rūpādibhyaḥ pṛthaktvam ātmana ity antaḥ |
ye punar ādhyātmikān saṃskārān rūpādīn ātmatvena pratipannā yaḥ paśyati śṛṇoti jānāti sa ātmeti bāhyāṃś cātmiyatvena kalpayanti te rūpādibhya ekatvam ātmana ity [Tib. 147,b] antaḥ |
yad sūtryate |
sa jīvas taccharīram anyo jīvo 'nyac charīram iti darśanam |
tad evātrāntaśabdenoktam |
tatparivārjanārtham iti rūpādibhyas tattvānyatvāntaparivarjanārtham ātmanaḥ |
madhyamā pratipad yad dharmamātraṃ viṣayabhūtaṃ jñānam |
etad eva pradarśayann āha |
yā nātmapratyavekṣā yāvan nāmanavapratyavekṣeti |
dharmamātradarśanād dhi bandhyāputravad ātmā nāstīty avagacchan nātmānaṃ pratyavekṣate nānusaratīty arthaḥ |
kuta eva tasya rūpādibhyas tattvam anyatvaṃ vā gṛhyata iti |
ātmaśabdenaiva tadarthe 'vagamyamāne (73,a) kim arthaṃ sattvādiparyāyaviśeṣābhidhānam |
na sarvasya sarvaparyāyavyutpattir ity ataḥ sarveṣāṃ tadarthāvabodhārthaṃ paryāyāntaranirdeśaḥ |
tatkālavikṣiptānāṃ vā paryāyāntareṇa tadarthaprapanārthaḥ |
tenaivābhidhānenocyamāne 'nyeṣāṃ prāpaṇāt |
rūpādayo nityam iti tīrthikāntaḥ |
te hi parikalpitarūpān vastutvenaābhiniveśya nityatvena kalpayanti nityaviparyāsopahatatvāt |
anityam iti śrāvakāntaḥ |
tair api kalpitasvabhāvānāṃ[235]
Yānānuttaryaparicchedaḥ pañcamaḥ
rūpādīnāṃ vasturūpeṇānityatvena samāropitād antatvam atyantasaṃsārodvegakarān nirvāṇasya cāntapātanād anityadarśanasyāntatvam |
tadubhayaparivarjanārtham iti nityānityadarśanaparivarjanārthaṃ madhyamā pratipat |
yā rūpādīnāṃ nānityapratyavekṣā nānityapratyavekṣeti |
tat punaḥ katamat |
nirvikalpajñānam |
yan na rūpādin rūpādiṣu eva nityatvenānityatvena cānugacchati |
rūpādīnām ity ādiśabdena vedanāsaṃjñāsaṃskāravijñānaskandhānāṃ pṛthivyaptejovayvākāśadhātūnāṃ cakṣuḥśrotraghraṇajihvākāyamanāāyatanānāṃ ca grahaṇam |
ātmāṣṭiśabdena pudgalasamāropānta iti skandhadhātvāyatanānāṃ vyatiriktam avyatiriktaṃ vā bandhyāputravad asantam ātmānaṃ dravyataḥ pacyataḥ pudgalasyātmano vasturūpasamāropaṇam antaḥ |
nairātmyam ity apavādāntaḥ |
apavādo hi sato 'sattvaparikalpa ity uktaḥ |
ātmanas tv asattvāt kathaṃ nairātmyam ity apavādānta āpadyata ity ata āha |
prajñaptisato 'py apavādāt |
nairātmyam apavādānta iti saṃbadhyate |
sa punar dharmāṇāṃ saṃtāno yasmin hetuphalasaṃbandhaḥ prajñāyate |
samudāyo vā yatra devamanuṣyaprajñaptiḥ |
tatparivarjanārthaṃ madhyamā pratipad iti |
ātmanaḥ sadasattādarśanaparivārjanārtham |
tasyāḥ punar yasyā madhyamāyāḥ pratipado nirūpaṇārtham āha |
yad ātmanairātmyayor madhyamam iti |
kiṃ punas tanmadhyaṃ nirvikalpaṃ jñānam |
grāhyārthavikapasyāntatvaṃ darśayitvā grāhakārthavika[236]
paśyāntatatvam pradarśanārtham āha bhūtam cittam ityādi |
atha vā pudgalasya samāropāpavādāntatvam darśayitvā dharmasya samāropā(73.b)pavādāntadarśanārtham āha bhūtam cittam ityādi |
yathā pratibhāsate yathā vā'bhilapyate cittam tathaiva bhūtam vidyata iti ayam dharmasamāropāntaḥ abhūtasya cittasvabhāva[sya] bhūtatvena samāropāt |
abhūtam cittam ity apavādāntaḥ tasya hi grāhyagrāhakarahitātmanā nirabhilāpyena ca vidyamānam apy abhūtam iti paśyataḥ sarvātmanā dharmasvabhāvāpavādād apavādāntaḥ |
tatparivarjanārtham iti samāropāpavādarahitasya dharmanairātmyasya prativedhārtham madhyamā pratipad |
sā yatrācittam 'mano 'vijñānam ity abhiniveśā [Tib. 149.a] na pravartanta iti vākyaśeṣaḥ |
yatra vā viṣaye na cittam sāsravayāsanābhiś citatmakam pravartate na cetanā 'bhisaṃskāralakṣaṇā na mano manyanākāram na vijñānam ca vikalpanam iyam madhyamā pratipad ity ucyate |
tatra cittam ālayavijñānam sarvasāsravadharmavāsanābhiś citatvāt cetanā manaskarma kuśalākuśalāvyākṛteṣu cittapreraṇakarmikā manaḥ kliṣṭam manaḥ vijñānam ṣaḍvijñānakāyāḥ |
akuśalādayo dharmāḥ saṃkleśa iti vipakṣāntaḥ kuśalādayas tu vyavadānam iti pratipakṣāntaḥ |
ādiśabdena sāvadyānavadyālaukikalokottarasāsravānāsravasaṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtādayo nirdeśāḥ saṃbadhyante |
tatra yathaivākuśalāḥ kuśalāś ca dharmāḥ pratibhāsante 'bhilapyante ca tathaiva saṃkleśo vyavadānam ceti darśanam vipakṣāntaḥ pratipakṣāntaś ca |
tatrākuśalā'kṣemārthatvāt sāvadyā duścaritasamutthāpakatvāt laukikā vi[237]
paryastatvāt |
sāsravā āsravānuśayitatvāt |
saṃskṛtāḥ pratyayotpāditatvāt |
tadviparītās tu kuśalādayo veditavyāḥ |
tatparivārjanārthaṃ madhyamā pratipad iti vipakṣapratipakṣayos tacchabdenābhisaṃbandhaḥ |
na madhyamā 'tra pratipaj jñāyata ity ata āha |
yo 'syāntadvayasyānupagamo 'nudāhāro 'pravyāhāra iti |
tatrānupagamo vipakṣapratipakṣayor anabhiniviśaḥ |
anudāhāras tasmin vipakṣapratipakṣānte pareṣām asamādāpanāt |
apravyāhāro vipakṣapratipakṣāntayor asaṃvarṇanāt |
tīrthikaśrāvakāntapratipakṣeṇa madhyamāṃ pratipadaṃ deśayitvā bodhisattvāntapratipakṣeṇa madhyamāṃ pratipadaṃ pratipādayann āha |
astīti śāśvatāntaḥ |
nāstīty ucchedāntaḥ |
tayor eva pudgaladharmayoḥ |
sarvadā svalakṣaṇasamāropād astīti śāśvatāntaḥ |
sarvathā svarūpāpavādān nāstīty ucchedāntaḥ |
tatparivārjanārthaṃ madhyamā pratipat |
yad anayor antayor madhyam iti |
ubhayāntavigamo 'tra madhyam abhipretam ity eke |
ubhayalakṣaṇam atra dvayor antayor madhyam ity apare |
kathaṃ kṛtvā |
yo hy abhūtaparikalpavad dharmadhatuvad vā jñeyam abhidheyaṃ ca vastu dravyataḥ paramārthataś cāstīty abhiniveśaḥ sa śāśvatāntaḥ |
tasya tathā'sattvāt |
tathā śaśaviṣāṇavan nāsty eveti darśanam ucchedāntaḥ |
kiṃ kāraṇam |
tasya prajñaptito 'stitvāt |
atas tannirākaraṇenocchedānta ucyate |
ataś cāstitvanāstitvayor madhyaṃ dravyataḥ paramārthataś ca nāsti prajñaptito 'stīty ubhayalakṣaṇaṃ bhavati |[238]
vipakṣapratipakṣayoḥ samāropāntam adhikṛtyāha avidyā grāhyā grāhakā cety antaḥ evaṃ vidyā saṃskārā asaṃskṛtaṃ ca tatpratipakṣo grāhyaṃ grāhakaṃ cety antaḥ atra ca yāvaj jarāmaraṇam iti paryantagrahaṇād vijñānānāmarūpādayo 'py ākṣipyante vijñānaṃ grāhyaṃ grāhakaṃ cety antaḥ evaṃ nāmarūpaṃ ṣaḍāyatanaṃ sparśo vedanā tṛṣṇā upādānaṃ bhavo jātir jarāmaraṇaṃ grāhyaṃ grāhakaṃ cety antaḥ
tatrāvidyā satyaratnāsaṃprakhyānātmikā puṇyāpuṇyāniñjyasvabhāvā sāsravā cetanā sā punarbhavābhisaṃskaraṇāt saṃskāraḥ evaṃ saṃkleśalakṣaṇavyākhyānānusāreṇa vijñānādayo 'pi vistareṇa vaktavyāḥ vidyā'nāsravaṃ jñānam saṃskārāṇāṃ [Tib. 150,b] jñānam evāsaṃskṛtaṃ pratipakṣaḥ ata evānyasmād asaṃskṛtād vyavacchedārtham āha tatpratipakṣa iti anye tv anutpattimattvād asaṃskṛtam aṣṭavidham apy atra [78,b] manyante pratipakṣas tv anāsravā cetanā punarbhavānabhisaṃskaraṇād iti tannirodho grāhyo grāhako vety antaḥ
tacchabdena vijñānādayo jarāmaraṇaparyavasānāḥ saṃbadhyante nirodho na vijñāyate kiṃ bhāvaḥ karaṇaṃ vety āha yena tan nāmarūpaṃ nirudhyata iti kena punas tan nāmarūpaṃ nirudhyate nāmarūpapratipakṣeṇānāsraveṇa mārgeṇa
vidyā cāvidyā cādvayam etad advaidhikāram ity etad evātra jñānaṃ saparivāraṃ mārgaḥ ataś ca nirudhyate 'neneti nirodhaḥ kaḥ punar atrāntaḥ ayam eva grāhyagrāhakābhinive[239]
śaḥ |
kiṁ kāraṇam advayaprakṛtiṣv avidyādiṣv avidyamānadvayādhyāropapravṛttatvāt |
evaṁ grāhyagrāhakānto dvidhā kṛṣṇaśuklapakṣabhedeneti tatra kṛṣṇapakṣe dvādaśabhavāṅgāni avidyā yāvaj jarāmaraṇam |
evaṁ śuklapakṣe 'py avidyādinirodhabhedena dvādaśaprakāra evāvidyānirodho yāvaj jarāmaraṇanirodha iti |
atra ca nirudhyante 'nenāvidyādaya iti tatpratipakṣabhūto marga eva nirodhaśabdenokta |
tatparivarjanārtham iti grāhyagrāhakāntaparivarjanārthaṁ madhyamā pratipad uktety abhisambadḥyate |
sā ca na vijñāyata ity ata āha vidyā cāvidyā cadvayam etad iti |
kathaṁ vidyā cāvidyā ca yāvaj jarāmaraṇaṁ tannirodhāś cadvayam ity ata āha vidyāvidyādināṁ grāhyagrāhakatvābhāvād iti |
tadabhāvas tv avidyādīnām advayaprakṛtitvāt |
atha vā vidyā'vidyeti parikalṉtasvabhāvo 'taś ca parikalṉtasvabhāvayor vidyāvidyayor abhāvasamatayā vidyā cāvidyā cadvayam iti |
evaṁ saṁskārāṇām asaṁskṛtasya ca vijñānasya tannirodhasya ca yāvaj jarāmaraṇasya tannirodhasyaceti vaktavyaḥ |
kā punar atra madhyamā pratipad etad eva jñānaṁ vidyā cāvidyā cadvayam etad advaidhikāram iti na vidyotpādayitavyā nāvidyā nirodhayitavyā |
na saṁskārādayaḥ praheyāḥ na tannirodhāḥ prāptavya iti yad evaṁ jñānam etad advaidhikāram |
trividhaḥ saṁkleśa iti tatra kleśaḥ karmajanmasaṁkleśanimittatvāt svayaṁ ca kliṣṭatvāt svapārātmanor vyābādhahetutvād vā saṁkleśā ucyante |
karmāpi kleśādhiṉatyanirvṛttatvāj janmahetutvāt svayam api ca kasya cit kliṣṭa[240]
tvāt saṃkleśa ucyate |
janmāpi karmakleśanimittatvāt sarvopadravāśrayabhūtatvāc ca saṃkleśaḥ |
punaḥ kleśasaṃkleśasya tridhabhedapradarśanārtham āha kleśasaṃkleśas trividha iti vistaraḥ |
tatra dṛṣṭayaḥ pañca satkāyadṛṣṭyādikāḥ |
rāgadveṣamohā eva nimittaṃ śokādihetutvāt punarbhavahetutvāc ca |
anye tv āhuḥ |
rāgādīnāṃ nimittaṃ tadanukulā indriyārthāḥ |
ayoniśomanaskāraḥ |
rāgādivāsanā ālayavijñānasaṃniviṣṭā |
evaṃ tu varṇayanti rāgādinimittaṃ tadābhāsa iti |
punarbhavapraṇidhānaṃ punarbhavaprārthanā |
yasya pratipakṣo jñānaśūnyateti vistaraḥ |
tatra dṛṣṭānāṃ jñānaśūnyatā pratipakṣaḥ |
śūnyatāviṣayaṃ jñānam atra jñānaśūnyatā |
rāgādinimittaprativakṣo jñānānimittaṃ sarvanimittavigamāt |
animittaviṣayaṃ vā jñānaṃ jñānānimittam |
parikalpitarāgādiśūnyatāviṣayatvāt punarbhavapraṇidhānapratipakṣo jñānāpraṇihitam |
punarbhavapraṇidhānavigataṃ punarbhavaśūnyatāviṣayaṃ vā jñānaṃ jñānāpraṇihitam |
karmasaṃkleśaḥ śubhāśubhakarmābhisaṃskāraḥ punarbhavākṣepakayoḥ śubhāśubhayoḥ karmaṇor upacayo 'bhinivṛttir abhisaṃskāraḥ |
yasya pratipakṣo jñānānabhisaṃskāraḥ |
yena jñānena karma punarbhavam asya nābhisaṃskaroti taj jñānaṃ jñānānabhisaṃskāraḥ |
tat punas tathatāviṣayaṃ |
janmasaṃkleśaḥ punarbhavajātiḥ pratisandhicittam |
jātasya cittacaittānāṃ pratikṣaṇotpādaḥ pratisandhibandhād ūrdhvam ā maraṇāt |
punarbhavaprabandhaś iti kleśa[241]
sahito maraṇabhavaḥ tasmād anantaram antarābhavaḥ tataś ca punar apy upapattibhavaḥ ārūpyasamāpattilābhinān tu maraṇabhavād evānantaram upapattibhavaḥ yasya pratipakṣo jñānajātir jñānānutpādo jñānāsvabhāvatā ca tatrājātāḥ sarvadharmāḥ parikalpitātmakatve yāj jñānaṃ śūnyatāviṣayaṃ vā taj jñānājātiḥ parikalpitadharmaśūnyatāviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ jñānānutpādaḥ tena tadanutpāda prativedhāt bandhyāputravad asattvād asvabhāvo bhāvaprabandha iti yaj jñānaṃ taj jñānāsvabhāvatā etaj jñānaśūnyatādikaṃ saptavidhajñānaṃ yathoktasya saptavidhasya dṛṣṭyādivipakṣasya pratipakṣaḥ etasyaiva trividhasya kleśakarmajanmasaṃkleśasya saptadhābhinnasyāpagamo vyavadānam apagamaḥ prahāṇam anye tu manyante tathatā mārgo nirvāṇaṃ ca vyavadānam apagacchaty asminn anenāpagatiś cety evaṃ vigrahabhādat tathatādayo vyavadānam iti tatra jñānaśūnyatādibhir iti ādiśabdāj jñānaśūnyatā jñānānimittaṃ jñānāpraṇihitaṃ jñānānabhisaṃskāro jñānajātir jñānānutpādo jñānāsvabhāvatā ca parigṛhyante jñeyaśūnyatādayo dharmā iti atrāpy ādiśabdena jñeyaśūnyatā jñeyānimittaṃ jñeyāpraṇihitaṃ jñeyānabhisaṃskāro jñeyājātir jñeyānutpādo jñeyāsvabhāvatā ca pariniṣpannalakṣaṇāḥ parigṛhyante etena trividhena saṃkleśena yathāyogaṃ yāvan na śūnyatādayaḥ kriyanta iti anantaroktena kleśakarmajanmasaṃkleśena pṛthagbhūtā jñeyaśūnyatādayo dharmā jñānaśūnyatādibhir na kriyante kiṃ kāraṇaṃ na kriyante yasmāt te prakṛtyaiva śūnyatādaya iti prakṛtyaivādṛṣṭyādisvabhāvā ity arthaḥ kuta[242]
etad ata āha dharmadhātoḥ prakṛtyasaṃkliṣṭatvāt |
ato jñeyaśūnyatādayo dharmāḥ prakṛtyaiva śūnyatādaya iti jñānaśūnyatādibhir na kriyante |
yathāyogam ity ayam atra yathāyogārthaḥ jñānaśūnyatayā jñeyaśūnyatā dṛṣṭisaṃkleśena vimuktā virahitā na kriyante |
dṛṣṭiśūnyatāyāḥ svayam evādṛṣṭisvabhāvatvāt |
evaṃ jñānānimittena jñeyānimittaṃ rāgādinimittair virahitaṃ na kriyate |
rāgādinimittaiḥ śūnyatāyāḥ svayam evānimittatvāt |
jñānāpraṇihitena jñeyāpraṇihitaṃ na kriyate |
praṇidhānaśūnyatāyāḥ prakṛtyaivāpraṇidhānalakṣaṇatvāt |
jñānānabhisaṃskāreṇa jñeyānabhisaṃskāro na kriyate |
abhisaṃskāraśūnyatāyāḥ prakṛtyaivānabhisaṃskāralakṣaṇatvāt |
jñānājātyā jñeyājātir na kriyate |
jātiśūnyatāyāḥ prakṛtyaivājātilakṣaṇatvāt |
jñānānutpādena jñeyānutpādo na kriyate |
cittacaittapratikṣaṇotpādaśūnyatāyāḥ prakṛtyaivānutpādalakṣaṇatvāt |
jñānāsvabhāvatayā jñeyāsvabhāvatā na kriyate |
[Tib. 153,b] punarbhavaśūnyatāyāḥ prakṛtyaivāsvabhāvatvāt |
atha vā trividhena saṃkleśena yathāyogam iti vistaraḥ kleśakarmajanmasaṃkleśena saptadhābhinnena jñeyaśūnyatādīnāṃ dharmāṇāṃ yasya dharmasya yena saṃkleśabhedena yogaḥ sa dharmo na jñānaśūnyatādibhiḥ kriyate kiṃ tarhy adhigamyate |
tatra dṛṣṭisaṃkleśena jñānaśūnyatāyogaḥ |
evaṃ yāvat punarbhavaprabandhena jñānāsvabhāvatāyogaḥ |
kiṃ kāraṇaṃ na kriyate |
yasmāt te prakṛtyaiva śūnyatādayo dharmā dharmadhātoḥ prakṛtyaivāsaṃkliṣṭatvād iti |
tatra dṛṣṭayo na jñānaśūnyatayā śūnyāḥ kriyante |
tāsāṃ prakṛtyaiva śūnyatālakṣaṇatvāt |
rāgādinimittaṃ jñānānimittena nānimittaṃ kriyate |
dharmadhātoḥ prakṛtyaivānimittatvāt |
evaṃ yāvat punarbhavaprabandho[243]
jñānāsvabhāvatayā nāsvabhāvaḥ kriyate yato 'nutpanne 'pi pratipakṣe parikalpaḥ svabhāvo 'sann eva |
yataś caitad evam ato yadi dharmadhātuḥ saṁkliśyate vā viśuddhyate veti kalpayaty ayam antaḥ kiṁ kāraṇam ity ato bravīti prakṛtyasaṁkliṣṭasya saṁkleśaśuddhyabhāvād ākāśavad iti etasyāntasya parivārjanārthaṁ madhyamā pratipad yan na śūnyatāyā vidyātmikāyā dharmān jñeyaśūnyatādin pariṇiṣpannasvabhāvātmakāṁ tadvipakṣeṇa paratantrasvabhāvena dṛṣṭyādikena śūnyān virahitān karoti api tv anutpannāyām api vidyāyāṁ parikalpitena svabhāvena dṛṣṭisvarūpeṇa dharmā eva śūnyāḥ evaṁ yan nānimittena dharmān animittan karoti dharmā evānimittāḥ yāvad yan nāsvabhāvatayā dharmān asvabhāvān karoti dharmā evāsvabhāvā iti yāivaṁ pratyavekṣā iyam ucyate Kāśyapa dharmāṇāṁ bhūtapratyavekṣeti evaṁ vidyāstitvaṁ tadvipakṣāstitvaṁ ca parikalpitasvabhāvavigatam paridīpayann adhyāropāpavādāntapratikṣepaḥ kṛto bhavati
yo 'yam anukrānto vikalpaprabhēdas tasmād ayam aparaḥ saptavidho vikalpadvayāntaḥ vikalpaprabhedapradarśanārtham āha tad yathā bhāve 'pi vikalpo 'ntaḥ abhāve 'pi vikalpo 'nta iti katham ity ato braviti pudgalo 'sti yasya vināśāya śūnyatā nairātmyam api vā nāstiti vikalpanāt yadi śunyatayā dharmān prakṛtiśūnyatvān na śūnyān karoti tena tarhi pudgalo 'sti yasya vināśāya śūnyatā nairātmyam[244]
api vā nāsti pudgalābhāvāt |
na hi pratipakṣo nirvipakṣo 'sti |
tasmād avaśyaṃ pudgalāstitvaṃ vā nairātmyābhāvo vā pratipattavya iti |
tadbhāvābhāve vikalpo 'ntaḥ |
etasya bhāvābhāvavikalpadyayāntasya parivarjanārtham iyaṃ madhyamā pratipat |
na khalu punaḥ Kāśyapa pudgalavinaśāya śūnyatā |
api tu śūnyataiva śūnyeti vistareṇa sūtrapāṭhaḥ |
tatra na saṃkliṣṭapudgalavinaśāya na vyavadānapudgalotpādāya śūnyatā |
senādivat prajñaptisattvāt pudgalasyotpādvinaśābhāvād api tu śūnyataiva śūnyā na tu pudgalena satkāyadṛṣṭisamāropitena tasyātyantam asattvāt |
evaṃ ca kṛtvā pudgaladharmātmanā na bhāvaḥ śūnyatā tadrahitatvāt |
pariniṣpannena nābhāvaḥ |
tac ca śūnyatāyāḥ pariniṣpannātmakatvaṃ pratipādayann āha |
pūrvāntaśūnyā |
aparāntaśūnyā |
pratyutpannaśūnyeti |
kālatraye 'pi tathaivastitvapratipādanāt |
atha vā pṛthagjanāṇaikṣaṇaikṣāvasthāsu tathaivastitvapratipādanāt |
tatra pṛthagjanāvasthā pūrvāntaḥ |
aṇaikṣāvasthāparāntaḥ |
ṇaikṣāvasthā pratyutpanna iti |
yasmāc caivaṃ bhāvābhāvasyabhāvaśūnyatā tasmād [Tib. 155,a] āha |
śūnyataṃ Kāśyapa pratisarateti nirvikalpajñānaviṣayatvāt |
na pudgalam ity abhidhānamātratvād iti |
ṇamye 'pi vikalpo 'ntaḥ ṇamane 'pi vikalpo 'nta ity anu[245]
vartate |
katham ity ata āha |
praheyaprahāṇakalpanayā śūnyatāyās trasanād iti |
tatra cāmyaṃ praheyaṃ praśamanaṃ prahāṇam |
dharmān praheyatvena śūnyatāṃ ca prahāṇatvena kalpayitvā śūnyatā(81,a)yām uttrāso jāyate |
kathaṃ trividho hi saṃkleśaḥ praheyaḥ sa yadi prakṛtiśūnyaḥ |
tataḥ pudgalavan na praheyaḥ syāt |
tataś ca praheyābhāvāt prahāṇābhāva iti śūnyatāyām uttrāso jāyate |
ayam apy anta eva |
kiṃ kāraṇam |
rūpavedanādayo hi dharmā yathāiva vijñānātmani viparivartante tathāiva te dravyataḥ santīty abhiniviṣṭāḥ |
teṣāṃ parikalpitasvarūpāṇāṃ rūpādīnām ātmavat prakṛtyaivāsattvam ajñānāc chūnyatāyā bibheti |
tasmād etasya vikalpadvayantsya parivarjanārtham ākāśadṛṣṭāntaḥ |
tad yathā Kāśyapa kaś cid eva puruṣa ākāśajo bhītaḥ soras tāḍaṃ kranded evaṃ ca vaded apanayaitad ākāśam iti |
tat kiṃ manyase Kāśyapa śakyam ākāśam apanetum |
āha |
no hidaṃ bhagavan |
bhagavān āha |
[Tib.155,b] evam eva Kāśyapa ete śramaṇabrāhmaṇāḥ śūnyatāyām uttrasanti tān aham mahāvikṣepaprāptān iti vadāmi |
tat kasmād dhetoḥ |
śūnyatāyām eva hi te Kāśyapa vicaranti tasyā eva bibhyantīti |
etad uktam bhavati |
yathākāśaṃ sarvarūpānugatatvād apanetum aśakyam evaṃ sarvadharmeṣu sarvakālaṃ śūnyatā vyavasthitety apanetuṃ ne śakyate |
na ca dharme 'prahāṇe śūnyatāvyavasthitād apanetuṃ śakyate na ca dharmaprahāṇena śūnyatā vyavasthā[246]
payitum ataś cāyam asthānasantrāsa iti darśayati |
trāsye 'pi kalpo 'ntaḥ |
tataś ca trāsyād bhaye 'pi |
kiṁ vikalpo 'nta ity adhikṛtam |
yatas trāsaḥ sambhavati tat trāsyam ucyate |
bhīyate 'smād iti bhayam |
tasmād yad aniṣṭaphalam |
tasmins trāsye tasminś ca bhaye yo vikalpayati so 'ntaḥ |
katham ity ato bravīti |
parikalpitarūpāditrasanād duḥkhabhirutayeti |
bālā hi parikalpitarūpādin vastutas kalpayitvā tebhyo bhayam aniṣṭaṁ phalaṁ ca kalpayanti |
tataś ca duḥkhabhirutayā tebhyo rūpādibhyas tannimittebhyaś ca bhayāt trasantīti |
etasya vikalpadvayāntasya parivarjanārthaṁ citrakaradṛṣṭāntaḥ |
tad yathā [Tib. 156,a] Kāśyapa citrakaraḥ svayam eva bhīṣanaṁ yakṣarūpaṁ kṛtvā tasmād eva bhītasrasto 'dhomukho nipatya saṁmohaṁ nigaccheta |
evam eva Kāśyapa sarvabālapṛthagjanaḥ (81,b) svayam eva rūpaśabdagandharasasparśān kṛtvā tebhyaḥ saṁsāre bhrāmyanti na ca tān dharmān yathābhūtaṁ prajānantīti |
yathā ca citrakaraḥ svayaṁkṛte bhīṣaṇake yakṣarūpe mithyāvikalpena bhūtayakṣabhāvam adhyāropya tasmād bhītaḥ saṁmoham āpadyate |
evaṁ sarva eva bālā mithyāvikalpaprasṛtāḥ svayaṁ kṛtair iti vikalpitaiḥ saṁsāre bhrāmyante kṣaṇāvasthā nikāyasabhāgānavasthitatvāt |
pūrvako dṛṣṭāntaḥ śrāvakān adhikṛtyāyan tu bodhisattvān iti śrāvako hi śūnyatāyām akṛtaparicayatvāc chūnyatāyas trasyati |[247]
bodhisattvas tu parikalpitadharmavikaḷpena saṃkliśyate vikalpakleśatvād ity eke |
nānayor arthaviseṣo 'sty api tu vargadvayavacad dvayābhidhānam ity anye |
śrāvakaḥ śūnyatātrāsād adhigantavyaṃ nādhigacchati |
bodhisattvaḥ punar vikalpitadharmatrāsād ity apare |
pūrvaka ātmagrāhapratipakṣeṇa ayaṃ punar dharmagrāhapratipakṣeṇeti ke cit |
grāhye 'pi vikalpo 'nto grāhake'pi vikalpo 'nta ity adhikṛtam |
katham ete grāhyā rūpādayas tadgrāhakāś ca vijñānakāyāḥ svalakṣaṇataḥ kāryataś ca gṛhyamāṇā ātmādivad adhyāropitātmāno bhaviṣyantīty evaṃ grāhyagrāhakayor vastutaḥ parikalpo 'nto 'bhipretaḥ |
etasya vikalpadvayāntasya parivarjanārthaṃ māyākāradṛṣṭāntaḥ |
tad yathā Kāśyapa māyākāraḥ puruṣo māyākṛtan nirmimīte atha samayā nirmitas tam eva māyākāraṃ khādeta evam eva Kāśyapa yogācāro bhikṣur yad yad evālambanam manaskaroti tat sarvam asya riktakam eva khyātīti vistaraḥ |
katham anenaivaṃ vidhaṃ vikalpadvayāntaṃ parivarjayatīti ata āha vijñaptimātrajñānakṛtaṃ hy arthābhāvajñānam tac cārthābhāvajñānaṃ tad eva vijñaptimātrajñānaṃ nivartayati arthābhāve vijñaptyasambhavād ity etad atra sādharmyam iti māyākārasthānīyaṃ vijñaptimātratajñānam |
nirmitayakṣasthānīyam arthābhāvajñānam vijñaptimātrajñānaprabhāvitatvād arthābhāvajñānasya |
yathā nirmito nirmātāraṃ bhakṣayed evam arthābhāvajñānaṃ tad eva vijñaptimātrajñānaṃ bhakṣayati |[248]
asaty arthe vijñaptimātrasyāsambhavād arthopalabdhir vijñaptir iti kṛtvā |
samyaktve 'pi vikalpo 'nto mithyātve 'pi vikalpo 'nta ity adhikṛtam |
nirvedhabhāgīyāvasthāyāṃ bhūtapratyavekṣāṃ samyaktvena vā vikalpayato mithyātvena vā 'ntadvayam |
kathaṃ samyaktvaṃ anāsravā dharmā mithyātvaṃ sāsravāḥ |
tatra yadi bhūtapratyavekṣāṃ samyaktvenāivādhyavasyaty evaṃ kuśalasāsravā na bhūtapratyavekṣā syāt |
atha punar asamyaktvalakṣaṇam eva |
evam api mithyātvam evopapadyate traidhātukasaṃyuktakuśalavad iti |
etasyāntadvayasya parivartanārthaṃ kāṣṭhadvayāgnidṛṣṭāntaḥ |
tad yathā Kāśyapa kāṣṭhadvayaṃ pratityāgnir jāyate 'gnijātaś ca samānas tad eva kāṣṭhadvayaṃ dahati |
evam eva Kāśyapa bhūtapratyavekṣāṃ pratītyāryaṃ prajñendriyaṃ jāyate jātaṃ ca tām eva bhūtapratyavekṣāṃ dahatīti |
ayam atrārtho yathā kāṣṭhadvayād anagnilakṣaṇād agnir jāyate |
jātaś ca kāṣṭhadvayaṃ dahatīti |
evam evāsamyaktvalakṣaṇāyā bhūtapratyavekṣāyāḥ samyaktvalakṣaṇam āryaṃ prajñendriyaṃ jāyate |
jātaṃ ca tām eva bhūtapratyavekṣāṃ dahati |
vibhāvayantīty arthaḥ |
[Tib. 157,b][249]
sā hi vibhāvanād eva dagdheva dagdhety ucyate vikṛtidarśanāt |
iyaṁ ca bhūtapratyavekṣā sāsravāpi satī samyaktvānukūlyād bhūtapratyavekṣety ucyata iti |
vyāpṛtāv api vikalpo 'ntaḥ avyāpṛtāv api vikalpo 'nta ity adhikṛtam katham ity ata āha jñānasya buddhipūrvaṁ kriyāṁ niḥsāmarthyaṁ vā kalpayata iti |
yadi jñānam idam ajñānaṁ prajahāmity avadhārya tatprahāṇāya pravartata ity ayaṁ vyāpṛtau vikalpo 'ntaḥ |
jñānasya kṣaṇikatvān na prahāṇavyāpārakāle saṁbhavaḥ ato 'jñānaprahāṇasya niḥsāmarthyam ity ayam avyāpṛtau vikalpo 'ntaḥ |
etasya vikalpadvayāntasya parivarjanārthaṁ tailapradyotadṛṣṭāntaḥ tadyathā Kāśyapa tailapradyote kṛte tamo 'ndhakāraṁ vigacchati na khalu punaḥ Kāśyapa tailapradyotasyaivaṁ bhavaty ahaṁ sarvaṁ andhakāraṁ vidhamāmīti api tu khalu tailapradyotaṁ pratitya tamo 'ndhakāraṁ vigacchatīti evam eva Kāśyapa jñāna utpanne 'jñānaṁ vigacchati na khalu punaḥ Kāśyapa jñānasyaivaṁ bhavaty ahaṁ ajñānaṁ vidhamāmīti api tu khalu jñānaṁ pratityājñānaṁ vigacchatīti etad uktaṁ bhavati yathā pradīpo vyāpāraśūnyas tamo 'ndhakāravyudāsabhīprayarahitaś ca punar yasmin pradeśe kriyate tasmāt pradeśāt pradīpasattāmātreṇa sarvatamo 'ndhakāraṁ vigacchatīti |
evam eva yasmin saṁtāne nirvikalpaṁ [Tib. 158, a] jñānam utpadyate tatsattāmātrasadbhāvād eva tasmāt saṁtānād ajñānaṁ vigacchati jñānasya nirvikalpatvāt kriyā'bhāvārtheti evaṁ ca jñānasya vyāpārasāmarthyābhāvāt pradīpa[250]
vat sattāmātre.ṇa vipakṣanirākaraṇād iti |
ajanmatve 'pi vikalpo 'ntaḥ samākālatve 'pi vikalpo 'nta ity anuvartate |
katham ity ata āha |
yadi pratipakṣasyānutpattiṁ vā kalpayati yatheyatā kālena notpannapūrvaḥ pratipakṣas tathedānīm api notpatsyata iti |
ayam ajanmatve vikalpo 'ntaḥ |
saṁkleśasya vā dīrghakālatvam iti na hy ajñāne jñānapratiyoginy avasthita eva jñānam utpattuṁ samartham |
athāpi kathaṁ cid utpadyeta tathāpi cirakālavirūdhatvād ajñānasya na jñānena tatkṣaṇikena vyudāsaḥ śakyate kartum |
tataś ca saṁkleśavyavadānayoḥ samānakālatvam iti |
etasya vikalpadvayāntasya parivarjanārthaṁ dvitīyas tailapradyotadṛṣṭānta iti |
tadyathā Kāśyapa gṛhe vā layane vāvacarake vā varṣasahasrasyātyayān na kena cit tailapradyotaḥ kṛto bhavati |
atha tatra kaś cid eva puruṣas tailapradyotaṁ kuryāt |
tat kiṁ manyase Kāśyapāpi tu tamo 'ndhakārasyaivaṁ bhaved varṣasahasrasaṁcito 'haṁ nāham ato vigamiṣyāmīti |
āha |
no bhagavan na hi tamo 'ndhakārasya śaktir asti tailapradyote kṛte na vigantum avaśyaṁ tena vigantavyam |
bhagavān āha |
evam eva Kāśyapa kalpaśatasahasrasaṁcito 'pi karmakleśa ekena yoniśomanaskārapratyavekṣayā vigacchati |
tailapradyota iti Kāśyapa āryasyaitat prajñendriyasyādhivacanam |
tamo 'ndhakāra iti Kāśyapa tat karmakleśasyādhivacanam iti |
nidarśane(83, a)na pratipakṣasyānutpattiṁ pariharati pratyayapratibanddhatvāt tadutpatteḥ saṁkleśatulyakālatāṁ ca |
kṛṣṇapakṣasya durbalatvaād balavattvaāc ca tatprati[251]
pakṣasya tamo 'ndhakārapradīpavad iti |
uktāntadvayavarjane pratipattīḥ ||
antadvayavarjanapratipatter anantaraṁ viśiṣṭā cāviśiṣṭā ca pratipattir uddiṣṭety ato 'ntadvayavarjanapratipattinirdeśānantaraṁ pṛcchati viśiṣṭā cāviśiṣṭā ca pratipattīḥ katameti |
ata āha
viśiṣṭā cāviśiṣṭā ca jñeyā daśasu bhūmiṣu ||
tāsu daśasu bhūmiṣv ekaikaśo dviprakārāpi pratipattīḥ |
katham ity ata āha |
yasyāṁ bhūmau yā pāramitā'tiriktakarā bhavatīti vistaraḥ |
yasyāṁ bodhisattvabhūmau vyavasthito bodhisattvo yasyāṁ pāramitāyām ādhikyena prayujyate sā tasyāṁ bodhisattvabhūmāv itarābhyaḥ pāramitābhyo 'tiriktakarā bhavatīti viśiṣṭocyate |
tad yathā prathamāyāṁ bhūmau dānapāramitā'tiriktakarā bhavatīti |
tatra pratipattir viśiṣṭocyate |
evaṁ yāvad daśamyāṁ jñānapāramiteti |
śeṣāsu sarvāsu pāramitāsu tasyāṁ bhūmau samudāgacchatīti tāsu pratipattir aviśiṣṭocyate |
yathoktaṁ pramuditāyāṁ bhūmau dānapāramitā 'tiriktakarā bhavatīti |
na ca śeṣāsu na samudāgacchati yathābalaṁ yathājñānaṁ iti |
evaṁ śeṣāsu bhūmiṣu yathāsūtraṁ nirdeśo veditavyaḥ |
uktaṁ pratipattyānuttaryam ||[252]

dvitīyaṃ tathatā dharmadhātuparāyatvāt |
tṛtīyacaturthe iti tatra tṛtīyaṃ sādhyālambanaṃ |
caturthaṃ sādhanālambanaṃ |
te eveti prathamadvitīya eva |

dvitīyaṃ sādhanālambanaṃ |
tatraiva kāraṇam āha |
dharmadhātuprativedhena pāramitādidharmādhigamād iti |
dharmadhātuprativedhād[253]
anāsravāḥ pāramitā adhigacchantīty arīhāḥ sādhyālambanam prathamaṃ sādhanālambanam dvitīyam iti pañcamaṃ dhāraṇālambanaṃ
tac chrutamayasya jñānasya śrutamātreṇa pāramitādin dharmān dharmadhātuṃ ca dhārayatīti śrutamayaṃ jñānaṃ dhāraṇety ucyate
dhāraṇāyā ālambanam dhāraṇālambanaṃ tat punaḥ prathamam eva dvayaṃ vyavasthānālambanam dharmadhātvālambanam ca
ṣaṣṭaṃ cintāmayasyāvagamya dhāranād iti apekṣākāryakāraṇopapattisādhanadharmātāyuktibhir avagamya śrutārthadhāraṇāt
avadhāraṇā cintāmayaṃ jñānaṃ tasyālambanaṃ avadhāraṇālambanam saptamaṃ bhāvanāmayasyālambanam iti prakṛtam
pratyātmaṃ dhāraṇād iti pratyātmam arthasya dhāraṇāt pradhāraṇā
prathamabhūmipraveśaprāyogikaṃ bhāvanāmayaṃ jñānam tat punaḥ smṛtyupasthānāny ārabhya yāvad balāni tasyālambanam pradhāraṇālambanaṃ
aṣṭamaṃ prathamāyāṃ bhūmau darśanamārgasyeti pratyātmam advayasya dharmadhātoḥ sarvatragatvaprativedhaḥ
atha vā darśanaheyānāṃ kleśānām akliṣṭasya cājñānasya sarvatrāgārthaviṣayasya pratyanīkavedhaḥ prativedhaḥ
sa punar darśanamārgaḥ tasyālambanaṃ prativedhalambanaṃ
navamaṃ bhāvanāmārgasya yāvat saptamyāṃ bhūmāv iti tasyaiva dharmadhātuprativedasyābhyāsād uttarottaraprakarṣaṃ gatiḥ pratānatā
bhāvanāmārgo yāvat saptamyāṃ bhūmau tasya bhāvanāmārgasyālambanaṃ pratānatālambanaṃ
daśamaṃ tatraiva laukikalokottarasya mārgasyālambanam iti prakṛtam
prakāraśo dharmādhigamād iti tasyām eva bhūmau sarvasūtrādidharmanimitttaprakārādyabhiniveśavigamamukhena prakāreṇa dharmādhigamāt pragama[254]
iti |
sa eva bhāvanāmārga ucyate saptamyām eva bhūmau |
pragamasyālambanam pragamālambanaṃ |
ekādaśam aṣṭamyāṃ bhūmau |
aṣṭamyāṃ hi bhūmau sarvanimitābhogamanasikāravigatasya svarasena pracaṭhavahanāt sa eva bhāvanāmārgaḥ pracaṭhasyālambanam pracaṭhālambanaṃ |
dvādaśaṃ navamyādibhūmitraye |
navamyāṃ pratisaṃvijñānaprakarṣalābhāt |
daśamyām karmavacitāprakarṣalābhāt |
buddhabhūmāv aśeṣakleśajñeyāvaraṇaviśuddhiprakarṣalābhāt |
evaṃ navamyādibhūmitraye jñānakarmavicuddhiprakarṣagamanāt tadbhūmivyavasthitasya yad ālambanam tat prakarṣālambanam ucyate |
anyaḥ punar āha |
mārgaprakarṣasyaiva mṛdumadhyādhimātrabhedaṃ kṛtvā prakarṣālambanaṃ tisṛṣu bhūmiṣūktam iti |
tad eva hi prathamadvayaṃ tasyāṃ tasyām avasthāyāṃ tat tad ālambanānāma pratilabhata iti |
etac cālambanam yad uta dharmavyavasthānālambanam dharmadhātvālambanaṃ ca |
tatraitad ālambanadvayaṃ tasyāṃ tasyāṃ jñānāvasthāyāṃ tat tan nāma labhate |

tena ca dhāraṇājñānaṃ dhāraṇety ucyate |

navamyāṃ daśamyāṃ buddhabhūmau ca prakarṣaprāptaṃ lokottaraṃ jñānaṃ tatpṛṣṭhalabdhaṃ ca laukikam |

vyāpiprādeśikodṛiktaṁ samaṁ cādaparityajaḥ |
āvaraṇaṁ dvayasyoktam
dvayor hy āvaraṇaṁ bodhisattvagottrāṇāṁ śrāvakādigottrāṇāṁ cāvaraṇam |
āvaraṇīyārthabhedena āvaraṇasya bhinnatvaṁ pradarśanārtham eṣa śloka ārabdhaḥ |
tatra vyāpi bodhisattvagottrāṇāṁ kleśajñeyāvaraṇam iti vyāpnotiti vyāpi sakalārtha āvaraṇatvāt |
sakalārtho hi svā[Tib. 51, b]rthaḥ parārthaś ca |
atha vā dvividhasya kleśajñeyāvaraṇasyopacāreṇa bodhisattvāvaraṇaṁ vyāpnotiti vyāpy ucyate |
kleśa evāvaraṇam ity āvaraṇaṁ kleśatvadharmasyaiva sārūpyād ihopakleśo 'pi gṛhyate[265]
kleśaśabde anyathā hy
navadhā kleśalakṣaṇam
iti īrṣyāmātsarye na gṛhyetām tayor upakleśatvāt jñeya āvaraṇam iti jñeyāvaraṇam tena jñeyaprāvaraṇāj jñānaviṣayo na bhavati jñeye vā jñānāvaraṇam jñeye jñānotpattipratibandhākatvāt
jñeyāvaraṇamadhyamapadalopaṁ kṛtvā tāilakuṇḍam iveti tat punar akliṣṭam ajñānam bodhisattvagotṛāṇāṁ svaparārthaprakarṣalābhaṁ buddhatvaṁ prāpaṇāya puṇyajñānasambhāraṁ sarvathā sarvatrānuṣṭhamānānāṁ tad dvayam api pratibandhakabhūtam ity atas tad āvaraṇaṁ vyavasthāpyate bodhisattvagotṛāṇām
tatra vyāpi sarvaviṣayaṁ sakalaṁ tu sarvathā iti kathaṁ sakalena vyāpi jñāpayatiti cet sākalyatvād vyāpyāvaraṇam ākhyāyate hetunirdeśeneti atha vā vyāpiśabdo niravaśeṣārtha iti pradarśanāya sākalyatvād iti vacanam
prādeśikaṁ tu śrāvakādigotṛāṇāṁ kleśāvaraṇam iti svārthamātra āvaraṇād āvaraṇopacāro 'vyāpitvāc ca prādeśikākhyā kiṁ śrāvakapratyekabuddhānām akliṣṭam ajñānaṁ nāsti
yasmāt teṣāṁ kleśāvaraṇam eva vyavasthāpyata iti bhavatu na tu tat āvaraṇaṁ teṣām tasya vidyamānasya śrāvakapratyekabuddhabodhiprāpteḥ nanv ajñānaṁ duḥkhādisatyaviṣaye jñānotpattipratibandhākatvam eva śrāvakādināṁ
tatas teṣām api dvayāvaraṇaṁ bhavet tasmān na vaktavyam śrāvakādināṁ hi kleśāvaraṇam eveti naitat evam avidyā hi kliṣṭād vidyāvipakṣabhūtāt saṁsāramūlatvāc ca kleśāvaraṇam eva vyavasthāpyate na tu jñeyāvaraṇaṁ saṁśaya iva
yathā vimatir hi duḥkhasatyādiviṣaye vicikitsā kleśaś cocyate na tu viṣayāntare viṣayāntare hi saṁśaya eva na kleśo na ca vicikitsā evaṁ duḥkhasatyādiviṣaya evājñānam avidyā kleśaś cocyate na tu viṣayāntare viṣayāntare hy ajñānam[266]
eva na cāvidyā naiva kleśaḥ |
tasmāt tasya jñeyajñānamātrapravṛttāv āvaraṇatvaj jñeyāvaraṇam ucyate na hi kleśakarmajjanmāny apy utpadyanta avidyāyā iva |
yady evaṃ sati bodhisattvānām āvaraṇatvaṃ na vyavasthāpyata iti |
naitad evaṃ |
bodhisattvānāṃ hi cṛtasambhāraprabhāvitatvāt teṣām āvaraṇaṃ jñeyāvaraṇam |
etad uktam
anārabdheṣu pañcasu [Tib. 52, b] vidyāsthāneṣu āryo 'py agraḥ sarvajñatvaṃ na bhavati |
tato 'nyeṣāṃ nigrahānugrahārthaṃ svayaṃ vā jñānārthaṃ sa tatrārabhate ||
iti |
udriktaṃ tu mṛdv api rāgādivastu teṣāṃ bodhisattvagotracṛāvakādigotrāṇām adhimātram abhikṣṇaṃ ca pravartanam |
rāgādicaritānām āvaraṇam iti yeṣāṃ mande 'pi viṣaye rāgādīnām anyatamad adhimātram abhikṣṇaṃ ca pravartate te rāgādicaritāḥ |
samānaṃ punas taddvayor bodhisattvagotrasya cṛāvakādigotrasya cāvaraṇam samānaṃ hy ubhayaprakārasyāpy abhāvāt |
samabhāgacaritānām āvaraṇam iti yeṣāṃ viṣayasādṛśye kleśa utpadyate na tv abhikṣṇaṃ te samabhāgacaritāḥ |
saṃsārādānaprahāṇaṃ cāvaraṇam ityādi bodhisattvo hi kṛpādhipatyataḥ saṃsāram upādatte prajñādhipatyataś ca saṃsāradoṣaṃ yathābhūtaṃ dṛṣṭvā tyajati |
tataḥ kleśābhāve sattvālocanāj janmaparigṛaho 'pratiṣṭhitaṃ nirvāṇam saṃsāra[267]
nirvāṇadvayoḥ apratiṣṭhitāt |
tadāvaraṇaṁ tu kleśena yathābhibhūtaṁ saṁsāropādānaṁ yathābhūtadoṣādarśanāc cākṛpayā saṁsāraparityajanam |
ajñānam ihāvaraṇam ucyate kṛpāprajñāyoḥ pratibandhakatvād ity anye |
atha vā yathoktaṁ
Mahāratnakūṭe tasyāśayaś nirvāṇe ca tiṣṭhati saṁsāre ca prayogena tiṣṭhatīti |
etad dhi bodhisattvānām apratiṣṭhitaṁ nirvāṇam |
tatra nirvāṇapṛṣṭhibhavata āśayaprayogābhyāṁ saṁsāram upādatte |
agotrasattva iva |
tathā ca saṁsārapṛṣṭhibhavato nirvāṇagatābhyām āśayaprayogābhyāṁ saṁsāraṁ tyajati yathā śrāvakādigotrāḥ |
evaṁ saṁsārādānaṁ tatparityajanaṁ ca bodhisattvānām apratiṣṭhitanirvāṇa āvaraṇam |
saṁsāranirvāṇayor ekāntapatitaṭvāt |
bodhisattvās tu tathāgataprāptikāle kleśajñeyāvaraṇaprahāṇāt saṁsāre na tiṣṭhanti nirupadhiśeṣanirvāṇe ca dharmakāyaprabandhān na tiṣṭhanti nirvāṇe śrāvakādivat |
tataḥ saṁsāranirvāṇayor ubhayatrāpi na pratiṣṭhantiti |
bodhisattvasyātra śrāvakādīnāṁ ca mārge taddvayasya prayoge phale 'pratiṣṭhitanirvāṇe ca pratibandhakaraṇād etāni pañcavidhāny āvaraṇāni saṁkhyātāni |
evam etāni yathāyogam iti yathāsambhavam |
tatra prathamam antaṁ ca bodhisattvagotrāṇām āvaraṇam |
prathamasya tu paraṁ śrāvakādigotrāṇām
āvaraṇam |
itaradvayaṁ punar ubhayasyāvaraṇam |
tatra ca jñeyāvaraṇam bodhisattvānām āvaraṇam uktam |
tac cākliṣṭam ajñānam iti jñāyate ||
[2] Prayoganāvasaṁyojanāvaraṇam |
dvayāvaraṇaṁ kleśāvaraṇaṁ katividhaṁ kiṁ svarūpaṁ kasya ca prativibandho na jñāyata |
ity ata āha |
navadhā kleśalakṣaṇam ||[268]
iti āvaraṇam iti sambadhyate |
kleśalakṣaṇaṃ kleśasvarūpam |
etan navavidhaṃ katham iti |
ata āha |
saṃyojanāny [āvaraṇam] |
iti |
nānāduhkhe saṃyojanāt saṃyojanāni |
nānāduḥkhaṃ punaḥ kāmarūpārūpyaparyāpannacarirā duḥkhaduḥkhatā vipariṇāmaduḥkhatā saṃskāraduḥkhatā ca |
yathāyogaṃ traidhātukāni [iti] sambadhyante |
tāni cānunayasaṃyojanaṃ yāvan mātsaryasaṃyojanam |
tatra dveṣaḥ pratighaḥ |
īrṣyā anyasamṛddher asahyatā |
mātsaryaṃ tv āgraha upakaraṇasya |
tattrayaṃ ca kāmaparyāpannam |
anunayasaṃyojanaṃ trāidhātukarāgaḥ |
evaṃ mānāvidyādṛṣṭiparāmarśavicikitsāsaṃyojanāni traidhātukāny eva |
satkāyāntagrāhadṛṣṭir mithyādṛṣṭiś ca dṛṣṭisaṃyojanam |
dṛṣṭiḥ śīlavrataparāmarśaś ca parāmarśasaṃyojanam |
vicikitsā satyaratneṣu saṃśayaḥ |
ye 'py anye krodhādyupakleśās [Tib. 54, a] te 'pi kleśāvaraṇam eva |
kleśaniṣyandāt kliṣṭatvāc ca |
īrṣyāmātsarye saṃyojane gṛhyete prādhānyatvāt |
sūtre 'py etad uktam |
Kauśika devamanusyāṇām īrṣyāmātsaryasaṃyojanam iti |
tāni ca katamasmin katamad āvaraṇam ity ata āha |
udvejana upekṣāṇe |
tattvasya darśane caiva |
iti |
udvego hy udvignamanḥparyāyāntarbhūtaḥ |
tata uktam anunayasaṃyojanaṃ samudvega āvaraṇam iti |
tena śarīrajīvitabhogaputrakalatradau dhyānarūpyeṣu cānunītāt saṃsāraduḥkhaiḥ piḍyamāno 'pi nodvijate tridhātoḥ |
pratighasaṃyojanam upekṣāyām āvaraṇam |
katham ity ata āha |
tena hi pratikūla iti vistaraḥ |
daurmanasyakāraṇa iti pratikūle |
pratighavastuni pratighakāraṇa upekṣā kartum anabhisaṃskartuṃ na śakyate |
anunayavastu hi pratikūlābhāvād upekṣitum aśakyaṃ yuktitaḥ |[269]
pratighavastu tu pratikūle 'pi pratigha sann upekṣitum aśakyam!
ity ata anunayād duṣṭatimātratvam pradarśayann āha ! apīti |
samādhiyamāno yadi saṁskāropekṣāṁ pratilabhate tatas tasya
cittaṁ samaṁ vartate |
yadi tasya pratighasaṁyojanam adhimātraṁ bhavati tadādhipatyatas tasmin pratighakaraṇa upekṣāṁ
na pratilabhate |
tasminn utpadyamāne 'vaśyam ātmālambanam
ālambeta |
evam anunayasaṁyojanaṁ viviktatvaṁ na labhate |
pratighasaṁyojanaṁ tu viviktam api samādhiṁ na labhate |
sapta punaḥ ceṣāṇi tattvadarcana āvaraṇam iti ! abhrānter
darśana āvaraṇam |
katham ity ajāntvā pṛcchatīty ata āha !
tāni hi yathākramam
satkāyadṛk tadāśrayaḥ ||

iti vistaraḥ |

tatra satkāyadṛṣṭim āśritya unnatacittam mānaḥ |
[p. 256 l. 22—p. 261 l. 8]
sūtrāntare 'py uktaṁ ! buddhakṣetraparicuddhādhyeṣaṇena svacittaṁ pariśodhayitavyam iti |
saṁsāranirvaṇāpratiṣṭhā avāivartikabhūmau vyākaraṇalābhasamudāgama iti katham ! ity ata āha saṁsāranirvaṇabhyām
avāivartikatvād iti |
evaṁ cittakarmanyatayā saṁsāranirvaṇayor
apratiṣṭhā bhavati ! sattvālocanād dānādipāramitātrayaphalābhyudayaparyādānān nirvāṇe na pratiṣṭhati |
viryādipāramitātrayaparyādānāt saṁsārakleśair upakliṣṭo na bhavati |
tasmāc
ca saṁsāre na pratiṣṭhati |
atha vā saṁsāranirvāṇāpratiṣṭhā hi tadavāivartikatā |
tatra
sattvālocanāt saṁsārān na vivartate kṛpayā saṁsārādānāt ![270]
prajñayā nirvāṇān na vivartate |
evaṁ saṁsāranirvaṇāpratiṣṭhito bodhisattvo 'vaivartikāyām acalabhūmau tiṣṭhati buddhayyākaraṇalābhasamudāgamaḥ |
atha vā avaivartikabhūmilambho vyākaraṇalambhaś ca samudāgamaḥ |
anāvaraṇaṁ buddhabhūmisamudāgama iti yad uta sarvasavāsanākleśajñeyāvaraṇaprahāṇāt trikāyasaṁgrīhitāyā buddhabhūmeḥ samudāgamaḥ |
tasmād anāvaraṇatvaṁ buddhabhūmisamudāgamaḥ |
tadavyuparamo bodhisaṁdarśanasamudāgama iti tadbuddhabodhiḥ avyuparataś mahākṛpāvaśād yāval lokas tāvad bodhipradarśanaṁ bodhisaṁdarśanasamudāgamaḥ |
atra ca tuṣitabhavanavasthānaṁ yonipraveco jātiḥ kāmabhogo 'bhinirāṇaṁ duṣkaracaraṇaṁ dharmacakrapravartanaṁ mahāparinirvāṇādayaś ca pradarśyante bodhisaṁdarśanasya tadabhinnatvāt |
bodhisaṁdarśanam iti dṛṣṭāntamātroktena śeṣāny api grīhitāni ||

evam iti |

arthānukūlanāmno 'bhidhānāya prati[Tib. 163, a]pattaye vā idaṁ śāstraṁ madhyavibhāgasya |
ity ucyate |

ayaṁ ca madhyāntavibhāgaḥ |
